# Phase 1: Core (local auth + TOTP, RBAC, audit, secret store, wizard basics) — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** A first administrator is created through the setup wizard, people sign in with a password plus a mandatory authenticator code, the three roles (Administrator, Operator, Auditor) see and may do different things on every API route and screen, every sensitive action lands in a hash-chained audit log that verifies, and secrets are stored with envelope encryption under a master key file.

**Architecture:** New leaf-to-top Go modules follow spec §5.4: `secrets` (envelope encryption, master key file, backup key pair), `audit` (append-only hash chain in PostgreSQL), `rbac` (pure permission decisions), `auth` (Argon2id passwords, TOTP, recovery codes, sessions, lockout, users, API tokens, rate limiters) and `setup` (setup code, wizard session, administrator bootstrap, recovery passphrase). `internal/api` stays thin: one route table where every route carries its `rbac.Permission` (checked against `x-permission` in `api/openapi.yaml`), a guard middleware (authenticate → rate limit → origin/CSRF → authorize → audit denials) and JSON handlers. The app connects to PostgreSQL as the restricted `alice_app` role (INSERT+SELECT only on `audit_log`); migrations run as the owner role. The React SPA gains a router, an API client with CSRF handling, the wizard, sign-in, users, API tokens, audit log and account screens in English and Turkish; a test-only `e2e` build adds `/test/*` endpoints (fake clock, reset) for Playwright.

**Tech Stack:** Go 1.27.1, stdlib `net/http`, pgx v5, goose v3, golang.org/x/crypto (argon2), github.com/pquerna/otp v1.5.0, golang.org/x/text, google/uuid, stdlib crypto (AES-GCM, X25519 via `crypto/ecdh`, `crypto/hkdf`), testify, testcontainers-go; React 18 + TypeScript 6 + Vite 8, Mantine 8, React Router 7, TanStack Query 5, i18next/react-i18next, Vitest 5 + Testing Library + msw 2, Playwright 1.63 + axe-core; Docker Compose with PostgreSQL 16.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-29-alice-v1-design.md` and `docs/superpowers/specs/alice-v1/` — especially `02-architecture.md` §5.4, §7.1, §7.2, §7.5, §9, §10; `04-security.md` SEC-001..SEC-009, SEC-016..SEC-026, SEC-040..SEC-052, SEC-060, SEC-061, SEC-070..SEC-075, SEC-086, SEC-088; `05-operations.md` §18.5 (steps 0–3); `06-quality.md` §22.3, §25.1–§25.4, §26.1, §26.3. `alice-v1/00-decisions.md` wins on conflict (R1–R34, plus R35 proposed by this plan in Task 25). The Phase 0 plan (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-29-phase-0-skeleton-and-ci.md`) is the style reference.

**Plan verification:** before commit, Tasks 1–24 were applied in order to a throw-away clone. All their checks passed: `golangci-lint` (default and `e2e` tags), `go test -race` unit and integration, `make i18n-check`, Vitest, `make web`, the e2e stack with the hardening check, and Playwright EN/TR with axe. Task 25's owner verification (Step 10) runs at execution time.

## Global Constraints

- Go module path is `alice`; toolchain `go 1.27.1` (pinned in `go.mod`). Every shell step assumes `export PATH="$HOME/.local/go/bin:$HOME/go/bin:$PATH"` and the repository root as working directory.
- All code, identifiers, comments, file names, commit messages and technical docs are in English. Turkish appears only as translation values in `tr.json` files.
- Commits: Conventional Commits, made with `git -c user.name="Eren Altan" -c user.email=erenaltan@gmail.com commit`, message ending with the line `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- Tests: Go stdlib `testing` plus `github.com/stretchr/testify/require` and `assert` only (no `suite`, no `mock`). Integration tests carry `//go:build integration`, start one PostgreSQL testcontainer per package through `pgtest.Main` in `TestMain`, and get a fresh database per test from `pgtest.New(t)` (Task 3). A red step in a brand-new package may fail with "no non-test Go files"; that is the expected failure.
- Runtime Go dependencies allowed after this phase: `github.com/jackc/pgx/v5`, `github.com/pressly/goose/v3`, `golang.org/x/text`, `github.com/google/uuid` (Phase 0), plus `golang.org/x/crypto` (Argon2id; spec §5.2, SEC-001, SEC-048) and `github.com/pquerna/otp` v1.5.0 (TOTP; spec R14, SEC-006; it pulls in `github.com/boombuler/barcode` for the QR image). Anything else needs a written justification in the commit body and an owner-approved spec ruling.
- Web dependencies added in this phase: `react-router` `^7.18.4` (runtime; spec §5.2 lists react-router; v8 needs React 19) and `msw` `^2.15.0` (dev; spec §22.3). `npm` resolves TypeScript 6, ESLint 10, Vite 8, Vitest 5 and Playwright 1.63 — all code must compile and lint under these versions; pin with caret ranges.
- Lint is golangci-lint v2 (`make lint`): gosec, errorlint, forbidigo, bodyclose, misspell, noctx, gofmt, goimports (`local-prefixes: alice`), build tag `integration` included. Forbidden: `fmt.Print*`, `strings.ToUpper`/`ToLower`/`Title` (Turkish dotted/dotless i; use `strings.EqualFold` for identifiers and `golang.org/x/text/cases` for user text), and — from Task 2 — `time.Now` outside `internal/platform/clock` and tests (use the injected `clock.Clock`). Every task's code must be lint-clean; no `//nolint` without a reason comment.
- Module import rules (spec §5.4) are enforced by `internal/archtest`; this phase adds rules for `internal/setup` and `internal/testutil`. `platform` stays a leaf.
- Never set `InsecureSkipVerify`. All SQL is parameterized pgx (no string-built SQL, SEC-060).
- No secret value (passwords, recovery passphrase, TOTP seeds, recovery codes, API tokens, setup code, master key, DEKs, database passwords) may appear in logs, error messages, audit details or HTTP responses. The only exceptions are the documented one-time responses: the TOTP enrollment secret and QR code, freshly issued recovery codes, and a newly created API token (SEC-007, SEC-008, SEC-026, S-02).
- Passwords (SEC-001..SEC-005): Argon2id m=64 MiB, t=3, p=2, 16-byte salt, 32-byte key, PHC string; 12–128 characters after NFKC normalization, no composition rules; rejected if in the bundled 100,000-entry breached list; generic "Incorrect username or password." with a dummy verify for unknown users; rehash on login when parameters are weaker.
- MFA (SEC-006..SEC-009): TOTP SHA-1, 6 digits, 30 s, ±1 step, last accepted step stored and the same or an earlier step rejected; 160-bit seeds stored envelope-encrypted and shown once; 10 single-use 80-bit recovery codes, Argon2id-hashed; MFA mandatory for every local account; step-up (fresh TOTP within 5 minutes) for user/role changes, API token creation, recovery-code regeneration and the backup recovery passphrase.
- Sessions (SEC-018, SEC-019): 256-bit random token, only its SHA-256 stored; cookie `__Host-alice_session` (setup wizard: `__Host-alice_setup`), `HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Strict; Path=/`, no Domain; idle 30 min, absolute 12 h (wizard session: 60 min idle); new session ID after login, MFA, step-up and password change; logout, password change, role change, disable and MFA reset revoke server-side.
- CSRF (SEC-024): every non-GET/HEAD request with cookie authentication must send `X-CSRF-Token` = base64url(HMAC-SHA256(K_csrf, session token)) and pass the `Origin`/`Sec-Fetch-Site` same-origin check; API tokens (`Authorization: Bearer alice_…`) are exempt and never accepted via cookie (SEC-025).
- Rate limits (SEC-020..SEC-022, R33): login 10/min per IP; per account 5 consecutive failures → progressive delay 1, 2, 4, 8, 16 s (cap 60 s), 10 → 15 min lockout, never longer; MFA verify and step-up 5/min per IP; 300 requests/min per session or token; 60/min per IP when unauthenticated; setup code 5 wrong attempts per IP → 15 min; `429` with `Retry-After`; `/livez` and `/healthz` exempt; `X-Forwarded-For` honoured only from `ALICE_TRUSTED_PROXIES`.
- Request bodies are capped at 1 MiB and decoded with `DisallowUnknownFields` (SEC-023, SEC-061). HTTP server timeouts stay `ReadHeaderTimeout` 10 s, `ReadTimeout` 30 s, `WriteTimeout` 60 s, `IdleTimeout` 120 s.
- TLS, security headers and CSP are unchanged from Phase 0 (SEC-030..SEC-034): minimum TLS 1.2, curves X25519MLKEM768/X25519/P-256; HSTS, nosniff, `X-Frame-Options: DENY`, `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`, COOP/CORP same-origin, Permissions-Policy; SPA CSP with per-request style nonce; API CSP `default-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'`; `Cache-Control: no-store` on API responses.
- Secrets (SEC-040..SEC-043): per-secret random 256-bit DEK, AES-256-GCM with 96-bit random nonces, DEK wrapped by the master key with AES-256-GCM; AAD payload `table/row_id/column`, wrap layer adds `/key_version`; master key 256-bit in `<ALICE_KEYS_DIR>/master.key` (default `/opt/alice/keys`), mode 0400, Alice refuses to start on wider permissions, on a missing file when the database already has key versions, or on a mismatching file; sub-keys only via HKDF-SHA256 with labels `alice/hwm/v1`, `alice/job/v1`, `alice/audit-hmac/v1`, `alice/csrf/v1`.
- Backup key (R4, SEC-048, SEC-086): the wizard generates an X25519 key pair; the private key is stored only wrapped by the recovery passphrase (Argon2id m=64 MiB, t=3, p=4, 16-byte salt, AES-256-GCM); the passphrase (at least 16 characters, typed twice) is never stored.
- Audit (SEC-070, SEC-071, R33): every authentication event, authorization denial (403) and mutating operation writes one row in the same transaction as the change where possible; `hash = SHA-256(prev_hash || canonical_json(row without hash))`, first `prev_hash` = 32 zero bytes, serialized by `pg_advisory_xact_lock`; the app role `alice_app` has only INSERT and SELECT on `audit_log`, and triggers reject UPDATE, DELETE and TRUNCATE; details are allowlisted key/value strings that can never carry secrets.
- Roles (SEC-016, SEC-017, R3, R10): Administrator everything; Operator view and operate but never users, settings or the audit log; Auditor read-only plus audit log; every route declares one permission in the route table (deny by default) and the same value as `x-permission` in `api/openapi.yaml`; authorization uses the role read from the database on every request; the last enabled local Administrator cannot be disabled or demoted.
- API JSON: `snake_case`, `application/json; charset=utf-8`, UTC RFC 3339 timestamps, UUID ids. Error body `{"error":{"code","reason","message_key","message","request_id","retryable","params"?,"details"?}}`; codes `ALC-<AREA>-<NNNN>`, never reused; every code has `errors.<reason>.title|cause|action` in `locales/common/{en,tr}.json` and a section in `docs/troubleshooting.md` (tests enforce both).
- i18n (spec §10): every user-visible string is a key present in both English and Turkish (`make i18n-check`); no sentence assembly from fragments and no suffixes attached to placeholders; placeholders are `{{name}}` without spaces; a Turkish value identical to English must be allow-listed in `.i18n-identical.json`. The English and Turkish UI copy listed in Tasks 20–24 is exact.
- Accessibility (§26.1 item 9): every new screen has no serious or critical axe violation in English and Turkish, is usable by keyboard, and works at 1280 px and 1920 px.
- Frontend never uses `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` or `toUpperCase`/`toLowerCase` (ESLint rules from Phase 0).
- Container: `alice` runs as UID/GID `10001:10001`, read-only root filesystem, all capabilities dropped, `no-new-privileges`, `pids_limit: 256`; PostgreSQL has no published port. Host port variable is `ALICE_PORT`; keys directory `/opt/alice/keys`; plain-HTTP health listener `127.0.0.1:8081` serves only `/livez` and `/healthz`.
- The `e2e` build tag (test endpoints under `/test/`) is used only for the Playwright stack; release images are built without it, and a test proves the release build has no test handler.
- If a GitHub Action major version used below is deprecated when you implement, bump it to the current major and say so in the commit message.

## Review Focus

1. **Audit hash chain round trip through PostgreSQL.** Entries whose details contain Turkish text ("İzmir", "ığdır"), whose client IP arrives as an IPv4-mapped IPv6 address (`::ffff:10.0.0.7`), whose timestamp has nanoseconds, and whose details have many keys must still verify as intact after being read back (PostgreSQL truncates to microseconds, `jsonb` reorders keys, `inet` renders addresses its own way). Expected: "The audit log is intact." Test in Task 6.
2. **Losing the last administrator.** Two administrators demote each other at the same moment, or the only administrator is disabled. Expected: at least one enabled local administrator always remains; exactly one of the two concurrent requests fails with `ALC-USR-0002`. Test in Task 11.
3. **Reusing a TOTP code.** Right after enrolling, a person signs in again within the same 30-second window with the same code; or a code is replayed. Expected: the reused code is rejected, the next code is accepted, and a code from the previous step is accepted only if it is newer than the last used one. Tests in Tasks 9 and 10.
4. **Master key file lost or swapped.** The container is recreated with an empty keys volume (or another installation's key) while the database already holds encrypted data. Expected: Alice refuses to start with a clear message and never writes a new key over existing data. Test in Task 5.
5. **Usernames with case and Turkish letters.** "Admin" signs into the account "admin"; creating "ADMIN" when "admin" exists is refused; "İsmail" is rejected with a clear validation message instead of creating a second, lookalike account; Turkish display names are stored exactly as typed. Tests in Tasks 10 and 11.

### Task 1: Phase 0 carry fixes: fatal DB errors, SPA deep links, i18n-check hardening

Three small fixes carried from Phase 0 (carries 3, 4, 5 and the identical-value half of 9). They share one
reviewer gate because each is a few lines with its own regression test.

**Files:**
- Modify: `internal/platform/db/db.go` (sentinels, SQLSTATE constants, `classify`, call in `Open`)
- Modify: `internal/platform/db/db_integration_test.go` (append two tests)
- Modify: `cmd/alice/main.go:110-114` (only say "not ready after 60s" for unreachable databases)
- Modify: `internal/api/spa.go` (replace the `path.Ext` rule with `isAssetPath`)
- Modify: `internal/api/server_test.go` (append two tests)
- Modify: `tools/i18n-check/check.go` (whole file replaced), `tools/i18n-check/check_test.go` (whole file replaced)
- Create: `locales/common/.i18n-identical.json`, `web/src/locales/.i18n-identical.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing new.
- Produces (`alice/internal/platform/db`):
  - `var ErrAuthFailed = errors.New("the database rejected the user name or password")`
  - `var ErrDatabaseMissing = errors.New("the database does not exist")`
  - `db.Open` returns, without retrying, `fmt.Errorf("the database server at %s rejected the user name or password; check the database URL: %w", where, ErrAuthFailed)` for SQLSTATE 28P01/28000 and `fmt.Errorf("the database named in the database URL does not exist on %s: %w", where, ErrDatabaseMissing)` for 3D000. All other errors are retried as before and end in `ErrUnreachable`.
- Produces (`internal/api`): `func isAssetPath(name string) bool` — a missing file is a 404 only under `assets/` or for `favicon.ico`, `favicon.svg`, `robots.txt`, `manifest.webmanifest`; every other path serves `index.html`.
- Produces (`tools/i18n-check`): new `Problem` messages `"placeholder must not contain spaces: {{ name }}"` and `"value is identical to en; translate it or allow-list the key in .i18n-identical.json"`; optional allow-list file `.i18n-identical.json` (JSON array of keys) per catalog directory; files starting with `.` are never catalogs.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing database tests**

Append to `internal/platform/db/db_integration_test.go` (the imports `context`, `errors`, `net/url`, `time`, `assert`, `require` and `db` already exist in that file):

```go

func TestOpenFailsFastOnWrongPassword(t *testing.T) {
	u, err := url.Parse(adminURL)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	u.User = url.UserPassword("alice", "wrong-pw-9f3a")
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	start := time.Now()
	pool, err := db.Open(ctx, u.String())
	require.Error(t, err)
	assert.Nil(t, pool)
	assert.True(t, errors.Is(err, db.ErrAuthFailed), err.Error())
	assert.False(t, errors.Is(err, db.ErrUnreachable))
	assert.NotContains(t, err.Error(), "wrong-pw-9f3a")
	assert.NotContains(t, err.Error(), "s3cret-pw")
	assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "rejected the user name or password")
	assert.Less(t, time.Since(start), 5*time.Second, "a rejected password must not be retried")
}

func TestOpenFailsFastOnMissingDatabase(t *testing.T) {
	u, err := url.Parse(adminURL)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	u.Path = "/no_such_database"
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	start := time.Now()
	_, err = db.Open(ctx, u.String())
	require.Error(t, err)
	assert.True(t, errors.Is(err, db.ErrDatabaseMissing), err.Error())
	assert.NotContains(t, err.Error(), "s3cret-pw")
	assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "does not exist")
	assert.Less(t, time.Since(start), 5*time.Second, "a missing database must not be retried")
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Classify fatal connection errors in `db.Open`**

In `internal/platform/db/db.go` replace the `var (...)` block with:

```go
var (
	// ErrUnreachable means the database cannot be reached.
	ErrUnreachable = errors.New("database is not reachable")
	// ErrMigrationsPending means the schema is older than this binary expects.
	ErrMigrationsPending = errors.New("database migrations are pending")
	// ErrAuthFailed means the server rejected the credentials; retrying cannot help.
	ErrAuthFailed = errors.New("the database rejected the user name or password")
	// ErrDatabaseMissing means the named database does not exist; retrying cannot help.
	ErrDatabaseMissing = errors.New("the database does not exist")
)
```

Replace the `const (...)` block with:

```go
const (
	retryInterval = 500 * time.Millisecond

	// undefinedTable is the PostgreSQL SQLSTATE for a missing relation.
	undefinedTable = "42P01"
	// invalidPassword and invalidAuthorization are the SQLSTATEs for rejected credentials.
	invalidPassword      = "28P01"
	invalidAuthorization = "28000"
	// invalidCatalogName is the SQLSTATE for a database that does not exist.
	invalidCatalogName = "3D000"
)
```

In `Open`, replace

```go
		if err == nil {
			return pool, nil
		}
		select {
```

with

```go
		if err == nil {
			return pool, nil
		}
		if fatal := classify(err, where); fatal != nil {
			pool.Close()
			return nil, fatal
		}
		select {
```

and add this function directly above `// Migrate applies all pending migrations.`:

```go
// classify turns errors that retrying cannot fix into a clear, final error.
// It returns nil for errors that may be transient (server starting, network).
// The driver message is never included: it could echo connection details.
func classify(err error, where string) error {
	var pgErr *pgconn.PgError
	if !errors.As(err, &pgErr) {
		return nil
	}
	switch pgErr.Code {
	case invalidPassword, invalidAuthorization:
		return fmt.Errorf("the database server at %s rejected the user name or password; check the database URL: %w", where, ErrAuthFailed)
	case invalidCatalogName:
		return fmt.Errorf("the database named in the database URL does not exist on %s: %w", where, ErrDatabaseMissing)
	default:
		return nil
	}
}
```

(`pgconn` is already imported. "The database system is starting up" is SQLSTATE 57P03 and stays retryable.)

In `cmd/alice/main.go` (`serve`) replace

```go
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("database not ready after %s: %w", dbStartupBudget, err)
	}
```

with

```go
	if err != nil {
		if errors.Is(err, db.ErrUnreachable) {
			return fmt.Errorf("database not ready after %s: %w", dbStartupBudget, err)
		}
		return err
	}
```

```go
// rootFiles are files a browser or crawler requests by convention. When they
// are missing the answer is 404, not the SPA.
var rootFiles = map[string]bool{
	"favicon.ico":          true,
	"favicon.svg":          true,
	"robots.txt":           true,
	"manifest.webmanifest": true,
}

// isAssetPath reports whether a missing file must be a 404. Everything else is
// a client-side route (even with a dot in it, such as /users/a.b) and gets index.html.
func isAssetPath(name string) bool {
	return strings.HasPrefix(name, "assets/") || rootFiles[name]
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Rewrite the checker**

Replace `tools/i18n-check/check.go` with:

```go
package main

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"regexp"
	"slices"
	"sort"
	"strings"

	"alice/internal/i18n"
)

// Problem is one catalog inconsistency.
type Problem struct {
	File, Key, Msg string
}

// allowListFile optionally lists keys whose translation may equal the base value
// (brand names, technical terms). Files starting with "." are never catalogs.
const allowListFile = ".i18n-identical.json"

var (
	// placeholderRE matches the only placeholder form the backend substitutes: {{name}}.
	placeholderRE = regexp.MustCompile(`\{\{([A-Za-z0-9_.]+)\}\}`)
	// spacedRE matches placeholders with inner whitespace, which i18n.T would not substitute.
	spacedRE = regexp.MustCompile(`\{\{\s*[A-Za-z0-9_.]+\s*\}\}`)
)

// CheckDir compares every <code>.json in fsys against <base>.json.
func CheckDir(fsys fs.FS, base string) ([]Problem, error) {
	baseFile := base + ".json"
	baseMsgs, err := load(fsys, baseFile)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	allowed, err := loadAllowList(fsys)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var problems []Problem
	for k, v := range baseMsgs {
		problems = append(problems, valueProblems(baseFile, k, v)...)
	}
	files, err := fs.Glob(fsys, "*.json")
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	for _, f := range files {
		if f == baseFile || strings.HasPrefix(f, ".") {
			continue
		}
		code := strings.TrimSuffix(f, ".json")
		msgs, err := load(fsys, f)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		for k, bv := range baseMsgs {
			v, ok := msgs[k]
			if !ok {
				problems = append(problems, Problem{f, k, "missing key"})
				continue
			}
			if vp := valueProblems(f, k, v); len(vp) > 0 {
				problems = append(problems, vp...)
				continue
			}
			switch {
			case !slices.Equal(placeholders(bv), placeholders(v)):
				problems = append(problems, Problem{f, k, fmt.Sprintf("placeholders differ: %s has %v, %s has %v", base, placeholders(bv), code, placeholders(v))})
			case v == bv && !allowed[k]:
				problems = append(problems, Problem{f, k, "value is identical to " + base + "; translate it or allow-list the key in " + allowListFile})
			}
		}
		for k := range msgs {
			if _, ok := baseMsgs[k]; !ok {
				problems = append(problems, Problem{f, k, "key not present in " + baseFile})
			}
		}
	}
	sort.Slice(problems, func(i, j int) bool {
		if problems[i].File != problems[j].File {
			return problems[i].File < problems[j].File
		}
		if problems[i].Key != problems[j].Key {
			return problems[i].Key < problems[j].Key
		}
		return problems[i].Msg < problems[j].Msg
	})
	return problems, nil
}

// valueProblems reports problems that depend on one value only.
func valueProblems(file, key, v string) []Problem {
	if strings.TrimSpace(v) == "" {
		return []Problem{{file, key, "value is empty"}}
	}
	var out []Problem
	for _, m := range spacedRE.FindAllString(v, -1) {
		if !placeholderRE.MatchString(m) {
			out = append(out, Problem{file, key, "placeholder must not contain spaces: " + m})
		}
	}
	return out
}

func loadAllowList(fsys fs.FS) (map[string]bool, error) {
	data, err := fs.ReadFile(fsys, allowListFile)
	if errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
		return map[string]bool{}, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("read %s: %w", allowListFile, err)
	}
	var keys []string
	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &keys); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s must be a JSON array of keys: %w", allowListFile, err)
	}
	out := make(map[string]bool, len(keys))
	for _, k := range keys {
		out[k] = true
	}
	return out, nil
}

func load(fsys fs.FS, name string) (map[string]string, error) {
	data, err := fs.ReadFile(fsys, name)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("read %s: %w", name, err)
	}
	m, err := i18n.Flatten(data)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", name, err)
	}
	return m, nil
}

func placeholders(s string) []string {
	var out []string
	for _, m := range placeholderRE.FindAllStringSubmatch(s, -1) {
		if !slices.Contains(out, m[1]) {
			out = append(out, m[1])
		}
	}
	sort.Strings(out)
	return out
}
```


```go
func TestIsUniqueViolation(t *testing.T) {
	dup := &pgconn.PgError{Code: "23505"}
	assert.True(t, db.IsUniqueViolation(dup))
	assert.True(t, db.IsUniqueViolation(fmt.Errorf("insert user: %w", dup)))
	assert.False(t, db.IsUniqueViolation(&pgconn.PgError{Code: "23503"}))
	assert.False(t, db.IsUniqueViolation(errors.New("23505")))
	assert.False(t, db.IsUniqueViolation(nil))
```

```go
//go:build integration

package db_test

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/platform/db"
	"alice/internal/platform/settings"
	"alice/migrations"
)

// migratedPool returns a pool on a fresh, fully migrated database.
func migratedPool(t *testing.T) *pgxpool.Pool {
	t.Helper()
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 60*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	pool, err := db.Open(ctx, freshDatabaseURL(t))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	t.Cleanup(pool.Close)
	require.NoError(t, db.Migrate(ctx, pool, migrations.FS))
	return pool
}

func countSettings(t *testing.T, pool *pgxpool.Pool) int {
	t.Helper()
	var n int
	require.NoError(t, pool.QueryRow(context.Background(), "SELECT count(*) FROM settings").Scan(&n))
	return n
}

func TestInTxCommitsOnSuccess(t *testing.T) {
	pool := migratedPool(t)
	err := db.InTx(context.Background(), pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		_, err := tx.Exec(context.Background(), "INSERT INTO settings (key, value) VALUES ('a', '1')")
		return err
	})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, 1, countSettings(t, pool))
}

func TestInTxRollsBackOnError(t *testing.T) {
	pool := migratedPool(t)
	boom := errors.New("boom")
	err := db.InTx(context.Background(), pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		if _, err := tx.Exec(context.Background(), "INSERT INTO settings (key, value) VALUES ('a', '1')"); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return boom
	})
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, boom)
	assert.Equal(t, 0, countSettings(t, pool))
}

func TestInTxRollsBackAndRepanicsOnPanic(t *testing.T) {
	pool := migratedPool(t)
	assert.PanicsWithValue(t, "kaboom", func() {
		_ = db.InTx(context.Background(), pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
			_, _ = tx.Exec(context.Background(), "INSERT INTO settings (key, value) VALUES ('a', '1')")
			panic("kaboom")
		})
	})
	assert.Equal(t, 0, countSettings(t, pool))
}

func TestUniqueViolationIsDetected(t *testing.T) {
	pool := migratedPool(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	_, err := pool.Exec(ctx, "INSERT INTO settings (key, value) VALUES ('dup', '1')")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = pool.Exec(ctx, "INSERT INTO settings (key, value) VALUES ('dup', '2')")
	assert.True(t, db.IsUniqueViolation(err))
}

type checkpoint struct {
	Seq  int64  `json:"seq"`
	Hash string `json:"hash"`
}

func TestSettingsRoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
	pool := migratedPool(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 30, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

	var missing string
	found, err := settings.Get(ctx, pool, "locale.default", &missing)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.False(t, found)

	require.NoError(t, settings.Set(ctx, pool, "locale.default", "tr", now))
	var lang string
	found, err = settings.Get(ctx, pool, "locale.default", &lang)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.True(t, found)
	assert.Equal(t, "tr", lang)

	require.NoError(t, settings.Set(ctx, pool, "audit.checkpoint", checkpoint{Seq: 7, Hash: "ab"}, now))
	require.NoError(t, settings.Set(ctx, pool, "audit.checkpoint", checkpoint{Seq: 9, Hash: "İzmir"}, now.Add(time.Hour)))
	var cp checkpoint
	found, err = settings.Get(ctx, pool, "audit.checkpoint", &cp)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.True(t, found)
	assert.Equal(t, checkpoint{Seq: 9, Hash: "İzmir"}, cp)

	var updated time.Time
	require.NoError(t, pool.QueryRow(ctx, "SELECT updated_at FROM settings WHERE key = 'audit.checkpoint'").Scan(&updated))
	assert.True(t, updated.UTC().Equal(now.Add(time.Hour)))

	require.NoError(t, settings.Delete(ctx, pool, "audit.checkpoint"))
	found, err = settings.Get(ctx, pool, "audit.checkpoint", &cp)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.False(t, found)
	require.NoError(t, settings.Delete(ctx, pool, "audit.checkpoint"), "deleting a missing key is not an error")
}

func TestSettingsWorkInsideTransaction(t *testing.T) {
	pool := migratedPool(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	err := db.InTx(ctx, pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		if err := settings.Set(ctx, tx, "k", map[string]int{"n": 1}, time.Now().UTC()); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return errors.New("roll back")
	})
	require.Error(t, err)
	var v map[string]int
	found, err := settings.Get(ctx, pool, "k", &v)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.False(t, found)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement transactions and settings**

`internal/platform/db/tx.go`:
```go
package db

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgconn"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
)

// uniqueViolation is the PostgreSQL SQLSTATE for a duplicate key.
const uniqueViolation = "23505"

// Querier is satisfied by *pgxpool.Pool, *pgxpool.Conn and pgx.Tx, so query
// helpers work both inside and outside a transaction.
type Querier interface {
	Exec(ctx context.Context, sql string, args ...any) (pgconn.CommandTag, error)
	Query(ctx context.Context, sql string, args ...any) (pgx.Rows, error)
	QueryRow(ctx context.Context, sql string, args ...any) pgx.Row
}

var (
	_ Querier = (*pgxpool.Pool)(nil)
	_ Querier = (pgx.Tx)(nil)
)

// InTx runs fn in a read-committed transaction. It commits when fn returns nil
// and rolls back when fn returns an error or panics (the panic is re-raised).
func InTx(ctx context.Context, pool *pgxpool.Pool, fn func(tx pgx.Tx) error) (err error) {
	tx, err := pool.BeginTx(ctx, pgx.TxOptions{IsoLevel: pgx.ReadCommitted})
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("begin transaction: %w", err)
	}
	defer func() {
		if p := recover(); p != nil {
			_ = tx.Rollback(context.WithoutCancel(ctx))
			panic(p)
		}
		if err != nil {
			_ = tx.Rollback(context.WithoutCancel(ctx))
		}
	}()
	if err = fn(tx); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err = tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("commit transaction: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

// IsUniqueViolation reports whether err is a PostgreSQL unique-constraint violation.
func IsUniqueViolation(err error) bool {
	var pgErr *pgconn.PgError
	return errors.As(err, &pgErr) && pgErr.Code == uniqueViolation
}
```

`internal/platform/settings/settings.go`:
```go
// Package settings reads and writes application settings stored as JSON in the
// settings table. Values here are never secret; secret settings use envelope
// encryption in their own columns.
package settings

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"alice/internal/platform/db"
)

// Get decodes the value of key into dst. It reports false (and no error) when
// the key does not exist.
func Get(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier, key string, dst any) (bool, error) {
	var raw []byte
	err := q.QueryRow(ctx, "SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key = $1", key).Scan(&raw)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return false, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("read setting %s: %w", key, err)
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, dst); err != nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("decode setting %s: %w", key, err)
	}
	return true, nil
}

// Set stores v (encoded as JSON) under key, replacing any previous value.
func Set(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier, key string, v any, now time.Time) error {
	raw, err := json.Marshal(v)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("encode setting %s: %w", key, err)
	}
	_, err = q.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO settings (key, value, updated_at) VALUES ($1, $2::jsonb, $3)
ON CONFLICT (key) DO UPDATE SET value = EXCLUDED.value, updated_at = EXCLUDED.updated_at`,
		key, string(raw), now.UTC())
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("write setting %s: %w", key, err)
	}
	return nil
}

// Delete removes key. Deleting a missing key is not an error.
func Delete(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier, key string) error {
	if _, err := q.Exec(ctx, "DELETE FROM settings WHERE key = $1", key); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("delete setting %s: %w", key, err)
	}
	return nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the redacting logger**

`internal/platform/logging/logging.go`:
```go
// Package logging builds Alice's structured logger. Every record passes a
// redacting handler so secret values never reach stdout (SEC-050).
package logging

import (
	"context"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"strings"
)

// Redacted replaces every value whose attribute key looks secret.
const Redacted = "[redacted]"

// New returns a JSON logger writing to w at level, with redaction applied.
func New(w io.Writer, level slog.Leveler) *slog.Logger {
	return slog.New(NewRedactingHandler(slog.NewJSONHandler(w, &slog.HandlerOptions{Level: level})))
}

// NewRedactingHandler wraps h so that attributes with secret-looking keys are
// replaced by Redacted, including inside groups and attributes added with With.
func NewRedactingHandler(h slog.Handler) slog.Handler {
	return &redactingHandler{next: h}
}

type redactingHandler struct {
	next slog.Handler
}

func (h *redactingHandler) Enabled(ctx context.Context, l slog.Level) bool {
	return h.next.Enabled(ctx, l)
}

func (h *redactingHandler) Handle(ctx context.Context, r slog.Record) error {
	out := slog.NewRecord(r.Time, r.Level, r.Message, r.PC)
	r.Attrs(func(a slog.Attr) bool {
		out.AddAttrs(redact(a))
		return true
	})
	return h.next.Handle(ctx, out)
}

func (h *redactingHandler) WithAttrs(attrs []slog.Attr) slog.Handler {
	clean := make([]slog.Attr, len(attrs))
	for i, a := range attrs {
		clean[i] = redact(a)
	}
	return &redactingHandler{next: h.next.WithAttrs(clean)}
}

func (h *redactingHandler) WithGroup(name string) slog.Handler {
	return &redactingHandler{next: h.next.WithGroup(name)}
}

// redact resolves LogValuers first, so a type that renders itself as a group
// is still inspected key by key.
func redact(a slog.Attr) slog.Attr {
	a.Value = a.Value.Resolve()
	if secretKey(a.Key) {
		return slog.String(a.Key, Redacted)
	}
	if a.Value.Kind() == slog.KindGroup {
		group := a.Value.Group()
		clean := make([]any, len(group))
		for i, g := range group {
			clean[i] = redact(g)
		}
		return slog.Group(a.Key, clean...)
	}
	return a
}

var secretParts = []string{"password", "secret", "token", "authorization", "cookie", "passphrase", "totp"}

// secretKey matches on the ASCII-folded key (strings.ToLower is banned
// repo-wide because of Turkish casing; keys are ASCII identifiers).
func secretKey(key string) bool {
	k := foldASCII(key)
	for _, p := range secretParts {
		if strings.Contains(k, p) {
			return true
		}
	}
	return strings.HasPrefix(k, "private") || k == "key" || strings.HasSuffix(k, "_key")
}

func foldASCII(s string) string {
	b := []byte(s)
	for i, c := range b {
		if 'A' <= c && c <= 'Z' {
			b[i] = c + ('a' - 'A')
		}
	}
	return string(b)
}
```

```go
package clock_test

import (
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"

	"alice/internal/platform/clock"
)

func TestOffsetShiftsNowOnly(t *testing.T) {
	start := time.Date(2026, 9, 30, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)
	base := clock.NewFake(start)
	o := clock.NewOffset(base)
	var _ clock.Clock = o

	assert.Equal(t, start, o.Now())
	o.Advance(31 * time.Minute)
	assert.Equal(t, start.Add(31*time.Minute), o.Now())
	o.Advance(-time.Minute)
	assert.Equal(t, start.Add(30*time.Minute), o.Now())

	ch := o.After(time.Second)
	base.Advance(time.Second)
	select {
	case <-ch:
	default:
		t.Fatal("After must fire on the base clock")
	}
	assert.Equal(t, start.Add(30*time.Minute+time.Second), o.Now())

	o.Reset()
	assert.Equal(t, start.Add(time.Second), o.Now())
}

func TestOffsetIsSafeForConcurrentUse(t *testing.T) {
	o := clock.NewOffset(clock.NewFake(time.Unix(0, 0)))
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	for range 50 {
		wg.Add(2)
		go func() { defer wg.Done(); o.Advance(time.Second) }()
		go func() { defer wg.Done(); _ = o.Now() }()
	}
	wg.Wait()
	assert.Equal(t, time.Unix(50, 0).UTC(), o.Now())
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Implement the offset clock**

`internal/platform/clock/offset.go`:
```go
package clock

import (
	"sync"
	"time"
)

// Offset is a clock shifted by an adjustable offset from a base clock. Only the
// test-only e2e build uses it (POST /test/clock); release builds use Real.
// Timers and tickers are not shifted: they measure durations on the base clock.
type Offset struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	base Clock
	off  time.Duration
}

// NewOffset returns an Offset with a zero offset.
func NewOffset(base Clock) *Offset { return &Offset{base: base} }

// Now returns the base time plus the offset.
func (o *Offset) Now() time.Time {
	o.mu.Lock()
	defer o.mu.Unlock()
	return o.base.Now().Add(o.off)
}

// After delegates to the base clock.
func (o *Offset) After(d time.Duration) <-chan time.Time { return o.base.After(d) }

// NewTicker delegates to the base clock.
func (o *Offset) NewTicker(d time.Duration) Ticker { return o.base.NewTicker(d) }

// Advance moves Now forward by d (backward for negative d).
func (o *Offset) Advance(d time.Duration) {
	o.mu.Lock()
	defer o.mu.Unlock()
	o.off += d
}

// Reset sets the offset back to zero.
func (o *Offset) Reset() {
	o.mu.Lock()
	defer o.mu.Unlock()
	o.off = 0
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Implement validation errors and their translations**

`internal/platform/validate/validate.go`:
```go
// Package validate carries field-level validation failures from services to the
// API, which returns them as error details with translatable codes.
package validate

import "strings"

// Codes are also the message keys in the shared locale catalog.
const (
	Required   = "validation.required"
	TooShort   = "validation.too_short"    // params: min
	TooLong    = "validation.too_long"     // params: max
	Pattern    = "validation.pattern"      // the value has characters or a form that is not allowed
	OneOf      = "validation.one_of"       // params: values ("a, b")
	OutOfRange = "validation.out_of_range" // params: min, max
	Email      = "validation.email"
)

// FieldError is one invalid field.
type FieldError struct {
	Field  string            `json:"field"`
	Code   string            `json:"code"`
	Params map[string]string `json:"params,omitempty"`
}

// Errors is a list of field errors. It implements error; callers detect it with
// errors.As(err, &validate.Errors{}).
type Errors []FieldError

// Error summarises the failures without values (values may be secrets).
func (e Errors) Error() string {
	parts := make([]string, len(e))
	for i, f := range e {
		parts[i] = f.Field + " " + f.Code
	}
	return "validation failed: " + strings.Join(parts, "; ")
}

// Add appends a field error.
func (e *Errors) Add(field, code string, params map[string]string) {
	*e = append(*e, FieldError{Field: field, Code: code, Params: params})
}

// Err returns nil when there are no failures, else the list itself.
func (e Errors) Err() error {
	if len(e) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	return e
}
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Forbid `time.Now` outside the clock package**

In `.golangci.yml`, under `linters.settings.forbidigo.forbid`, add after the `fmt.Print` entry:
```yaml
        - pattern: ^time\.Now$
          msg: "use the injected clock.Clock (time-dependent logic must be testable)"
```
and under `linters.exclusions.rules` add after the existing `_test\.go` rule:
```yaml
      - path: (_test\.go|internal/platform/clock/)
        linters: [forbidigo]
        text: "use the injected clock.Clock"
```
The exclusion is narrowed by `text`, so `fmt.Print*` and `strings.ToUpper` stay forbidden in tests and in
the clock package. The only production `time.Now` today is `clock.Real.Now` (allowed). Code that needs the
current time takes a `clock.Clock` (or a `now time.Time` parameter, like `tlscert.EnsureSelfSigned`).

```go

import "time"

// F is a probe.
func F() time.Time { return time.Now() }
```

### Task 3: Core schema, separate DB roles, config and test database helper

Creates every Phase 1 table, the `alice_app` role with least privilege (R33: INSERT+SELECT only on
`audit_log`), the owner/app split in configuration, Compose and `cmd/alice`, and the `pgtest` helper that all
later integration tests use (carry 8).

**Files:**
- Create: `migrations/00002_core.sql`, `migrations/roles_integration_test.go`
- Create: `internal/testutil/pgtest/pgtest.go`
- Create: `deploy/postgres/initdb/10-alice-app-role.sh` (mode 0755)
- Modify: `internal/platform/config/config.go` (whole file), `internal/platform/config/config_test.go` (append)
- Modify: `internal/platform/db/db_integration_test.go:119-122` (newer-migration test uses `99999_more.sql`)
- Modify: `cmd/alice/main.go` (`migrate`, new `applyMigrations`, `dbStartupError`, `serve` start), `cmd/alice/main_test.go`
- Modify: `deploy/docker-compose.yml`, `deploy/dev/init-env.sh` (whole file), `Makefile` (`reset` target)
- Modify: `internal/archtest/arch_test.go` (rules for `setup` and `testutil`, api may import `setup`), `.gitleaks.toml`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `db.Open`, `db.Migrate`, `db.ErrUnreachable`, `db.ErrAuthFailed` (Task 1), `migrations.FS`.
- Produces:
  - Tables `users`, `user_mfa`, `recovery_codes`, `sessions`, `api_tokens`, `setup_sessions`, `master_key_versions`, `audit_log` exactly as in `migrations/00002_core.sql` below; role `alice_app` with SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE on `settings, users, user_mfa, recovery_codes, sessions, api_tokens, setup_sessions`, SELECT/INSERT on `master_key_versions, audit_log`, SELECT on `goose_db_version`; `audit_log` UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE raise `audit_log is append-only` even for the owner. Every later migration that adds a table must grant it to `alice_app` (`TestAppRoleGrants` fails otherwise).
  - `config.Config` fields `MigrateDatabaseURL string` (`ALICE_MIGRATE_DATABASE_URL`) and `TrustedProxies []netip.Prefix` (`ALICE_TRUSTED_PROXIES`, comma-separated CIDRs or single IPs; bad entry → `Load` error `ALICE_TRUSTED_PROXIES entry "<x>" is not an IP address or CIDR`); `func (c Config) MigrationURL() string`; `func (c Config) SeparateMigrationRole() bool`.
  - `cmd/alice`: `func applyMigrations(ctx context.Context, cfg config.Config, openBudget time.Duration) error` (short-lived owner pool) and `func dbStartupError(err error, budget time.Duration) error`; `serve` migrates as the owner, then serves with an `alice_app` pool (`cfg.DatabaseURL`), and logs a warning when `!cfg.SeparateMigrationRole()`.
  - `alice/internal/testutil/pgtest` (build tag `integration`): `func Main(m *testing.M) int`; `type DB struct { Owner, App *pgxpool.Pool; OwnerURL, AppURL string }`; `func New(t *testing.T) *DB`; `const AppPassword = "app-test-pw"`. Usage in any package: `func TestMain(m *testing.M) { os.Exit(pgtest.Main(m)) }` then `d := pgtest.New(t)`; application code under test gets `d.App`.
  - Compose: postgres receives `ALICE_APP_DB_PASSWORD` and runs `deploy/postgres/initdb/10-alice-app-role.sh` on an empty volume; alice gets `ALICE_DATABASE_URL` (alice_app) and `ALICE_MIGRATE_DATABASE_URL` (owner `alice`). `make reset` deletes the local volumes and starts fresh.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing role and grant tests**

`migrations/roles_integration_test.go`:
```go
//go:build integration

package migrations_test

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"os"
	"sort"
	"testing"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgconn"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/testutil/pgtest"
)

func TestMain(m *testing.M) { os.Exit(pgtest.Main(m)) }

// grants returns table -> sorted privileges held by alice_app in the public schema.
func grants(t *testing.T, d *pgtest.DB) map[string][]string {
	t.Helper()
	rows, err := d.Owner.Query(context.Background(), `
SELECT table_name, privilege_type FROM information_schema.role_table_grants
WHERE grantee = 'alice_app' AND table_schema = 'public'`)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	defer rows.Close()
	out := map[string][]string{}
	for rows.Next() {
		var table, priv string
		require.NoError(t, rows.Scan(&table, &priv))
		out[table] = append(out[table], priv)
	}
	require.NoError(t, rows.Err())
	for k := range out {
		sort.Strings(out[k])
	}
	return out
}

func TestAppRoleGrants(t *testing.T) {
	d := pgtest.New(t)
	g := grants(t, d)

	assert.Equal(t, []string{"INSERT", "SELECT"}, g["audit_log"], "audit_log is append-only for the app (R33, SEC-071)")
	assert.Equal(t, []string{"INSERT", "SELECT"}, g["master_key_versions"])
	assert.Equal(t, []string{"SELECT"}, g["goose_db_version"])

	rows, err := d.Owner.Query(context.Background(),
		"SELECT tablename FROM pg_tables WHERE schemaname = 'public'")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	defer rows.Close()
	full := []string{"DELETE", "INSERT", "SELECT", "UPDATE"}
	for rows.Next() {
		var table string
		require.NoError(t, rows.Scan(&table))
		switch table {
		case "audit_log", "master_key_versions", "goose_db_version":
			continue
		}
		assert.Equal(t, full, g[table], "table %s needs grants for alice_app (add them in its migration)", table)
	}
	require.NoError(t, rows.Err())
}

func TestAppRoleIsNotSuperuser(t *testing.T) {
	d := pgtest.New(t)
	var super, createRole bool
	require.NoError(t, d.App.QueryRow(context.Background(),
		"SELECT rolsuper, rolcreaterole FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = current_user").Scan(&super, &createRole))
	assert.False(t, super)
	assert.False(t, createRole)
}

const insertAudit = `INSERT INTO audit_log (seq, at, actor_type, actor_name, action, result, prev_hash, hash)
VALUES (1, now(), 'system', 'test', 'test.event', 'success', '\x00', '\x01')`

func TestAppRoleCannotRewriteAuditLog(t *testing.T) {
	d := pgtest.New(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	_, err := d.App.Exec(ctx, insertAudit)
	require.NoError(t, err, "the app may append")

	for _, stmt := range []string{
		"UPDATE audit_log SET action = 'forged'",
		"DELETE FROM audit_log",
		"TRUNCATE audit_log",
	} {
		_, err := d.App.Exec(ctx, stmt)
		var pgErr *pgconn.PgError
		require.True(t, errors.As(err, &pgErr), stmt)
		assert.Equal(t, "42501", pgErr.Code, "%s must be denied by privileges (F-94)", stmt)
	}
}

func TestTriggersBlockOwnerRewrites(t *testing.T) {
	d := pgtest.New(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	_, err := d.Owner.Exec(ctx, insertAudit)
	require.NoError(t, err)

	for _, stmt := range []string{
		"UPDATE audit_log SET action = 'forged'",
		"DELETE FROM audit_log",
		"TRUNCATE audit_log",
	} {
		_, err := d.Owner.Exec(ctx, stmt)
		require.Error(t, err, stmt)
		assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "audit_log is append-only", stmt)
	}
	var action string
	require.NoError(t, d.Owner.QueryRow(ctx, "SELECT action FROM audit_log WHERE seq = 1").Scan(&action))
	assert.Equal(t, "test.event", action)
}

func TestDatabasesAreIsolated(t *testing.T) {
	a, b := pgtest.New(t), pgtest.New(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	_, err := a.App.Exec(ctx, "INSERT INTO settings (key, value) VALUES ('only-in-a', '1')")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	var n int
	require.NoError(t, b.App.QueryRow(ctx, "SELECT count(*) FROM settings").Scan(&n))
	assert.Equal(t, 0, n)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the test database helper**

`internal/testutil/pgtest/pgtest.go`:
```go
//go:build integration

// Package pgtest gives integration tests a real PostgreSQL 16: one container per
// test package (started from TestMain via Main) and a fresh, fully migrated
// database per test, cloned from a template so each test starts in ~30 ms.
//
// Every database has two pools, mirroring production: Owner (the migration
// role, a superuser here) and App (the alice_app role with the grants from the
// migrations). Tests must exercise the application through App.
package pgtest

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"net/url"
	"os"
	"sync/atomic"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
	"github.com/testcontainers/testcontainers-go"
	"github.com/testcontainers/testcontainers-go/modules/postgres"

	"alice/internal/platform/db"
	"alice/migrations"
)

const (
	template = "alice_template"
	// AppPassword is the alice_app password inside the throw-away test container.
	AppPassword = "app-test-pw" //nolint:gosec // G101: fixed password of a throw-away test container
)

var (
	ownerBase string // superuser URL of the container's default database
	counter   atomic.Int64
)

// Main starts the container, prepares the template database, runs the tests and
// removes the container. Use it as: func TestMain(m *testing.M) { os.Exit(pgtest.Main(m)) }.
func Main(m *testing.M) int {
	ctx := context.Background()
	ctr, err := postgres.Run(ctx, "postgres:16-alpine",
		postgres.WithDatabase("alice"),
		postgres.WithUsername("alice"),
		postgres.WithPassword("s3cret-pw"),
		postgres.BasicWaitStrategies(),
	)
	if ctr != nil {
		defer func() { _ = testcontainers.TerminateContainer(ctr) }()
	}
	if err != nil {
		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "pgtest: start postgres container:", err)
		return 1
	}
	ownerBase, err = ctr.ConnectionString(ctx, "sslmode=disable")
	if err != nil {
		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "pgtest: connection string:", err)
		return 1
	}
	if err := prepareTemplate(ctx); err != nil {
		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "pgtest: prepare template:", err)
		return 1
	}
	return m.Run()
}

// prepareTemplate migrates the template database once and gives alice_app a
// login so App pools can connect with the migration grants.
func prepareTemplate(ctx context.Context) error {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 2*time.Minute)
	defer cancel()
	admin, err := db.Open(ctx, ownerBase)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer admin.Close()
	if _, err := admin.Exec(ctx, "CREATE DATABASE "+template); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	tpl, err := db.Open(ctx, withDatabase(ownerBase, template, nil))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	err = db.Migrate(ctx, tpl, migrations.FS)
	tpl.Close() // a template must have no open connections when it is cloned
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	_, err = admin.Exec(ctx, "ALTER ROLE alice_app LOGIN PASSWORD '"+AppPassword+"'")
	return err
}

// DB is one fresh database for one test.
type DB struct {
	Owner, App       *pgxpool.Pool
	OwnerURL, AppURL string
}

// New creates a fresh, migrated database and returns pools for both roles.
// The pools are closed when the test ends.
func New(t *testing.T) *DB {
	t.Helper()
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	admin, err := db.Open(ctx, ownerBase)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("pgtest: open admin pool: %v", err)
	}
	defer admin.Close()
	name := fmt.Sprintf("test_%d_%d", os.Getpid(), counter.Add(1))
	if _, err := admin.Exec(ctx, "CREATE DATABASE "+name+" TEMPLATE "+template); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("pgtest: create database: %v", err)
	}

	d := &DB{
		OwnerURL: withDatabase(ownerBase, name, nil),
		AppURL:   withDatabase(ownerBase, name, url.UserPassword("alice_app", AppPassword)),
	}
	if d.Owner, err = db.Open(ctx, d.OwnerURL); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("pgtest: open owner pool: %v", err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(d.Owner.Close)
	if d.App, err = db.Open(ctx, d.AppURL); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("pgtest: open app pool: %v", err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(d.App.Close)
	return d
}

// withDatabase returns base with another database name and, if user is not
// nil, other credentials.
func withDatabase(base, name string, user *url.Userinfo) string {
	u, err := url.Parse(base)
	if err != nil {
		panic("pgtest: container URL does not parse: " + err.Error())
	}
	u.Path = "/" + name
	if user != nil {
		u.User = user
	}
	return u.String()
}
```

`migrations/00002_core.sql`:
```sql
-- +goose Up
-- +goose StatementBegin
DO $$
BEGIN
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'alice_app') THEN
        CREATE ROLE alice_app NOLOGIN;
    END IF;
END
$$;
-- +goose StatementEnd

CREATE TABLE users (
    id                  uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    username            citext NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    email               citext NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    display_name        text NOT NULL,
    auth_source         text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'local' CHECK (auth_source IN ('local', 'oidc')),
    password_hash       text,
    role                text NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'operator', 'auditor')),
    role_source         text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'manual' CHECK (role_source IN ('manual', 'oidc_mapping')),
    locale              text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'en',
    timezone            text,
    is_break_glass      boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    disabled_at         timestamptz,
    failed_logins       integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    locked_until        timestamptz,
    password_changed_at timestamptz,
    last_login_at       timestamptz,
    created_at          timestamptz NOT NULL,
    updated_at          timestamptz NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT users_local_has_password CHECK (auth_source <> 'local' OR password_hash IS NOT NULL)
);

CREATE TABLE user_mfa (
    user_id          uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    totp_secret_ct   bytea NOT NULL,
    totp_nonce       bytea NOT NULL,
    totp_wrapped_dek bytea NOT NULL,
    totp_key_version integer NOT NULL,
    enrolled_at      timestamptz,
    last_used_step   bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE recovery_codes (
    id         uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id    uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    code_hash  text NOT NULL,
    used_at    timestamptz,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX recovery_codes_unused ON recovery_codes (user_id) WHERE used_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE sessions (
    id_hash         bytea PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id         uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    kind            text NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('pending', 'full')),
    created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL,
    last_seen_at    timestamptz NOT NULL,
    expires_at      timestamptz NOT NULL,
    idle_expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    ip              inet,
    user_agent      text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    mfa_verified_at timestamptz,
    stepup_until    timestamptz,
    revoked_at      timestamptz
);
CREATE INDEX sessions_user ON sessions (user_id);
CREATE INDEX sessions_expires ON sessions (expires_at);

CREATE TABLE api_tokens (
    id           uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id      uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    name         text NOT NULL,
    token_hash   bytea NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    token_prefix text NOT NULL,
    scopes       text[] NOT NULL,
    expires_at   timestamptz NOT NULL,
    last_used_at timestamptz,
    revoked_at   timestamptz,
    created_at   timestamptz NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX api_tokens_user ON api_tokens (user_id);

CREATE TABLE setup_sessions (
    id_hash               bytea PRIMARY KEY,
    created_at            timestamptz NOT NULL,
    idle_expires_at       timestamptz NOT NULL,
    pending_username      citext,
    pending_display_name  text,
    pending_email         citext,
    pending_password_hash text,
    totp_secret_ct        bytea,
    totp_nonce            bytea,
    totp_wrapped_dek      bytea,
    totp_key_version      integer
);

CREATE TABLE master_key_versions (
    version    integer PRIMARY KEY,
    key_check  bytea NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    retired_at timestamptz
);

CREATE TABLE audit_log (
    seq         bigint PRIMARY KEY CHECK (seq > 0),
    at          timestamptz NOT NULL,
    actor_type  text NOT NULL CHECK (actor_type IN ('user', 'api_token', 'system', 'oidc')),
    actor_id    uuid,
    actor_name  text NOT NULL,
    ip          inet,
    action      text NOT NULL,
    object_type text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    object_id   text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    result      text NOT NULL CHECK (result IN ('success', 'failure', 'denied')),
    details     jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
    request_id  text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    prev_hash   bytea NOT NULL,
    hash        bytea NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX audit_log_action ON audit_log (action);

-- +goose StatementBegin
CREATE FUNCTION audit_log_immutable() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'audit_log is append-only';
END
$$;
-- +goose StatementEnd

CREATE TRIGGER audit_log_no_update_delete BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON audit_log
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION audit_log_immutable();
CREATE TRIGGER audit_log_no_truncate BEFORE TRUNCATE ON audit_log
    FOR EACH STATEMENT EXECUTE FUNCTION audit_log_immutable();

GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA public TO alice_app;
GRANT SELECT ON goose_db_version TO alice_app;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON settings, users, user_mfa, recovery_codes, sessions, api_tokens, setup_sessions TO alice_app;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON master_key_versions, audit_log TO alice_app;

-- +goose Down
DROP TABLE audit_log;
DROP FUNCTION audit_log_immutable();
DROP TABLE master_key_versions;
DROP TABLE setup_sessions;
DROP TABLE api_tokens;
DROP TABLE sessions;
DROP TABLE recovery_codes;
DROP TABLE user_mfa;
DROP TABLE users;
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Extend the configuration**

Replace `internal/platform/config/config.go` with:
```go
// Package config loads Alice's process configuration from environment variables.
// Values that contain secrets are never rendered in logs or formatted output.
package config

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"net/netip"
	"net/url"
	"strings"
)

// Config is the process configuration.
type Config struct {
	// DatabaseURL is the application role (alice_app) connection used at runtime.
	DatabaseURL string
	// MigrateDatabaseURL is the schema owner connection used only for migrations.
	// Empty means DatabaseURL is used for both (single-role mode, warned at startup).
	MigrateDatabaseURL string
	ListenAddr         string
	HealthAddr         string
	KeysDir            string
	LogLevel           slog.Level
	TLSHosts           []string
	// TrustedProxies are the reverse proxies whose X-Forwarded-For header is honoured.
	TrustedProxies []netip.Prefix
}

// Load reads configuration using getenv (os.Getenv in production).
func Load(getenv func(string) string) (Config, error) {
	c := Config{
		DatabaseURL:        getenv("ALICE_DATABASE_URL"),
		MigrateDatabaseURL: strings.TrimSpace(getenv("ALICE_MIGRATE_DATABASE_URL")),
		ListenAddr:         withDefault(getenv("ALICE_LISTEN_ADDR"), ":8443"),
		HealthAddr:         withDefault(getenv("ALICE_HEALTH_ADDR"), "127.0.0.1:8081"),
		KeysDir:            withDefault(getenv("ALICE_KEYS_DIR"), "/opt/alice/keys"),
		LogLevel:           slog.LevelInfo,
	}
	if lvl := strings.TrimSpace(getenv("ALICE_LOG_LEVEL")); lvl != "" {
		// slog.Level.UnmarshalText is case-insensitive.
		if err := c.LogLevel.UnmarshalText([]byte(lvl)); err != nil {
			return Config{}, fmt.Errorf("ALICE_LOG_LEVEL must be one of debug, info, warn, error (got %q)", lvl)
		}
	}
	for _, h := range strings.Split(getenv("ALICE_TLS_HOSTS"), ",") {
		if h = strings.TrimSpace(h); h != "" {
			c.TLSHosts = append(c.TLSHosts, h)
		}
	}
	for _, p := range strings.Split(getenv("ALICE_TRUSTED_PROXIES"), ",") {
		if p = strings.TrimSpace(p); p == "" {
			continue
		}
		prefix, err := parsePrefix(p)
		if err != nil {
			return Config{}, fmt.Errorf("ALICE_TRUSTED_PROXIES entry %q is not an IP address or CIDR", p)
		}
		c.TrustedProxies = append(c.TrustedProxies, prefix)
	}
	return c, nil
}

// parsePrefix accepts a CIDR ("10.0.0.0/8") or a single address ("10.0.0.1").
func parsePrefix(s string) (netip.Prefix, error) {
	if strings.Contains(s, "/") {
		p, err := netip.ParsePrefix(s)
		if err != nil {
			return netip.Prefix{}, err
		}
		return p.Masked(), nil
	}
	a, err := netip.ParseAddr(s)
	if err != nil {
		return netip.Prefix{}, err
	}
	a = a.Unmap()
	return netip.PrefixFrom(a, a.BitLen()), nil
}

// MigrationURL is the connection used to apply migrations: the owner role when
// configured, otherwise the application role.
func (c Config) MigrationURL() string {
	if c.MigrateDatabaseURL != "" {
		return c.MigrateDatabaseURL
	}
	return c.DatabaseURL
}

// SeparateMigrationRole reports whether migrations run as a different database
// role than the application (required for the append-only audit log, SEC-071).
func (c Config) SeparateMigrationRole() bool {
	return c.MigrateDatabaseURL != "" && c.MigrateDatabaseURL != c.DatabaseURL
}

// RequireDatabase reports a user-friendly error when no database is configured.
func (c Config) RequireDatabase() error {
	if strings.TrimSpace(c.DatabaseURL) == "" {
		return errors.New("ALICE_DATABASE_URL is not set; set it in deploy/.env (see docs/dev/getting-started.md)")
	}
	return nil
}

func (c Config) proxies() []string {
	out := make([]string, len(c.TrustedProxies))
	for i, p := range c.TrustedProxies {
		out[i] = p.String()
	}
	return out
}

// LogValue implements slog.LogValuer so database passwords are never logged.
func (c Config) LogValue() slog.Value {
	return slog.GroupValue(
		slog.String("database_url", redactURL(c.DatabaseURL)),
		slog.String("migrate_database_url", redactURL(c.MigrateDatabaseURL)),
		slog.String("listen_addr", c.ListenAddr),
		slog.String("health_addr", c.HealthAddr),
		slog.String("keys_dir", c.KeysDir),
		slog.String("log_level", c.LogLevel.String()),
		slog.Any("tls_hosts", c.TLSHosts),
		slog.Any("trusted_proxies", c.proxies()),
	)
}

// String renders the configuration with secrets redacted.
func (c Config) String() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("Config{DatabaseURL:%s MigrateDatabaseURL:%s ListenAddr:%s HealthAddr:%s KeysDir:%s LogLevel:%s TLSHosts:%v TrustedProxies:%v}",
		redactURL(c.DatabaseURL), redactURL(c.MigrateDatabaseURL), c.ListenAddr, c.HealthAddr, c.KeysDir, c.LogLevel, c.TLSHosts, c.proxies())
}

// GoString makes %#v safe as well.
func (c Config) GoString() string { return c.String() }

const redactedPlaceholder = "[redacted]"

// redactURL is allowlist-based: only well-formed postgres URLs are rendered,
// with every password masked. Anything else (key/value DSNs, other schemes,
// parse failures) becomes a fixed placeholder.
//
// The url.Parse error must never be returned or logged: it echoes the input,
// which may contain the password.
func redactURL(raw string) string {
	if raw == "" {
		return ""
	}
	u, err := url.Parse(raw)
	if err != nil || (u.Scheme != "postgres" && u.Scheme != "postgresql") {
		return redactedPlaceholder
	}
	q, err := url.ParseQuery(u.RawQuery)
	if err != nil {
		return redactedPlaceholder
	}
	for name := range q {
		if containsFold(name, "password") {
			q[name] = []string{"xxxxx"}
		}
	}
	u.RawQuery = q.Encode()
	return u.Redacted()
}

// containsFold reports whether s contains substr, ignoring ASCII case.
func containsFold(s, substr string) bool {
	for i := 0; i+len(substr) <= len(s); i++ {
		if strings.EqualFold(s[i:i+len(substr)], substr) {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

// MarshalJSON emits the configuration with secrets redacted.
func (c Config) MarshalJSON() ([]byte, error) {
	return json.Marshal(struct {
		DatabaseURL        string   `json:"database_url"`
		MigrateDatabaseURL string   `json:"migrate_database_url"`
		ListenAddr         string   `json:"listen_addr"`
		HealthAddr         string   `json:"health_addr"`
		KeysDir            string   `json:"keys_dir"`
		LogLevel           string   `json:"log_level"`
		TLSHosts           []string `json:"tls_hosts"`
		TrustedProxies     []string `json:"trusted_proxies"`
	}{redactURL(c.DatabaseURL), redactURL(c.MigrateDatabaseURL), c.ListenAddr, c.HealthAddr, c.KeysDir,
		c.LogLevel.String(), c.TLSHosts, c.proxies()})
}

func withDefault(v, def string) string {
	if strings.TrimSpace(v) == "" {
		return def
	}
	return strings.TrimSpace(v)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Migrate as the owner, serve as `alice_app`**

In `cmd/alice/main.go` replace the whole `migrate` function with:
```go
func migrate(cfg config.Config) error {
	if err := cfg.RequireDatabase(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2*time.Minute)
	defer cancel()
	return applyMigrations(ctx, cfg, 2*time.Minute)
}

// applyMigrations opens a short-lived pool as the migration (owner) role,
// applies all migrations and closes it, so the running server never keeps an
// owner connection (the app role cannot rewrite the audit log, SEC-071).
func applyMigrations(ctx context.Context, cfg config.Config, openBudget time.Duration) error {
	openCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, openBudget)
	owner, err := db.Open(openCtx, cfg.MigrationURL())
	cancel()
	if err != nil {
		return dbStartupError(err, openBudget)
	}
	defer owner.Close()
	return db.Migrate(ctx, owner, migrations.FS)
}

// dbStartupError adds the waiting time to "not reachable" errors only; a
// rejected password or a missing database is reported as is (it is final).
func dbStartupError(err error, budget time.Duration) error {
	if errors.Is(err, db.ErrUnreachable) {
		return fmt.Errorf("database not ready after %s: %w", budget, err)
	}
	return err
}
```

```go
	cancel()
	if err != nil {
		if errors.Is(err, db.ErrUnreachable) {
			return fmt.Errorf("database not ready after %s: %w", dbStartupBudget, err)
		}
		return err
	}
	defer pool.Close()
	if err := db.Migrate(ctx, pool, migrations.FS); err != nil {
		return err
	}
```

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Creates deploy/.env with random database passwords, or adds any variable a
# newer version needs to an existing file. Existing values are never changed.
set -euo pipefail
env_file="$(dirname "$0")/../.env"
umask 077

random_password() { openssl rand -base64 48 | tr -dc 'A-Za-z0-9' | head -c 32; }

if [[ ! -f "$env_file" ]]; then
  cat > "$env_file" <<ENV
ALICE_DB_PASSWORD=$(random_password)
ALICE_APP_DB_PASSWORD=$(random_password)
ALICE_PORT=443
ENV
  echo "Created deploy/.env (mode 600)."
  exit 0
fi

if ! grep -q '^ALICE_APP_DB_PASSWORD=' "$env_file"; then
  echo "ALICE_APP_DB_PASSWORD=$(random_password)" >> "$env_file"
  echo "Added ALICE_APP_DB_PASSWORD to deploy/.env. An existing database volume does not know this role yet: run 'make reset' (deletes local data)."
  exit 0
fi
echo "deploy/.env already exists; keeping it."
```

### Task 4: Secret store: envelope encryption, redacted plaintext, key derivation

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/secrets/plaintext.go`, `internal/secrets/envelope.go`
- Test: `internal/secrets/envelope_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing from earlier Phase 1 tasks (stdlib `crypto/aes`, `crypto/cipher`, `crypto/hkdf`, `crypto/rand`, `crypto/sha256`).
- Produces (package `alice/internal/secrets`):
  - `const KeySize = 32`; `var ErrDecrypt = errors.New("secrets: decryption failed")`; `var ErrMasterKeyMismatch = errors.New("the master key does not match this database")`
  - `type Plaintext struct{ /* b []byte */ }`; `func NewPlaintext(b []byte) Plaintext`; `func (p Plaintext) Bytes() []byte`; `func (p Plaintext) Zero()`; `String()`, `GoString()`, `Format(fmt.State, rune)`, `MarshalJSON()`, `LogValue()` all render `[redacted]`
  - `type Location struct{ Table, RowID, Column string }`
  - `type Envelope struct{ Ciphertext, Nonce, WrappedDEK []byte; KeyVersion int }` (WrappedDEK = 12-byte nonce || AES-256-GCM(dek))
  - `type Keyring`; `func NewKeyring(version int, key []byte) (*Keyring, error)`; `func (k *Keyring) Version() int`; `func (k *Keyring) Seal(loc Location, plaintext []byte) (Envelope, error)`; `func (k *Keyring) Open(loc Location, env Envelope) (Plaintext, error)`; `func (k *Keyring) Derive(label string) []byte`; `func (k *Keyring) KeyCheck() ([]byte, error)`; `func (k *Keyring) VerifyKeyCheck(check []byte) error`
  - `const LabelHWM = "alice/hwm/v1"`, `LabelJob = "alice/job/v1"`, `LabelAuditHMAC = "alice/audit-hmac/v1"`, `LabelCSRF = "alice/csrf/v1"`
  - AAD (SEC-041 as amended in Task 25): payload layer `table/row_id/column`; DEK-wrap layer the same string plus `/<key_version>`. Key check: AES-GCM under the master key of `alice-master-key-check-v1`, AAD `master_key_versions/<version>/key_check`, stored as nonce || ciphertext.

Design notes: every secret gets its own random 256-bit DEK (SEC-040); the payload is AES-256-GCM with a random 96-bit nonce; the DEK is wrapped with the master key, so master-key rotation (later phase) rewraps DEKs only. `Open` returns the single error `ErrDecrypt` for every failure so callers cannot learn which layer failed. Sub-keys come from stdlib `crypto/hkdf` (Go 1.24+), never from the raw master key (SEC-043). Go 1.24+ `crypto/rand.Read` never returns an error, so it is called without error handling.

```go
package secrets_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/json"

	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

func testKey(b byte) []byte { return bytes.Repeat([]byte{b}, secrets.KeySize) }

func keyring(t *testing.T) *secrets.Keyring {
	t.Helper()
	k, err := secrets.NewKeyring(1, testKey(7))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return k
}

var loc = secrets.Location{Table: "user_mfa", RowID: "0192e0a0-0000-7000-8000-000000000001", Column: "totp_secret"}

func TestNewKeyringValidatesInput(t *testing.T) {
	_, err := secrets.NewKeyring(1, make([]byte, 16))
	assert.ErrorContains(t, err, "32 bytes")
	_, err = secrets.NewKeyring(0, testKey(1))
	assert.ErrorContains(t, err, "version")
	k, err := secrets.NewKeyring(3, testKey(1))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, 3, k.Version())
}

func TestSealOpenRoundTrip(t *testing.T) {

	env, err := k.Seal(loc, []byte("CANARY-totp-seed"))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Len(t, env.Nonce, 12)
	assert.Equal(t, 1, env.KeyVersion)
	assert.NotContains(t, string(env.Ciphertext), "CANARY")
	assert.NotContains(t, string(env.WrappedDEK), "CANARY")

	pt, err := k.Open(loc, env)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, "CANARY-totp-seed", string(pt.Bytes()))
}

func TestIdenticalPlaintextsGiveDifferentCiphertextsAndDEKs(t *testing.T) {
	k := keyring(t)
	a, err := k.Seal(loc, []byte("same"))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	b, err := k.Seal(loc, []byte("same"))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.NotEqual(t, a.Ciphertext, b.Ciphertext)
	assert.NotEqual(t, a.Nonce, b.Nonce)
	assert.NotEqual(t, a.WrappedDEK, b.WrappedDEK)
}

func flip(b []byte, i int) []byte {
	c := append([]byte(nil), b...)
	c[i] ^= 0x01
	return c
}

func TestTamperingAnyFieldFails(t *testing.T) {
	k := keyring(t)
	env, err := k.Seal(loc, []byte("secret value"))
	require.NoError(t, err)

	cases := map[string]secrets.Envelope{
		"ciphertext first byte": {Ciphertext: flip(env.Ciphertext, 0), Nonce: env.Nonce, WrappedDEK: env.WrappedDEK, KeyVersion: 1},
		"ciphertext tag":        {Ciphertext: flip(env.Ciphertext, len(env.Ciphertext)-1), Nonce: env.Nonce, WrappedDEK: env.WrappedDEK, KeyVersion: 1},
		"nonce":                 {Ciphertext: env.Ciphertext, Nonce: flip(env.Nonce, 3), WrappedDEK: env.WrappedDEK, KeyVersion: 1},
		"wrapped DEK nonce":     {Ciphertext: env.Ciphertext, Nonce: env.Nonce, WrappedDEK: flip(env.WrappedDEK, 0), KeyVersion: 1},
		"wrapped DEK body":      {Ciphertext: env.Ciphertext, Nonce: env.Nonce, WrappedDEK: flip(env.WrappedDEK, 20), KeyVersion: 1},
		"truncated wrapped DEK": {Ciphertext: env.Ciphertext, Nonce: env.Nonce, WrappedDEK: env.WrappedDEK[:12], KeyVersion: 1},
		"short nonce":           {Ciphertext: env.Ciphertext, Nonce: env.Nonce[:8], WrappedDEK: env.WrappedDEK, KeyVersion: 1},
		"unknown key version":   {Ciphertext: env.Ciphertext, Nonce: env.Nonce, WrappedDEK: env.WrappedDEK, KeyVersion: 2},
	}
	for name, bad := range cases {
		_, err := k.Open(loc, bad)
		assert.ErrorIs(t, err, secrets.ErrDecrypt, name)
	}
}

func TestCiphertextBoundToLocation(t *testing.T) {
	k := keyring(t)
	env, err := k.Seal(loc, []byte("row one secret"))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	for name, other := range map[string]secrets.Location{
		"other row":    {Table: loc.Table, RowID: "0192e0a0-0000-7000-8000-000000000002", Column: loc.Column},
		"other column": {Table: loc.Table, RowID: loc.RowID, Column: "other"},
		"other table":  {Table: "setup_sessions", RowID: loc.RowID, Column: loc.Column},
	} {
		_, err := k.Open(other, env)
		assert.ErrorIs(t, err, secrets.ErrDecrypt, name)
	}
}

func TestOtherMasterKeyCannotOpen(t *testing.T) {
	env, err := keyring(t).Seal(loc, []byte("x"))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	other, err := secrets.NewKeyring(1, testKey(8))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = other.Open(loc, env)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, secrets.ErrDecrypt)
}

func TestKeyCheck(t *testing.T) {
	k := keyring(t)
	check, err := k.KeyCheck()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NoError(t, k.VerifyKeyCheck(check))

	other, err := secrets.NewKeyring(1, testKey(9))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, other.VerifyKeyCheck(check), secrets.ErrMasterKeyMismatch)
	v2, err := secrets.NewKeyring(2, testKey(7))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, v2.VerifyKeyCheck(check), secrets.ErrMasterKeyMismatch, "check is bound to the version")
	assert.ErrorIs(t, k.VerifyKeyCheck([]byte("short")), secrets.ErrMasterKeyMismatch)
}

func TestDeriveIsDeterministicAndLabelSeparated(t *testing.T) {
	k := keyring(t)
	labels := []string{secrets.LabelHWM, secrets.LabelJob, secrets.LabelAuditHMAC, secrets.LabelCSRF}
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for _, l := range labels {
		a, b := k.Derive(l), k.Derive(l)
		assert.Len(t, a, 32)
		assert.Equal(t, a, b, l)
		assert.NotEqual(t, testKey(7), a, "sub-key must differ from the master key")
		seen[string(a)] = true
	}
	assert.Len(t, seen, len(labels), "each label yields a distinct key")

	other, err := secrets.NewKeyring(1, testKey(8))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.NotEqual(t, k.Derive(secrets.LabelCSRF), other.Derive(secrets.LabelCSRF))
}

func TestPlaintextIsRedactedEverywhere(t *testing.T) {
	p := secrets.NewPlaintext([]byte("CANARY-plain"))
	for _, verb := range []string{"%v", "%+v", "%#v", "%s", "%q", "%x"} {
		out := fmt.Sprintf(verb, p)
		assert.Equal(t, "[redacted]", out, verb)
	}
	assert.NotContains(t, fmt.Sprintf("%v", struct{ P secrets.Plaintext }{p}), "CANARY")
	assert.Equal(t, "[redacted]", p.String())

	b, err := json.Marshal(map[string]any{"p": p})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"p":"[redacted]"}`, string(b))

	var buf bytes.Buffer
	slog.New(slog.NewJSONHandler(&buf, nil)).Info("x", "value", p)
	assert.NotContains(t, buf.String(), "CANARY")
	assert.Contains(t, buf.String(), "[redacted]")
}

func TestPlaintextZero(t *testing.T) {
	raw := []byte("CANARY-zero")
	p := secrets.NewPlaintext(raw)
	p.Zero()
	assert.Equal(t, make([]byte, len(raw)), raw)
	assert.Equal(t, make([]byte, len(raw)), p.Bytes())
}
```
