		"$argon2id$v=19$m=99999999,t=1,p=1$c2FsdHNhbHRzYWx0$a2V5a2V5a2V5a2V5a2V5a2V5",
		"$argon2id$v=19$m=64,t=1,p=0$c2FsdHNhbHRzYWx0$a2V5a2V5a2V5a2V5a2V5a2V5",
		"$argon2id$v=19$m=64,t=1,p=1$!!!$a2V5a2V5a2V5a2V5a2V5a2V5",
		"$argon2id$v=19$m=64,t=1,p=1$c2FsdHNhbHRzYWx0$",
	} {
		ok, _, err := h.Verify(context.Background(), enc, "x")
		assert.Error(t, err, enc)
		assert.False(t, ok, enc)
	}
}

func TestDummyVerifyDoesComparableWork(t *testing.T) {
	h := NewHasher(Argon2Params{MemoryKiB: 8 * 1024, Time: 1, Threads: 1, SaltLen: 16, KeyLen: 32}, 1)
	ctx := context.Background()
	enc, err := h.Hash(ctx, "some-password-x")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	start := time.Now()
	_, _, _ = h.Verify(ctx, enc, "wrong-password-x")
	realDur := time.Since(start)
	start = time.Now()
	h.DummyVerify(ctx, "wrong-password-x")
	dummy := time.Since(start)
	assert.Greater(t, dummy, realDur/4, "dummy verification must not be much cheaper than a real one")
}

func TestHasherBoundsConcurrency(t *testing.T) {
	h := NewHasher(testParams, 2)
	var inFlight, peak atomic.Int32
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	for range 12 {
		wg.Add(1)
		go func() {
			defer wg.Done()
			require.NoError(t, h.acquire(context.Background()))
			n := inFlight.Add(1)
			for {
				p := peak.Load()
				if n <= p || peak.CompareAndSwap(p, n) {
					break
				}
			}
			time.Sleep(5 * time.Millisecond)
			inFlight.Add(-1)
			h.release()
		}()
	}
	wg.Wait()
	assert.LessOrEqual(t, peak.Load(), int32(2))
	assert.Equal(t, int32(2), peak.Load(), "both slots are used under load")
}

func TestHashHonoursCancelledContextWhenSaturated(t *testing.T) {
	h := NewHasher(testParams, 1)
	require.NoError(t, h.acquire(context.Background()))
	defer h.release()
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	cancel()
	_, err := h.Hash(ctx, "whatever-password")
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, context.Canceled)
}

`internal/auth/password.go`:
```go
// Package auth implements local authentication: password hashing and policy,
// TOTP, recovery codes, sessions, users and API tokens.
package auth

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/subtle"
	"encoding/base64"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strings"

	"golang.org/x/crypto/argon2"
)

// Argon2Params are Argon2id cost parameters.
type Argon2Params struct {
	MemoryKiB uint32
	Time      uint32
	Threads   uint8
	SaltLen   uint32
	KeyLen    uint32
}

// PasswordParams are the production parameters for passwords (SEC-001).
var PasswordParams = Argon2Params{MemoryKiB: 64 * 1024, Time: 3, Threads: 2, SaltLen: 16, KeyLen: 32}

// RecoveryCodeParams are lighter parameters for recovery codes: the codes carry
// 80 bits of randomness, so brute force is infeasible regardless of cost, and
// a login may verify up to ten of them (SEC-008).
var RecoveryCodeParams = Argon2Params{MemoryKiB: 8 * 1024, Time: 1, Threads: 1, SaltLen: 16, KeyLen: 32}

var errMalformedHash = errors.New("auth: malformed password hash")

// Hasher hashes and verifies secrets with Argon2id. A semaphore bounds how many
// hashes run at once so concurrent logins cannot exhaust memory (R-07).
type Hasher struct {
	params Argon2Params
	sem    chan struct{}
}

// NewHasher returns a hasher that runs at most maxConcurrent hashes at a time
// (values below 1 mean 1).
func NewHasher(p Argon2Params, maxConcurrent int) *Hasher {
	return &Hasher{params: p, sem: make(chan struct{}, max(1, maxConcurrent))}
}

func (h *Hasher) acquire(ctx context.Context) error {
	select {
	case h.sem <- struct{}{}:
		return nil
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return ctx.Err()
	}
}

func (h *Hasher) release() { <-h.sem }

// Hash returns the PHC string "$argon2id$v=19$m=<KiB>,t=<time>,p=<threads>$<salt>$<key>"
// with unpadded standard base64 for salt and key.
func (h *Hasher) Hash(ctx context.Context, secret string) (string, error) {
	if err := h.acquire(ctx); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	defer h.release()
	salt := make([]byte, h.params.SaltLen)
	_, _ = rand.Read(salt) // never returns an error since Go 1.24
	key := argon2.IDKey([]byte(secret), salt, h.params.Time, h.params.MemoryKiB, h.params.Threads, h.params.KeyLen)
	return fmt.Sprintf("$argon2id$v=%d$m=%d,t=%d,p=%d$%s$%s", argon2.Version,
		h.params.MemoryKiB, h.params.Time, h.params.Threads,
		base64.RawStdEncoding.EncodeToString(salt), base64.RawStdEncoding.EncodeToString(key)), nil
}

// Verify checks secret against an encoded hash in constant time. needsRehash is
// true when the stored parameters are weaker than the hasher's (SEC-005).
func (h *Hasher) Verify(ctx context.Context, encoded, secret string) (ok, needsRehash bool, err error) {
	p, salt, want, err := decodePHC(encoded)
	if err != nil {
		return false, false, err
	}
	if err := h.acquire(ctx); err != nil {
		return false, false, err
	}
	defer h.release()
	// Lengths come from decodePHC and are bounded, so the uint32 conversion is safe.
	got := argon2.IDKey([]byte(secret), salt, p.Time, p.MemoryKiB, p.Threads, uint32(len(want))) //nolint:gosec // G115: len(want) <= 1024
	ok = subtle.ConstantTimeCompare(got, want) == 1
	needsRehash = p.MemoryKiB < h.params.MemoryKiB || p.Time < h.params.Time || p.Threads < h.params.Threads ||
		uint32(len(want)) < h.params.KeyLen //nolint:gosec // G115: len(want) <= 1024
	return ok, needsRehash, nil
}

// DummyVerify spends the same work as Verify for an unknown user, so response
// time does not reveal whether an account exists (SEC-004).
func (h *Hasher) DummyVerify(ctx context.Context, secret string) {
	if err := h.acquire(ctx); err != nil {
		return
	}
	defer h.release()
	salt := make([]byte, h.params.SaltLen)
	_ = argon2.IDKey([]byte(secret), salt, h.params.Time, h.params.MemoryKiB, h.params.Threads, h.params.KeyLen)
}

// maxDecodeMemoryKiB caps the memory an encoded hash may ask for (1 GiB), so a
// corrupted row cannot make verification exhaust memory.
const maxDecodeMemoryKiB = 1024 * 1024

func decodePHC(encoded string) (Argon2Params, []byte, []byte, error) {
	parts := strings.Split(encoded, "$")
	if len(parts) != 6 || parts[0] != "" || parts[1] != "argon2id" {
		return Argon2Params{}, nil, nil, errMalformedHash
	}
	var version int
	if _, err := fmt.Sscanf(parts[2], "v=%d", &version); err != nil || version != argon2.Version {
		return Argon2Params{}, nil, nil, errMalformedHash
	}
	var p Argon2Params
	var threads uint32
	if _, err := fmt.Sscanf(parts[3], "m=%d,t=%d,p=%d", &p.MemoryKiB, &p.Time, &threads); err != nil {
		return Argon2Params{}, nil, nil, errMalformedHash
	}
	if p.MemoryKiB == 0 || p.MemoryKiB > maxDecodeMemoryKiB || p.Time == 0 || p.Time > 100 || threads == 0 || threads > 255 {
		return Argon2Params{}, nil, nil, errMalformedHash
	}
	p.Threads = uint8(threads)
	salt, err := base64.RawStdEncoding.DecodeString(parts[4])
	if err != nil || len(salt) < 8 || len(salt) > 1024 {
		return Argon2Params{}, nil, nil, errMalformedHash
	}
	key, err := base64.RawStdEncoding.DecodeString(parts[5])
	if err != nil || len(key) < 16 || len(key) > 1024 {
		return Argon2Params{}, nil, nil, errMalformedHash
	}
	return p, salt, key, nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Implement the policy**

`internal/auth/policy.go`:
```go
package auth

import (
	"errors"
	"unicode/utf8"

	"golang.org/x/text/unicode/norm"

	"alice/internal/auth/breached"
)

// Password length limits in runes, measured after NFKC normalization (SEC-002).
const (
	MinPasswordLength = 12
	MaxPasswordLength = 128
)

// Password policy errors.
var (
	ErrPasswordTooShort = errors.New("auth: password too short")
	ErrPasswordTooLong  = errors.New("auth: password too long")
	ErrPasswordBreached = errors.New("auth: password is on the breached-password list")
)

// NormalizePassword applies Unicode NFKC so that visually identical input
// (for example full-width digits) hashes the same way on every device.
// Callers hash and verify the normalized form.
func NormalizePassword(pw string) string { return norm.NFKC.String(pw) }

// CheckPasswordPolicy enforces length and the breached-password list. There are
// no composition rules (SEC-002, SEC-003).
func CheckPasswordPolicy(pw string) error {
	n := NormalizePassword(pw)
	switch l := utf8.RuneCountInString(n); {
	case l < MinPasswordLength:
		return ErrPasswordTooShort
	case l > MaxPasswordLength:
		return ErrPasswordTooLong
	}
	if breached.Contains(n) {
		return ErrPasswordBreached
	}
	return nil
}
```

### Task 9: TOTP, recovery codes and rate limiters

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/auth/totp.go`, `internal/auth/totp_test.go`
- Create: `internal/auth/recovery.go`, `internal/auth/recovery_test.go`
- Create: `internal/auth/ratelimit/ratelimit.go`, `internal/auth/ratelimit/ratelimit_test.go`
- Modify: `go.mod`, `go.sum` (`github.com/pquerna/otp v1.5.0`, indirect `github.com/boombuler/barcode`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `clock.Clock`, `clock.NewFake` (Phase 0, `alice/internal/platform/clock`).
- Produces (exact, package `alice/internal/auth`):
  - `const TOTPIssuer = "Alice"`; `type TOTPEnrollment struct { Secret string; URI string; QRPNG []byte }`; `func NewTOTP(accountName string) (TOTPEnrollment, error)` (20-byte secret → 32 base32 chars, SHA1, 6 digits, 30 s, 256×256 PNG).
  - `func VerifyTOTP(secret, code string, now time.Time, lastStep int64) (step int64, ok bool)` — steps now−1..now+1, only steps `> lastStep`, exactly 6 ASCII digits, constant-time compare. Callers persist the returned step as `user_mfa.last_used_step`.
  - `const RecoveryCodeCount = 10`; `func GenerateRecoveryCodes() []string` (`XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX`, Crockford base32, 80 bits); `func NormalizeRecoveryCode(s string) string` (drops spaces/tabs/dashes, ASCII upper-case, O→0, I/L→1). Callers hash and compare `NormalizeRecoveryCode(code)` with `RecoveryCodeParams`.
- Produces (package `alice/internal/auth/ratelimit`):
  - `type Limiter`; `func New(c clock.Clock, limit int, per time.Duration) *Limiter`; `func (l *Limiter) Allow(key string) (bool, time.Duration)` (retry-after rounded up to whole seconds, ≥ 1 s); `func (l *Limiter) Reset()`.
  - `type Lockout`; `func NewLockout(c clock.Clock, maxFailures int, window, lockFor time.Duration) *Lockout`; `Locked(key string) (bool, time.Duration)`, `Fail(key string) bool` (true when this failure locked the key; failures while locked are ignored), `Succeed(key string)`, `Reset()`.
  - Both keep at most 10,000 keys (idle/expired evicted first, then the oldest).
- Used by Task 10 (login, MFA, step-up), Task 12 (setup code lockout: `NewLockout(clock, 5, 15*time.Minute, 15*time.Minute)`) and Task 13 (API limiters).

Dependency note: `github.com/pquerna/otp` is the spec's chosen TOTP library (decision R14, SEC-006) and is in the §5.2 budget. Its `Key.Image` pulls in `github.com/boombuler/barcode` transitively to render the enrollment QR code as a PNG, which the UI shows as a `data:` image (allowed by the CSP `img-src 'self' data:`), so no frontend QR library is needed.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing TOTP tests** (includes Review Focus #3: reuse rejected, previous step accepted only when newer than the last used step, ±1 window, malformed codes)

`internal/auth/totp_test.go`:
```go
package auth

import (
	"bytes"
	"image/png"
	"net/url"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/pquerna/otp"
	"github.com/pquerna/otp/hotp"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
)

// RFC 6238 Appendix B secret ("12345678901234567890") in base32.
const rfcSecret = "GEZDGNBVGY3TQOJQGEZDGNBVGY3TQOJQ"

func codeAt(t *testing.T, secret string, step int64) string {
	t.Helper()
	c, err := hotp.GenerateCodeCustom(secret, uint64(step), hotp.ValidateOpts{Digits: otp.DigitsSix, Algorithm: otp.AlgorithmSHA1})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return c
}

func TestVerifyTOTPKnownAnswer(t *testing.T) {
	// RFC 6238: T = 59 s → 94287082 (8 digits); the 6-digit code is the last six digits.
	step, ok := VerifyTOTP(rfcSecret, "287082", time.Unix(59, 0), 0)
	assert.True(t, ok)
	assert.Equal(t, int64(1), step)
}

func TestVerifyTOTPWindowAndReplay(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 30, 12, 0, 10, 0, time.UTC)
	cur := now.Unix() / 30

	// Current code accepted once; the same code (same step) is then rejected.
	step, ok := VerifyTOTP(rfcSecret, codeAt(t, rfcSecret, cur), now, 0)
	require.True(t, ok)
	assert.Equal(t, cur, step)
	_, ok = VerifyTOTP(rfcSecret, codeAt(t, rfcSecret, cur), now, step)
	assert.False(t, ok, "a code must not be accepted twice")

	// The next step's code works after the current one was used.
	step2, ok := VerifyTOTP(rfcSecret, codeAt(t, rfcSecret, cur+1), now.Add(30*time.Second), step)
	assert.True(t, ok)
	assert.Equal(t, cur+1, step2)

	// A code from the previous step is accepted when newer than the last used step…
	step3, ok := VerifyTOTP(rfcSecret, codeAt(t, rfcSecret, cur-1), now, cur-2)
	assert.True(t, ok)
	assert.Equal(t, cur-1, step3)
	// …and rejected once the current step was used.
	_, ok = VerifyTOTP(rfcSecret, codeAt(t, rfcSecret, cur-1), now, cur)
	assert.False(t, ok)

	// ±1 step skew: next step accepted, two steps away rejected.
	_, ok = VerifyTOTP(rfcSecret, codeAt(t, rfcSecret, cur+1), now, 0)
	assert.True(t, ok)
	_, ok = VerifyTOTP(rfcSecret, codeAt(t, rfcSecret, cur+2), now, 0)
	assert.False(t, ok)
	_, ok = VerifyTOTP(rfcSecret, codeAt(t, rfcSecret, cur-2), now, 0)
	assert.False(t, ok)
}

func TestVerifyTOTPRejectsMalformedCodes(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 9, 30, 12, 0, 10, 0, time.UTC)
	good := codeAt(t, rfcSecret, now.Unix()/30)
	for _, c := range []string{"", good[:5], good + "0", "12a456", " " + good[1:], "１２３４５６", "-12345"} {
		_, ok := VerifyTOTP(rfcSecret, c, now, 0)
		assert.False(t, ok, "%q", c)
	}
	_, ok := VerifyTOTP("not base32!", good, now, 0)
	assert.False(t, ok, "a malformed secret never verifies")
}

func TestNewTOTP(t *testing.T) {
	e, err := NewTOTP("admin")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Len(t, e.Secret, 32, "160-bit secret in unpadded base32")
	assert.NotContains(t, e.Secret, "=")

	u, err := url.Parse(e.URI)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, "otpauth", u.Scheme)
	assert.Equal(t, "totp", u.Host)
	assert.Equal(t, "/Alice:admin", u.Path)
	q := u.Query()
	assert.Equal(t, e.Secret, q.Get("secret"))
	assert.Equal(t, "Alice", q.Get("issuer"))
	assert.Equal(t, "SHA1", q.Get("algorithm"))
	assert.Equal(t, "6", q.Get("digits"))
	assert.Equal(t, "30", q.Get("period"))

	img, err := png.Decode(bytes.NewReader(e.QRPNG))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, 256, img.Bounds().Dx())
	assert.Equal(t, 256, img.Bounds().Dy())

	other, err := NewTOTP("admin")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.NotEqual(t, e.Secret, other.Secret)

	// A freshly generated secret verifies with its own current code.
	now := time.Now()
	_, ok := VerifyTOTP(e.Secret, codeAt(t, e.Secret, now.Unix()/30), now, 0)
	assert.True(t, ok)
	assert.False(t, strings.Contains(e.URI, " "))
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement TOTP**

`internal/auth/totp.go`:
```go
package auth

import (
	"bytes"
	"crypto/subtle"
	"fmt"
	"image/png"
	"time"

	"github.com/pquerna/otp"
	"github.com/pquerna/otp/hotp"
	"github.com/pquerna/otp/totp"
)

// TOTPIssuer is the issuer shown in authenticator apps.
const TOTPIssuer = "Alice"

// TOTP parameters (SEC-006): RFC 6238 with SHA-1, 6 digits, 30-second steps.
const (
	totpPeriod     = 30
	totpDigits     = 6
	totpSecretSize = 20 // 160 bits (SEC-007)
	totpQRSize     = 256
)

// TOTPEnrollment is what a user needs to add Alice to an authenticator app.
// It is shown once and must never be logged.
type TOTPEnrollment struct {
	Secret string // base32 without padding (32 characters)
	URI    string // otpauth://totp/Alice:<account>?...
	QRPNG  []byte // 256x256 PNG of URI
}

// NewTOTP creates a fresh 160-bit TOTP secret for accountName.
func NewTOTP(accountName string) (TOTPEnrollment, error) {
	key, err := totp.Generate(totp.GenerateOpts{
		Issuer:      TOTPIssuer,
		AccountName: accountName,
		Period:      totpPeriod,
		SecretSize:  totpSecretSize,
		Digits:      otp.DigitsSix,
		Algorithm:   otp.AlgorithmSHA1,
	})
	if err != nil {
		return TOTPEnrollment{}, fmt.Errorf("generate TOTP secret: %w", err)
	}
	img, err := key.Image(totpQRSize, totpQRSize)
	if err != nil {
		return TOTPEnrollment{}, fmt.Errorf("render TOTP QR code: %w", err)
	}
	var buf bytes.Buffer
	if err := png.Encode(&buf, img); err != nil {
		return TOTPEnrollment{}, fmt.Errorf("encode TOTP QR code: %w", err)
	}
	return TOTPEnrollment{Secret: key.Secret(), URI: key.URL(), QRPNG: buf.Bytes()}, nil
}

// VerifyTOTP checks code against secret at now, accepting the current step and
// one step either side. Only steps strictly greater than lastStep are accepted,
// so a code (or an older one) can never be replayed (SEC-006). On success it
// returns the matched step, which the caller stores as the new lastStep.
func VerifyTOTP(secret, code string, now time.Time, lastStep int64) (int64, bool) {
	if !isDigits(code, totpDigits) {
		return 0, false
	}
	current := now.Unix() / totpPeriod
	for _, step := range []int64{current - 1, current, current + 1} {
		if step <= lastStep || step < 0 {
			continue
		}
		want, err := hotp.GenerateCodeCustom(secret, uint64(step), hotp.ValidateOpts{ //nolint:gosec // G115: step >= 0 checked above
			Digits:    otp.DigitsSix,
			Algorithm: otp.AlgorithmSHA1,
		})
		if err != nil {
			return 0, false // malformed secret
		}
		if subtle.ConstantTimeCompare([]byte(want), []byte(code)) == 1 {
			return step, true
		}
	}
	return 0, false
}

func isDigits(s string, n int) bool {
	if len(s) != n {
		return false
	}
	for i := range len(s) {
		if s[i] < '0' || s[i] > '9' {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}
```

var recoveryRE = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{4}(-[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{4}){3}$`)

func TestGenerateRecoveryCodes(t *testing.T) {

	for range 20 {
		codes := GenerateRecoveryCodes()
		assert.Len(t, codes, 10)
		for _, c := range codes {
			assert.Regexp(t, recoveryRE, c)
			assert.False(t, seen[c], "duplicate recovery code %s", c)
			seen[c] = true
			assert.Equal(t, NormalizeRecoveryCode(c), NormalizeRecoveryCode(NormalizeRecoveryCode(c)))
		}
	}
}

func TestNormalizeRecoveryCode(t *testing.T) {

		"ABCD-EFGH-JKMN-PQRS":   "ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRS",

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement recovery codes**

`internal/auth/recovery.go`:
```go
package auth

import (
	"crypto/rand"
	"strings"
)

// RecoveryCodeCount is how many single-use recovery codes a user gets (SEC-008).
const RecoveryCodeCount = 10

// crockford is Crockford's base32 alphabet (no I, L, O, U), easy to read aloud.
const crockford = "0123456789ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTVWXYZ"

// GenerateRecoveryCodes returns 10 codes of 80 random bits each, formatted as
// "XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX" in Crockford base32. They are shown once and stored hashed.
func GenerateRecoveryCodes() []string {
	codes := make([]string, RecoveryCodeCount)
	for i := range codes {
		codes[i] = newRecoveryCode()
	}
	return codes
}

func newRecoveryCode() string {
	var raw [10]byte
	_, _ = rand.Read(raw[:]) // never returns an error since Go 1.24
	// 80 bits = 16 groups of 5 bits.
	var sb strings.Builder
	var acc uint64
	var bits uint
	n := 0
	for _, b := range raw {
		acc = acc<<8 | uint64(b)
		bits += 8
		for bits >= 5 {
			bits -= 5
			if n > 0 && n%4 == 0 {
				sb.WriteByte('-')
			}
			sb.WriteByte(crockford[(acc>>bits)&31])
			n++
		}
	}
	return sb.String()
}

// NormalizeRecoveryCode makes typed codes comparable: spaces and dashes are
// removed, ASCII letters upper-cased (byte-wise, locale-independent), and the
// look-alikes O→0, I→1, L→1 mapped as Crockford base32 prescribes.
func NormalizeRecoveryCode(s string) string {
	var sb strings.Builder
	for i := range len(s) {
		c := s[i]
		switch {
		case c == ' ' || c == '-' || c == '\t':
			continue
		case c >= 'a' && c <= 'z':
			c -= 'a' - 'A'
		}
		switch c {
		case 'O':
			c = '0'
		case 'I', 'L':
			c = '1'
		}
		sb.WriteByte(c)
	}
	return sb.String()
}
```

package ratelimit

import (
	"fmt"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/platform/clock"
)

var t0 = time.Date(2026, 9, 30, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

func TestLimiterBurstThenRefill(t *testing.T) {
	c := clock.NewFake(t0)
	l := New(c, 10, time.Minute)
	for i := range 10 {
		ok, _ := l.Allow("ip:10.0.0.1")
		require.True(t, ok, "request %d", i+1)
	}
	ok, retry := l.Allow("ip:10.0.0.1")
	assert.False(t, ok)
	assert.Equal(t, 6*time.Second, retry, "one token every 6 s")

	c.Advance(5 * time.Second)
	ok, retry = l.Allow("ip:10.0.0.1")
	assert.False(t, ok)
	assert.Equal(t, time.Second, retry)

	c.Advance(time.Second)
	ok, _ = l.Allow("ip:10.0.0.1")
	assert.True(t, ok)

	c.Advance(10 * time.Minute) // refill never exceeds the bucket size
	for range 10 {
		ok, _ = l.Allow("ip:10.0.0.1")
		assert.True(t, ok)
	}
	ok, _ = l.Allow("ip:10.0.0.1")
	assert.False(t, ok)
}

func TestLimiterKeysAreIndependent(t *testing.T) {
	l := New(clock.NewFake(t0), 1, time.Minute)
	ok, _ := l.Allow("a")
	assert.True(t, ok)
	ok, _ = l.Allow("a")
	assert.False(t, ok)
	ok, _ = l.Allow("b")
	assert.True(t, ok, "another key has its own bucket")
}

func TestLimiterReset(t *testing.T) {
	l := New(clock.NewFake(t0), 1, time.Minute)
	l.Allow("a")
	ok, _ := l.Allow("a")
	require.False(t, ok)
	l.Reset()
	ok, _ = l.Allow("a")
	assert.True(t, ok)
}

func TestLimiterBoundsMemory(t *testing.T) {
	c := clock.NewFake(t0)
	l := New(c, 5, time.Minute)
	for i := range maxKeys + 500 {
		l.Allow(fmt.Sprintf("ip:%d", i))
	}
	assert.LessOrEqual(t, len(l.buckets), maxKeys)
	c.Advance(time.Hour) // every bucket is full again, so all are evictable
	l.Allow("fresh")
	assert.Len(t, l.buckets, 1)
}

func TestLockoutLocksAfterMaxFailures(t *testing.T) {
	c := clock.NewFake(t0)
	l := NewLockout(c, 5, 15*time.Minute, 15*time.Minute)
	for i := range 4 {
		assert.False(t, l.Fail("10.0.0.9"), "failure %d", i+1)
		locked, _ := l.Locked("10.0.0.9")
		assert.False(t, locked)
	}
	assert.True(t, l.Fail("10.0.0.9"), "fifth failure locks")
	locked, retry := l.Locked("10.0.0.9")
	assert.True(t, locked)
	assert.Equal(t, 15*time.Minute, retry)
	assert.False(t, l.Fail("10.0.0.9"), "failures while locked do not extend the lock")

	locked, _ = l.Locked("10.0.0.10")
	assert.False(t, locked, "other keys are unaffected")

	c.Advance(15*time.Minute - time.Second)
	locked, retry = l.Locked("10.0.0.9")
	assert.True(t, locked)
	assert.Equal(t, time.Second, retry)
	c.Advance(time.Second)
	locked, _ = l.Locked("10.0.0.9")
	assert.False(t, locked)
	assert.False(t, l.Fail("10.0.0.9"), "counting starts again after the lock")
}

func TestLockoutWindowForgetsOldFailures(t *testing.T) {
	c := clock.NewFake(t0)
	l := NewLockout(c, 5, 15*time.Minute, 15*time.Minute)
	for range 4 {
		l.Fail("k")
	}
	c.Advance(15 * time.Minute)
	assert.False(t, l.Fail("k"), "the window expired, so this is failure 1")
	for range 3 {
		assert.False(t, l.Fail("k"))
	}
	assert.True(t, l.Fail("k"))
}

func TestLockoutSucceedAndReset(t *testing.T) {
	c := clock.NewFake(t0)
	l := NewLockout(c, 5, 15*time.Minute, 15*time.Minute)
	for range 4 {
		l.Fail("k")
	}
	l.Succeed("k")
	for range 4 {
		assert.False(t, l.Fail("k"), "success cleared the earlier failures")
	}
	assert.True(t, l.Fail("k"))
	l.Reset()
	locked, _ := l.Locked("k")
	assert.False(t, locked)
}

func TestLockoutBoundsMemory(t *testing.T) {
	c := clock.NewFake(t0)
	l := NewLockout(c, 5, time.Minute, time.Minute)
	for i := range maxKeys + 500 {
		l.Fail(fmt.Sprintf("ip:%d", i))
	}
	assert.LessOrEqual(t, len(l.entries), maxKeys)
}

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement the limiters**

`internal/auth/ratelimit/ratelimit.go`:
```go
// Package ratelimit provides in-memory limiters for a single Alice process
// (R20): token buckets for request rates and a failure lockout for guessing
// attacks. State resets on restart by design.
package ratelimit

import (
	"sync"
	"time"

	"alice/internal/platform/clock"
)

// maxKeys bounds memory: at most this many keys are tracked per limiter.
const maxKeys = 10_000

// Limiter is a set of token buckets, one per key. Each bucket holds up to
// limit tokens and refills at limit per period.
type Limiter struct {
	mu      sync.Mutex
	clock   clock.Clock
	limit   float64
	per     time.Duration
	buckets map[string]*bucket
}

type bucket struct {
	tokens float64
	last   time.Time
}

// New returns a limiter allowing limit requests per period per key (bursts up to limit).
func New(c clock.Clock, limit int, per time.Duration) *Limiter {
	return &Limiter{clock: c, limit: float64(limit), per: per, buckets: map[string]*bucket{}}
}

// Allow takes one token for key. When none is left it returns false and how
// long until the next token (rounded up to whole seconds, at least 1 s).
func (l *Limiter) Allow(key string) (bool, time.Duration) {
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()
	now := l.clock.Now()
	b, ok := l.buckets[key]
	if !ok {
		if len(l.buckets) >= maxKeys {
			l.evict(now)
		}
		b = &bucket{tokens: l.limit, last: now}
		l.buckets[key] = b
	}
	l.refill(b, now)
	if b.tokens >= 1 {
		b.tokens--
		return true, 0
	}
	wait := time.Duration((1 - b.tokens) / l.limit * float64(l.per))
	return false, ceilSecond(wait)
}

// Reset forgets every key.
func (l *Limiter) Reset() {
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()
	l.buckets = map[string]*bucket{}
}

func (l *Limiter) refill(b *bucket, now time.Time) {
	if elapsed := now.Sub(b.last); elapsed > 0 {
		b.tokens = min(l.limit, b.tokens+elapsed.Seconds()*l.limit/l.per.Seconds())
		b.last = now
	}
}

// evict drops buckets that have refilled completely (idle keys); if none, the
// least recently used one. The caller holds l.mu.
func (l *Limiter) evict(now time.Time) {
	var oldestKey string
	var oldest time.Time
	for k, b := range l.buckets {
		l.refill(b, now)
		if b.tokens >= l.limit {
			delete(l.buckets, k)
			continue
		}
		if oldestKey == "" || b.last.Before(oldest) {
			oldestKey, oldest = k, b.last
		}
	}
	if len(l.buckets) >= maxKeys && oldestKey != "" {
		delete(l.buckets, oldestKey)
	}
}

// Lockout locks a key for lockFor after maxFailures failures within window.
type Lockout struct {
	mu          sync.Mutex
	clock       clock.Clock
	maxFailures int
	window      time.Duration
	lockFor     time.Duration
	entries     map[string]*entry
}

type entry struct {
	failures    int
	first       time.Time // start of the current failure window
	lockedUntil time.Time
}

// NewLockout returns a lockout tracker.
func NewLockout(c clock.Clock, maxFailures int, window, lockFor time.Duration) *Lockout {
	return &Lockout{clock: c, maxFailures: maxFailures, window: window, lockFor: lockFor, entries: map[string]*entry{}}
}

// Locked reports whether key is locked and for how much longer (rounded up to seconds).
func (l *Lockout) Locked(key string) (bool, time.Duration) {
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()
	e, ok := l.entries[key]
	if !ok {
		return false, 0
	}
	now := l.clock.Now()
	if now.Before(e.lockedUntil) {
		return true, ceilSecond(e.lockedUntil.Sub(now))
	}
	return false, 0
}

// Fail records a failure and reports whether this failure locked the key.
func (l *Lockout) Fail(key string) bool {
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()
	now := l.clock.Now()
	e, ok := l.entries[key]
	if !ok {
		if len(l.entries) >= maxKeys {
			l.evict(now)
		}
		e = &entry{first: now}
		l.entries[key] = e
	}
	if now.Before(e.lockedUntil) {
		return false
	}
	if !e.lockedUntil.IsZero() || now.Sub(e.first) >= l.window {
		*e = entry{first: now} // previous lock or window is over: start counting again
	}
	e.failures++
	if e.failures >= l.maxFailures {
		e.lockedUntil = now.Add(l.lockFor)
		return true
	}
	return false
}

// Succeed clears the failures of key.
func (l *Lockout) Succeed(key string) {
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()
	delete(l.entries, key)
}

// Reset forgets every key.
func (l *Lockout) Reset() {
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()
	l.entries = map[string]*entry{}
}

// evict drops expired entries; if none, the oldest. The caller holds l.mu.
func (l *Lockout) evict(now time.Time) {
	var oldestKey string
	var oldest time.Time
	for k, e := range l.entries {
		if !now.Before(e.lockedUntil) && now.Sub(e.first) >= l.window {
			delete(l.entries, k)
			continue
		}
		if oldestKey == "" || e.first.Before(oldest) {
			oldestKey, oldest = k, e.first
		}
	}
	if len(l.entries) >= maxKeys && oldestKey != "" {
		delete(l.entries, oldestKey)
	}
}

func ceilSecond(d time.Duration) time.Duration {
	if d <= 0 {
		return time.Second
	}
	s := (d + time.Second - 1) / time.Second
	return s * time.Second
}
```

---

### Task 10: Auth service: sessions, login with lockout, MFA, step-up, password change

This task implements the first half of `internal/auth`: server-side sessions (SEC-018, SEC-019), the two-step
login (password → pending session → TOTP or recovery code → new full session), progressive delay and lockout
(SEC-020), TOTP enrollment at first login (SEC-007), step-up (SEC-009), logout, password change, recovery-code
regeneration (SEC-008), the per-user UI language, the CSRF token derivation (SEC-024) and `BootstrapAdmin`, which the
setup wizard (Task 12) uses to create the first administrator. Every mutation writes its audit event in the same
transaction (spec §5.4 rule 5); failures are audited too. All commands assume `export PATH="$HOME/.local/go/bin:$HOME/go/bin:$PATH"` and the repository root as working directory. Integration tests need Docker.

Design notes the implementer must keep:
- Passwords are always hashed and verified in NFKC form: callers pass `NormalizePassword(pw)` to `Hasher.Hash` / `Hasher.Verify`.
- Lockout rule (Global Constraints): failures are counted per account for a wrong password **or** a wrong MFA code. After failure
  `n`: `n >= 10` → `locked_until = now+15m` and audit `auth.lockout`; `n >= 5` → `locked_until = now + min(2^(n-5) s, 60 s)`.
  While `now < locked_until` every login / MFA / step-up attempt is refused with the generic error, **not counted**, and the
  password is not checked (a dummy Argon2 run keeps the timing identical). A completed MFA (or step-up) resets the counter.
- Unknown users are audited with `actor_name = "(unknown user)"`; the typed name is never stored (people type passwords
  into the username field).
- A full session rotation keeps the absolute expiry; a new login always gets a fresh 12 h absolute lifetime and a 5-minute
  step-up window (fresh MFA counts as step-up).
- Role and disabled state are read from `users` on every `Authenticate` call (SEC-017).

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/auth/service.go` (errors, constants, types, `NewService`)
- Create: `internal/auth/store.go` (SQL helpers for users and TOTP state)
- Create: `internal/auth/sessions.go` (session tokens, `Authenticate`, `SessionInfo`, CSRF, `Logout`)
- Create: `internal/auth/login.go` (`Login`, failure counting, delay and lockout)
- Create: `internal/auth/mfa.go` (`EnrollMFA`, `VerifyMFA`, `StepUp`, recovery codes)
- Create: `internal/auth/account.go` (`ChangePassword`, `SetLocale`)
- Create: `internal/auth/bootstrap.go` (`BootstrapAdmin`)
- Test: `internal/auth/env_integration_test.go`, `internal/auth/login_integration_test.go`, `internal/auth/session_integration_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 2: `db.Querier`, `db.InTx(ctx, pool, func(pgx.Tx) error) error`, `validate.Errors` (`Add`, `OneOf`).
  - Task 3: tables `users`, `user_mfa`, `recovery_codes`, `sessions` (migration `00002_core.sql`); `pgtest.Main(m) int`, `pgtest.New(t) *pgtest.DB` (`Owner`, `App` pools).
  - Task 4: `secrets.Keyring` (`Seal(loc, pt) (Envelope, error)`, `Open(loc, env) (Plaintext, error)`, `Derive(label) []byte`), `secrets.Location`, `secrets.Envelope`, `secrets.Plaintext` (`Bytes`, `Zero`), `secrets.LabelCSRF`, `secrets.NewKeyring(1, key)`.
  - Task 6: `audit.Log.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event) (audit.Entry, error)`, `audit.New(clock)`, `audit.Actor`, `audit.ActorUser`, `audit.ActorToken`, `audit.Success`, `audit.Failure`.
  - Task 7: `rbac.Role`, `rbac.Admin`, `rbac.SubjectKind` (`PendingSession`, `UserSession`, `APIToken`), `rbac.Subject`, `rbac.Scope`.
  - Task 8: `auth.Hasher` (`Hash`, `Verify`, `DummyVerify`), `auth.NewHasher`, `auth.Argon2Params`, `auth.NormalizePassword`, `auth.CheckPasswordPolicy`, `auth.ErrPasswordTooShort`, `auth.ErrPasswordBreached`.
  - Task 9: `auth.NewTOTP(account) (TOTPEnrollment, error)`, `auth.VerifyTOTP(secret, code, now, lastStep) (int64, bool)`, `auth.GenerateRecoveryCodes() []string`, `auth.NormalizeRecoveryCode(s) string`, `auth.TOTPEnrollment`.
  - `clock.Clock`, `clock.NewFake`.
- Produces (package `alice/internal/auth`; exact names used by later tasks, plus the additions marked NEW):
  - Errors: `ErrInvalidCredentials`, `ErrUnauthenticated`, `ErrMFAInvalid`, `ErrMFAAlreadyEnrolled`, `ErrCurrentPasswordInvalid`, `ErrAdminExists`, `ErrNotFound`.
  - Constants: `SessionIdle`, `SessionAbsolute`, `PendingLifetime`, `StepUpWindow`, `DelayThreshold`, `LockThreshold`, `LockDuration`, `MaxDelay`; NEW `StatusMFARequired = "mfa_required"`, `StatusMFAEnrollmentRequired = "mfa_enrollment_required"`.
  - NEW `var SupportedLocales = []string{"en", "tr"}` (setup reuses it).
  - Types: `Config`, `Service`, `Meta`, `User`, `Principal` (+ `Subject(now) rbac.Subject`, `Actor(m Meta) audit.Actor`), `LoginResult`, `MFAInput`, `SessionInfo`, `SessionResult`, `BootstrapAdmin`.
  - `func NewService(c Config) *Service`
  - `func (s *Service) Login(ctx, username, password string, m Meta) (LoginResult, error)`
  - `func (s *Service) EnrollMFA(ctx, p Principal, m Meta) (TOTPEnrollment, error)` — pending sessions only
  - `func (s *Service) VerifyMFA(ctx, p Principal, in MFAInput, m Meta) (SessionResult, error)` — pending sessions only; `RecoveryCodes` non-empty only when this call finished enrollment
  - `func (s *Service) Authenticate(ctx, sessionToken string) (Principal, error)`
  - `func (s *Service) SessionInfo(ctx, p Principal, sessionToken string) (SessionInfo, error)` — full sessions only; `StepUpUntil` is nil when the window is closed
  - `func (s *Service) CSRFToken(sessionToken string) string`, `func (s *Service) CheckCSRF(sessionToken, header string) bool`
  - `func (s *Service) StepUp(ctx, p Principal, code string, m Meta) (SessionResult, error)`
  - `func (s *Service) Logout(ctx, p Principal, m Meta) error`
  - `func (s *Service) ChangePassword(ctx, p Principal, current, next string, m Meta) (SessionResult, error)`
  - `func (s *Service) RegenerateRecoveryCodes(ctx, p Principal, m Meta) ([]string, error)`
  - `func (s *Service) SetLocale(ctx, p Principal, locale string) error`
  - `func (s *Service) BootstrapAdmin(ctx, in BootstrapAdmin, m Meta, inTx func(tx pgx.Tx) error) (SessionResult, error)`
  - Audit actions: `auth.login` (failure reasons `unknown_user|bad_password|locked|disabled`; success details `method: totp|recovery_code`), `auth.mfa_verify` (failure, reasons `bad_totp|bad_recovery_code|locked`), `auth.mfa_enrolled`, `auth.lockout`, `auth.logout`, `auth.stepup`, `auth.password_changed`, `auth.recovery_codes_regenerated`, `auth.recovery_code_used`, `setup.admin_created`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the integration test harness**

The harness uses a fake clock set to a TOTP step boundary, a fixed test master key and tiny Argon2 parameters (the
production parameters are asserted in Task 8). `signIn` advances the clock by one TOTP step before each login so a code is
never reused.

`internal/auth/env_integration_test.go`:
```go
//go:build integration

package auth_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"net/netip"
	"os"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/pquerna/otp/totp"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/platform/clock"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
	"alice/internal/secrets"
	"alice/internal/testutil/pgtest"
)

func TestMain(m *testing.M) { os.Exit(pgtest.Main(m)) }

// Small Argon2 parameters keep the suite fast; production values are tested in password_test.go.
var fastParams = auth.Argon2Params{MemoryKiB: 64, Time: 1, Threads: 1, SaltLen: 16, KeyLen: 32}

var meta = auth.Meta{IP: netip.MustParseAddr("192.0.2.10"), UserAgent: "test-agent", RequestID: "req-1"}

const adminPassword = "correct horse battery staple"

type env struct {
	ctx    context.Context
	svc    *auth.Service
	clk    *clock.Fake
	db     *pgtest.DB
	hasher *auth.Hasher
}

func newEnv(t *testing.T) *env {
	t.Helper()
	d := pgtest.New(t)
	// 09:00:00 is a TOTP step boundary, so codes computed at clk.Now() are stable.
	clk := clock.NewFake(time.Date(2026, 10, 1, 9, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC))
	kr, err := secrets.NewKeyring(1, bytes.Repeat([]byte{7}, 32))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	h := auth.NewHasher(fastParams, 4)
	svc := auth.NewService(auth.Config{Pool: d.App, Keyring: kr, Audit: audit.New(clk), Clock: clk, Hasher: h, RecoveryHasher: h})
	return &env{ctx: context.Background(), svc: svc, clk: clk, db: d, hasher: h}
}

func code(t *testing.T, secret string, at time.Time) string {
	t.Helper()
	c, err := totp.GenerateCode(secret, at)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return c
}

// bootstrap creates the first admin "admin" and returns its TOTP secret and session.
func (e *env) bootstrap(t *testing.T) (string, auth.SessionResult) {
	t.Helper()
	enr, err := auth.NewTOTP("admin")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	hash, err := e.hasher.Hash(e.ctx, auth.NormalizePassword(adminPassword))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	res, err := e.svc.BootstrapAdmin(e.ctx, auth.BootstrapAdmin{
		Username: "admin", DisplayName: "Ayşe Yılmaz", Email: "ayse@example.com", PasswordHash: hash,
		TOTPSecret: enr.Secret, Code: code(t, enr.Secret, e.clk.Now()), Locale: "tr",
	}, meta, nil)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return enr.Secret, res
}

// insertUser adds a local user without MFA directly in the database.
func (e *env) insertUser(t *testing.T, username string, role rbac.Role, password string) uuid.UUID {
	t.Helper()
	hash, err := e.hasher.Hash(e.ctx, auth.NormalizePassword(password))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	id := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	_, err = e.db.Owner.Exec(e.ctx, `INSERT INTO users (id, username, display_name, password_hash, role, created_at, updated_at)
VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $6)`, id, username, username, hash, string(role), e.clk.Now())
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return id
}

// enroll performs the first login of a user without MFA and returns the TOTP secret and result.
func (e *env) enroll(t *testing.T, username, password string) (string, auth.SessionResult) {
	t.Helper()
	lr, err := e.svc.Login(e.ctx, username, password, meta)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.Equal(t, auth.StatusMFAEnrollmentRequired, lr.Status)
	p := e.principal(t, lr.SessionToken)
	enr, err := e.svc.EnrollMFA(e.ctx, p, meta)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	res, err := e.svc.VerifyMFA(e.ctx, p, auth.MFAInput{Code: code(t, enr.Secret, e.clk.Now())}, meta)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return enr.Secret, res
}

// signIn performs password + TOTP login one step later than any earlier code.
func (e *env) signIn(t *testing.T, username, password, secret string) auth.SessionResult {
	t.Helper()
	e.clk.Advance(30 * time.Second)
	lr, err := e.svc.Login(e.ctx, username, password, meta)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.Equal(t, auth.StatusMFARequired, lr.Status)
	res, err := e.svc.VerifyMFA(e.ctx, e.principal(t, lr.SessionToken), auth.MFAInput{Code: code(t, secret, e.clk.Now())}, meta)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return res
}

func (e *env) principal(t *testing.T, token string) auth.Principal {
	t.Helper()
	p, err := e.svc.Authenticate(e.ctx, token)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return p
}

func (e *env) actions(t *testing.T) []string {
	t.Helper()
	rows, err := e.db.Owner.Query(e.ctx, "SELECT action || ':' || result FROM audit_log ORDER BY seq")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	defer rows.Close()
	var out []string
	for rows.Next() {
		var s string
		require.NoError(t, rows.Scan(&s))
		out = append(out, s)
	}
	require.NoError(t, rows.Err())
	return out
}

func (e *env) failedLogins(t *testing.T, username string) int {
	t.Helper()
	var n int
	require.NoError(t, e.db.Owner.QueryRow(e.ctx, "SELECT failed_logins FROM users WHERE username = $1", username).Scan(&n))
	return n
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing login and MFA tests**

These cover: bootstrap as first audit link; pending → full session with a new ID (A-04); case-insensitive usernames;
generic errors and "(unknown user)" auditing (SEC-004); progressive delay and 15-minute lockout (R-01, R-02); a wrong
TOTP counting as a failure; Review Focus #3 (the enrollment code cannot be reused for login in the same step, the
previous step's code is accepted when newer than the last used step); disabled users; the role read from the database on
each request (SEC-017).

`internal/auth/login_integration_test.go`:
```go
//go:build integration

package auth_test

import (
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

func TestBootstrapAdminCreatesFirstChainLinkAndSession(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	enr, err := auth.NewTOTP("admin")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = e.svc.BootstrapAdmin(e.ctx, auth.BootstrapAdmin{Username: "admin", DisplayName: "A", PasswordHash: "x",
		TOTPSecret: enr.Secret, Code: code(t, enr.Secret, e.clk.Now().Add(time.Hour))}, meta, nil)
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrMFAInvalid)

	_, res := e.bootstrap(t)
	assert.Len(t, res.RecoveryCodes, 10)
	assert.NotEmpty(t, res.Token)
	assert.Equal(t, rbac.Admin, res.Info.User.Role)
	assert.True(t, res.Info.User.MFAEnrolled)
	assert.Equal(t, "Ayşe Yılmaz", res.Info.User.DisplayName)
	assert.Equal(t, "tr", res.Info.User.Locale)
	require.NotNil(t, res.Info.StepUpUntil)
	assert.Equal(t, e.clk.Now().Add(auth.StepUpWindow), *res.Info.StepUpUntil)
	assert.Equal(t, e.svc.CSRFToken(res.Token), res.Info.CSRFToken)

	var seq int64
	var action string
	require.NoError(t, e.db.Owner.QueryRow(e.ctx, "SELECT seq, action FROM audit_log ORDER BY seq LIMIT 1").Scan(&seq, &action))
	assert.Equal(t, int64(1), seq)
	assert.Equal(t, "setup.admin_created", action)

	enr2, err := auth.NewTOTP("second")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = e.svc.BootstrapAdmin(e.ctx, auth.BootstrapAdmin{Username: "second", DisplayName: "B", PasswordHash: "x",
		TOTPSecret: enr2.Secret, Code: code(t, enr2.Secret, e.clk.Now())}, meta, nil)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrAdminExists)
}

func TestLoginNeedsMFAAndIssuesANewSessionID(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	secret, _ := e.bootstrap(t)
	e.clk.Advance(30 * time.Second)

	lr, err := e.svc.Login(e.ctx, "ADMIN", adminPassword, meta) // usernames are case-insensitive
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, auth.StatusMFARequired, lr.Status)
	pending := e.principal(t, lr.SessionToken)
	assert.Equal(t, rbac.PendingSession, pending.Kind)
	_, err = e.svc.SessionInfo(e.ctx, pending, lr.SessionToken)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUnauthenticated, "a pending session is not a full session")

	res, err := e.svc.VerifyMFA(e.ctx, pending, auth.MFAInput{Code: code(t, secret, e.clk.Now())}, meta)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Empty(t, res.RecoveryCodes, "codes are only returned at enrollment")
	assert.NotEqual(t, lr.SessionToken, res.Token, "session ID changes after MFA (A-04)")
	_, err = e.svc.Authenticate(e.ctx, lr.SessionToken)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUnauthenticated, "the pending session is gone")
	full := e.principal(t, res.Token)
	assert.Equal(t, rbac.UserSession, full.Kind)
	assert.True(t, full.Subject(e.clk.Now()).StepUp, "a fresh MFA opens the step-up window")
	assert.Contains(t, e.actions(t), "auth.login:success")
}

func TestUnknownUserGetsTheGenericErrorAndIsNotRecordedByName(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	e.bootstrap(t)
	_, err := e.svc.Login(e.ctx, "my-secret-password-typed-as-name", "whatever whatever", meta)
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrInvalidCredentials)
	_, err = e.svc.Login(e.ctx, "admin", "wrong password here", meta)
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrInvalidCredentials, "same error for a wrong password (SEC-004)")

	var name, reason string
	require.NoError(t, e.db.Owner.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT actor_name, details->>'reason' FROM audit_log
WHERE action = 'auth.login' AND result = 'failure' ORDER BY seq LIMIT 1`).Scan(&name, &reason))
	assert.Equal(t, "(unknown user)", name)
	assert.Equal(t, "unknown_user", reason)
	var n int
	require.NoError(t, e.db.Owner.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM audit_log
WHERE actor_name LIKE '%my-secret%' OR details::text LIKE '%my-secret%'`).Scan(&n))
	assert.Zero(t, n)
}

func TestProgressiveDelayThenLockout(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	secret, _ := e.bootstrap(t)
	wrong := func() {
		t.Helper()
		_, err := e.svc.Login(e.ctx, "admin", "definitely not it", meta)
		require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrInvalidCredentials)
	}
	right := func() error {
		_, err := e.svc.Login(e.ctx, "admin", adminPassword, meta)
		return err
	}
	for range 5 {
		wrong()
	}
	assert.Equal(t, 5, e.failedLogins(t, "admin"))
	require.ErrorIs(t, right(), auth.ErrInvalidCredentials, "correct password is refused during the delay (R-01)")
	assert.Equal(t, 5, e.failedLogins(t, "admin"), "attempts during the delay are not counted")
	e.clk.Advance(time.Second)
	require.NoError(t, right(), "works once the 1 s delay has passed")

	for _, d := range []time.Duration{0, 2 * time.Second, 4 * time.Second, 8 * time.Second, 16 * time.Second} {
		e.clk.Advance(d)
		wrong()
	}
	assert.Equal(t, 10, e.failedLogins(t, "admin"))
	assert.Contains(t, e.actions(t), "auth.lockout:failure")

	require.ErrorIs(t, right(), auth.ErrInvalidCredentials, "locked (R-02)")
	e.clk.Advance(auth.LockDuration - time.Second)
	require.ErrorIs(t, right(), auth.ErrInvalidCredentials, "still locked just before 15 min")
	e.clk.Advance(time.Second)
	e.signIn(t, "admin", adminPassword, secret)
	assert.Equal(t, 0, e.failedLogins(t, "admin"), "a full login resets the counter")
}

func TestWrongTOTPCountsAsAFailure(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	secret, _ := e.bootstrap(t)
	e.clk.Advance(30 * time.Second)
	lr, err := e.svc.Login(e.ctx, "admin", adminPassword, meta)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = e.svc.VerifyMFA(e.ctx, e.principal(t, lr.SessionToken), auth.MFAInput{Code: code(t, secret, e.clk.Now().Add(time.Hour))}, meta)
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrMFAInvalid)
	assert.Equal(t, 1, e.failedLogins(t, "admin"))
	assert.Contains(t, e.actions(t), "auth.mfa_verify:failure")
}

func TestEnrollmentCodeCannotBeReusedButLaterStepsWork(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	e.bootstrap(t)
	e.insertUser(t, "operator1", rbac.Operator, adminPassword)
	secret, res := e.enroll(t, "operator1", adminPassword)
	assert.Len(t, res.RecoveryCodes, 10, "first enrollment returns recovery codes")
	assert.True(t, res.Info.User.MFAEnrolled)
	assert.Contains(t, e.actions(t), "auth.mfa_enrolled:success")

	lr, err := e.svc.Login(e.ctx, "operator1", adminPassword, meta)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, auth.StatusMFARequired, lr.Status)
	pending := e.principal(t, lr.SessionToken)
	enrollCode := code(t, secret, e.clk.Now())
	_, err = e.svc.VerifyMFA(e.ctx, pending, auth.MFAInput{Code: enrollCode}, meta)
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrMFAInvalid, "the enrollment code is used up for this step")

	e.clk.Advance(60 * time.Second) // two steps later; the previous step is still newer than the used one
	prev := code(t, secret, e.clk.Now().Add(-30*time.Second))
	_, err = e.svc.VerifyMFA(e.ctx, pending, auth.MFAInput{Code: prev}, meta)
	require.NoError(t, err, "a code from the previous step is accepted when it is newer than the last used step")
}

func TestEnrollMFARefusedOnceEnrolled(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	e.bootstrap(t)
	e.clk.Advance(30 * time.Second)
	lr, err := e.svc.Login(e.ctx, "admin", adminPassword, meta)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = e.svc.EnrollMFA(e.ctx, e.principal(t, lr.SessionToken), meta)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrMFAAlreadyEnrolled)
}

func TestDisabledUserIsRejectedAndLosesSessions(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	e.bootstrap(t)
	e.insertUser(t, "op", rbac.Operator, adminPassword)
	_, res := e.enroll(t, "op", adminPassword)
	_, err := e.db.Owner.Exec(e.ctx, "UPDATE users SET disabled_at = now() WHERE username = 'op'")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = e.svc.Authenticate(e.ctx, res.Token)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUnauthenticated)
	_, err = e.svc.Login(e.ctx, "op", adminPassword, meta)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrInvalidCredentials)
	var reason string
	require.NoError(t, e.db.Owner.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT details->>'reason' FROM audit_log WHERE action = 'auth.login' ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT 1`).Scan(&reason))
	assert.Equal(t, "disabled", reason)
}

func TestRoleIsReadFromTheDatabaseOnEveryRequest(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	e.bootstrap(t)
	e.insertUser(t, "op", rbac.Operator, adminPassword)
	_, res := e.enroll(t, "op", adminPassword)
	assert.Equal(t, rbac.Operator, e.principal(t, res.Token).Role)
	_, err := e.db.Owner.Exec(e.ctx, "UPDATE users SET role = 'auditor' WHERE username = 'op'")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, rbac.Auditor, e.principal(t, res.Token).Role, "SEC-017")
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing session tests**

Idle 29/31 minutes and absolute 12 h (A-02, A-03), step-up window and rotation, logout (A-05), password change revoking
other sessions, recovery codes single use and regeneration, CSRF binding, locale.

`internal/auth/session_integration_test.go`:
```go
//go:build integration

package auth_test

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/platform/validate"
)

func TestIdleTimeoutSlidesAndAbsoluteLimitHolds(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	secret, _ := e.bootstrap(t)

	res := e.signIn(t, "admin", adminPassword, secret)
	e.clk.Advance(29 * time.Minute)
	e.principal(t, res.Token) // 29 min idle: valid, and the idle timer restarts (A-02)
	e.clk.Advance(29 * time.Minute)
	e.principal(t, res.Token)
	e.clk.Advance(31 * time.Minute)
	_, err := e.svc.Authenticate(e.ctx, res.Token)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUnauthenticated, "31 min idle ends the session")

	res = e.signIn(t, "admin", adminPassword, secret)
	start := e.clk.Now() // the session was created now
	for e.clk.Now().Add(29 * time.Minute).Before(start.Add(auth.SessionAbsolute)) {
		e.clk.Advance(29 * time.Minute)
		e.principal(t, res.Token)
	}
	e.clk.Set(start.Add(auth.SessionAbsolute))
	_, err = e.svc.Authenticate(e.ctx, res.Token)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUnauthenticated, "12 h absolute limit even when active (A-03)")
}

func TestStepUpOpensWindowAndRotatesSession(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	secret, _ := e.bootstrap(t)
	res := e.signIn(t, "admin", adminPassword, secret)
	e.clk.Advance(6 * time.Minute)
	p := e.principal(t, res.Token)
	assert.False(t, p.Subject(e.clk.Now()).StepUp, "the login step-up window lasts 5 minutes")
	info, err := e.svc.SessionInfo(e.ctx, p, res.Token)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Nil(t, info.StepUpUntil)

	_, err = e.svc.StepUp(e.ctx, p, code(t, secret, e.clk.Now().Add(time.Hour)), meta)
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrMFAInvalid)

	up, err := e.svc.StepUp(e.ctx, p, code(t, secret, e.clk.Now()), meta)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.NotEqual(t, res.Token, up.Token, "step-up issues a new session ID (SEC-019)")
	_, err = e.svc.Authenticate(e.ctx, res.Token)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUnauthenticated)
	np := e.principal(t, up.Token)
	assert.True(t, np.Subject(e.clk.Now()).StepUp)
	require.NotNil(t, up.Info.StepUpUntil)
	assert.Equal(t, e.clk.Now().Add(auth.StepUpWindow), *up.Info.StepUpUntil)
	assert.Equal(t, info.ExpiresAt, up.Info.ExpiresAt, "rotation keeps the absolute expiry")
	assert.Contains(t, e.actions(t), "auth.stepup:success")
	assert.Contains(t, e.actions(t), "auth.stepup:failure")
}

func TestLogoutInvalidatesTheSession(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	secret, _ := e.bootstrap(t)
	res := e.signIn(t, "admin", adminPassword, secret)
	require.NoError(t, e.svc.Logout(e.ctx, e.principal(t, res.Token), meta))
	_, err := e.svc.Authenticate(e.ctx, res.Token)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUnauthenticated, "A-05")
	assert.Contains(t, e.actions(t), "auth.logout:success")
}

func TestChangePasswordRevokesOtherSessions(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	secret, _ := e.bootstrap(t)
	a := e.signIn(t, "admin", adminPassword, secret)
	b := e.signIn(t, "admin", adminPassword, secret)
	p := e.principal(t, a.Token)

	_, err := e.svc.ChangePassword(e.ctx, p, "not the current one", "another long passphrase", meta)
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrCurrentPasswordInvalid)
	_, err = e.svc.ChangePassword(e.ctx, p, adminPassword, "short", meta)
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrPasswordTooShort)
	_, err = e.svc.ChangePassword(e.ctx, p, adminPassword, "qwerty123456", meta)
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrPasswordBreached)

	res, err := e.svc.ChangePassword(e.ctx, p, adminPassword, "another long passphrase", meta)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	for _, old := range []string{a.Token, b.Token} {
		_, err = e.svc.Authenticate(e.ctx, old)
		assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUnauthenticated)
	}
	e.principal(t, res.Token)
	e.signIn(t, "admin", "another long passphrase", secret)
	assert.Contains(t, e.actions(t), "auth.password_changed:success")
	assert.Contains(t, e.actions(t), "auth.password_changed:failure")
}

func TestRecoveryCodesAreSingleUseAndRegenerable(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	_, boot := e.bootstrap(t)
	login := func(rc string) error {
		lr, err := e.svc.Login(e.ctx, "admin", adminPassword, meta)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		_, err = e.svc.VerifyMFA(e.ctx, e.principal(t, lr.SessionToken), auth.MFAInput{RecoveryCode: rc}, meta)
		return err
	}
	typed := " " + strings.ReplaceAll(boot.RecoveryCodes[0], "-", " ") + " " // spaces instead of dashes still work
	require.NoError(t, login(typed))
	require.ErrorIs(t, login(boot.RecoveryCodes[0]), auth.ErrMFAInvalid, "single use")
	assert.Contains(t, e.actions(t), "auth.recovery_code_used:success")

	codes, err := e.svc.RegenerateRecoveryCodes(e.ctx, e.principal(t, boot.Token), meta)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.Len(t, codes, 10)
	require.ErrorIs(t, login(boot.RecoveryCodes[1]), auth.ErrMFAInvalid, "old set is invalid")
	require.NoError(t, login(codes[0]))
}

func TestCSRFTokenIsBoundToTheSession(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	_, res := e.bootstrap(t)
	tok := e.svc.CSRFToken(res.Token)
	assert.True(t, e.svc.CheckCSRF(res.Token, tok))
	assert.False(t, e.svc.CheckCSRF(res.Token, tok+"x"))
	assert.False(t, e.svc.CheckCSRF("other-session", tok))
	assert.False(t, e.svc.CheckCSRF(res.Token, ""))
	assert.False(t, e.svc.CheckCSRF("", ""))
}

func TestSetLocale(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	_, res := e.bootstrap(t)
	p := e.principal(t, res.Token)
	var v validate.Errors
	require.ErrorAs(t, e.svc.SetLocale(e.ctx, p, "de"), &v)
	assert.Equal(t, validate.OneOf, v[0].Code)
	require.NoError(t, e.svc.SetLocale(e.ctx, p, "en"))
	info, err := e.svc.SessionInfo(e.ctx, p, res.Token)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, "en", info.User.Locale)
}
```
