- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the service types**

`internal/auth/service.go`:
```go
package auth

import (
	"errors"
	"net/netip"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/platform/clock"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
	"alice/internal/secrets"
)

// Errors returned by the authentication service. Handlers map them to API error codes.
var (
	ErrInvalidCredentials     = errors.New("auth: invalid credentials")
	ErrUnauthenticated        = errors.New("auth: not authenticated")
	ErrMFAInvalid             = errors.New("auth: verification code invalid")
	ErrMFAAlreadyEnrolled     = errors.New("auth: MFA already enrolled")
	ErrCurrentPasswordInvalid = errors.New("auth: current password invalid")
	ErrAdminExists            = errors.New("auth: an administrator already exists")
	ErrNotFound               = errors.New("auth: not found")
)

// Session and lockout limits (SEC-019, SEC-020).
const (
	SessionIdle     = 30 * time.Minute
	SessionAbsolute = 12 * time.Hour
	PendingLifetime = 5 * time.Minute
	StepUpWindow    = 5 * time.Minute
	DelayThreshold  = 5
	LockThreshold   = 10
	LockDuration    = 15 * time.Minute
	MaxDelay        = 60 * time.Second
)

// unknownUserName is recorded in the audit log instead of a username that does not
// exist, because people sometimes type their password into the username field.
const unknownUserName = "(unknown user)"

// Config holds the service dependencies.
type Config struct {
	Pool           *pgxpool.Pool
	Keyring        *secrets.Keyring
	Audit          *audit.Log
	Clock          clock.Clock
	Hasher         *Hasher // PasswordParams in production
	RecoveryHasher *Hasher // RecoveryCodeParams in production
}

// Service implements local authentication, sessions, MFA, users and API tokens.
type Service struct {
	pool     *pgxpool.Pool
	keyring  *secrets.Keyring
	audit    *audit.Log
	clock    clock.Clock
	hasher   *Hasher
	recovery *Hasher
	csrfKey  []byte
}

// NewService builds the service.
func NewService(c Config) *Service {
	return &Service{
		pool:     c.Pool,
		keyring:  c.Keyring,
		audit:    c.Audit,
		clock:    c.Clock,
		hasher:   c.Hasher,
		recovery: c.RecoveryHasher,
		csrfKey:  c.Keyring.Derive(secrets.LabelCSRF),
	}
}

// Meta describes the request that triggered an operation.
type Meta struct {
	IP        netip.Addr
	UserAgent string
	RequestID string
}

// User is the public view of an account. It never contains secrets.
type User struct {
	ID          uuid.UUID
	Username    string
	DisplayName string
	Email       string
	Role        rbac.Role
	Locale      string
	Disabled    bool
	MFAEnrolled bool
	LastLoginAt *time.Time
	CreatedAt   time.Time
}

// Principal is the authenticated caller of one request.
type Principal struct {
	Kind        rbac.SubjectKind
	UserID      uuid.UUID
	Username    string
	DisplayName string
	Role        rbac.Role
	Locale      string
	SessionHash []byte
	StepUpUntil time.Time
	TokenID     uuid.UUID
	Scope       rbac.Scope
}

// Subject converts the principal into the input of rbac.Decide.
func (p Principal) Subject(now time.Time) rbac.Subject {
	return rbac.Subject{
		Kind:   p.Kind,
		Role:   p.Role,
		Scope:  p.Scope,
		StepUp: p.Kind == rbac.UserSession && now.Before(p.StepUpUntil),
	}
}

// Actor returns the audit actor for the principal.
func (p Principal) Actor(m Meta) audit.Actor {
	t := audit.ActorUser
	if p.Kind == rbac.APIToken {
		t = audit.ActorToken
	}
	return audit.Actor{Type: t, ID: p.UserID, Name: p.Username, IP: m.IP, RequestID: m.RequestID}
}

// LoginResult is the outcome of the password step.
type LoginResult struct {
	Status       string // "mfa_required" | "mfa_enrollment_required"
	SessionToken string // pending session cookie value
}

// Login status values.
const (
	StatusMFARequired           = "mfa_required"
	StatusMFAEnrollmentRequired = "mfa_enrollment_required"
)

// MFAInput carries either a TOTP code or a recovery code.
type MFAInput struct{ Code, RecoveryCode string }

// SessionInfo describes the caller's current full session.
type SessionInfo struct {
	User          User
	CSRFToken     string
	ExpiresAt     time.Time
	IdleExpiresAt time.Time
	StepUpUntil   *time.Time
}

// SessionResult is returned whenever a new full session is issued.
type SessionResult struct {
	Token         string
	Info          SessionInfo
	RecoveryCodes []string
}

func userActor(id uuid.UUID, name string, m Meta) audit.Actor {
	return audit.Actor{Type: audit.ActorUser, ID: id, Name: name, IP: m.IP, RequestID: m.RequestID}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the storage helpers**

`username` and `email` are `citext`; they are cast to `text` in every SELECT so pgx scans them into `string`.

`internal/auth/store.go`:
```go
package auth

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"net/netip"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"alice/internal/platform/db"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
	"alice/internal/secrets"
)

// userColumns selects a User from "users u".
const userColumns = `u.id, u.username::text, u.display_name, u.email::text, u.role, u.locale,
u.disabled_at IS NOT NULL,
EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM user_mfa m WHERE m.user_id = u.id AND m.enrolled_at IS NOT NULL),
u.last_login_at, u.created_at`

func scanUser(row pgx.Row) (User, error) {
	var u User
	var role string
	err := row.Scan(&u.ID, &u.Username, &u.DisplayName, &u.Email, &role, &u.Locale,
		&u.Disabled, &u.MFAEnrolled, &u.LastLoginAt, &u.CreatedAt)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return User{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	if err != nil {
		return User{}, fmt.Errorf("read user: %w", err)
	}
	u.Role = rbac.Role(role)
	u.CreatedAt = u.CreatedAt.UTC()
	if u.LastLoginAt != nil {
		t := u.LastLoginAt.UTC()
		u.LastLoginAt = &t
	}
	return u, nil
}

func getUser(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier, id uuid.UUID) (User, error) {
	return scanUser(q.QueryRow(ctx, "SELECT "+userColumns+" FROM users u WHERE u.id = $1", id))
}

// ipParam returns a value for an inet parameter ($n::inet): NULL when unknown.
func ipParam(a netip.Addr) any {
	if !a.IsValid() {
		return nil
	}
	return a.Unmap().String()
}

// truncateUA bounds the stored user agent.
func truncateUA(s string) string {
	const maxUA = 256
	r := []rune(s)
	if len(r) > maxUA {
		return string(r[:maxUA])
	}
	return s
}

func totpLocation(userID uuid.UUID) secrets.Location {
	return secrets.Location{Table: "user_mfa", RowID: userID.String(), Column: "totp_secret"}
}

// mfaRow is the stored TOTP state of a user.
type mfaRow struct {
	env      secrets.Envelope
	enrolled bool
	lastStep int64
}

// loadMFA returns the TOTP state, or found=false.
func loadMFA(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier, userID uuid.UUID) (mfaRow, bool, error) {
	var r mfaRow
	var enrolledAt *time.Time
	err := q.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT totp_secret_ct, totp_nonce, totp_wrapped_dek, totp_key_version, enrolled_at, last_used_step
FROM user_mfa WHERE user_id = $1`, userID).Scan(&r.env.Ciphertext, &r.env.Nonce, &r.env.WrappedDEK, &r.env.KeyVersion, &enrolledAt, &r.lastStep)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return mfaRow{}, false, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return mfaRow{}, false, fmt.Errorf("read MFA state: %w", err)
	}
	r.enrolled = enrolledAt != nil
	return r, true, nil
}

// openSecret decrypts the TOTP secret. The caller must zero the result.
func (s *Service) openSecret(userID uuid.UUID, r mfaRow) (secrets.Plaintext, error) {
	return s.keyring.Open(totpLocation(userID), r.env)
}

// consumeStep records an accepted TOTP step; it fails when the step was already used (replay).
func consumeStep(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier, userID uuid.UUID, step int64, enroll bool, now time.Time) (bool, error) {
	tag, err := q.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE user_mfa SET last_used_step = $2,
enrolled_at = CASE WHEN $3 THEN coalesce(enrolled_at, $4) ELSE enrolled_at END
WHERE user_id = $1 AND last_used_step < $2`, userID, step, enroll, now)
	if err != nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("record TOTP step: %w", err)
	}
	return tag.RowsAffected() == 1, nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement sessions and CSRF**

`internal/auth/sessions.go`:
```go
package auth

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/hmac"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/base64"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/platform/db"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

// newSessionToken returns a 256-bit random cookie value (SEC-018).
func newSessionToken() string {
	b := make([]byte, 32)
	_, _ = rand.Read(b)
	return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(b)
}

// hashToken is the value stored in sessions.id_hash; the raw token is never stored.
func hashToken(token string) []byte {
	h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(token))
	return h[:]
}

// newSession describes a session row to insert.
type newSession struct {
	userID    uuid.UUID
	kind      string // "pending" | "full"
	createdAt time.Time
	expiresAt time.Time
	stepUp    *time.Time
}

// insertSession stores a session and returns its cookie value.
func (s *Service) insertSession(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier, ns newSession, m Meta) (string, error) {
	now := s.clock.Now().UTC()
	token := newSessionToken()
	idle := now.Add(SessionIdle)
	if ns.kind == "pending" || idle.After(ns.expiresAt) {
		idle = ns.expiresAt
	}
	var mfaAt *time.Time
	if ns.kind == "full" {
		mfaAt = &now
	}
	_, err := q.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO sessions (id_hash, user_id, kind, created_at, last_seen_at, expires_at, idle_expires_at, ip, user_agent, mfa_verified_at, stepup_until)
VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8::inet, $9, $10, $11)`,
		hashToken(token), ns.userID, ns.kind, ns.createdAt, now, ns.expiresAt, idle, ipParam(m.IP), truncateUA(m.UserAgent), mfaAt, ns.stepUp)
	if err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("create session: %w", err)
	}
	return token, nil
}

// startFullSession creates a new full session with a fresh absolute lifetime and an open step-up window.
func (s *Service) startFullSession(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier, userID uuid.UUID, m Meta) (string, error) {
	now := s.clock.Now().UTC()
	stepUp := now.Add(StepUpWindow)
	return s.insertSession(ctx, q, newSession{userID: userID, kind: "full", createdAt: now, expiresAt: now.Add(SessionAbsolute), stepUp: &stepUp}, m)
}

// rotateSession replaces a full session by a new ID, keeping its absolute expiry (fixation defence, SEC-019).
func (s *Service) rotateSession(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, oldHash []byte, userID uuid.UUID, stepUp *time.Time, m Meta) (string, error) {
	var createdAt, expiresAt time.Time
	err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, "DELETE FROM sessions WHERE id_hash = $1 AND user_id = $2 RETURNING created_at, expires_at", oldHash, userID).Scan(&createdAt, &expiresAt)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return "", ErrUnauthenticated
	}
	if err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("rotate session: %w", err)
	}
	return s.insertSession(ctx, tx, newSession{userID: userID, kind: "full", createdAt: createdAt.UTC(), expiresAt: expiresAt.UTC(), stepUp: stepUp}, m)
}

// Authenticate resolves a session cookie. Role and disabled state are read from users on
// every call (SEC-017). Full sessions slide their idle timeout; pending sessions do not.
func (s *Service) Authenticate(ctx context.Context, sessionToken string) (Principal, error) {
	if sessionToken == "" {
		return Principal{}, ErrUnauthenticated
	}
	now := s.clock.Now().UTC()
	h := hashToken(sessionToken)
	var (
		p                 Principal
		kind, role        string
		expires, idle     time.Time
		stepUp            *time.Time
		revoked, disabled bool
	)
	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT s.kind, s.expires_at, s.idle_expires_at, s.stepup_until, s.revoked_at IS NOT NULL,
u.id, u.username::text, u.display_name, u.role, u.locale, u.disabled_at IS NOT NULL
FROM sessions s JOIN users u ON u.id = s.user_id WHERE s.id_hash = $1`, h).Scan(
		&kind, &expires, &idle, &stepUp, &revoked, &p.UserID, &p.Username, &p.DisplayName, &role, &p.Locale, &disabled)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return Principal{}, ErrUnauthenticated
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Principal{}, fmt.Errorf("read session: %w", err)
	}
	if revoked || disabled || !now.Before(expires) || !now.Before(idle) {
		return Principal{}, ErrUnauthenticated
	}
	p.Role = rbac.Role(role)
	p.SessionHash = h
	if stepUp != nil {
		p.StepUpUntil = stepUp.UTC()
	}
	p.Kind = rbac.PendingSession
	if kind == "full" {
		p.Kind = rbac.UserSession
		newIdle := now.Add(SessionIdle)
		if newIdle.After(expires) {
			newIdle = expires
		}
		if _, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx, "UPDATE sessions SET last_seen_at = $2, idle_expires_at = $3 WHERE id_hash = $1", h, now, newIdle); err != nil {
			return Principal{}, fmt.Errorf("touch session: %w", err)
		}
	}
	return p, nil
}

// SessionInfo returns the caller's session details and CSRF token.
func (s *Service) SessionInfo(ctx context.Context, p Principal, sessionToken string) (SessionInfo, error) {
	if p.Kind != rbac.UserSession {
		return SessionInfo{}, ErrUnauthenticated
	}
	var info SessionInfo
	var stepUp *time.Time
	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, "SELECT expires_at, idle_expires_at, stepup_until FROM sessions WHERE id_hash = $1 AND user_id = $2",
		p.SessionHash, p.UserID).Scan(&info.ExpiresAt, &info.IdleExpiresAt, &stepUp)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return SessionInfo{}, ErrUnauthenticated
	}
	if err != nil {
		return SessionInfo{}, fmt.Errorf("read session: %w", err)
	}
	u, err := getUser(ctx, s.pool, p.UserID)
	if err != nil {
		return SessionInfo{}, err
	}
	info.User = u
	info.CSRFToken = s.CSRFToken(sessionToken)
	info.ExpiresAt = info.ExpiresAt.UTC()
	info.IdleExpiresAt = info.IdleExpiresAt.UTC()
	if stepUp != nil && s.clock.Now().Before(*stepUp) {
		t := stepUp.UTC()
		info.StepUpUntil = &t
	}
	return info, nil
}

// resultFor builds a SessionResult for a freshly issued session token.
func (s *Service) resultFor(ctx context.Context, userID uuid.UUID, token string, codes []string) (SessionResult, error) {
	p, err := s.Authenticate(ctx, token)
	if err != nil {
		return SessionResult{}, err
	}
	info, err := s.SessionInfo(ctx, Principal{Kind: rbac.UserSession, UserID: userID, SessionHash: p.SessionHash}, token)
	if err != nil {
		return SessionResult{}, err
	}
	return SessionResult{Token: token, Info: info, RecoveryCodes: codes}, nil
}

// CSRFToken is HMAC-SHA256(K_csrf, session token), base64url (SEC-024).
func (s *Service) CSRFToken(sessionToken string) string {
	mac := hmac.New(sha256.New, s.csrfKey)
	mac.Write([]byte(sessionToken))
	return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(mac.Sum(nil))
}

// CheckCSRF compares the header with the expected token in constant time.
func (s *Service) CheckCSRF(sessionToken, header string) bool {
	if sessionToken == "" || header == "" {
		return false
	}
	return hmac.Equal([]byte(s.CSRFToken(sessionToken)), []byte(header))
}

// Logout deletes the caller's session.
func (s *Service) Logout(ctx context.Context, p Principal, m Meta) error {
	return db.InTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "DELETE FROM sessions WHERE id_hash = $1", p.SessionHash); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("delete session: %w", err)
		}
		_, err := s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: p.Actor(m), Action: "auth.logout", ObjectType: "user", ObjectID: p.UserID.String(), Result: audit.Success})
		return err
	})
}

// revokeUserSessions deletes every session of a user.
func revokeUserSessions(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier, userID uuid.UUID) error {
	if _, err := q.Exec(ctx, "DELETE FROM sessions WHERE user_id = $1", userID); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("revoke sessions: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Implement login, failure counting, delay and lockout**

`internal/auth/login.go`:
```go
package auth

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strconv"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/platform/db"
)

// failureDelay returns how long an account is blocked after its n-th consecutive failure,
// and whether that block is the 15-minute lockout (SEC-020).
func failureDelay(n int) (time.Duration, bool) {
	switch {
	case n >= LockThreshold:
		return LockDuration, true
	case n >= DelayThreshold:
		d := time.Duration(1<<(n-DelayThreshold)) * time.Second
		return min(d, MaxDelay), false
	default:
		return 0, false
	}
}

// recordFailure counts a failed password or code for a known account, applies the
// progressive delay or lockout, and audits the failure (and the lockout).
func (s *Service) recordFailure(ctx context.Context, userID uuid.UUID, username string, m Meta, action, reason string) error {
	now := s.clock.Now().UTC()
	return db.InTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		var n int
		if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, "UPDATE users SET failed_logins = failed_logins + 1, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $1 RETURNING failed_logins",
			userID, now).Scan(&n); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("count failure: %w", err)
		}
		d, locked := failureDelay(n)
		if d > 0 {
			if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "UPDATE users SET locked_until = $2 WHERE id = $1", userID, now.Add(d)); err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("delay account: %w", err)
			}
		}
		actor := userActor(userID, username, m)
		if _, err := s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: actor, Action: action, ObjectType: "user", ObjectID: userID.String(),
			Result: audit.Failure, Details: map[string]string{"reason": reason}}); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if locked {
			if _, err := s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: actor, Action: "auth.lockout", ObjectType: "user", ObjectID: userID.String(),
				Result: audit.Failure, Details: map[string]string{"failed_logins": strconv.Itoa(n), "minutes": strconv.Itoa(int(LockDuration / time.Minute))}}); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return nil
	})
}

// auditFailure writes a failure event without touching the counters.
func (s *Service) auditFailure(ctx context.Context, actor audit.Actor, action, objectID, reason string) error {
	return db.InTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		_, err := s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: actor, Action: action, ObjectType: "user", ObjectID: objectID,
			Result: audit.Failure, Details: map[string]string{"reason": reason}})
		return err
	})
}

// Login verifies username and password and opens a pending session that can only
// complete MFA. Every failure returns ErrInvalidCredentials (SEC-004).
func (s *Service) Login(ctx context.Context, username, password string, m Meta) (LoginResult, error) {
	now := s.clock.Now().UTC()
	pw := NormalizePassword(password)
	var (
		id                 uuid.UUID
		name               string
		hash               *string
		disabled, enrolled bool
		lockedUntil        *time.Time
	)
	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT u.id, u.username::text, u.password_hash, u.disabled_at IS NOT NULL, u.locked_until,
EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM user_mfa m WHERE m.user_id = u.id AND m.enrolled_at IS NOT NULL)
FROM users u WHERE u.username = $1 AND u.auth_source = 'local'`, username).Scan(&id, &name, &hash, &disabled, &lockedUntil, &enrolled)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) || (err == nil && hash == nil) {
		s.hasher.DummyVerify(ctx, pw)
		if aerr := s.auditFailure(ctx, audit.Actor{Type: audit.ActorUser, Name: unknownUserName, IP: m.IP, RequestID: m.RequestID}, "auth.login", "", "unknown_user"); aerr != nil {
			return LoginResult{}, aerr
		}
		return LoginResult{}, ErrInvalidCredentials
	}
	if err != nil {
		return LoginResult{}, fmt.Errorf("read user: %w", err)
	}
	if lockedUntil != nil && now.Before(*lockedUntil) {
		s.hasher.DummyVerify(ctx, pw)
		if aerr := s.auditFailure(ctx, userActor(id, name, m), "auth.login", id.String(), "locked"); aerr != nil {
			return LoginResult{}, aerr
		}
		return LoginResult{}, ErrInvalidCredentials
	}
	ok, rehash, err := s.hasher.Verify(ctx, *hash, pw)
	if err != nil {
		return LoginResult{}, err
	}
	if !ok {
		if ferr := s.recordFailure(ctx, id, name, m, "auth.login", "bad_password"); ferr != nil {
			return LoginResult{}, ferr
		}
		return LoginResult{}, ErrInvalidCredentials
	}
	if disabled {
		if aerr := s.auditFailure(ctx, userActor(id, name, m), "auth.login", id.String(), "disabled"); aerr != nil {
			return LoginResult{}, aerr
		}
		return LoginResult{}, ErrInvalidCredentials
	}
	if rehash {
		newHash, err := s.hasher.Hash(ctx, pw)
		if err != nil {
			return LoginResult{}, err
		}
		if _, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx, "UPDATE users SET password_hash = $2, updated_at = $3 WHERE id = $1", id, newHash, now); err != nil {
			return LoginResult{}, fmt.Errorf("upgrade password hash: %w", err)
		}
	}
	token, err := s.insertSession(ctx, s.pool, newSession{userID: id, kind: "pending", createdAt: now, expiresAt: now.Add(PendingLifetime)}, m)
	if err != nil {
		return LoginResult{}, err
	}
	status := StatusMFARequired
	if !enrolled {
		status = StatusMFAEnrollmentRequired
	}
	return LoginResult{Status: status, SessionToken: token}, nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Implement MFA verification, enrollment, step-up and recovery codes**

`consumeStep` only updates `last_used_step` when the new step is larger, so the same code can never be accepted twice,
even by two concurrent requests (SEC-006).

`internal/auth/mfa.go`:
```go
package auth

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/platform/db"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

// accountGate reports whether the account is currently delayed or locked, or disabled.
func (s *Service) accountGate(ctx context.Context, userID uuid.UUID) (blocked bool, err error) {
	var lockedUntil *time.Time
	var disabled bool
	err = s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, "SELECT locked_until, disabled_at IS NOT NULL FROM users WHERE id = $1", userID).Scan(&lockedUntil, &disabled)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return true, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("read account state: %w", err)
	}
	return disabled || (lockedUntil != nil && s.clock.Now().Before(*lockedUntil)), nil
}

// EnrollMFA starts (or restarts) TOTP enrollment for a pending session whose user has no MFA yet.
func (s *Service) EnrollMFA(ctx context.Context, p Principal, m Meta) (TOTPEnrollment, error) {
	if p.Kind != rbac.PendingSession {
		return TOTPEnrollment{}, ErrUnauthenticated
	}
	cur, found, err := loadMFA(ctx, s.pool, p.UserID)
	if err != nil {
		return TOTPEnrollment{}, err
	}
	if found && cur.enrolled {
		return TOTPEnrollment{}, ErrMFAAlreadyEnrolled
	}
	enr, err := NewTOTP(p.Username)
	if err != nil {
		return TOTPEnrollment{}, err
	}
	env, err := s.keyring.Seal(totpLocation(p.UserID), []byte(enr.Secret))
	if err != nil {
		return TOTPEnrollment{}, err
	}
	_, err = s.pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO user_mfa (user_id, totp_secret_ct, totp_nonce, totp_wrapped_dek, totp_key_version, enrolled_at, last_used_step, created_at)
VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, NULL, 0, $6)
ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE SET totp_secret_ct = EXCLUDED.totp_secret_ct, totp_nonce = EXCLUDED.totp_nonce,
  totp_wrapped_dek = EXCLUDED.totp_wrapped_dek, totp_key_version = EXCLUDED.totp_key_version, last_used_step = 0
WHERE user_mfa.enrolled_at IS NULL`, p.UserID, env.Ciphertext, env.Nonce, env.WrappedDEK, env.KeyVersion, s.clock.Now().UTC())
	if err != nil {
		return TOTPEnrollment{}, fmt.Errorf("store TOTP secret: %w", err)
	}
	return enr, nil
}

// checkTOTP verifies a code against the stored secret and consumes its step.
func (s *Service) checkTOTP(ctx context.Context, userID uuid.UUID, row mfaRow, code string, enroll bool) (bool, error) {
	secret, err := s.openSecret(userID, row)
	if err != nil {
		return false, err
	}
	defer secret.Zero()
	step, ok := VerifyTOTP(string(secret.Bytes()), code, s.clock.Now(), row.lastStep)
	if !ok {
		return false, nil
	}
	return consumeStep(ctx, s.pool, userID, step, enroll, s.clock.Now().UTC())
}

// useRecoveryCode marks a matching unused recovery code as used.
func (s *Service) useRecoveryCode(ctx context.Context, userID uuid.UUID, code string) (bool, error) {
	norm := NormalizeRecoveryCode(code)
	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx, "SELECT id, code_hash FROM recovery_codes WHERE user_id = $1 AND used_at IS NULL", userID)
	if err != nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("read recovery codes: %w", err)
	}
	type rc struct {
		id   uuid.UUID
		hash string
	}
	var list []rc
	for rows.Next() {
		var r rc
		if err := rows.Scan(&r.id, &r.hash); err != nil {
			rows.Close()
			return false, fmt.Errorf("read recovery code: %w", err)
		}
		list = append(list, r)
	}
	rows.Close()
	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("read recovery codes: %w", err)
	}
	for _, r := range list {
		ok, _, err := s.recovery.Verify(ctx, r.hash, norm)
		if err != nil {
			return false, err
		}
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		tag, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx, "UPDATE recovery_codes SET used_at = $2 WHERE id = $1 AND used_at IS NULL", r.id, s.clock.Now().UTC())
		if err != nil {
			return false, fmt.Errorf("use recovery code: %w", err)
		}
		return tag.RowsAffected() == 1, nil
	}
	return false, nil
}

// hashRecoveryCodes hashes normalized codes with the recovery-code hasher.
func (s *Service) hashRecoveryCodes(ctx context.Context, codes []string) ([]string, error) {
	out := make([]string, len(codes))
	for i, c := range codes {
		h, err := s.recovery.Hash(ctx, NormalizeRecoveryCode(c))
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		out[i] = h
	}
	return out, nil
}

// replaceRecoveryCodes deletes a user's codes and stores new hashes.
func (s *Service) replaceRecoveryCodes(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, userID uuid.UUID, hashes []string) error {
	if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "DELETE FROM recovery_codes WHERE user_id = $1", userID); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("delete recovery codes: %w", err)
	}
	now := s.clock.Now().UTC()
	for _, h := range hashes {
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "INSERT INTO recovery_codes (id, user_id, code_hash, created_at) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)",
			uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), userID, h, now); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("store recovery code: %w", err)
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// VerifyMFA completes a login: it checks a TOTP code (finishing enrollment when needed)
// or a recovery code, deletes the pending session and issues a new full session.
func (s *Service) VerifyMFA(ctx context.Context, p Principal, in MFAInput, m Meta) (SessionResult, error) {
	if p.Kind != rbac.PendingSession {
		return SessionResult{}, ErrUnauthenticated
	}
	blocked, err := s.accountGate(ctx, p.UserID)
	if err != nil {
		return SessionResult{}, err
	}
	if blocked {
		if err := s.auditFailure(ctx, userActor(p.UserID, p.Username, m), "auth.mfa_verify", p.UserID.String(), "locked"); err != nil {
			return SessionResult{}, err
		}
		return SessionResult{}, ErrMFAInvalid
	}
	row, found, err := loadMFA(ctx, s.pool, p.UserID)
	if err != nil {
		return SessionResult{}, err
	}
	method := "totp"
	ok := false
	switch {
	case !found:
	case in.RecoveryCode != "":
		method = "recovery_code"
		if row.enrolled {
			ok, err = s.useRecoveryCode(ctx, p.UserID, in.RecoveryCode)
		}
	default:
		ok, err = s.checkTOTP(ctx, p.UserID, row, in.Code, !row.enrolled)
	}
	if err != nil {
		return SessionResult{}, err
	}
	if !ok {
		if err := s.recordFailure(ctx, p.UserID, p.Username, m, "auth.mfa_verify", "bad_"+method); err != nil {
			return SessionResult{}, err
		}
		return SessionResult{}, ErrMFAInvalid
	}
	var codes, hashes []string
	enrolledNow := found && !row.enrolled
	if enrolledNow {
		codes = GenerateRecoveryCodes()
		if hashes, err = s.hashRecoveryCodes(ctx, codes); err != nil {
			return SessionResult{}, err
		}
	}
	actor := userActor(p.UserID, p.Username, m)
	var token string
	err = db.InTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "DELETE FROM sessions WHERE id_hash = $1", p.SessionHash); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("delete pending session: %w", err)
		}
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "UPDATE users SET failed_logins = 0, locked_until = NULL, last_login_at = $2, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $1",
			p.UserID, s.clock.Now().UTC()); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("reset failures: %w", err)
		}
		if enrolledNow {
			if err := s.replaceRecoveryCodes(ctx, tx, p.UserID, hashes); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if _, err := s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: actor, Action: "auth.mfa_enrolled", ObjectType: "user", ObjectID: p.UserID.String(), Result: audit.Success}); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		if method == "recovery_code" {
			if _, err := s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: actor, Action: "auth.recovery_code_used", ObjectType: "user", ObjectID: p.UserID.String(), Result: audit.Success}); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		if _, err := s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: actor, Action: "auth.login", ObjectType: "user", ObjectID: p.UserID.String(),
			Result: audit.Success, Details: map[string]string{"method": method}}); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		var err error
		token, err = s.startFullSession(ctx, tx, p.UserID, m)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return SessionResult{}, err
	}
	return s.resultFor(ctx, p.UserID, token, codes)
}

// StepUp re-verifies a TOTP code, opens a 5-minute step-up window and rotates the session ID.
func (s *Service) StepUp(ctx context.Context, p Principal, code string, m Meta) (SessionResult, error) {
	if p.Kind != rbac.UserSession {
		return SessionResult{}, ErrUnauthenticated
	}
	actor := p.Actor(m)
	blocked, err := s.accountGate(ctx, p.UserID)
	if err != nil {
		return SessionResult{}, err
	}
	if blocked {
		if err := s.auditFailure(ctx, actor, "auth.stepup", p.UserID.String(), "locked"); err != nil {
			return SessionResult{}, err
		}
		return SessionResult{}, ErrMFAInvalid
	}
	row, found, err := loadMFA(ctx, s.pool, p.UserID)
	if err != nil {
		return SessionResult{}, err
	}
	ok := false
	if found && row.enrolled {
		if ok, err = s.checkTOTP(ctx, p.UserID, row, code, false); err != nil {
			return SessionResult{}, err
		}
	}
	if !ok {
		if err := s.recordFailure(ctx, p.UserID, p.Username, m, "auth.stepup", "bad_totp"); err != nil {
			return SessionResult{}, err
		}
		return SessionResult{}, ErrMFAInvalid
	}
	stepUp := s.clock.Now().UTC().Add(StepUpWindow)
	var token string
	err = db.InTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		var err error
		if token, err = s.rotateSession(ctx, tx, p.SessionHash, p.UserID, &stepUp, m); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "UPDATE users SET failed_logins = 0, locked_until = NULL WHERE id = $1", p.UserID); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("reset failures: %w", err)
		}
		_, err = s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: actor, Action: "auth.stepup", ObjectType: "user", ObjectID: p.UserID.String(), Result: audit.Success})
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return SessionResult{}, err
	}
	return s.resultFor(ctx, p.UserID, token, nil)
}

// RegenerateRecoveryCodes replaces the caller's recovery codes (SEC-008; the route requires step-up).
func (s *Service) RegenerateRecoveryCodes(ctx context.Context, p Principal, m Meta) ([]string, error) {
	if p.Kind != rbac.UserSession {
		return nil, ErrUnauthenticated
	}
	codes := GenerateRecoveryCodes()
	hashes, err := s.hashRecoveryCodes(ctx, codes)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	err = db.InTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		if err := s.replaceRecoveryCodes(ctx, tx, p.UserID, hashes); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		_, err := s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: p.Actor(m), Action: "auth.recovery_codes_regenerated", ObjectType: "user", ObjectID: p.UserID.String(), Result: audit.Success})
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return codes, nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Implement password change and locale**

`internal/auth/account.go`:
```go
package auth

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/platform/db"
	"alice/internal/platform/validate"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

// SupportedLocales are the UI languages a user may choose.
var SupportedLocales = []string{"en", "tr"}

// ChangePassword verifies the current password, applies the policy to the new one,
// revokes every session of the user and returns a new full session (SEC-019).
func (s *Service) ChangePassword(ctx context.Context, p Principal, current, next string, m Meta) (SessionResult, error) {
	if p.Kind != rbac.UserSession {
		return SessionResult{}, ErrUnauthenticated
	}
	actor := p.Actor(m)
	var hash string
	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, "SELECT password_hash FROM users WHERE id = $1 AND password_hash IS NOT NULL", p.UserID).Scan(&hash)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return SessionResult{}, ErrUnauthenticated
	}
	if err != nil {
		return SessionResult{}, fmt.Errorf("read password: %w", err)
	}
	ok, _, err := s.hasher.Verify(ctx, hash, NormalizePassword(current))
	if err != nil {
		return SessionResult{}, err
	}
	if !ok {
		if err := s.auditFailure(ctx, actor, "auth.password_changed", p.UserID.String(), "current_password_invalid"); err != nil {
			return SessionResult{}, err
		}
		return SessionResult{}, ErrCurrentPasswordInvalid
	}
	if err := CheckPasswordPolicy(next); err != nil {
		return SessionResult{}, err
	}
	newHash, err := s.hasher.Hash(ctx, NormalizePassword(next))
	if err != nil {
		return SessionResult{}, err
	}
	now := s.clock.Now().UTC()
	var token string
	err = db.InTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		var createdAt, expiresAt time.Time
		err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, "SELECT created_at, expires_at FROM sessions WHERE id_hash = $1", p.SessionHash).Scan(&createdAt, &expiresAt)
		if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
			return ErrUnauthenticated
		}
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("read session: %w", err)
		}
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "UPDATE users SET password_hash = $2, password_changed_at = $3, updated_at = $3 WHERE id = $1", p.UserID, newHash, now); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("update password: %w", err)
		}
		if err := revokeUserSessions(ctx, tx, p.UserID); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		token, err = s.insertSession(ctx, tx, newSession{userID: p.UserID, kind: "full", createdAt: createdAt.UTC(), expiresAt: expiresAt.UTC()}, m)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		_, err = s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: actor, Action: "auth.password_changed", ObjectType: "user", ObjectID: p.UserID.String(), Result: audit.Success})
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return SessionResult{}, err
	}
	return s.resultFor(ctx, p.UserID, token, nil)
}

// SetLocale stores the caller's preferred UI language.
func (s *Service) SetLocale(ctx context.Context, p Principal, locale string) error {
	if p.Kind != rbac.UserSession {
		return ErrUnauthenticated
	}
	supported := false
	for _, l := range SupportedLocales {
		supported = supported || l == locale
	}
	if !supported {
		var v validate.Errors
		v.Add("locale", validate.OneOf, map[string]string{"values": "en, tr"})
		return v
	}
	if _, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx, "UPDATE users SET locale = $2, updated_at = $3 WHERE id = $1", p.UserID, locale, s.clock.Now().UTC()); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("update locale: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Implement the first-administrator bootstrap**

`LOCK TABLE users IN EXCLUSIVE MODE` makes the "no user exists yet" check race-free (two browsers finishing the wizard at
the same time create exactly one administrator).

`internal/auth/bootstrap.go`:
```go
package auth

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/platform/db"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

// BootstrapAdmin is the first administrator created by the setup wizard.
type BootstrapAdmin struct {
	Username, DisplayName, Email string
	PasswordHash                 string // already hashed with Hasher
	TOTPSecret                   string
	Code                         string
	Locale                       string
}

// BootstrapAdmin creates the first (break-glass) administrator with enrolled TOTP and
// recovery codes and signs them in. inTx runs in the same transaction (setup uses it to
// delete the setup code). The audit event setup.admin_created is the first chain link.
func (s *Service) BootstrapAdmin(ctx context.Context, in BootstrapAdmin, m Meta, inTx func(tx pgx.Tx) error) (SessionResult, error) {
	step, ok := VerifyTOTP(in.TOTPSecret, in.Code, s.clock.Now(), 0)
	if !ok {
		return SessionResult{}, ErrMFAInvalid
	}
	codes := GenerateRecoveryCodes()
	hashes, err := s.hashRecoveryCodes(ctx, codes)
	if err != nil {
		return SessionResult{}, err
	}
	locale := in.Locale
	if locale == "" {
		locale = "en"
	}
	id := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	env, err := s.keyring.Seal(totpLocation(id), []byte(in.TOTPSecret))
	if err != nil {
		return SessionResult{}, err
	}
	now := s.clock.Now().UTC()
	var token string
	err = db.InTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "LOCK TABLE users IN EXCLUSIVE MODE"); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("lock users: %w", err)
		}
		var exists bool
		if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, "SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM users)").Scan(&exists); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("check users: %w", err)
		}
		if exists {
			return ErrAdminExists
		}
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO users (id, username, email, display_name, auth_source, password_hash, role, locale,
is_break_glass, password_changed_at, last_login_at, created_at, updated_at)
VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, 'local', $5, $6, $7, true, $8, $8, $8, $8)`,
			id, in.Username, in.Email, in.DisplayName, in.PasswordHash, string(rbac.Admin), locale, now); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("create administrator: %w", err)
		}
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO user_mfa (user_id, totp_secret_ct, totp_nonce, totp_wrapped_dek, totp_key_version, enrolled_at, last_used_step, created_at)
VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $6)`, id, env.Ciphertext, env.Nonce, env.WrappedDEK, env.KeyVersion, now, step); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("store TOTP secret: %w", err)
		}
		if err := s.replaceRecoveryCodes(ctx, tx, id, hashes); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if _, err := s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: userActor(id, in.Username, m), Action: "setup.admin_created",
			ObjectType: "user", ObjectID: id.String(), Result: audit.Success, Details: map[string]string{"username": in.Username}}); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if inTx != nil {
			if err := inTx(tx); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		var err error
		token, err = s.startFullSession(ctx, tx, id, m)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return SessionResult{}, err
	}
	return s.resultFor(ctx, id, token, codes)
}
```

---

### Task 11: Auth service: user administration, break-glass and API tokens

This task adds user management for administrators (create, edit, change role, disable/enable, reset MFA, reset password,
sign out everywhere), the host-root CLI MFA reset (R18), the break-glass rule that the last enabled local administrator
cannot be disabled or demoted (R10), and personal API tokens (SEC-025, SEC-026). Route permissions (Admin only, step-up)
are enforced by the API layer (Task 13); this layer enforces object-level rules (a non-admin can only see and revoke
their own tokens) and data rules. All commands assume `export PATH="$HOME/.local/go/bin:$HOME/go/bin:$PATH"` and the repository root as working directory. Integration tests need Docker.

Design notes:
- Break-glass race: every change that could remove an administrator first locks **all** enabled local administrator rows
  with `SELECT ... ORDER BY id FOR UPDATE`. Two concurrent demotions of two different admins therefore serialize; the
  second one re-evaluates the locked set, sees one admin left and gets `ErrLastAdmin` (Review Focus #2). Locking the
  target row first would deadlock instead.
- A role change or disabling revokes the user's sessions (A-05); a profile change does not.
- Usernames: ASCII pattern `^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,63}$`, stored as typed, unique and matched case-insensitively via
  `citext` (Review Focus #5). Display names keep Turkish text byte-for-byte.
- API token secret: `"alice_" + base64url(32 random bytes)`; only SHA-256 is stored; the list shows the first 12 characters.
  A token keeps working only while its scope is still allowed by the owner's **current** role and the owner is enabled.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/auth/profile.go` (`ValidateProfile`)
- Create: `internal/auth/users.go` (user administration, break-glass, CLI reset)
- Create: `internal/auth/tokens.go` (API tokens)
- Test: `internal/auth/profile_test.go`, `internal/auth/users_integration_test.go`, `internal/auth/tokens_integration_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything listed for Task 10, plus Task 10's `Service`, `Principal`, `Meta`, `User`, `ErrNotFound`,
  `ErrUnauthenticated`, `revokeUserSessions`, `scanUser`, `getUser`, `userColumns`; Task 2 `db.IsUniqueViolation`,
  `validate.Required|TooLong|Pattern|Email|OneOf|OutOfRange`; Task 7 `rbac.ParseRole`, `rbac.ParseScope`,
  `rbac.Role.AllowsScope`, `rbac.ScopeRead`, `rbac.ScopeOperate`; Task 6 `audit.SystemActor`.
- Produces (package `alice/internal/auth`):
  - `var ErrUsernameTaken, ErrLastAdmin, ErrTokenScopeNotAllowed error`
  - `func ValidateProfile(username, displayName, email string) validate.Errors`
  - `type NewUser struct{ Username, DisplayName, Email string; Role rbac.Role; Password string }`
  - `type UserUpdate struct{ DisplayName, Email *string; Role *rbac.Role; Disabled *bool }`
  - `func (s *Service) ListUsers(ctx) ([]User, error)` (ordered by username)
  - `func (s *Service) GetUser(ctx, id uuid.UUID) (User, error)`
  - `func (s *Service) CreateUser(ctx, p Principal, m Meta, in NewUser) (User, error)`
  - `func (s *Service) UpdateUser(ctx, p Principal, m Meta, id uuid.UUID, in UserUpdate) (User, error)`
  - `func (s *Service) ResetMFA(ctx, p Principal, m Meta, id uuid.UUID) error`
  - `func (s *Service) ResetPassword(ctx, p Principal, m Meta, id uuid.UUID, password string) error`
  - `func (s *Service) RevokeSessions(ctx, p Principal, m Meta, id uuid.UUID) error`
  - `func (s *Service) ResetMFAByUsername(ctx, username string) error` (actor `audit.SystemActor("cli")`)
  - `const TokenPrefix = "alice_"`, `const MaxTokenDays = 365`
  - `type Token struct{ ID, UserID uuid.UUID; Name, Prefix string; Scope rbac.Scope; ExpiresAt time.Time; LastUsedAt *time.Time; CreatedAt time.Time }`
  - `type NewToken struct{ Name string; Scope rbac.Scope; ExpiresInDays int }`
  - `func (s *Service) CreateToken(ctx, p Principal, m Meta, in NewToken) (string, Token, error)` — user sessions only (a token cannot mint tokens)
  - `func (s *Service) ListTokens(ctx, p Principal, userID uuid.UUID) ([]Token, error)` — `uuid.Nil` means the caller; never returns nil for an empty list
  - `func (s *Service) RevokeToken(ctx, p Principal, m Meta, id uuid.UUID) error`
  - `func (s *Service) AuthenticateToken(ctx, bearer string) (Principal, error)` — `bearer` is the raw token without the `Bearer ` prefix
  - Audit actions: `user.created` (details `username`, `role`; failure reason `username_taken`), `user.updated` (details
    `display_name|email: changed`, `role_from`/`role_to`, `disabled`; failure reason `last_admin`), `user.mfa_reset`
    (details `via: ui|cli`), `user.password_reset`, `user.sessions_revoked`, `token.created` (details `token_id`, `scope`),
    `token.revoked` (details `token_id`, `owner_id`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement profile validation**

`internal/auth/profile.go`:
```go
package auth

import (
	"net/mail"
	"regexp"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"unicode/utf8"

	"alice/internal/platform/validate"
)

// usernameRE allows ASCII letters, digits, dot, underscore and dash. Case is kept as typed;
// the citext column makes uniqueness and login case-insensitive. Non-ASCII letters such as
// "İ" are rejected so the Turkish-I casing problem cannot reach identifiers.
var usernameRE = regexp.MustCompile(`^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,63}$`)

const (
	maxDisplayName = 100
	maxEmail       = 254
	maxTokenName   = 100
)

// ValidateProfile checks the user-editable account fields.
func ValidateProfile(username, displayName, email string) validate.Errors {
	var v validate.Errors
	switch {
	case username == "":
		v.Add("username", validate.Required, nil)
	case !usernameRE.MatchString(username):
		v.Add("username", validate.Pattern, nil)
	}
	validateDisplayName(&v, displayName)
	validateEmail(&v, email)
	return v
}

func validateDisplayName(v *validate.Errors, name string) {
	switch {
	case strings.TrimSpace(name) == "":
		v.Add("display_name", validate.Required, nil)
	case utf8.RuneCountInString(name) > maxDisplayName:
		v.Add("display_name", validate.TooLong, map[string]string{"max": strconv.Itoa(maxDisplayName)})
	}
}

func validateEmail(v *validate.Errors, email string) {
	if email == "" {
		return
	}
	if len(email) > maxEmail {
		v.Add("email", validate.TooLong, map[string]string{"max": strconv.Itoa(maxEmail)})
		return
	}
	addr, err := mail.ParseAddress(email)
	if err != nil || addr.Address != email {
		v.Add("email", validate.Email, nil)
	}
}
```

```go
//go:build integration

package auth_test

import (
	"sync"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/platform/validate"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

func ptr[T any](v T) *T { return &v }

// adminPrincipal bootstraps the first admin and returns its principal.
func (e *env) adminPrincipal(t *testing.T) (auth.Principal, string) {
	t.Helper()
	secret, res := e.bootstrap(t)
	return e.principal(t, res.Token), secret
}

func (e *env) createUser(t *testing.T, admin auth.Principal, username string, role rbac.Role) auth.User {
	t.Helper()
	u, err := e.svc.CreateUser(e.ctx, admin, meta, auth.NewUser{Username: username, DisplayName: username, Role: role, Password: adminPassword})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return u
}

func TestCreateUserKeepsTurkishNamesAndTreatsUsernamesCaseInsensitively(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	admin, _ := e.adminPrincipal(t)

	u, err := e.svc.CreateUser(e.ctx, admin, meta, auth.NewUser{Username: "Operator1", DisplayName: "Işıl Çağlar Öztürk",
		Email: "isil@example.com", Role: rbac.Operator, Password: adminPassword})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, "Işıl Çağlar Öztürk", u.DisplayName, "Turkish text is stored byte-for-byte")
	assert.Equal(t, "Operator1", u.Username)
	assert.False(t, u.MFAEnrolled)

	_, err = e.svc.CreateUser(e.ctx, admin, meta, auth.NewUser{Username: "ADMIN", DisplayName: "x", Role: rbac.Auditor, Password: adminPassword})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUsernameTaken)

	var v validate.Errors
	_, err = e.svc.CreateUser(e.ctx, admin, meta, auth.NewUser{Username: "İsmail", DisplayName: "İsmail", Role: rbac.Auditor, Password: adminPassword})
	require.ErrorAs(t, err, &v)
	assert.Equal(t, validate.Pattern, v[0].Code)
	_, err = e.svc.CreateUser(e.ctx, admin, meta, auth.NewUser{Username: "x2", DisplayName: "x", Role: "root", Password: adminPassword})
	require.ErrorAs(t, err, &v)
	assert.Equal(t, "role", v[0].Field)
	_, err = e.svc.CreateUser(e.ctx, admin, meta, auth.NewUser{Username: "x3", DisplayName: "x", Role: rbac.Auditor, Password: "passwordpassword"})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrPasswordBreached)

	e.enroll(t, "OPERATOR1", adminPassword) // login is case-insensitive
	users, err := e.svc.ListUsers(e.ctx)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.Len(t, users, 2)
	assert.Equal(t, "admin", users[0].Username)
	_, err = e.svc.GetUser(e.ctx, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()))
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrNotFound)
	assert.Contains(t, e.actions(t), "user.created:success")
	assert.Contains(t, e.actions(t), "user.created:failure")
}

func TestRoleChangeAndDisableEndSessions(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	admin, _ := e.adminPrincipal(t)
	op := e.createUser(t, admin, "op", rbac.Operator)
	secret, res := e.enroll(t, "op", adminPassword)

	u, err := e.svc.UpdateUser(e.ctx, admin, meta, op.ID, auth.UserUpdate{DisplayName: ptr("Ömer"), Email: ptr("omer@example.com")})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, "Ömer", u.DisplayName)
	e.principal(t, res.Token) // profile changes keep sessions

	u, err = e.svc.UpdateUser(e.ctx, admin, meta, op.ID, auth.UserUpdate{Role: ptr(rbac.Auditor)})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, rbac.Auditor, u.Role)
	_, err = e.svc.Authenticate(e.ctx, res.Token)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUnauthenticated, "a role change ends the user's sessions (A-05)")
	var from, to string
	require.NoError(t, e.db.Owner.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT details->>'role_from', details->>'role_to' FROM audit_log
WHERE action = 'user.updated' ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT 1`).Scan(&from, &to))
	assert.Equal(t, []string{"operator", "auditor"}, []string{from, to})

	res = e.signIn(t, "op", adminPassword, secret)
	_, err = e.svc.UpdateUser(e.ctx, admin, meta, op.ID, auth.UserUpdate{Disabled: ptr(true)})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = e.svc.Authenticate(e.ctx, res.Token)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUnauthenticated, "disabling ends sessions")
	_, err = e.svc.Login(e.ctx, "op", adminPassword, meta)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrInvalidCredentials)
	u, err = e.svc.UpdateUser(e.ctx, admin, meta, op.ID, auth.UserUpdate{Disabled: ptr(false)})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.False(t, u.Disabled)
	_, err = e.svc.Login(e.ctx, "op", adminPassword, meta)
	assert.NoError(t, err)

	_, err = e.svc.UpdateUser(e.ctx, admin, meta, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), auth.UserUpdate{DisplayName: ptr("x")})
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrNotFound)
}

func TestTheOnlyAdminCannotBeDisabledOrDemoted(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	admin, _ := e.adminPrincipal(t)
	_, err := e.svc.UpdateUser(e.ctx, admin, meta, admin.UserID, auth.UserUpdate{Disabled: ptr(true)})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrLastAdmin)
	_, err = e.svc.UpdateUser(e.ctx, admin, meta, admin.UserID, auth.UserUpdate{Role: ptr(rbac.Operator)})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrLastAdmin)
	assert.Contains(t, e.actions(t), "user.updated:failure")
}

func TestConcurrentMutualDemotionKeepsOneAdmin(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	admin, _ := e.adminPrincipal(t)
	second := e.createUser(t, admin, "admin2", rbac.Admin)
	for round := range 5 {
		ids := []uuid.UUID{admin.UserID, second.ID}
		errs := make([]error, 2)
		var wg sync.WaitGroup
		for i, id := range ids {
			wg.Add(1)
			go func() {
				defer wg.Done()
				_, errs[i] = e.svc.UpdateUser(e.ctx, admin, meta, id, auth.UserUpdate{Role: ptr(rbac.Operator)})
			}()
		}
		wg.Wait()
		var ok, last int
		for _, err := range errs {
			switch {
			case err == nil:
				ok++
			case assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrLastAdmin, "round %d", round):
				last++
			}
		}
		require.Equal(t, 1, ok, "round %d", round)
		require.Equal(t, 1, last, "round %d", round)
		var admins int
		require.NoError(t, e.db.Owner.QueryRow(e.ctx, "SELECT count(*) FROM users WHERE role = 'admin' AND disabled_at IS NULL").Scan(&admins))
		require.Equal(t, 1, admins)
		for _, id := range ids { // restore both admins for the next round
			_, err := e.db.Owner.Exec(e.ctx, "UPDATE users SET role = 'admin' WHERE id = $1", id)
			require.NoError(t, err)
		}
	}
}

func TestResetMFAPasswordAndSessions(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	admin, _ := e.adminPrincipal(t)
	op := e.createUser(t, admin, "op", rbac.Operator)
	_, res := e.enroll(t, "op", adminPassword)

	require.NoError(t, e.svc.ResetMFA(e.ctx, admin, meta, op.ID))
	_, err := e.svc.Authenticate(e.ctx, res.Token)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUnauthenticated)
	lr, err := e.svc.Login(e.ctx, "op", adminPassword, meta)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, auth.StatusMFAEnrollmentRequired, lr.Status)

	_, err = e.db.Owner.Exec(e.ctx, "UPDATE users SET failed_logins = 10, locked_until = now() + interval '1 hour' WHERE id = $1", op.ID)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.ErrorIs(t, e.svc.ResetPassword(e.ctx, admin, meta, op.ID, "short"), auth.ErrPasswordTooShort)
	require.NoError(t, e.svc.ResetPassword(e.ctx, admin, meta, op.ID, "a brand new long password"))
	_, err = e.svc.Login(e.ctx, "op", adminPassword, meta)
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrInvalidCredentials)
	_, res = e.enroll(t, "op", "a brand new long password")

	require.NoError(t, e.svc.RevokeSessions(e.ctx, admin, meta, op.ID))
	_, err = e.svc.Authenticate(e.ctx, res.Token)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUnauthenticated)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, e.svc.RevokeSessions(e.ctx, admin, meta, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())), auth.ErrNotFound)

	actions := e.actions(t)
	for _, a := range []string{"user.mfa_reset:success", "user.password_reset:success", "user.sessions_revoked:success"} {
		assert.Contains(t, actions, a)
	}
}

func TestResetMFAByUsernameIsAuditedAsCLI(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	e.adminPrincipal(t)
	require.NoError(t, e.svc.ResetMFAByUsername(e.ctx, "ADMIN"))
	require.ErrorIs(t, e.svc.ResetMFAByUsername(e.ctx, "nobody"), auth.ErrNotFound)
	var actorType, actorName, via string
	require.NoError(t, e.db.Owner.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT actor_type, actor_name, details->>'via' FROM audit_log
WHERE action = 'user.mfa_reset'`).Scan(&actorType, &actorName, &via))
	assert.Equal(t, []string{"system", "cli", "cli"}, []string{actorType, actorName, via})
	lr, err := e.svc.Login(e.ctx, "admin", adminPassword, meta)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, auth.StatusMFAEnrollmentRequired, lr.Status)
}
```

`internal/auth/tokens_integration_test.go`:
```go
//go:build integration

package auth_test

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/platform/validate"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

func TestTokenLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	admin, _ := e.adminPrincipal(t)

	secret, tok, err := e.svc.CreateToken(e.ctx, admin, meta, auth.NewToken{Name: "CI yedekleme", Scope: rbac.ScopeRead, ExpiresInDays: 30})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.True(t, strings.HasPrefix(secret, auth.TokenPrefix))
	assert.Equal(t, secret[:12], tok.Prefix)
	assert.Equal(t, e.clk.Now().Add(30*24*time.Hour), tok.ExpiresAt)

	var stored []byte
	require.NoError(t, e.db.Owner.QueryRow(e.ctx, "SELECT token_hash FROM api_tokens WHERE id = $1", tok.ID).Scan(&stored))
	assert.Len(t, stored, 32, "only the SHA-256 is stored")
	assert.NotContains(t, string(stored), secret)

	p, err := e.svc.AuthenticateToken(e.ctx, secret)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, rbac.APIToken, p.Kind)
	assert.Equal(t, rbac.ScopeRead, p.Scope)
	assert.Equal(t, tok.ID, p.TokenID)
	assert.Equal(t, rbac.Admin, p.Role)

	list, err := e.svc.ListTokens(e.ctx, admin, uuid.Nil)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.Len(t, list, 1)
	require.NotNil(t, list[0].LastUsedAt, "use is recorded")

	require.NoError(t, e.svc.RevokeToken(e.ctx, admin, meta, tok.ID))
	_, err = e.svc.AuthenticateToken(e.ctx, secret)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUnauthenticated)
	list, err = e.svc.ListTokens(e.ctx, admin, uuid.Nil)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Empty(t, list)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, e.svc.RevokeToken(e.ctx, admin, meta, tok.ID), auth.ErrNotFound, "already revoked")

	for _, bad := range []string{"", "alice_", "alice_nope", "Bearer " + secret, strings.TrimPrefix(secret, auth.TokenPrefix)} {
		_, err = e.svc.AuthenticateToken(e.ctx, bad)
		assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUnauthenticated, bad)
	}
	actions := e.actions(t)
	assert.Contains(t, actions, "token.created:success")
	assert.Contains(t, actions, "token.revoked:success")
}

func TestTokenValidation(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	admin, _ := e.adminPrincipal(t)
	fields := func(err error) []string {
		var v validate.Errors
		require.ErrorAs(t, err, &v)
		var out []string
		for _, f := range v {
			out = append(out, f.Field+":"+f.Code)
		}
		return out
	}
	_, _, err := e.svc.CreateToken(e.ctx, admin, meta, auth.NewToken{Name: " ", Scope: "write", ExpiresInDays: 0})
	assert.Equal(t, []string{"name:validation.required", "scope:validation.one_of", "expires_in_days:validation.out_of_range"}, fields(err))
	_, _, err = e.svc.CreateToken(e.ctx, admin, meta, auth.NewToken{Name: "x", Scope: rbac.ScopeRead, ExpiresInDays: auth.MaxTokenDays + 1})
	assert.Equal(t, []string{"expires_in_days:validation.out_of_range"}, fields(err))
}

func TestTokenObjectLevelAccess(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	admin, _ := e.adminPrincipal(t)
	e.createUser(t, admin, "op", rbac.Operator)
	_, res := e.enroll(t, "op", adminPassword)
	op := e.principal(t, res.Token)

	_, adminTok, err := e.svc.CreateToken(e.ctx, admin, meta, auth.NewToken{Name: "a", Scope: rbac.ScopeOperate, ExpiresInDays: 1})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, opTok, err := e.svc.CreateToken(e.ctx, op, meta, auth.NewToken{Name: "b", Scope: rbac.ScopeOperate, ExpiresInDays: 1})
	require.NoError(t, err)

	_, err = e.svc.ListTokens(e.ctx, op, admin.UserID)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrNotFound, "operators cannot see other users' tokens")
	assert.ErrorIs(t, e.svc.RevokeToken(e.ctx, op, meta, adminTok.ID), auth.ErrNotFound)

	list, err := e.svc.ListTokens(e.ctx, admin, op.UserID)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.Len(t, list, 1)
	assert.Equal(t, opTok.ID, list[0].ID)
	require.NoError(t, e.svc.RevokeToken(e.ctx, admin, meta, opTok.ID), "admins may revoke anyone's token")
}

func TestTokenScopeFollowsTheOwnersRole(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	admin, _ := e.adminPrincipal(t)
	e.createUser(t, admin, "aud", rbac.Auditor)
	_, res := e.enroll(t, "aud", adminPassword)
	auditor := e.principal(t, res.Token)
	_, _, err := e.svc.CreateToken(e.ctx, auditor, meta, auth.NewToken{Name: "x", Scope: rbac.ScopeOperate, ExpiresInDays: 1})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrTokenScopeNotAllowed)

	op := e.createUser(t, admin, "op", rbac.Operator)
	_, res = e.enroll(t, "op", adminPassword)
	opP := e.principal(t, res.Token)
	operate, _, err := e.svc.CreateToken(e.ctx, opP, meta, auth.NewToken{Name: "w", Scope: rbac.ScopeOperate, ExpiresInDays: 1})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	read, _, err := e.svc.CreateToken(e.ctx, opP, meta, auth.NewToken{Name: "r", Scope: rbac.ScopeRead, ExpiresInDays: 1})
	require.NoError(t, err)

	_, err = e.svc.UpdateUser(e.ctx, admin, meta, op.ID, auth.UserUpdate{Role: ptr(rbac.Auditor)})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = e.svc.AuthenticateToken(e.ctx, operate)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUnauthenticated, "an operate token stops working when the owner becomes an auditor")
	p, err := e.svc.AuthenticateToken(e.ctx, read)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, rbac.Auditor, p.Role)

	_, err = e.svc.UpdateUser(e.ctx, admin, meta, op.ID, auth.UserUpdate{Disabled: ptr(true)})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = e.svc.AuthenticateToken(e.ctx, read)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUnauthenticated, "disabled owner")
}

func TestExpiredTokenIsRejected(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	admin, _ := e.adminPrincipal(t)
	secret, _, err := e.svc.CreateToken(e.ctx, admin, meta, auth.NewToken{Name: "x", Scope: rbac.ScopeRead, ExpiresInDays: 1})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	e.clk.Advance(24*time.Hour - time.Second)
	_, err = e.svc.AuthenticateToken(e.ctx, secret)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	e.clk.Advance(time.Second)
	_, err = e.svc.AuthenticateToken(e.ctx, secret)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrUnauthenticated)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Implement user administration**

`internal/auth/users.go`:
```go
package auth

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"slices"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/platform/db"
	"alice/internal/platform/validate"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

// Errors of the user administration and token functions.
var (
	ErrUsernameTaken        = errors.New("auth: username already taken")
	ErrLastAdmin            = errors.New("auth: the last enabled local administrator cannot be removed")
	ErrTokenScopeNotAllowed = errors.New("auth: token scope exceeds the owner's role")
)

// NewUser is the input of CreateUser.
type NewUser struct {
	Username, DisplayName, Email string
	Role                         rbac.Role
	Password                     string
}

// UserUpdate changes selected fields; nil fields stay unchanged.
type UserUpdate struct {
	DisplayName *string
	Email       *string
	Role        *rbac.Role
	Disabled    *bool
}

func validateRole(v *validate.Errors, r rbac.Role) {
	if _, ok := rbac.ParseRole(string(r)); !ok {
		v.Add("role", validate.OneOf, map[string]string{"values": "admin, operator, auditor"})
	}
}

// ListUsers returns all users ordered by username.
func (s *Service) ListUsers(ctx context.Context) ([]User, error) {
	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx, "SELECT "+userColumns+" FROM users u ORDER BY u.username")
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list users: %w", err)
	}
	defer rows.Close()
	var out []User
	for rows.Next() {
		u, err := scanUser(rows)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		out = append(out, u)
	}
	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list users: %w", err)
	}
	return out, nil
}

// GetUser returns one user or ErrNotFound.
func (s *Service) GetUser(ctx context.Context, id uuid.UUID) (User, error) {
	return getUser(ctx, s.pool, id)
}

// CreateUser adds a local user. The user enrolls TOTP at first login.
func (s *Service) CreateUser(ctx context.Context, p Principal, m Meta, in NewUser) (User, error) {
	v := ValidateProfile(in.Username, in.DisplayName, in.Email)
	validateRole(&v, in.Role)
	if err := v.Err(); err != nil {
		return User{}, err
	}
	if err := CheckPasswordPolicy(in.Password); err != nil {
		return User{}, err
	}
	hash, err := s.hasher.Hash(ctx, NormalizePassword(in.Password))
	if err != nil {
		return User{}, err
	}
	id := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	now := s.clock.Now().UTC()
	err = db.InTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO users (id, username, email, display_name, auth_source, password_hash, role, password_changed_at, created_at, updated_at)
VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, 'local', $5, $6, $7, $7, $7)`, id, in.Username, in.Email, in.DisplayName, hash, string(in.Role), now); err != nil {
			if db.IsUniqueViolation(err) {
				return ErrUsernameTaken
			}
			return fmt.Errorf("create user: %w", err)
		}
		_, err := s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: p.Actor(m), Action: "user.created", ObjectType: "user", ObjectID: id.String(),
			Result: audit.Success, Details: map[string]string{"username": in.Username, "role": string(in.Role)}})
		return err
	})
	if errors.Is(err, ErrUsernameTaken) {
		if aerr := s.adminFailure(ctx, p, m, "user.created", "", "username_taken"); aerr != nil {
			return User{}, aerr
		}
		return User{}, ErrUsernameTaken
	}
	if err != nil {
		return User{}, err
	}
	return getUser(ctx, s.pool, id)
}

// adminFailure audits a refused administrative change.
func (s *Service) adminFailure(ctx context.Context, p Principal, m Meta, action, objectID, reason string) error {
	return db.InTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		_, err := s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: p.Actor(m), Action: action, ObjectType: "user", ObjectID: objectID,
			Result: audit.Failure, Details: map[string]string{"reason": reason}})
		return err
	})
}

// lockAdmins locks every enabled local administrator row in a fixed order and returns their IDs.
// Every change that could remove an administrator takes these locks first, so concurrent
// changes serialize and the break-glass rule (R10) cannot be raced.
func lockAdmins(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx) ([]uuid.UUID, error) {
	rows, err := tx.Query(ctx, `SELECT id FROM users WHERE role = 'admin' AND disabled_at IS NULL AND auth_source = 'local'
ORDER BY id FOR UPDATE`)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("lock administrators: %w", err)
	}
	defer rows.Close()
	var ids []uuid.UUID
	for rows.Next() {
		var id uuid.UUID
		if err := rows.Scan(&id); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("lock administrators: %w", err)
		}
		ids = append(ids, id)
	}
	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("lock administrators: %w", err)
	}
	return ids, nil
}

// UpdateUser changes profile, role or enabled state. A role change or disabling ends the
// user's sessions. The last enabled local administrator cannot be demoted or disabled.
func (s *Service) UpdateUser(ctx context.Context, p Principal, m Meta, id uuid.UUID, in UserUpdate) (User, error) {
	var v validate.Errors
	if in.DisplayName != nil {
		validateDisplayName(&v, *in.DisplayName)
	}
	if in.Email != nil {
		validateEmail(&v, *in.Email)
	}
	if in.Role != nil {
		validateRole(&v, *in.Role)
	}
	if err := v.Err(); err != nil {
		return User{}, err
	}
	now := s.clock.Now().UTC()
	err := db.InTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		admins, err := lockAdmins(ctx, tx)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		cur, err := scanUser(tx.QueryRow(ctx, "SELECT "+userColumns+" FROM users u WHERE u.id = $1 FOR UPDATE", id))
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		roleChanged := in.Role != nil && *in.Role != cur.Role
		disable := in.Disabled != nil && *in.Disabled && !cur.Disabled
		if (roleChanged || disable) && slices.Contains(admins, id) && len(admins) <= 1 {
			return ErrLastAdmin
		}
		details := map[string]string{}
		if in.DisplayName != nil && *in.DisplayName != cur.DisplayName {
			details["display_name"] = "changed"
			if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "UPDATE users SET display_name = $2 WHERE id = $1", id, *in.DisplayName); err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("update user: %w", err)
			}
		}
		if in.Email != nil && *in.Email != cur.Email {
			details["email"] = "changed"
			if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "UPDATE users SET email = $2 WHERE id = $1", id, *in.Email); err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("update user: %w", err)
			}
		}
		if roleChanged {
			details["role_from"], details["role_to"] = string(cur.Role), string(*in.Role)
			if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "UPDATE users SET role = $2 WHERE id = $1", id, string(*in.Role)); err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("update user: %w", err)
			}
		}
		if in.Disabled != nil && *in.Disabled != cur.Disabled {
			details["disabled"] = fmt.Sprint(*in.Disabled)
			var at any
			if *in.Disabled {
				at = now
			}
			if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "UPDATE users SET disabled_at = $2 WHERE id = $1", id, at); err != nil {
				return fmt.Errorf("update user: %w", err)
			}
		}
		if len(details) == 0 {
			return nil
		}
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "UPDATE users SET updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $1", id, now); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("update user: %w", err)
		}
		if roleChanged || disable {
			if err := revokeUserSessions(ctx, tx, id); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		_, err = s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: p.Actor(m), Action: "user.updated", ObjectType: "user", ObjectID: id.String(),
			Result: audit.Success, Details: details})
		return err
	})
	if errors.Is(err, ErrLastAdmin) {
		if aerr := s.adminFailure(ctx, p, m, "user.updated", id.String(), "last_admin"); aerr != nil {
			return User{}, aerr
		}
		return User{}, ErrLastAdmin
	}
	if err != nil {
		return User{}, err
	}
	return getUser(ctx, s.pool, id)
}

// adminChange runs fn for an existing user inside a transaction and audits action.
func (s *Service) adminChange(ctx context.Context, actor audit.Actor, id uuid.UUID, action string, details map[string]string, fn func(tx pgx.Tx) error) error {
	return db.InTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		var exists bool
		if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, "SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE id = $1)", id).Scan(&exists); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("read user: %w", err)
		}
		if !exists {
			return ErrNotFound
		}
		if err := fn(tx); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		_, err := s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: actor, Action: action, ObjectType: "user", ObjectID: id.String(), Result: audit.Success, Details: details})
		return err
	})
}

func resetMFA(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, id uuid.UUID) error {
	if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "DELETE FROM user_mfa WHERE user_id = $1", id); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("reset MFA: %w", err)
	}
	if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "DELETE FROM recovery_codes WHERE user_id = $1", id); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("reset MFA: %w", err)
	}
	return revokeUserSessions(ctx, tx, id)
}

// ResetMFA removes a user's TOTP and recovery codes; they enroll again at next login.
func (s *Service) ResetMFA(ctx context.Context, p Principal, m Meta, id uuid.UUID) error {
	return s.adminChange(ctx, p.Actor(m), id, "user.mfa_reset", map[string]string{"via": "ui"}, func(tx pgx.Tx) error { return resetMFA(ctx, tx, id) })
}

// ResetMFAByUsername is the host-root CLI break-glass path (R18).
func (s *Service) ResetMFAByUsername(ctx context.Context, username string) error {
	var id uuid.UUID
	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = $1", username).Scan(&id)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return ErrNotFound
	}
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("read user: %w", err)
	}
	return s.adminChange(ctx, audit.SystemActor("cli"), id, "user.mfa_reset", map[string]string{"via": "cli"}, func(tx pgx.Tx) error { return resetMFA(ctx, tx, id) })
}

// ResetPassword sets a new password chosen by an administrator, clears the lockout and ends sessions.
func (s *Service) ResetPassword(ctx context.Context, p Principal, m Meta, id uuid.UUID, password string) error {
	if err := CheckPasswordPolicy(password); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	hash, err := s.hasher.Hash(ctx, NormalizePassword(password))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	now := s.clock.Now().UTC()
	return s.adminChange(ctx, p.Actor(m), id, "user.password_reset", nil, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE users SET password_hash = $2, password_changed_at = $3, updated_at = $3,
failed_logins = 0, locked_until = NULL WHERE id = $1`, id, hash, now); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("reset password: %w", err)
		}
		return revokeUserSessions(ctx, tx, id)
	})
}

// RevokeSessions signs a user out everywhere.
func (s *Service) RevokeSessions(ctx context.Context, p Principal, m Meta, id uuid.UUID) error {
	return s.adminChange(ctx, p.Actor(m), id, "user.sessions_revoked", nil, func(tx pgx.Tx) error { return revokeUserSessions(ctx, tx, id) })
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Implement API tokens**

`internal/auth/tokens.go`:
```go
package auth

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/base64"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"
	"unicode/utf8"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/platform/db"
	"alice/internal/platform/validate"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

// API token format (SEC-026).
const (
	TokenPrefix  = "alice_"
	MaxTokenDays = 365
	prefixLen    = 12 // "alice_" + 6 characters, shown in lists
)

// Token is the public view of a personal API token; the secret is never stored.
type Token struct {
	ID, UserID uuid.UUID
	Name       string
	Prefix     string
	Scope      rbac.Scope
	ExpiresAt  time.Time
	LastUsedAt *time.Time
	CreatedAt  time.Time
}

// NewToken is the input of CreateToken.
type NewToken struct {
	Name          string
	Scope         rbac.Scope
	ExpiresInDays int
}

func tokenHash(secret string) []byte {
	h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(secret))
	return h[:]
}

// CreateToken issues a token for the caller. The secret is returned once.
func (s *Service) CreateToken(ctx context.Context, p Principal, m Meta, in NewToken) (string, Token, error) {
	if p.Kind != rbac.UserSession {
		return "", Token{}, ErrUnauthenticated
	}
	var v validate.Errors
	switch {
	case strings.TrimSpace(in.Name) == "":
		v.Add("name", validate.Required, nil)
	case utf8.RuneCountInString(in.Name) > maxTokenName:
		v.Add("name", validate.TooLong, map[string]string{"max": strconv.Itoa(maxTokenName)})
	}
	if _, ok := rbac.ParseScope(string(in.Scope)); !ok {
		v.Add("scope", validate.OneOf, map[string]string{"values": "read, operate"})
	}
	if in.ExpiresInDays < 1 || in.ExpiresInDays > MaxTokenDays {
		v.Add("expires_in_days", validate.OutOfRange, map[string]string{"min": "1", "max": strconv.Itoa(MaxTokenDays)})
	}
	if err := v.Err(); err != nil {
		return "", Token{}, err
	}
	if !p.Role.AllowsScope(in.Scope) {
		return "", Token{}, ErrTokenScopeNotAllowed
	}
	b := make([]byte, 32)
	_, _ = rand.Read(b)
	secret := TokenPrefix + base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(b)
	now := s.clock.Now().UTC()
	t := Token{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), UserID: p.UserID, Name: in.Name, Prefix: secret[:prefixLen], Scope: in.Scope,
		ExpiresAt: now.Add(time.Duration(in.ExpiresInDays) * 24 * time.Hour), CreatedAt: now}
	err := db.InTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO api_tokens (id, user_id, name, token_hash, token_prefix, scopes, expires_at, created_at)
VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8)`, t.ID, t.UserID, t.Name, tokenHash(secret), t.Prefix, []string{string(t.Scope)}, t.ExpiresAt, now); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("create token: %w", err)
		}
		_, err := s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: p.Actor(m), Action: "token.created", ObjectType: "api_token", ObjectID: t.ID.String(),
			Result: audit.Success, Details: map[string]string{"token_id": t.ID.String(), "scope": string(t.Scope)}})
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return "", Token{}, err
	}
	return secret, t, nil
}

const tokenColumns = "id, user_id, name, token_prefix, scopes[1], expires_at, last_used_at, created_at" //nolint:gosec // G101: column list, not a credential

func scanToken(row pgx.Row) (Token, error) {
	var t Token
	var scope string
	if err := row.Scan(&t.ID, &t.UserID, &t.Name, &t.Prefix, &scope, &t.ExpiresAt, &t.LastUsedAt, &t.CreatedAt); err != nil {
		return Token{}, err
	}
	t.Scope = rbac.Scope(scope)
	t.ExpiresAt, t.CreatedAt = t.ExpiresAt.UTC(), t.CreatedAt.UTC()
	if t.LastUsedAt != nil {
		u := t.LastUsedAt.UTC()
		t.LastUsedAt = &u
	}
	return t, nil
}

// ListTokens returns the non-revoked tokens of userID (uuid.Nil = the caller).
// Only administrators may list another user's tokens; others get ErrNotFound.
func (s *Service) ListTokens(ctx context.Context, p Principal, userID uuid.UUID) ([]Token, error) {
	if userID == uuid.Nil {
		userID = p.UserID
	}
	if userID != p.UserID && p.Role != rbac.Admin {
		return nil, ErrNotFound
	}
	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx, "SELECT "+tokenColumns+" FROM api_tokens WHERE user_id = $1 AND revoked_at IS NULL ORDER BY created_at DESC", userID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list tokens: %w", err)
	}
	defer rows.Close()
	out := []Token{}
	for rows.Next() {
		t, err := scanToken(rows)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("list tokens: %w", err)
		}
		out = append(out, t)
	}
	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list tokens: %w", err)
	}
	return out, nil
}

// RevokeToken revokes a token of the caller, or any token when the caller is an administrator.
func (s *Service) RevokeToken(ctx context.Context, p Principal, m Meta, id uuid.UUID) error {
	return db.InTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		var owner uuid.UUID
		err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, "SELECT user_id FROM api_tokens WHERE id = $1 AND revoked_at IS NULL FOR UPDATE", id).Scan(&owner)
		if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) || (err == nil && owner != p.UserID && p.Role != rbac.Admin) {
			return ErrNotFound
		}
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("read token: %w", err)
		}
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "UPDATE api_tokens SET revoked_at = $2 WHERE id = $1", id, s.clock.Now().UTC()); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("revoke token: %w", err)
		}
		_, err = s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: p.Actor(m), Action: "token.revoked", ObjectType: "api_token", ObjectID: id.String(),
			Result: audit.Success, Details: map[string]string{"token_id": id.String(), "owner_id": owner.String()}})
		return err
	})
}

// AuthenticateToken resolves "Authorization: Bearer alice_..." (SEC-025). The token's scope must
// still be allowed by the owner's current role.
func (s *Service) AuthenticateToken(ctx context.Context, bearer string) (Principal, error) {
	if !strings.HasPrefix(bearer, TokenPrefix) {
		return Principal{}, ErrUnauthenticated
	}
	now := s.clock.Now().UTC()
	var (
		p        Principal
		role     string
		scope    string
		expires  time.Time
		revoked  bool
		disabled bool
	)
	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT t.id, t.scopes[1], t.expires_at, t.revoked_at IS NOT NULL,
u.id, u.username::text, u.display_name, u.role, u.locale, u.disabled_at IS NOT NULL
FROM api_tokens t JOIN users u ON u.id = t.user_id WHERE t.token_hash = $1`, tokenHash(bearer)).Scan(
		&p.TokenID, &scope, &expires, &revoked, &p.UserID, &p.Username, &p.DisplayName, &role, &p.Locale, &disabled)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return Principal{}, ErrUnauthenticated
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Principal{}, fmt.Errorf("read token: %w", err)
	}
	p.Kind, p.Role, p.Scope = rbac.APIToken, rbac.Role(role), rbac.Scope(scope)
	if revoked || disabled || !now.Before(expires) || !p.Role.AllowsScope(p.Scope) {
		return Principal{}, ErrUnauthenticated
	}
	if _, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx, "UPDATE api_tokens SET last_used_at = $2 WHERE id = $1", p.TokenID, now); err != nil {
		return Principal{}, fmt.Errorf("touch token: %w", err)
	}
	return p, nil
}
```
