---

### Task 12: Setup service: setup code, wizard session, admin creation, recovery passphrase

This task implements `internal/setup`, the backend of the first-run wizard (spec §18.5, as scoped for Phase 1): the
one-time setup code (hash in settings `setup.token_hash`, created by `alice setup-token --reset` in Task 16), the wizard
session (60-minute idle, persisted so a browser refresh or server restart does not restart the wizard), the language step,
administrator enrollment (form → TOTP QR → first code → administrator + recovery codes + signed-in session), the recovery
passphrase that wraps the X25519 backup key (R4, SEC-048, SEC-086) and the completion flag. All commands assume `export PATH="$HOME/.local/go/bin:$HOME/go/bin:$PATH"` and the repository root as working directory. Integration tests need Docker.

Design notes:
- Protection before the first administrator exists: five wrong codes from one IP lock that IP for 15 minutes (in-memory
  `ratelimit.Lockout`, per R20); comparison is constant-time on the SHA-256 of the normalized code (dashes, spaces, case and
  O/0, I/L/1 do not matter). Wrong-code attempts are not audited (the admin creation must be the first audit chain link);
  the API layer logs them with the client IP (Task 14).
- Creating the administrator deletes the setup code and every wizard session in the same transaction as the user insert
  (via `auth.BootstrapAdmin`'s `inTx`), so afterwards every setup-session call fails and `StartSession` returns
  `ErrAlreadySetUp` (the API maps it to 404).
- The pending administrator password is hashed immediately; the pending TOTP secret is envelope-encrypted with
  `Location{"setup_sessions", hex(id_hash), "totp_secret"}`.
- The recovery passphrase is never stored: only `secrets.WrappedBackupKey` (public key + private key wrapped by
  Argon2id(passphrase)) is written to settings `backup.key`. Minimum 16 characters after NFKC, maximum 1024, must be typed
  twice, can be set once.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/setup/setup.go` (errors, limits, `Config`, `Service`, `Status`)
- Create: `internal/setup/code.go` (setup code, wizard session, language)
- Create: `internal/setup/admin.go` (`BeginAdminEnrollment`, `CreateAdmin`)
- Create: `internal/setup/passphrase.go` (`SetRecoveryPassphrase`, `Complete`)
- Test: `internal/setup/setup_integration_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 10/11: `auth.Service` (`BootstrapAdmin`, `Authenticate`), `auth.BootstrapAdmin`, `auth.Meta`, `auth.Principal`
    (`Actor`), `auth.SessionResult`, `auth.TOTPEnrollment`, `auth.ValidateProfile`, `auth.SupportedLocales`,
    `auth.ErrAdminExists`, `auth.ErrMFAInvalid`; Task 8 `auth.Hasher`, `auth.NormalizePassword`, `auth.CheckPasswordPolicy`;
    Task 9 `auth.NewTOTP`, `auth.NormalizeRecoveryCode`, `ratelimit.NewLockout(c, max, window, lockFor)` with `Locked`, `Fail`, `Succeed`, `Reset`.
  - Task 2: `settings.Get/Set/Delete`, `db.InTx`, `db.Querier`, `validate.Errors`.
  - Task 4/5: `secrets.Keyring.Seal/Open`, `secrets.Location`, `secrets.Envelope`, `secrets.KDFParams`, `secrets.BackupKDF`,
    `secrets.NewBackupKey(passphrase, params) (WrappedBackupKey, error)`, `secrets.WrappedBackupKey.Unwrap`, `secrets.ErrDecrypt`.
  - Task 6: `audit.Log.Append`, `audit.Event`, `audit.Success`.
  - Task 3: table `setup_sessions`, `settings`; archtest rule `alice/internal/setup/...`; `pgtest`.
- Produces (package `alice/internal/setup`), exactly as follows:
  - `var ErrAlreadySetUp, ErrCodeInvalid, ErrSessionInvalid, ErrEnrollmentMissing, ErrPassphraseTooShort, ErrPassphraseMismatch, ErrPassphraseAlreadySet, ErrIncomplete error`
  - `type LockedError struct{ RetryAfter time.Duration }` with `Error() string`
  - `const MinPassphraseLength = 16, MaxPassphraseLength = 1024, SessionIdle = 60 * time.Minute, MaxCodeFailures = 5, CodeLockDuration = 15 * time.Minute`
  - `type Config struct{ Pool; Auth *auth.Service; Keyring; Audit *audit.Log; Clock; Hasher *auth.Hasher; BackupKDF secrets.KDFParams }` (zero `BackupKDF` = `secrets.BackupKDF`)
  - `func New(c Config) *Service`
  - `type Status struct{ AdminExists, RecoveryPassphraseSet, Completed bool; DefaultLocale string }`
  - `type AdminInput struct{ Username, DisplayName, Email, Password string }`
  - `Status(ctx) (Status, error)`, `ResetToken(ctx) (string, error)`, `StartSession(ctx, code string, m auth.Meta) (string, error)`,
    `Authenticate(ctx, token string) error`, `SetLanguage(ctx, token, language string) error`,
    `BeginAdminEnrollment(ctx, token string, in AdminInput) (auth.TOTPEnrollment, error)`,
    `CreateAdmin(ctx, token, code string, m auth.Meta) (auth.SessionResult, error)` (wrong code → `auth.ErrMFAInvalid`),
    `SetRecoveryPassphrase(ctx, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, passphrase, confirm string) error` (too long → `validate.Errors` field `passphrase` code `validation.too_long`),
    `Complete(ctx, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta) (Status, error)` (idempotent), `ResetLockouts()`.
  - Audit actions: `setup.admin_created` (via `auth.BootstrapAdmin`), `setup.recovery_passphrase_set`, `setup.completed`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing integration tests**

The tests use tiny Argon2 and backup-KDF parameters (`Config.BackupKDF`) so they run in seconds.

`internal/setup/setup_integration_test.go`:
```go
//go:build integration

package setup_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"net/netip"
	"os"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"
	"unicode"

	"github.com/pquerna/otp/totp"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/platform/clock"
	"alice/internal/platform/validate"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
	"alice/internal/secrets"
	"alice/internal/setup"
	"alice/internal/testutil/pgtest"
)

func TestMain(m *testing.M) { os.Exit(pgtest.Main(m)) }

var (
	fastParams = auth.Argon2Params{MemoryKiB: 64, Time: 1, Threads: 1, SaltLen: 16, KeyLen: 32}
	fastKDF    = secrets.KDFParams{MemoryKiB: 64, Time: 1, Threads: 1}
	ip1        = auth.Meta{IP: netip.MustParseAddr("192.0.2.1"), RequestID: "r1"}
	ip2        = auth.Meta{IP: netip.MustParseAddr("192.0.2.2"), RequestID: "r2"}
)

const (
	password   = "correct horse battery staple"
	passphrase = "uzun ve güvenli bir kurtarma parolası"
)

type env struct {
	ctx   context.Context
	clk   *clock.Fake
	db    *pgtest.DB
	auth  *auth.Service
	setup *setup.Service
}

func newEnv(t *testing.T) *env {
	t.Helper()
	d := pgtest.New(t)
	clk := clock.NewFake(time.Date(2026, 10, 1, 9, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC))
	kr, err := secrets.NewKeyring(1, bytes.Repeat([]byte{9}, 32))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	h := auth.NewHasher(fastParams, 4)
	log := audit.New(clk)
	a := auth.NewService(auth.Config{Pool: d.App, Keyring: kr, Audit: log, Clock: clk, Hasher: h, RecoveryHasher: h})
	s := setup.New(setup.Config{Pool: d.App, Auth: a, Keyring: kr, Audit: log, Clock: clk, Hasher: h, BackupKDF: fastKDF})
	return &env{ctx: context.Background(), clk: clk, db: d, auth: a, setup: s}
}

func (e *env) session(t *testing.T) string {
	t.Helper()
	code, err := e.setup.ResetToken(e.ctx)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	token, err := e.setup.StartSession(e.ctx, code, ip1)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return token
}

// createAdmin runs wizard steps 0-2 and returns the new session and TOTP secret.
func (e *env) createAdmin(t *testing.T) (auth.SessionResult, string) {
	t.Helper()
	token := e.session(t)
	require.NoError(t, e.setup.SetLanguage(e.ctx, token, "tr"))
	enr, err := e.setup.BeginAdminEnrollment(e.ctx, token, setup.AdminInput{Username: "admin", DisplayName: "Ayşe Yılmaz", Password: password})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	code, err := totp.GenerateCode(enr.Secret, e.clk.Now())
	require.NoError(t, err)
	res, err := e.setup.CreateAdmin(e.ctx, token, code, ip1)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return res, enr.Secret
}

func (e *env) settingRaw(t *testing.T, key string) string {
	t.Helper()
	var raw string
	err := e.db.Owner.QueryRow(e.ctx, "SELECT value::text FROM settings WHERE key = $1", key).Scan(&raw)
	if err != nil {
		return ""
	}
	return raw
}

func TestResetTokenWorksOnlyBeforeTheAdminExists(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	st, err := e.setup.Status(e.ctx)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, setup.Status{DefaultLocale: "en"}, st)

	code, err := e.setup.ResetToken(e.ctx)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Regexp(t, `^[0-9A-Z]{6}(-[0-9A-Z]{6}){3}$`, code)
	stored := e.settingRaw(t, "setup.token_hash")
	assert.Len(t, strings.Trim(stored, `"`), 64, "only the SHA-256 is stored")
	assert.NotContains(t, stored, strings.ReplaceAll(code, "-", ""))

	e.createAdmin(t)
	_, err = e.setup.ResetToken(e.ctx)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, setup.ErrAlreadySetUp)
}

func TestStartSessionChecksTheCodeAndLocksOneAddress(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	_, err := e.setup.StartSession(e.ctx, "anything", ip1)
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, setup.ErrCodeInvalid, "no code has been created yet")
	e.setup.ResetLockouts()

	code, err := e.setup.ResetToken(e.ctx)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	for range setup.MaxCodeFailures {
		_, err = e.setup.StartSession(e.ctx, "WRONG1-WRONG2-WRONG3-WRONG4", ip1)
		require.ErrorIs(t, err, setup.ErrCodeInvalid)
	}
	_, err = e.setup.StartSession(e.ctx, code, ip1)
	var locked *setup.LockedError
	require.ErrorAs(t, err, &locked, "five wrong codes lock this address, even for the right code")
	assert.Equal(t, setup.CodeLockDuration, locked.RetryAfter)

	lower := strings.Map(unicode.ToLower, strings.ReplaceAll(code, "-", " ")) // ASCII code typed in lower case
	_, err = e.setup.StartSession(e.ctx, lower, ip2)
	require.NoError(t, err, "another address is not locked; case and separators do not matter")

	e.clk.Advance(setup.CodeLockDuration)
	_, err = e.setup.StartSession(e.ctx, code, ip1)
	require.NoError(t, err)

	first, err := e.setup.StartSession(e.ctx, code, ip1)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = e.setup.ResetToken(e.ctx)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, e.setup.Authenticate(e.ctx, first), setup.ErrSessionInvalid, "a new code ends old wizard sessions")
	_, err = e.setup.StartSession(e.ctx, code, ip2)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, setup.ErrCodeInvalid, "the old code no longer works")
}

func TestSetupSessionIdleTimeout(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	token := e.session(t)
	e.clk.Advance(59 * time.Minute)
	require.NoError(t, e.setup.Authenticate(e.ctx, token))
	e.clk.Advance(59 * time.Minute)
	require.NoError(t, e.setup.Authenticate(e.ctx, token), "activity restarts the 60 minute timer")
	e.clk.Advance(61 * time.Minute)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, e.setup.Authenticate(e.ctx, token), setup.ErrSessionInvalid)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, e.setup.Authenticate(e.ctx, ""), setup.ErrSessionInvalid)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, e.setup.Authenticate(e.ctx, "garbage"), setup.ErrSessionInvalid)
}

func TestSetLanguage(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	token := e.session(t)
	var v validate.Errors
	require.ErrorAs(t, e.setup.SetLanguage(e.ctx, token, "de"), &v)
	assert.Equal(t, "language", v[0].Field)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, e.setup.SetLanguage(e.ctx, "bad", "tr"), setup.ErrSessionInvalid)
	require.NoError(t, e.setup.SetLanguage(e.ctx, token, "tr"))
	st, err := e.setup.Status(e.ctx)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, "tr", st.DefaultLocale)
}

func TestBeginAdminEnrollmentValidatesAndEncrypts(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	token := e.session(t)
	var v validate.Errors
	_, err := e.setup.BeginAdminEnrollment(e.ctx, token, setup.AdminInput{Username: "İsmail", DisplayName: "İsmail", Password: password})
	require.ErrorAs(t, err, &v)
	assert.Equal(t, validate.Pattern, v[0].Code)
	_, err = e.setup.BeginAdminEnrollment(e.ctx, token, setup.AdminInput{Username: "admin", DisplayName: "A", Password: "kısa"})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrPasswordTooShort)
	_, err = e.setup.BeginAdminEnrollment(e.ctx, token, setup.AdminInput{Username: "admin", DisplayName: "A", Password: "qwerty123456"})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrPasswordBreached)

	enr, err := e.setup.BeginAdminEnrollment(e.ctx, token, setup.AdminInput{Username: "admin", DisplayName: "A", Password: password})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.NotEmpty(t, enr.Secret)
	assert.NotEmpty(t, enr.QRPNG)
	var hash string
	var ct []byte
	require.NoError(t, e.db.Owner.QueryRow(e.ctx, "SELECT pending_password_hash, totp_secret_ct FROM setup_sessions").Scan(&hash, &ct))
	assert.True(t, strings.HasPrefix(hash, "$argon2id$"))
	assert.NotContains(t, string(ct), enr.Secret, "the pending TOTP secret is encrypted")
}

func TestCreateAdminEndsTheWizard(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	token := e.session(t)
	_, err := e.setup.CreateAdmin(e.ctx, token, "123456", ip1)
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, setup.ErrEnrollmentMissing)

	require.NoError(t, e.setup.SetLanguage(e.ctx, token, "tr"))
	enr, err := e.setup.BeginAdminEnrollment(e.ctx, token, setup.AdminInput{Username: "admin", DisplayName: "Ayşe Yılmaz", Password: password})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	wrong, err := totp.GenerateCode(enr.Secret, e.clk.Now().Add(time.Hour))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = e.setup.CreateAdmin(e.ctx, token, wrong, ip1)
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, auth.ErrMFAInvalid)

	right, err := totp.GenerateCode(enr.Secret, e.clk.Now())
	require.NoError(t, err)
	res, err := e.setup.CreateAdmin(e.ctx, token, right, ip1)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Len(t, res.RecoveryCodes, 10)
	assert.Equal(t, rbac.Admin, res.Info.User.Role)
	assert.Equal(t, "tr", res.Info.User.Locale, "the wizard language becomes the admin's language")
	assert.Equal(t, "Ayşe Yılmaz", res.Info.User.DisplayName)

	var seq int64
	var action string
	require.NoError(t, e.db.Owner.QueryRow(e.ctx, "SELECT seq, action FROM audit_log ORDER BY seq LIMIT 1").Scan(&seq, &action))
	assert.Equal(t, int64(1), seq)
	assert.Equal(t, "setup.admin_created", action, "the admin creation is the first link of the audit chain")
	assert.Empty(t, e.settingRaw(t, "setup.token_hash"), "the setup code is deleted")
	var sessions int
	require.NoError(t, e.db.Owner.QueryRow(e.ctx, "SELECT count(*) FROM setup_sessions").Scan(&sessions))
	assert.Zero(t, sessions)

	assert.ErrorIs(t, e.setup.Authenticate(e.ctx, token), setup.ErrSessionInvalid)
	_, err = e.setup.StartSession(e.ctx, "whatever", ip2)
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, setup.ErrAlreadySetUp)
	st, err := e.setup.Status(e.ctx)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.True(t, st.AdminExists)

	p, err := e.auth.Authenticate(e.ctx, res.Token)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, rbac.UserSession, p.Kind)
}

func TestRecoveryPassphraseAndCompletion(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	res, _ := e.createAdmin(t)
	p, err := e.auth.Authenticate(e.ctx, res.Token)
	require.NoError(t, err)

	require.ErrorIs(t, e.setup.SetRecoveryPassphrase(e.ctx, p, ip1, "too short 15 ch", "too short 15 ch"), setup.ErrPassphraseTooShort)
	require.ErrorIs(t, e.setup.SetRecoveryPassphrase(e.ctx, p, ip1, passphrase, passphrase+"x"), setup.ErrPassphraseMismatch)
	var v validate.Errors
	long := strings.Repeat("ş", setup.MaxPassphraseLength+1)
	require.ErrorAs(t, e.setup.SetRecoveryPassphrase(e.ctx, p, ip1, long, long), &v)
	_, err = e.setup.Complete(e.ctx, p, ip1)
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, setup.ErrIncomplete)

	require.NoError(t, e.setup.SetRecoveryPassphrase(e.ctx, p, ip1, passphrase, passphrase))
	raw := e.settingRaw(t, "backup.key")
	assert.NotContains(t, raw, "kurtarma", "the passphrase itself is never stored")
	var w secrets.WrappedBackupKey
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal([]byte(raw), &w))
	priv, err := w.Unwrap([]byte(passphrase))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, w.PublicKey, priv.PublicKey().Bytes())
	_, err = w.Unwrap([]byte("wrong passphrase, long enough"))
	assert.ErrorIs(t, err, secrets.ErrDecrypt)
	require.ErrorIs(t, e.setup.SetRecoveryPassphrase(e.ctx, p, ip1, passphrase, passphrase), setup.ErrPassphraseAlreadySet)

	st, err := e.setup.Complete(e.ctx, p, ip1)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, setup.Status{AdminExists: true, RecoveryPassphraseSet: true, Completed: true, DefaultLocale: "tr"}, st)
	_, err = e.setup.Complete(e.ctx, p, ip1)
	require.NoError(t, err, "completing twice is harmless")

	rows, err := e.db.Owner.Query(e.ctx, "SELECT action FROM audit_log WHERE action LIKE 'setup.%' ORDER BY seq")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	defer rows.Close()
	var actions []string
	for rows.Next() {
		var a string
		require.NoError(t, rows.Scan(&a))
		actions = append(actions, a)
	}
	assert.Equal(t, []string{"setup.admin_created", "setup.recovery_passphrase_set", "setup.completed"}, actions)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the service, status and errors**

`internal/setup/setup.go`:
```go
// Package setup implements the first-run wizard: the one-time setup code, the wizard
// session, creation of the first administrator and the recovery passphrase (spec §18.5).
package setup

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/auth/ratelimit"
	"alice/internal/platform/clock"
	"alice/internal/platform/db"
	"alice/internal/platform/settings"
	"alice/internal/secrets"
)

// Errors returned by the setup service.
var (
	ErrAlreadySetUp         = errors.New("setup: an administrator already exists")
	ErrCodeInvalid          = errors.New("setup: setup code invalid")
	ErrSessionInvalid       = errors.New("setup: setup session missing or expired")
	ErrEnrollmentMissing    = errors.New("setup: start the administrator enrollment first")
	ErrPassphraseTooShort   = errors.New("setup: recovery passphrase too short")
	ErrPassphraseMismatch   = errors.New("setup: recovery passphrases differ")
	ErrPassphraseAlreadySet = errors.New("setup: recovery passphrase already set")
	ErrIncomplete           = errors.New("setup: recovery passphrase not set yet")
)

// LockedError means too many wrong setup codes came from this address.
type LockedError struct{ RetryAfter time.Duration }

func (e *LockedError) Error() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("setup: too many wrong setup codes; try again in %s", e.RetryAfter.Round(time.Second))
}

// Limits (spec §18.5, SEC-086).
const (
	MinPassphraseLength = 16
	MaxPassphraseLength = 1024
	SessionIdle         = 60 * time.Minute
	MaxCodeFailures     = 5
	CodeLockDuration    = 15 * time.Minute
)

// Settings keys owned by setup.
const (
	keyTokenHash   = "setup.token_hash"
	keyLocale      = "locale.default"
	keyBackupKey   = "backup.key"
	keyCompletedAt = "setup.completed_at"
)

// Config holds the service dependencies.
type Config struct {
	Pool      *pgxpool.Pool
	Auth      *auth.Service
	Keyring   *secrets.Keyring
	Audit     *audit.Log
	Clock     clock.Clock
	Hasher    *auth.Hasher
	BackupKDF secrets.KDFParams // zero value means secrets.BackupKDF
}

// Service is the setup wizard backend.
type Service struct {
	pool    *pgxpool.Pool
	auth    *auth.Service
	keyring *secrets.Keyring
	audit   *audit.Log
	clock   clock.Clock
	hasher  *auth.Hasher
	kdf     secrets.KDFParams
	lockout *ratelimit.Lockout
}

// New builds the service.
func New(c Config) *Service {
	kdf := c.BackupKDF
	if kdf == (secrets.KDFParams{}) {
		kdf = secrets.BackupKDF
	}
	return &Service{
		pool: c.Pool, auth: c.Auth, keyring: c.Keyring, audit: c.Audit, clock: c.Clock, hasher: c.Hasher, kdf: kdf,
		lockout: ratelimit.NewLockout(c.Clock, MaxCodeFailures, CodeLockDuration, CodeLockDuration),
	}
}

// Status is what the wizard and the "finish setting up" banner need.
type Status struct {
	AdminExists           bool
	RecoveryPassphraseSet bool
	Completed             bool
	DefaultLocale         string
}

func adminExists(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier) (bool, error) {
	var exists bool
	if err := q.QueryRow(ctx, "SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM users)").Scan(&exists); err != nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf("check users: %w", err)
	}
	return exists, nil
}

func hasSetting(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier, key string) (bool, error) {
	var raw any
	return settings.Get(ctx, q, key, &raw)
}

// Status reports the setup progress.
func (s *Service) Status(ctx context.Context) (Status, error) {
	var st Status
	var err error
	if st.AdminExists, err = adminExists(ctx, s.pool); err != nil {
		return Status{}, err
	}
	if st.RecoveryPassphraseSet, err = hasSetting(ctx, s.pool, keyBackupKey); err != nil {
		return Status{}, err
	}
	if st.Completed, err = hasSetting(ctx, s.pool, keyCompletedAt); err != nil {
		return Status{}, err
	}
	st.DefaultLocale = "en"
	var locale string
	found, err := settings.Get(ctx, s.pool, keyLocale, &locale)
	if err != nil {
		return Status{}, err
	}
	if found && locale != "" {
		st.DefaultLocale = locale
	}
	return st, nil
}

// ResetLockouts clears the wrong-code counters (used by the e2e reset endpoint only).
func (s *Service) ResetLockouts() { s.lockout.Reset() }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the setup code, wizard session and language step**

`internal/setup/code.go`:
```go
package setup

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"crypto/subtle"
	"encoding/base32"
	"encoding/hex"
	"fmt"
	"slices"
	"strings"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/platform/db"
	"alice/internal/platform/settings"
	"alice/internal/platform/validate"
)

var crockford = base32.NewEncoding("0123456789ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTVWXYZ").WithPadding(base32.NoPadding)

// newCode returns 24 Crockford base32 characters (120 bits) as "XXXXXX-XXXXXX-XXXXXX-XXXXXX".
func newCode() string {
	b := make([]byte, 15)
	_, _ = rand.Read(b)
	s := crockford.EncodeToString(b)
	return strings.Join([]string{s[0:6], s[6:12], s[12:18], s[18:24]}, "-")
}

// hashCode normalizes like recovery codes (dashes, spaces, case, O/0, I/L/1 do not matter).
func hashCode(code string) string {
	h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(auth.NormalizeRecoveryCode(code)))
	return hex.EncodeToString(h[:])
}

// hashSessionToken is the setup_sessions primary key.
func hashSessionToken(token string) []byte {
	h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(token))
	return h[:]
}

func newSessionToken() string {
	b := make([]byte, 32)
	_, _ = rand.Read(b)
	return strings.TrimRight(base32.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(b), "=")
}

// ResetToken creates a new setup code (host-root CLI) and invalidates wizard sessions.
// It only works while no administrator exists.
func (s *Service) ResetToken(ctx context.Context) (string, error) {
	code := newCode()
	err := db.InTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		exists, err := adminExists(ctx, tx)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if exists {
			return ErrAlreadySetUp
		}
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "DELETE FROM setup_sessions"); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("clear setup sessions: %w", err)
		}
		return settings.Set(ctx, tx, keyTokenHash, hashCode(code), s.clock.Now().UTC())
	})
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return code, nil
}

func lockKey(m auth.Meta) string {
	if !m.IP.IsValid() {
		return "unknown"
	}
	return m.IP.Unmap().String()
}

// StartSession checks the setup code and opens a wizard session. Five wrong codes from
// one address lock that address for 15 minutes.
func (s *Service) StartSession(ctx context.Context, code string, m auth.Meta) (string, error) {
	exists, err := adminExists(ctx, s.pool)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	if exists {
		return "", ErrAlreadySetUp
	}
	key := lockKey(m)
	if locked, wait := s.lockout.Locked(key); locked {
		return "", &LockedError{RetryAfter: wait}
	}
	var stored string
	found, err := settings.Get(ctx, s.pool, keyTokenHash, &stored)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	if !found || subtle.ConstantTimeCompare([]byte(stored), []byte(hashCode(code))) != 1 {
		s.lockout.Fail(key)
		return "", ErrCodeInvalid
	}
	s.lockout.Succeed(key)
	token := newSessionToken()
	now := s.clock.Now().UTC()
	if _, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx, "INSERT INTO setup_sessions (id_hash, created_at, idle_expires_at) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)",
		hashSessionToken(token), now, now.Add(SessionIdle)); err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("create setup session: %w", err)
	}
	return token, nil
}

// Authenticate validates a wizard session and slides its 60-minute idle timeout.
func (s *Service) Authenticate(ctx context.Context, token string) error {
	if token == "" {
		return ErrSessionInvalid
	}
	now := s.clock.Now().UTC()
	tag, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE setup_sessions SET idle_expires_at = $2
WHERE id_hash = $1 AND idle_expires_at > $3 AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM users)`, hashSessionToken(token), now.Add(SessionIdle), now)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("check setup session: %w", err)
	}
	if tag.RowsAffected() != 1 {
		return ErrSessionInvalid
	}
	return nil
}

// SetLanguage stores the default UI language chosen in the wizard.
func (s *Service) SetLanguage(ctx context.Context, token, language string) error {
	if err := s.Authenticate(ctx, token); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if !slices.Contains(auth.SupportedLocales, language) {
		var v validate.Errors
		v.Add("language", validate.OneOf, map[string]string{"values": "en, tr"})
		return v
	}
	return settings.Set(ctx, s.pool, keyLocale, language, s.clock.Now().UTC())
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement administrator enrollment and creation**

`internal/setup/admin.go`:
```go
package setup

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/hex"
	"errors"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/platform/settings"
	"alice/internal/secrets"
)

// AdminInput is the administrator form of wizard step 2.
type AdminInput struct{ Username, DisplayName, Email, Password string }

func pendingLocation(idHash []byte) secrets.Location {
	return secrets.Location{Table: "setup_sessions", RowID: hex.EncodeToString(idHash), Column: "totp_secret"}
}

// BeginAdminEnrollment validates the administrator form, hashes the password and creates a
// TOTP secret that is kept (encrypted) with the wizard session until CreateAdmin.
func (s *Service) BeginAdminEnrollment(ctx context.Context, token string, in AdminInput) (auth.TOTPEnrollment, error) {
	if err := s.Authenticate(ctx, token); err != nil {
		return auth.TOTPEnrollment{}, err
	}
	if err := auth.ValidateProfile(in.Username, in.DisplayName, in.Email).Err(); err != nil {
		return auth.TOTPEnrollment{}, err
	}
	if err := auth.CheckPasswordPolicy(in.Password); err != nil {
		return auth.TOTPEnrollment{}, err
	}
	hash, err := s.hasher.Hash(ctx, auth.NormalizePassword(in.Password))
	if err != nil {
		return auth.TOTPEnrollment{}, err
	}
	enr, err := auth.NewTOTP(in.Username)
	if err != nil {
		return auth.TOTPEnrollment{}, err
	}
	idHash := hashSessionToken(token)
	env, err := s.keyring.Seal(pendingLocation(idHash), []byte(enr.Secret))
	if err != nil {
		return auth.TOTPEnrollment{}, err
	}
	tag, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE setup_sessions SET pending_username = $2, pending_display_name = $3, pending_email = $4,
pending_password_hash = $5, totp_secret_ct = $6, totp_nonce = $7, totp_wrapped_dek = $8, totp_key_version = $9 WHERE id_hash = $1`,
		idHash, in.Username, in.DisplayName, in.Email, hash, env.Ciphertext, env.Nonce, env.WrappedDEK, env.KeyVersion)
	if err != nil {
		return auth.TOTPEnrollment{}, fmt.Errorf("store enrollment: %w", err)
	}
	if tag.RowsAffected() != 1 {
		return auth.TOTPEnrollment{}, ErrSessionInvalid
	}
	return enr, nil
}

// CreateAdmin verifies the first TOTP code and creates the administrator. In the same
// transaction the setup code and all wizard sessions are deleted, so the wizard cannot be
// used again (spec §18.5 rule 4). The new administrator is signed in.
func (s *Service) CreateAdmin(ctx context.Context, token, code string, m auth.Meta) (auth.SessionResult, error) {
	if err := s.Authenticate(ctx, token); err != nil {
		return auth.SessionResult{}, err
	}
	idHash := hashSessionToken(token)
	var (
		username, displayName, email, hash *string
		env                                secrets.Envelope
		version                            *int
	)
	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT pending_username::text, pending_display_name, pending_email::text, pending_password_hash,
totp_secret_ct, totp_nonce, totp_wrapped_dek, totp_key_version FROM setup_sessions WHERE id_hash = $1`, idHash).Scan(
		&username, &displayName, &email, &hash, &env.Ciphertext, &env.Nonce, &env.WrappedDEK, &version)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return auth.SessionResult{}, ErrSessionInvalid
	}
	if err != nil {
		return auth.SessionResult{}, fmt.Errorf("read enrollment: %w", err)
	}
	if username == nil || hash == nil || version == nil {
		return auth.SessionResult{}, ErrEnrollmentMissing
	}
	env.KeyVersion = *version
	secret, err := s.keyring.Open(pendingLocation(idHash), env)
	if err != nil {
		return auth.SessionResult{}, err
	}
	defer secret.Zero()
	locale := "en"
	if _, err := settings.Get(ctx, s.pool, keyLocale, &locale); err != nil {
		return auth.SessionResult{}, err
	}
	in := auth.BootstrapAdmin{Username: *username, DisplayName: deref(displayName), Email: deref(email),
		PasswordHash: *hash, TOTPSecret: string(secret.Bytes()), Code: code, Locale: locale}
	res, err := s.auth.BootstrapAdmin(ctx, in, m, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		if err := settings.Delete(ctx, tx, keyTokenHash); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "DELETE FROM setup_sessions"); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("delete setup sessions: %w", err)
		}
		return nil
	})
	if errors.Is(err, auth.ErrAdminExists) {
		return auth.SessionResult{}, ErrAlreadySetUp
	}
	return res, err
}

func deref(s *string) string {
	if s == nil {
		return ""
	}
	return *s
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the recovery passphrase and completion**

`internal/setup/passphrase.go`:
```go
package setup

import (
	"context"
	"strconv"
	"unicode/utf8"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
	"golang.org/x/text/unicode/norm"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/platform/db"
	"alice/internal/platform/settings"
	"alice/internal/platform/validate"
	"alice/internal/secrets"
)

// passphraseLock serializes concurrent attempts to set the recovery passphrase.
const passphraseLock int64 = 7160927315633811301

// SetRecoveryPassphrase generates the X25519 backup key pair and stores the private key
// wrapped by the passphrase (R4, SEC-048). Neither the passphrase nor a key derived from it
// is stored. The passphrase can be set only once.
func (s *Service) SetRecoveryPassphrase(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, passphrase, confirm string) error {
	pass := norm.NFKC.String(passphrase)
	n := utf8.RuneCountInString(pass)
	switch {
	case n < MinPassphraseLength:
		return ErrPassphraseTooShort
	case n > MaxPassphraseLength:
		var v validate.Errors
		v.Add("passphrase", validate.TooLong, map[string]string{"max": strconv.Itoa(MaxPassphraseLength)})
		return v
	case pass != norm.NFKC.String(confirm):
		return ErrPassphraseMismatch
	}
	if set, err := hasSetting(ctx, s.pool, keyBackupKey); err != nil {
		return err
	} else if set {
		return ErrPassphraseAlreadySet
	}
	wrapped, err := secrets.NewBackupKey([]byte(pass), s.kdf)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return db.InTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock($1)", passphraseLock); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		set, err := hasSetting(ctx, tx, keyBackupKey)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if set {
			return ErrPassphraseAlreadySet
		}
		if err := settings.Set(ctx, tx, keyBackupKey, wrapped, s.clock.Now().UTC()); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		_, err = s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: p.Actor(m), Action: "setup.recovery_passphrase_set", ObjectType: "setting",
			ObjectID: keyBackupKey, Result: audit.Success})
		return err
	})
}

// Complete finishes the wizard. It requires the recovery passphrase and is idempotent.
func (s *Service) Complete(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta) (Status, error) {
	err := db.InTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, "SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock($1)", passphraseLock); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		set, err := hasSetting(ctx, tx, keyBackupKey)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if !set {
			return ErrIncomplete
		}
		done, err := hasSetting(ctx, tx, keyCompletedAt)
		if err != nil || done {
			return err
		}
		now := s.clock.Now().UTC()
		if err := settings.Set(ctx, tx, keyCompletedAt, now.Format("2006-01-02T15:04:05Z07:00"), now); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		_, err = s.audit.Append(ctx, tx, audit.Event{Actor: p.Actor(m), Action: "setup.completed", ObjectType: "setting",
			ObjectID: keyCompletedAt, Result: audit.Success})
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return Status{}, err
	}
	return s.Status(ctx)
}
```

**Interfaces:**

The archtest rule letting `internal/api` import `internal/setup` was added in Task 3.
- Produces (used by Tasks 14, 15, 16, 17, 18):
  - `type Deps struct { Health HealthChecker; Catalog *i18n.Catalog; Version version.Info; UI fs.FS; Log *slog.Logger; Clock clock.Clock; Auth AuthService; Setup SetupService; Audit AuditService; TrustedProxies []netip.Prefix; TestHandler http.Handler }` and the interfaces `AuthService`, `SetupService`, `AuditService` exactly as listed in the Interfaces code below (every method of `auth.Service`, `setup.Service`, `audit.Service` the HTTP layer uses).
  - `func New(d Deps) *Server`, `func (s *Server) Handler() http.Handler`, `func (s *Server) ResetLimits()`, `func NewHandler(d Deps) http.Handler`, `func NewHealthHandler(d Deps) http.Handler`. `New` panics if a route declares an unknown permission.
  - Route table: `type route struct { method, pattern string; perm rbac.Permission; limit limitClass; handler http.HandlerFunc }`, `limitDefault | limitNone | limitLogin | limitMFA`; `func (s *server) apiRoutes() []route` (Tasks 14 and 15 append their route groups here), `func (s *server) handler(routes []route) http.Handler`, `func newServer(d Deps) *server`.
  - Helpers: `(s *server) decode(w, r, dst any) bool`, `(s *server) fail(w, r, err error)`, `(s *server) writeError(w, r, ec ErrorCode)`, `(s *server) writeErrorWith(w, r, ec ErrorCode, params map[string]string, details []validate.FieldError)`, `principalFrom(ctx) auth.Principal`, `sessionTokenFrom(r) string`, `setupTokenFrom(r) string`, `(s *server) meta(r) auth.Meta`, `setSessionCookie`, `clearSessionCookie`, `setSetupCookie`, `clearSetupCookie`, `pathUUID(r, name) (uuid.UUID, bool)`, `clientIP(r, trusted) netip.Addr`, `writeJSON(w, status, v)`; constants `sessionCookie = "__Host-alice_session"`, `setupCookie = "__Host-alice_setup"`, `maxBody = 1 << 20`.
  - The 29 `Err*` codes of the error registry in this task and `AllErrorCodes()`.
  - Test fixtures (package `api`, `_test.go`): `fakeAuth`, `fakeSetup`, `fakeAudit` (function field `<Method>Fn` per method; `fakeAudit.Events()`, `fakeAudit.Count(action)`), `identityAuth(now)`, IDs `adminID`, `operatorID`, `auditorID`, `testNow`, `testDeps(t, health)`, `as(req, identity)`, `call(h, method, target, identity, body)`, `decodeErr`, `pathParamRE`. Identities: `""` (anonymous), `"expired"`, `"disabled"`, `"pending"`, `"setup"`, `"admin"`, `"operator"`, `"auditor"`, `"admin-stepup"`, `"operator-stepup"`, `"auditor-stepup"`, bearer `"alice_read_admin"`, `"alice_operate_operator"`, `"alice_read_auditor"`.
  - `make authz-doc` regenerates `docs/authz-matrix.md`; `TestAuthzMatrixDocIsCurrent` fails when it is stale (Tasks 14 and 15 re-run it).

Design notes for the implementer:
- `guard` runs, per route: authentication (only for non-public permissions: `Authorization: Bearer ...` → `AuthenticateToken`; else cookie `__Host-alice_session` → `Authenticate`; else cookie `__Host-alice_setup` → `Setup.Authenticate`; any failure leaves the caller anonymous) → rate limit (route class, then 300/min per session/token or 60/min per IP for anonymous callers; probes are never limited) → same-origin check on non-GET/HEAD without a bearer token → CSRF check (`X-CSRF-Token`) on non-GET/HEAD for cookie callers of non-public routes → `rbac.Decide`.
- Public routes (login, setup code, setup status, locales, probes) never look at cookies, so a stale or pending cookie can never block sign-in and public traffic costs no database lookup.
- Only `register` puts routes on a mux; `TestNoRouteBypassesTable` enforces it with `go/parser`.
- `TestAuthzMatrix` uses a hand-written expectation table (spec §15.4, §25.1). It must not be rewritten from `rbac.Decide`; when a policy changes on purpose, both tables change in the same review.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test fixtures (fakes and identities)**

`internal/api/fakes_test.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
	"alice/internal/setup"
)

// errNotConfigured is returned by fake methods whose function field is nil.
var errNotConfigured = errors.New("fake: method not configured")

// fakeAuth implements AuthService. Each method calls its function field when
// set; otherwise it returns zero values and errNotConfigured (Authenticate and
// AuthenticateToken return auth.ErrUnauthenticated; CSRFToken and CheckCSRF
// use the "csrf-<token>" convention).
type fakeAuth struct {
	AuthenticateFn            func(ctx context.Context, sessionToken string) (auth.Principal, error)
	AuthenticateTokenFn       func(ctx context.Context, bearer string) (auth.Principal, error)
	LoginFn                   func(ctx context.Context, username, password string, m auth.Meta) (auth.LoginResult, error)
	EnrollMFAFn               func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta) (auth.TOTPEnrollment, error)
	VerifyMFAFn               func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, in auth.MFAInput, m auth.Meta) (auth.SessionResult, error)
	SessionInfoFn             func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, sessionToken string) (auth.SessionInfo, error)
	StepUpFn                  func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, code string, m auth.Meta) (auth.SessionResult, error)
	LogoutFn                  func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta) error
	ChangePasswordFn          func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, current, next string, m auth.Meta) (auth.SessionResult, error)
	RegenerateRecoveryCodesFn func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta) ([]string, error)
	SetLocaleFn               func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, locale string) error
	ListUsersFn               func(ctx context.Context) ([]auth.User, error)
	GetUserFn                 func(ctx context.Context, id uuid.UUID) (auth.User, error)
	CreateUserFn              func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, in auth.NewUser) (auth.User, error)
	UpdateUserFn              func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, id uuid.UUID, in auth.UserUpdate) (auth.User, error)
	ResetMFAFn                func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, id uuid.UUID) error
	ResetPasswordFn           func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, id uuid.UUID, password string) error
	RevokeSessionsFn          func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, id uuid.UUID) error
	CreateTokenFn             func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, in auth.NewToken) (string, auth.Token, error)
	ListTokensFn              func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, userID uuid.UUID) ([]auth.Token, error)
	RevokeTokenFn             func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, id uuid.UUID) error
}

func (f *fakeAuth) Authenticate(ctx context.Context, t string) (auth.Principal, error) {
	if f.AuthenticateFn != nil {
		return f.AuthenticateFn(ctx, t)
	}
	return auth.Principal{}, auth.ErrUnauthenticated
}

func (f *fakeAuth) AuthenticateToken(ctx context.Context, b string) (auth.Principal, error) {
	if f.AuthenticateTokenFn != nil {
		return f.AuthenticateTokenFn(ctx, b)
	}
	return auth.Principal{}, auth.ErrUnauthenticated
}

func (f *fakeAuth) CSRFToken(t string) string       { return "csrf-" + t }
func (f *fakeAuth) CheckCSRF(t, header string) bool { return header == "csrf-"+t }

func (f *fakeAuth) Login(ctx context.Context, u, pw string, m auth.Meta) (auth.LoginResult, error) {
	if f.LoginFn != nil {
		return f.LoginFn(ctx, u, pw, m)
	}
	return auth.LoginResult{}, errNotConfigured
}

func (f *fakeAuth) EnrollMFA(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta) (auth.TOTPEnrollment, error) {
	if f.EnrollMFAFn != nil {
		return f.EnrollMFAFn(ctx, p, m)
	}
	return auth.TOTPEnrollment{}, errNotConfigured
}

func (f *fakeAuth) VerifyMFA(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, in auth.MFAInput, m auth.Meta) (auth.SessionResult, error) {
	if f.VerifyMFAFn != nil {
		return f.VerifyMFAFn(ctx, p, in, m)
	}
	return auth.SessionResult{}, errNotConfigured
}

// fakeSetup implements SetupService with the same conventions as fakeAuth.
// Authenticate accepts only the token "setup-ok" unless AuthenticateFn is set.
type fakeSetup struct {
	StatusFn                func(ctx context.Context) (setup.Status, error)
	StartSessionFn          func(ctx context.Context, code string, m auth.Meta) (string, error)
	AuthenticateFn          func(ctx context.Context, token string) error
	SetLanguageFn           func(ctx context.Context, token, language string) error
	BeginAdminEnrollmentFn  func(ctx context.Context, token string, in setup.AdminInput) (auth.TOTPEnrollment, error)
	CreateAdminFn           func(ctx context.Context, token, code string, m auth.Meta) (auth.SessionResult, error)
	SetRecoveryPassphraseFn func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, passphrase, confirm string) error
	CompleteFn              func(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta) (setup.Status, error)
}

func (f *fakeSetup) Status(ctx context.Context) (setup.Status, error) {
	if f.StatusFn != nil {
		return f.StatusFn(ctx)
	}
	return setup.Status{}, errNotConfigured
}

func (f *fakeSetup) StartSession(ctx context.Context, code string, m auth.Meta) (string, error) {
	if f.StartSessionFn != nil {
		return f.StartSessionFn(ctx, code, m)
	}
	return "", errNotConfigured
}

func (f *fakeSetup) Authenticate(ctx context.Context, token string) error {
	if f.AuthenticateFn != nil {
		return f.AuthenticateFn(ctx, token)
	}
	if token == "setup-ok" {
		return nil
	}
	return setup.ErrSessionInvalid
}

	if f.SetLanguageFn != nil {

	return errNotConfigured
}

// fakeAudit implements AuditService and records written events.
type fakeAudit struct {
	mu        sync.Mutex
	events    []audit.Event
	ListFn    func(ctx context.Context, f audit.Filter) ([]audit.Entry, int64, error)
	IterateFn func(ctx context.Context, fn func(audit.Entry) error) error
	VerifyFn  func(ctx context.Context, actor audit.Actor) (audit.Report, error)
}

func (f *fakeAudit) Write(_ context.Context, e audit.Event) error {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.events = append(f.events, e)
	return nil
}

// Events returns a copy of the recorded events.
func (f *fakeAudit) Events() []audit.Event {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]audit.Event(nil), f.events...)
}

// Count returns how many recorded events have the given action.
func (f *fakeAudit) Count(action string) int {
	n := 0
	for _, e := range f.Events() {
		if e.Action == action {
			n++
		}
	}
	return n
}

func (f *fakeAudit) List(ctx context.Context, fl audit.Filter) ([]audit.Entry, int64, error) {
	if f.ListFn != nil {
		return f.ListFn(ctx, fl)
	}
	return nil, 0, errNotConfigured
}

func (f *fakeAudit) Iterate(ctx context.Context, fn func(audit.Entry) error) error {
	if f.IterateFn != nil {
		return f.IterateFn(ctx, fn)
	}
	return errNotConfigured
}

func (f *fakeAudit) Verify(ctx context.Context, a audit.Actor) (audit.Report, error) {
	if f.VerifyFn != nil {
		return f.VerifyFn(ctx, a)
	}
	return audit.Report{}, errNotConfigured
}

// Test identities. Session cookie values and bearer tokens map to principals
// in identityAuth; the setup cookie "setup-ok" is accepted by fakeSetup.
var (
	adminID    = uuid.MustParse("0190f5a0-0000-7000-8000-000000000001")
	operatorID = uuid.MustParse("0190f5a0-0000-7000-8000-000000000002")
	auditorID  = uuid.MustParse("0190f5a0-0000-7000-8000-000000000003")
)

// identityAuth returns a fakeAuth that recognises the matrix identities.
// now is the fake clock time used for step-up windows.
func identityAuth(now time.Time) *fakeAuth {
	session := func(id uuid.UUID, name string, role rbac.Role, stepUp bool) auth.Principal {
		p := auth.Principal{Kind: rbac.UserSession, UserID: id, Username: name, DisplayName: name, Role: role, Locale: "en", SessionHash: []byte(name)}
		if stepUp {
			p.StepUpUntil = now.Add(5 * time.Minute)
		}
		return p
	}
	sessions := map[string]auth.Principal{
		"admin":           session(adminID, "admin", rbac.Admin, false),
		"operator":        session(operatorID, "operator", rbac.Operator, false),
		"auditor":         session(auditorID, "auditor", rbac.Auditor, false),
		"admin-stepup":    session(adminID, "admin", rbac.Admin, true),
		"operator-stepup": session(operatorID, "operator", rbac.Operator, true),
		"auditor-stepup":  session(auditorID, "auditor", rbac.Auditor, true),
		"pending":         {Kind: rbac.PendingSession, UserID: operatorID, Username: "operator", Role: rbac.Operator, SessionHash: []byte("pending")},
	}
	token := func(id uuid.UUID, name string, role rbac.Role, scope rbac.Scope) auth.Principal {
		return auth.Principal{Kind: rbac.APIToken, UserID: id, Username: name, Role: role, TokenID: uuid.NewSHA1(uuid.NameSpaceOID, []byte(name+string(scope))), Scope: scope}
	}
	tokens := map[string]auth.Principal{
		"alice_read_admin":       token(adminID, "admin", rbac.Admin, rbac.ScopeRead),
		"alice_operate_operator": token(operatorID, "operator", rbac.Operator, rbac.ScopeOperate),
		"alice_read_auditor":     token(auditorID, "auditor", rbac.Auditor, rbac.ScopeRead),
	}
	return &fakeAuth{
		AuthenticateFn: func(_ context.Context, t string) (auth.Principal, error) {
			if p, ok := sessions[t]; ok {
				return p, nil
			}
			return auth.Principal{}, auth.ErrUnauthenticated // includes "expired" and "disabled"
		},
		AuthenticateTokenFn: func(_ context.Context, b string) (auth.Principal, error) {
			if p, ok := tokens[b]; ok {
				return p, nil
			}
			return auth.Principal{}, auth.ErrUnauthenticated
		},
	}
}
```

```go
// as makes req come from a test identity: "" is anonymous, "setup" uses the
// wizard cookie, "alice_*" is an API token, anything else is a session cookie
// value understood by identityAuth. Cookie identities also send the matching
// CSRF token and a same-origin Origin header.
func as(req *http.Request, id string) *http.Request {
	req.Header.Set("Origin", "https://"+req.Host)
	switch {
	case id == "":
	case id == "setup":
		req.AddCookie(&http.Cookie{Name: setupCookie, Value: "setup-ok"})
		req.Header.Set("X-CSRF-Token", "csrf-setup-ok")
	case strings.HasPrefix(id, "alice_"):
		req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+id)
	default:
		req.AddCookie(&http.Cookie{Name: sessionCookie, Value: id})
		req.Header.Set("X-CSRF-Token", "csrf-"+id)
	}
	return req
}

// call sends a request with an optional JSON body as the given identity.
func call(h http.Handler, method, target, id, body string) *httptest.ResponseRecorder {
	var rdr io.Reader
	if body != "" {
		rdr = strings.NewReader(body)
	}
	req := as(httptest.NewRequest(method, target, rdr), id)
	if body != "" {
		req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	}
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	h.ServeHTTP(rec, req)
	return rec
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the guard, helper, matrix and spec tests**

`internal/api/guard_test.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"net/netip"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/platform/clock"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

// guardedHandler serves one test route with the given permission and limit;
// the handler echoes the principal's username.
func guardedHandler(t *testing.T, d Deps, perm rbac.Permission, limit limitClass) (http.Handler, *server) {
	t.Helper()
	s := newServer(d)
	h := s.handler([]route{
		{method: http.MethodGet, pattern: "/api/v1/probe", perm: perm, limit: limit, handler: func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
			writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{"user": principalFrom(r.Context()).Username})
		}},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/probe", perm: perm, limit: limit, handler: func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
			w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
		}},
	})
	return h, s
}

func TestGuardPassesPrincipalToHandler(t *testing.T) {
	h, _ := guardedHandler(t, testDeps(t, nil), rbac.Session, limitDefault)
	rec := call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/probe", "operator", "")
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code)
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"user":"operator"}`, rec.Body.String())
}

func TestStateChangeWithoutCSRFTokenIsRejected(t *testing.T) {
	h, _ := guardedHandler(t, testDeps(t, nil), rbac.Session, limitDefault)
	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/probe", nil)
	req.AddCookie(&http.Cookie{Name: sessionCookie, Value: "admin"})
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	h.ServeHTTP(rec, req)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusForbidden, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-AUTH-0004", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)

	req.Header.Set("X-CSRF-Token", "csrf-somebody-else")
	rec = httptest.NewRecorder()
	h.ServeHTTP(rec, req)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusForbidden, rec.Code, "a token bound to another session is rejected")

	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNoContent, call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/probe", "admin", "").Code)
}

func TestCrossOriginStateChangeIsRejected(t *testing.T) {
	h, _ := guardedHandler(t, testDeps(t, nil), rbac.Session, limitDefault)
	for name, set := range map[string]func(*http.Request){
		"foreign origin":      func(r *http.Request) { r.Header.Set("Origin", "https://evil.example") },
		"cross-site fetch":    func(r *http.Request) { r.Header.Set("Sec-Fetch-Site", "cross-site") },
		"plain http origin":   func(r *http.Request) { r.Header.Set("Origin", "http://"+r.Host) },
		"same-site subdomain": func(r *http.Request) { r.Header.Set("Sec-Fetch-Site", "same-site") },
	} {
		req := as(httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/probe", nil), "admin")
		set(req)
		rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
		h.ServeHTTP(rec, req)
		assert.Equal(t, http.StatusForbidden, rec.Code, name)
		assert.Equal(t, "ALC-AUTH-0004", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code, name)
	}
}

func TestBearerTokensNeedNoCSRFToken(t *testing.T) {
	h, _ := guardedHandler(t, testDeps(t, nil), rbac.Read, limitDefault)
	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/probe", nil)
	req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer alice_read_admin")
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	h.ServeHTTP(rec, req)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNoContent, rec.Code)
}

	req.AddCookie(&http.Cookie{Name: sessionCookie, Value: "alice_read_admin"})
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	h.ServeHTTP(rec, req)

}

func TestStepUpRequiredIsDistinctAndAudited(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	h, _ := guardedHandler(t, d, rbac.UsersManage, limitDefault)
	rec := call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/probe", "admin", "")
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusForbidden, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-AUTH-0003", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, 1, d.Audit.(*fakeAudit).Count("authz.denied"))

	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/probe", "admin-stepup", "").Code)
}

func TestSetupPermissionWithoutSessionSaysSetupExpired(t *testing.T) {
	h, _ := guardedHandler(t, testDeps(t, nil), rbac.Setup, limitDefault)
	rec := call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/probe", "", "")
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnauthorized, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-SET-0003", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/probe", "setup", "").Code)

func TestRateLimitReturns429WithRetryAfter(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	fake := d.Clock.(*clock.Fake)
	h, srv := guardedHandler(t, d, rbac.Public, limitLogin)
	for i := range 10 {
		require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/probe", "", "").Code, "attempt %d", i+1)
	}
	rec := call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/probe", "", "")
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusTooManyRequests, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-SYS-0005", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
	assert.NotEmpty(t, rec.Header().Get("Retry-After"))

	fake.Advance(7 * time.Second)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/probe", "", "").Code, "tokens refill over time")

	for range 20 {
		call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/probe", "", "")
	}
	(&Server{s: srv}).ResetLimits()
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/probe", "", "").Code, "ResetLimits clears buckets")
}

func TestGeneralLimitIsPerIdentity(t *testing.T) {
	h, _ := guardedHandler(t, testDeps(t, nil), rbac.Session, limitDefault)
	for range 300 {
		require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/probe", "admin", "").Code)
	}
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusTooManyRequests, call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/probe", "admin", "").Code)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/probe", "operator", "").Code, "another session has its own bucket")
}

func TestProbesAreNeverRateLimited(t *testing.T) {
	h := NewHandler(testDeps(t, nil))
	for range 200 {
		require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, do(h, http.MethodGet, "/livez", nil).Code)
	}
}

func TestForwardedForIsIgnoredWithoutTrustedProxy(t *testing.T) {
	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/", nil)
	req.RemoteAddr = "192.0.2.10:5555"
	req.Header.Set("X-Forwarded-For", "203.0.113.9")
	assert.Equal(t, "192.0.2.10", clientIP(req, nil).String())

	trusted := []netip.Prefix{netip.MustParsePrefix("192.0.2.0/24")}
	assert.Equal(t, "203.0.113.9", clientIP(req, trusted).String())

	req.Header.Set("X-Forwarded-For", "198.51.100.1, 203.0.113.9, 192.0.2.11")
	assert.Equal(t, "203.0.113.9", clientIP(req, trusted).String(), "right-most untrusted hop wins")

	req.Header.Set("X-Forwarded-For", "not-an-ip")
	assert.Equal(t, "192.0.2.10", clientIP(req, trusted).String())

	req.RemoteAddr = "[::ffff:192.0.2.10]:5555"
	req.Header.Del("X-Forwarded-For")
	assert.Equal(t, "192.0.2.10", clientIP(req, nil).String(), "IPv4-mapped addresses are unmapped")
}

func TestSpoofedForwardedForCannotEvadeLoginLimit(t *testing.T) {
	h, _ := guardedHandler(t, testDeps(t, nil), rbac.Public, limitLogin)
	codes := map[int]int{}
	for i := range 15 {
		req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/probe", nil)
		req.Header.Set("X-Forwarded-For", netip.AddrFrom4([4]byte{203, 0, 113, byte(i)}).String())
		rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
		h.ServeHTTP(rec, req)
		codes[rec.Code]++
	}
	assert.Equal(t, 10, codes[http.StatusOK])
	assert.Equal(t, 5, codes[http.StatusTooManyRequests])
}

func TestUnknownPermissionPanicsAtStartup(t *testing.T) {
	s := newServer(testDeps(t, nil))
	assert.Panics(t, func() {
		s.handler([]route{{method: http.MethodGet, pattern: "/api/v1/x", perm: "nobody.knows", handler: func(http.ResponseWriter, *http.Request) {}}})
	})
}
```

`internal/api/httpx_test.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/platform/validate"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
	"alice/internal/setup"
)

type probeBody struct {
	Name string `json:"name"`
}

// decodeHandler serves POST /api/v1/probe (public) and echoes the decoded name.
func decodeHandler(t *testing.T) http.Handler {
	t.Helper()
	s := newServer(testDeps(t, nil))
	return s.handler([]route{{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/probe", perm: rbac.Public, handler: func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		var b probeBody
		if !s.decode(w, r, &b) {
			return
		}
		writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, b)
	}}})
}

func TestDecodeAcceptsKnownFields(t *testing.T) {
	rec := call(decodeHandler(t), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/probe", "", `{"name":"İzmir"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code)
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"name":"İzmir"}`, rec.Body.String())
}

func TestDecodeRejectsUnknownFieldsBadJSONAndTrailingData(t *testing.T) {
	for _, body := range []string{`{"name":"a","role":"admin"}`, `{"name":`, `{"name":"a"}{"name":"b"}`, `[]`, ""} {
		rec := call(decodeHandler(t), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/probe", "", body)
		assert.Equal(t, http.StatusBadRequest, rec.Code, body)
		assert.Equal(t, "ALC-SYS-0004", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code, body)
	}
}

func TestDecodeRejectsBodiesOverOneMiB(t *testing.T) {
	big := `{"name":"` + strings.Repeat("a", maxBody) + `"}`
	rec := call(decodeHandler(t), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/probe", "", big)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-SYS-0007", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
}

func TestCookiesUseHostPrefixAndStrictAttributes(t *testing.T) {
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	setSessionCookie(rec, "tok")
	setSetupCookie(rec, "stp")
	clearSessionCookie(rec)
	clearSetupCookie(rec)
	cookies := rec.Result().Cookies()
	require.Len(t, cookies, 4)
	for _, c := range cookies {
		assert.True(t, strings.HasPrefix(c.Name, "__Host-"), c.Name)
		assert.True(t, c.Secure, c.Name)
		assert.True(t, c.HttpOnly, c.Name)
		assert.Equal(t, http.SameSiteStrictMode, c.SameSite, c.Name)
		assert.Equal(t, "/", c.Path, c.Name)
		assert.Empty(t, c.Domain, c.Name)
	}
	assert.Equal(t, sessionCookie, cookies[0].Name)
	assert.Equal(t, "tok", cookies[0].Value)
	assert.Equal(t, setupCookie, cookies[1].Name)
	assert.Equal(t, -1, cookies[2].MaxAge, "clearing expires the cookie")
	assert.Equal(t, setupCookie, cookies[3].Name)
	assert.Equal(t, -1, cookies[3].MaxAge)
}

func TestPathUUID(t *testing.T) {
	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/", nil)
	req.SetPathValue("id", "0190f5a0-0000-7000-8000-000000000001")
	id, ok := pathUUID(req, "id")
	assert.True(t, ok)
	assert.Equal(t, adminID, id)

	req.SetPathValue("id", "not-a-uuid")
	_, ok = pathUUID(req, "id")
	assert.False(t, ok)
}

// failRecorder runs s.fail for err and returns the response.
func failRecorder(t *testing.T, err error, logs io.Writer) *httptest.ResponseRecorder {
	t.Helper()
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	if logs != nil {
		d.Log = slog.New(slog.NewJSONHandler(logs, nil))
	}
	s := newServer(d)
	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/x", nil)
	req.Header.Set("Accept-Language", "tr")
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	s.requestID(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) { s.fail(w, r, err) })).ServeHTTP(rec, req)
	return rec
}

func TestFailMapsDomainErrors(t *testing.T) {
	cases := []struct {
		err    error
		status int
		code   string
	}{
		{auth.ErrInvalidCredentials, 401, "ALC-AUTH-0005"},
		{fmt.Errorf("wrapped: %w", auth.ErrMFAInvalid), 401, "ALC-AUTH-0006"},
		{auth.ErrNotFound, 404, "ALC-SYS-0001"},
		{auth.ErrUnauthenticated, 401, "ALC-AUTH-0001"},
		{auth.ErrLastAdmin, 409, "ALC-USR-0002"},
		{auth.ErrUsernameTaken, 409, "ALC-USR-0001"},
		{auth.ErrTokenScopeNotAllowed, 422, "ALC-TOK-0001"},
		{setup.ErrAlreadySetUp, 404, "ALC-SYS-0001"},
		{setup.ErrCodeInvalid, 401, "ALC-SET-0001"},
		{setup.ErrSessionInvalid, 401, "ALC-SET-0003"},
		{setup.ErrIncomplete, 409, "ALC-SET-0008"},
	}
	for _, tc := range cases {
		rec := failRecorder(t, tc.err, nil)
		assert.Equal(t, tc.status, rec.Code, tc.err.Error())
		assert.Equal(t, tc.code, decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code, tc.err.Error())
	}
}

func TestFailAddsParamsForLengthErrors(t *testing.T) {
	rec := failRecorder(t, auth.ErrPasswordTooShort, nil)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, rec.Code)
	var body struct {
		Error struct {
			Params  map[string]string `json:"params"`
			Message string            `json:"message"`
		} `json:"error"`
	}
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &body))
	assert.Equal(t, map[string]string{"min": "12"}, body.Error.Params)
	assert.Contains(t, body.Error.Message, "Parola çok kısa.")

	rec = failRecorder(t, setup.ErrPassphraseTooShort, nil)
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &body))
	assert.Equal(t, map[string]string{"min": "16"}, body.Error.Params)
}

func TestFailReturnsValidationDetails(t *testing.T) {
	var v validate.Errors
	v.Add("username", validate.Pattern, nil)
	v.Add("display_name", validate.TooLong, map[string]string{"max": "100"})
	rec := failRecorder(t, fmt.Errorf("create user: %w", v), nil)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, rec.Code)
	var body struct {
		Error struct {
			Code    string                `json:"code"`
			Details []validate.FieldError `json:"details"`
		} `json:"error"`
	}
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &body))
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-SYS-0006", body.Error.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, []validate.FieldError(v), body.Error.Details)
}

func TestFailSetsRetryAfterForSetupLock(t *testing.T) {
	rec := failRecorder(t, &setup.LockedError{RetryAfter: 14*time.Minute + 30*time.Second}, nil)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusTooManyRequests, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "870", rec.Header().Get("Retry-After"))
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-SET-0002", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
}

func TestFailHidesUnknownErrorsButLogsThem(t *testing.T) {
	var logs bytes.Buffer
	rec := failRecorder(t, errors.New("pq: relation users does not exist"), &logs)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusInternalServerError, rec.Code)
	assert.NotContains(t, rec.Body.String(), "relation")
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-SYS-0003", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
	assert.Contains(t, logs.String(), "relation users does not exist")
	assert.Contains(t, logs.String(), rec.Header().Get("X-Request-Id"))
}

func TestMetaTruncatesUserAgent(t *testing.T) {
	s := newServer(testDeps(t, nil))
	req := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/", nil)
	req.Header.Set("User-Agent", strings.Repeat("ğ", 200)) // 2 bytes per rune
	m := s.meta(req)
	assert.LessOrEqual(t, len(m.UserAgent), maxUserAgent)
	assert.Equal(t, strings.Repeat("ğ", 128), m.UserAgent)
	assert.Equal(t, "192.0.2.1", m.IP.String())
}
```

`internal/api/authz_test.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"flag"
	"fmt"
	"go/ast"
	"go/parser"
	"go/token"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

var update = flag.Bool("update", false, "rewrite docs/authz-matrix.md from the route table")

// Matrix identities (see as() and identityAuth).
var identities = []string{
	"", "expired", "disabled", "pending", "setup",
	"admin", "operator", "auditor",
	"admin-stepup", "operator-stepup", "auditor-stepup",
	"alice_read_admin", "alice_operate_operator", "alice_read_auditor",
}

const (
	allw = http.StatusOK
	unau = http.StatusUnauthorized
	forb = http.StatusForbidden
	step = 4031 // 403 with auth.stepup_required
)

// expectedMatrix is the documented role table (spec §15.4, decisions R3,
// §25.1) written out by hand. It is deliberately NOT computed from
// rbac.Decide: a wrong policy must make this test fail.
var expectedMatrix = map[rbac.Permission]map[string]int{
	// columns: anonymous, expired, disabled, pending, setup, admin, operator, auditor,
	// admin+step-up, operator+step-up, auditor+step-up, token read (admin),
	// token operate (operator), token read (auditor)
	rbac.Public:         row(allw, allw, allw, allw, allw, allw, allw, allw, allw, allw, allw, allw, allw, allw),
	rbac.Setup:          row(unau, unau, unau, forb, allw, forb, forb, forb, forb, forb, forb, forb, forb, forb),
	rbac.MFAPending:     row(unau, unau, unau, allw, forb, forb, forb, forb, forb, forb, forb, forb, forb, forb),
	rbac.Session:        row(unau, unau, unau, unau, unau, allw, allw, allw, allw, allw, allw, forb, forb, forb),
	rbac.SessionStepUp:  row(unau, unau, unau, unau, unau, step, step, step, allw, allw, allw, forb, forb, forb),
	rbac.Read:           row(unau, unau, unau, unau, unau, allw, allw, allw, allw, allw, allw, allw, allw, allw),
	rbac.AuditRead:      row(unau, unau, unau, unau, unau, allw, forb, allw, allw, forb, allw, allw, forb, allw),
	rbac.UsersRead:      row(unau, unau, unau, unau, unau, allw, forb, forb, allw, forb, forb, allw, forb, forb),
	rbac.UsersManage:    row(unau, unau, unau, unau, unau, step, forb, forb, allw, forb, forb, forb, forb, forb),
	rbac.SettingsManage: row(unau, unau, unau, unau, unau, step, forb, forb, allw, forb, forb, forb, forb, forb),
	rbac.SetupFinish:    row(unau, unau, unau, unau, unau, allw, forb, forb, allw, forb, forb, forb, forb, forb),
}

func row(codes ...int) map[string]int {
	m := map[string]int{}
	for i, c := range codes {
		m[identities[i]] = c
	}
	return m
}

// authenticatedUser reports whether a denial for this identity must be audited.
func authenticatedUser(id string) bool {
	return id != "" && id != "expired" && id != "disabled" && id != "pending" && id != "setup"
}

// TestAuthzMatrix sends every route in the table as every identity and checks
// the status, that forbidden requests never reach the handler, and that
// authenticated denials are audited (spec §25.1, SEC-016).
func TestAuthzMatrix(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	s := newServer(d)
	routes := s.apiRoutes()
	var called bool
	for i := range routes {
		routes[i].handler = func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
			called = true
			w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
		}
	}
	h := s.handler(routes)
	fa := d.Audit.(*fakeAudit)

	for _, rt := range routes {
		want, known := expectedMatrix[rt.perm]
		require.True(t, known, "permission %q of %s %s has no row in expectedMatrix", rt.perm, rt.method, rt.pattern)
		target := pathParamRE.ReplaceAllString(rt.pattern, uuid.NewString())
		for _, id := range identities {
			(&Server{s: s}).ResetLimits()
			called = false
			before := fa.Count("authz.denied")
			rec := call(h, rt.method, target, id, "")
			name := fmt.Sprintf("%s %s as %q", rt.method, rt.pattern, id)

			switch w := want[id]; w {
			case allw:
				assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code, name)
				assert.True(t, called, name)
			case step:
				assert.Equal(t, http.StatusForbidden, rec.Code, name)
				assert.Equal(t, "auth.stepup_required", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Reason, name)
			default:
				assert.Equal(t, w, rec.Code, name)
			}
			if want[id] != allw {
				assert.False(t, called, "handler must not run: %s", name)
				assert.NotContains(t, rec.Body.String(), "\"items\"", name)
			}
			denied := want[id] == forb || want[id] == step
			if denied && authenticatedUser(id) {
				assert.Equal(t, before+1, fa.Count("authz.denied"), "denial must be audited: %s", name)
			} else {
				assert.Equal(t, before, fa.Count("authz.denied"), "no audit expected: %s", name)
			}
		}
	}
}

// Every permission rbac knows is covered by the hand-written table.
func TestExpectedMatrixCoversEveryPermission(t *testing.T) {
	for _, p := range rbac.Permissions() {
		_, ok := expectedMatrix[p]
		assert.True(t, ok, "expectedMatrix lacks %q", p)
	}
}

func yesNo(b bool) string {
	if b {
		return "yes"
	}
	return "no"
}

// authzMatrixDoc renders docs/authz-matrix.md from the route table and policies.
func authzMatrixDoc() string {
	var b strings.Builder
	b.WriteString("# Authorization matrix\n\n")
	b.WriteString("Generated by `make authz-doc` from the route table in `internal/api` and the policies in\n")
	b.WriteString("`internal/rbac`. Do not edit by hand; CI fails when this file is out of date.\n\n")
	b.WriteString("Anonymous: no sign-in needed. Wizard: only with a setup-wizard session. Pending: only between\n")
	b.WriteString("password and verification code. Token columns: minimum API token access (the token owner's role\n")
	b.WriteString("must also allow the route). Step-up: a fresh verification code (5 minutes) is required.\n\n")
	b.WriteString("| Method | Path | Permission | Anonymous | Admin | Operator | Auditor | API token | Step-up |\n")
	b.WriteString("|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n")
	for _, rt := range newServer(Deps{}).apiRoutes() {
		pol, _ := rbac.PolicyFor(rt.perm)
		anon := yesNo(pol.Anonymous)
		switch {
		case pol.Setup:
			anon = "wizard"
		case pol.Pending:
			anon = "pending"
		}
		role := func(r rbac.Role) string { return yesNo(pol.Anonymous || slices.Contains(pol.Roles, r)) }
		token := "no"
		if pol.Anonymous {
			token = "yes"
		} else if pol.TokenScope != "" {
			token = string(pol.TokenScope)
		}
		fmt.Fprintf(&b, "| %s | `%s` | `%s` | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s |\n",
			rt.method, rt.pattern, rt.perm, anon, role(rbac.Admin), role(rbac.Operator), role(rbac.Auditor), token, yesNo(pol.StepUp))
	}
	return b.String()
}

func TestAuthzMatrixDocIsCurrent(t *testing.T) {
	path := filepath.Join("..", "..", "docs", "authz-matrix.md")
	want := authzMatrixDoc()
	if *update {
		require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(path, []byte(want), 0o644))
	}
	got, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	require.NoError(t, err, "run: make authz-doc")
	assert.Equal(t, want, string(got), "docs/authz-matrix.md is stale; run: make authz-doc")
}

// TestNoRouteBypassesTable parses the package's non-test sources and allows
// Handle/HandleFunc calls only inside register (the guarded route table) and
// for the documented catch-alls "/api/", "/test/" and "/" (spec §25.1).
func TestNoRouteBypassesTable(t *testing.T) {
	files, err := filepath.Glob("*.go")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	allowedLiterals := map[string]bool{`"/api/"`: true, `"/test/"`: true, `"/"`: true}
	fset := token.NewFileSet()
	checked := 0
	for _, name := range files {
		if strings.HasSuffix(name, "_test.go") {
			continue
		}
		f, err := parser.ParseFile(fset, name, nil, 0)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		for _, decl := range f.Decls {
			fn, ok := decl.(*ast.FuncDecl)
			if !ok || fn.Body == nil {
				continue
			}
			ast.Inspect(fn.Body, func(n ast.Node) bool {
				call, ok := n.(*ast.CallExpr)
				if !ok {
					return true
				}
				sel, ok := call.Fun.(*ast.SelectorExpr)
				if !ok || (sel.Sel.Name != "Handle" && sel.Sel.Name != "HandleFunc") || len(call.Args) == 0 {
					return true
				}
				checked++
				pos := fset.Position(call.Pos())
				if lit, ok := call.Args[0].(*ast.BasicLit); ok && allowedLiterals[lit.Value] {
					return true
				}
				if fn.Name.Name == "register" {
					return true
				}
				t.Errorf("%s: %s.%s registers a route outside the route table", pos, fn.Name.Name, sel.Sel.Name)
				return true
			})
		}
	}
	assert.Positive(t, checked, "the scan found no registrations at all; is it looking at the right files?")
}
```

Replace `internal/api/openapi_test.go` completely (the parser now ends an operation at any other four-space key such as a path-level `parameters:`, and the new tests compare permissions):

`internal/api/openapi_test.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"bufio"
	"bytes"
	"net/http"
	"regexp"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	apispec "alice/api"
)

var (
	pathKeyRE    = regexp.MustCompile(`^  (/[^:]*):\s*$`)
	methodKeyRE  = regexp.MustCompile(`^    (get|post|put|patch|delete):\s*$`)
	pathLevelRE  = regexp.MustCompile(`^    [^ ]`)
	pathParamRE  = regexp.MustCompile(`\{[a-z_]+\}`)
	permissionRE = regexp.MustCompile(`^\s*x-permission:\s*(\S+)\s*$`)
)

const errorDefaultRef = `default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }`

// operation is one method block under a path in the spec's paths section.
type operation struct {
	path, method string
	lines        []string
}

// httpMethod maps a lowercase OpenAPI method key to its HTTP method
// (strings.ToUpper is forbidden repo-wide).
func httpMethod(m string) string {
	return map[string]string{
		"get": http.MethodGet, "post": http.MethodPost, "put": http.MethodPut,
		"patch": http.MethodPatch, "delete": http.MethodDelete,
	}[m]
}

func (o operation) key() string { return httpMethod(o.method) + " " + o.path }

func (o operation) has(prefix string) bool {
	for _, l := range o.lines {
		if strings.HasPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(l), prefix) {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

// permission returns the operation's x-permission value, or "".
func (o operation) permission() string {
	for _, l := range o.lines {
		if m := permissionRE.FindStringSubmatch(l); m != nil {
			return m[1]
		}
	}
	return ""
}

// parseOperations reads the paths section into operation blocks: a method key
// at four spaces under a path key at two, up to the next sibling or path.
// Other keys at four spaces (path-level "parameters:", "summary:", ...) end the
// current operation, so their lines are never attributed to it.
func parseOperations(yaml []byte) []operation {
	var ops []operation
	inPaths, inOp := false, false
	path := ""
	sc := bufio.NewScanner(bytes.NewReader(yaml))
	for sc.Scan() {
		line := sc.Text()
		if line == "paths:" {
			inPaths = true
			continue
		}
		if !inPaths {
			continue
		}
		if len(line) > 0 && line[0] != ' ' {
			break
		}
		if m := pathKeyRE.FindStringSubmatch(line); m != nil {
			path, inOp = m[1], false
			continue
		}
		if m := methodKeyRE.FindStringSubmatch(line); m != nil {
			ops = append(ops, operation{path: path, method: m[1]})
			inOp = true
			continue
		}
		if pathLevelRE.MatchString(line) {
			inOp = false
			continue
		}
		if inOp {
			ops[len(ops)-1].lines = append(ops[len(ops)-1].lines, line)
		}
	}
	return ops
}

// missing returns the operations lacking the given line prefix.
func missing(ops []operation, prefix string) []string {
	var out []string
	for _, o := range ops {
		if !o.has(prefix) {
			out = append(out, o.key())
		}
	}
	return out
}

func specOps() []operation { return parseOperations(apispec.YAML) }

func specKeys() []string {
	var keys []string
	for _, o := range specOps() {
		keys = append(keys, o.key())
	}
	return keys
}

func tableRoutes() []route { return newServer(Deps{}).apiRoutes() }

// The route table and the spec must list exactly the same operations.
// Excluded on purpose (not documented operations): the SPA fallback "/" and
// the "/api/" and "/test/" JSON 404 catch-alls, registered outside the table.
func TestRouteTableMatchesSpec(t *testing.T) {
	var table []string
	for _, r := range tableRoutes() {
		table = append(table, r.method+" "+r.pattern)
	}
	assert.NotEmpty(t, table)
	assert.ElementsMatch(t, table, specKeys())
}

// Each route's permission in the table must equal its x-permission in the spec (SEC-016).
func TestRoutePermissionsMatchSpec(t *testing.T) {
	spec := map[string]string{}
	for _, o := range specOps() {
		spec[o.key()] = o.permission()
	}
	for _, r := range tableRoutes() {
		k := r.method + " " + r.pattern
		assert.Equal(t, string(r.perm), spec[k], "x-permission of %s", k)
	}
}

// Every documented operation must be routed: an anonymous request gets a JSON
// answer (401 for protected routes, 400 for public ones without a body) and
// never the SPA or the catch-all 404.
func TestEveryDocumentedPathIsServed(t *testing.T) {
	h := NewHandler(testDeps(t, nil))
	ops := specOps()
	assert.NotEmpty(t, ops)
	for _, o := range ops {
		target := pathParamRE.ReplaceAllString(o.path, uuid.NewString())
		rec := do(h, httpMethod(o.method), target, nil)
		assert.NotEqual(t, http.StatusNotFound, rec.Code, o.key())
		assert.Equal(t, "application/json; charset=utf-8", rec.Header().Get("Content-Type"), o.key())
	}
}

// Every operation must declare x-permission.
func TestEveryOperationDeclaresPermission(t *testing.T) {
	ops := specOps()
	assert.NotEmpty(t, ops)
	assert.Empty(t, missing(ops, "x-permission:"))
}

// Every operation must fall back to the shared Error response for undeclared statuses.
func TestEveryOperationReferencesSharedError(t *testing.T) {
	ops := specOps()
	assert.NotEmpty(t, ops)
	assert.Empty(t, missing(ops, errorDefaultRef))
}

func TestParseOperationsFlagsMissingDefault(t *testing.T) {
	snippet := `openapi: 3.1.0
paths:
  /a:
    get:
      x-permission: public
      responses:
        "200": { description: ok }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
  /b:
    get:
      x-permission: public
      responses:
        "200": { description: ok }
    post:
      x-permission: public
      responses:
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
components:
  schemas:
    get:
      default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
`
	ops := parseOperations([]byte(snippet))
	assert.Len(t, ops, 3)
	assert.Equal(t, []string{"GET /b"}, missing(ops, errorDefaultRef))
	assert.Empty(t, missing(ops, "x-permission:"))
}

// Path-level keys after an operation must not be attributed to that operation.
func TestParseOperationsIgnoresPathLevelKeys(t *testing.T) {
	snippet := `paths:
  /items/{id}:
    get:
      x-permission: read
      responses:
        "200": { description: ok }
    parameters:
      - name: id
        in: path
        x-permission: public
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
    delete:
      x-permission: session
      responses:
        "204": { description: gone }
`
	ops := parseOperations([]byte(snippet))
	require.Len(t, ops, 2)
	assert.Equal(t, "read", ops[0].permission())
	assert.Equal(t, "session", ops[1].permission())
	assert.Equal(t, []string{"GET /items/{id}", "DELETE /items/{id}"}, missing(ops, errorDefaultRef),
		"the default line under parameters belongs to no operation")
}
```
