```go
// Package api is Alice's HTTP layer. It holds no business logic and no SQL.
package api

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"net/netip"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/auth/ratelimit"
	"alice/internal/i18n"
	"alice/internal/platform/clock"
	"alice/internal/platform/db"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
	"alice/internal/setup"
	"alice/internal/version"
)

// HealthChecker reports whether dependencies (the database) are ready.
type HealthChecker interface {
	Check(ctx context.Context) error
}

// AuthService is the part of auth.Service the HTTP layer uses.
type AuthService interface {
	Authenticate(ctx context.Context, sessionToken string) (auth.Principal, error)
	AuthenticateToken(ctx context.Context, bearer string) (auth.Principal, error)
	CSRFToken(sessionToken string) string
	CheckCSRF(sessionToken, header string) bool
	Login(ctx context.Context, username, password string, m auth.Meta) (auth.LoginResult, error)
	EnrollMFA(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta) (auth.TOTPEnrollment, error)
	VerifyMFA(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, in auth.MFAInput, m auth.Meta) (auth.SessionResult, error)
	SessionInfo(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, sessionToken string) (auth.SessionInfo, error)
	StepUp(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, code string, m auth.Meta) (auth.SessionResult, error)
	Logout(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta) error
	ChangePassword(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, current, next string, m auth.Meta) (auth.SessionResult, error)
	RegenerateRecoveryCodes(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta) ([]string, error)
	SetLocale(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, locale string) error
	ListUsers(ctx context.Context) ([]auth.User, error)
	GetUser(ctx context.Context, id uuid.UUID) (auth.User, error)
	CreateUser(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, in auth.NewUser) (auth.User, error)
	UpdateUser(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, id uuid.UUID, in auth.UserUpdate) (auth.User, error)
	ResetMFA(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, id uuid.UUID) error
	ResetPassword(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, id uuid.UUID, password string) error
	RevokeSessions(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, id uuid.UUID) error
	CreateToken(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, in auth.NewToken) (string, auth.Token, error)
	ListTokens(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, userID uuid.UUID) ([]auth.Token, error)
	RevokeToken(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, id uuid.UUID) error
}

// SetupService is the part of setup.Service the HTTP layer uses.
type SetupService interface {
	Status(ctx context.Context) (setup.Status, error)
	StartSession(ctx context.Context, code string, m auth.Meta) (string, error)
	Authenticate(ctx context.Context, token string) error
	SetLanguage(ctx context.Context, token, language string) error
	BeginAdminEnrollment(ctx context.Context, token string, in setup.AdminInput) (auth.TOTPEnrollment, error)
	CreateAdmin(ctx context.Context, token, code string, m auth.Meta) (auth.SessionResult, error)
	SetRecoveryPassphrase(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta, passphrase, confirm string) error
	Complete(ctx context.Context, p auth.Principal, m auth.Meta) (setup.Status, error)
}

// AuditService is the part of audit.Service the HTTP layer uses.
type AuditService interface {
	Write(ctx context.Context, e audit.Event) error
	List(ctx context.Context, f audit.Filter) ([]audit.Entry, int64, error)
	Iterate(ctx context.Context, fn func(audit.Entry) error) error
	Verify(ctx context.Context, actor audit.Actor) (audit.Report, error)
}

// Deps are the handler's dependencies, wired in cmd/alice.
type Deps struct {
	Health         HealthChecker
	Catalog        *i18n.Catalog
	Version        version.Info
	UI             fs.FS
	Log            *slog.Logger
	Clock          clock.Clock
	Auth           AuthService
	Setup          SetupService
	Audit          AuditService
	TrustedProxies []netip.Prefix
	TestHandler    http.Handler // nil in release builds; mounted at "/test/" by the e2e build
}

// limitClass selects the rate limits applied to a route in addition to the general limit.
type limitClass int

const (
	limitDefault limitClass = iota // general limit only
	limitNone                      // no limit (health probes)
	limitLogin                     // 10 per minute per IP (password and setup-code guessing)
	limitMFA                       // 5 per minute per IP (verification codes)
)

// route is one documented operation. The route table is the single source of
// truth for what the muxes serve and which permission each operation needs;
// openapi.yaml must list exactly these operations with the same x-permission.
type route struct {
	method  string
	pattern string
	perm    rbac.Permission
	limit   limitClass
	handler http.HandlerFunc
}

type server struct {
	deps    Deps
	general *ratelimit.Limiter // authenticated subjects, per session or token: 300/min
	anon    *ratelimit.Limiter // anonymous and setup subjects, per IP: 60/min
	login   *ratelimit.Limiter // per IP: 10/min
	mfa     *ratelimit.Limiter // per IP: 5/min
}

func newServer(d Deps) *server {
	if d.Clock == nil {
		d.Clock = clock.Real{}
	}
	return &server{
		deps:    d,
		general: ratelimit.New(d.Clock, 300, time.Minute),
		anon:    ratelimit.New(d.Clock, 60, time.Minute),
		login:   ratelimit.New(d.Clock, 10, time.Minute),
		mfa:     ratelimit.New(d.Clock, 5, time.Minute),
	}
}

// Server is the public HTTPS handler plus the controls the e2e build needs.
type Server struct {
	s *server
	h http.Handler
}

// New builds the public HTTPS handler. It panics if a route declares an
// unknown permission, so a misconfigured route table never starts (SEC-016).
func New(d Deps) *Server {
	s := newServer(d)
	return &Server{s: s, h: s.handler(s.apiRoutes())}
}

// Handler returns the HTTP handler.
func (s *Server) Handler() http.Handler { return s.h }

// ResetLimits clears all in-memory rate limiters (used by the e2e reset endpoint).
func (s *Server) ResetLimits() {
	s.s.general.Reset()
	s.s.anon.Reset()
	s.s.login.Reset()
	s.s.mfa.Reset()
}

// NewHandler returns the public HTTPS handler.
func NewHandler(d Deps) http.Handler { return New(d).Handler() }

// NewHealthHandler returns the handler for the internal, container-local health listener.
func NewHealthHandler(d Deps) http.Handler {
	s := newServer(d)
	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	s.register(mux, s.probeRoutes())
	return s.requestID(securityHeaders(s.recoverer(mux)))
}

func (s *server) handler(routes []route) http.Handler {
	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	s.register(mux, routes)
	notFound := func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) { s.writeError(w, r, ErrNotFound) }
	mux.HandleFunc("/api/", notFound)
	if s.deps.TestHandler != nil {
		mux.Handle("/test/", s.deps.TestHandler)
	} else {
		mux.HandleFunc("/test/", notFound) // reserved for test-only routes; never the SPA
	}
	mux.Handle("/", s.spa())
	return s.requestID(securityHeaders(s.recoverer(mux)))
}

// register is the only place routes reach a mux; every route passes through guard.
func (s *server) register(mux *http.ServeMux, routes []route) {
	for _, r := range routes {
		if _, ok := rbac.PolicyFor(r.perm); !ok {
			panic(fmt.Sprintf("api: route %s %s has unknown permission %q", r.method, r.pattern, r.perm))
		}
		mux.HandleFunc(r.method+" "+r.pattern, s.guard(r))
	}
}

// probeRoutes are served on both the public and the internal health listener.
func (s *server) probeRoutes() []route {
	return []route{
		{method: http.MethodGet, pattern: "/livez", perm: rbac.Public, limit: limitNone, handler: func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
			writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{"status": "ok"})
		}},
		{method: http.MethodGet, pattern: "/healthz", perm: rbac.Public, limit: limitNone, handler: func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
			if err := s.deps.Health.Check(r.Context()); err != nil {
				s.deps.Log.Warn("health check failed", "request_id", RequestID(r.Context()), "reason", healthReason(err))
				s.writeError(w, r, ErrUnavailable)
				return
			}
			writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{"status": "ok"})
		}},
	}
}

// systemRoutes are small informational endpoints.
func (s *server) systemRoutes() []route {
	return []route{
		{method: http.MethodGet, pattern: "/api/v1/version", perm: rbac.Read, handler: func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
			writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, s.deps.Version)
		}},
		{method: http.MethodGet, pattern: "/api/v1/i18n/locales", perm: rbac.Public, handler: func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
			writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"default": s.deps.Catalog.Default(), "locales": s.deps.Catalog.Locales()})
		}},
	}
}

// apiRoutes are the documented operations on the public listener.
// Undocumented catch-alls (the /api/ and /test/ JSON 404s and the SPA
// fallback) are registered separately in handler and are not operations.
func (s *server) apiRoutes() []route {
	var routes []route
	routes = append(routes, s.probeRoutes()...)
	routes = append(routes, s.systemRoutes()...)
	return routes
}

// healthReason maps an error to a fixed label so no driver text (which could
// include connection details) ever reaches the logs.
func healthReason(err error) string {
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, db.ErrUnreachable):
		return "database_unreachable"
	case errors.Is(err, db.ErrMigrationsPending):
		return "migrations_pending"
	default:
		return "unknown"
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the guard middleware**

`internal/api/guard.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/hex"
	"net/http"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

type principalKey struct{}

// principalFrom returns the authenticated principal stored by guard. For
// public routes it is the zero Principal (Kind rbac.Anonymous).
func principalFrom(ctx context.Context) auth.Principal {
	p, _ := ctx.Value(principalKey{}).(auth.Principal)
	return p
}

// guard wraps a route handler with, in order: authentication, rate limiting,
// the same-origin check, the CSRF check and the permission decision (SEC-016,
// SEC-017, SEC-021, SEC-024). Nothing reaches rt.handler unless rbac allows it.
func (s *server) guard(rt route) http.HandlerFunc {
	policy, _ := rbac.PolicyFor(rt.perm) // register has checked the permission exists
	return func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		ip := clientIP(r, s.deps.TrustedProxies)
		p, csrfToken := auth.Principal{}, ""
		if !policy.Anonymous {
			p, csrfToken = s.authenticate(r)
		}
		if !s.allowRate(w, r, rt.limit, p, ip.String()) {
			return
		}
		if !safeMethod(r.Method) && !hasBearer(r) {
			if !sameOrigin(r) {
				s.writeError(w, r, ErrCSRF)
				return
			}
			if !policy.Anonymous && csrfToken != "" && !s.deps.Auth.CheckCSRF(csrfToken, r.Header.Get("X-CSRF-Token")) {
				s.writeError(w, r, ErrCSRF)
				return
			}
		}
		switch rbac.Decide(rt.perm, p.Subject(s.deps.Clock.Now())) {
		case rbac.Allow:
			rt.handler(w, r.WithContext(context.WithValue(r.Context(), principalKey{}, p)))
		case rbac.DenyUnauthenticated:
			if policy.Setup {
				s.writeError(w, r, ErrSetupSessionExpired)
				return
			}
			s.writeError(w, r, ErrUnauthenticated)
		case rbac.DenyStepUp:
			s.auditDenied(r, rt, p, "stepup")
			s.writeError(w, r, ErrStepUpRequired)
		default:
			s.auditDenied(r, rt, p, "permission")
			s.writeError(w, r, ErrForbidden)
		}
	}
}

// authenticate resolves the caller. It returns the principal and the cookie
// value that the CSRF token must be bound to ("" for API tokens and anonymous
// callers). Invalid credentials simply leave the caller anonymous.
func (s *server) authenticate(r *http.Request) (auth.Principal, string) {
	ctx := r.Context()
	if hasBearer(r) {
		bearer := strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(r.Header.Get("Authorization"), "Bearer "))
		if p, err := s.deps.Auth.AuthenticateToken(ctx, bearer); err == nil {
			return p, ""
		}
		return auth.Principal{}, ""
	}
	if tok := sessionTokenFrom(r); tok != "" {
		if p, err := s.deps.Auth.Authenticate(ctx, tok); err == nil {
			return p, tok
		}
	}
	if tok := setupTokenFrom(r); tok != "" {
		if err := s.deps.Setup.Authenticate(ctx, tok); err == nil {
			return auth.Principal{Kind: rbac.SetupSession}, tok
		}
	}
	return auth.Principal{}, ""
}

// allowRate applies the route's specific limit and then the general limit.
// It writes the 429 response itself and returns false when the caller is over a limit.
func (s *server) allowRate(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, class limitClass, p auth.Principal, ip string) bool {
	if class == limitNone {
		return true
	}
	checks := make([]func() (bool, time.Duration), 0, 2)
	switch class {
	case limitLogin:
		checks = append(checks, func() (bool, time.Duration) { return s.login.Allow(ip) })
	case limitMFA:
		checks = append(checks, func() (bool, time.Duration) { return s.mfa.Allow(ip) })
	}
	switch p.Kind {
	case rbac.UserSession, rbac.PendingSession:
		key := "s:" + hex.EncodeToString(p.SessionHash)
		checks = append(checks, func() (bool, time.Duration) { return s.general.Allow(key) })
	case rbac.APIToken:
		key := "t:" + p.TokenID.String()
		checks = append(checks, func() (bool, time.Duration) { return s.general.Allow(key) })
	default:
		checks = append(checks, func() (bool, time.Duration) { return s.anon.Allow(ip) })
	}
	for _, check := range checks {
		if ok, retry := check(); !ok {
			w.Header().Set("Retry-After", strconv.Itoa(int(retry.Round(time.Second)/time.Second)))
			s.writeError(w, r, ErrRateLimited)
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}

// auditDenied records an authorization failure of an authenticated user or
// token (spec §25.1). Failures to write are logged, never shown to the caller.
func (s *server) auditDenied(r *http.Request, rt route, p auth.Principal, reason string) {
	if p.Kind != rbac.UserSession && p.Kind != rbac.APIToken {
		return
	}
	ev := audit.Event{
		Actor:  p.Actor(s.meta(r)),
		Action: "authz.denied",
		Result: audit.Denied,
		Details: map[string]string{
			"permission": string(rt.perm),
			"method":     rt.method,
			"path":       rt.pattern,
			"reason":     reason,
		},
	}
	if err := s.deps.Audit.Write(r.Context(), ev); err != nil {
		s.deps.Log.Error("audit write failed", "request_id", RequestID(r.Context()), "action", ev.Action, "error", err.Error())
	}
}

func safeMethod(m string) bool { return m == http.MethodGet || m == http.MethodHead }

func hasBearer(r *http.Request) bool {
	return strings.HasPrefix(r.Header.Get("Authorization"), "Bearer ")
}

// sameOrigin rejects cross-site state-changing requests (defence in depth
// next to SameSite=Strict cookies and the CSRF token).
func sameOrigin(r *http.Request) bool {
	switch r.Header.Get("Sec-Fetch-Site") {
	case "", "same-origin", "none":
	default:
		return false
	}
	origin := r.Header.Get("Origin")
	return origin == "" || origin == "https://"+r.Host
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement the request/response helpers**

`internal/api/httpx.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"net/netip"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"unicode/utf8"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"alice/internal/auth"
)

const (
	sessionCookie = "__Host-alice_session"
	setupCookie   = "__Host-alice_setup"

	// maxBody caps JSON request bodies (SEC-023).
	maxBody = 1 << 20
	// maxUserAgent bounds the stored User-Agent header.
	maxUserAgent = 256
)

// decode reads a JSON request body into dst. Unknown fields, malformed JSON,
// trailing data and bodies over 1 MiB are rejected (SEC-061, SEC-023). On
// failure it writes the error response and returns false.
func (s *server) decode(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, dst any) bool {
	body := http.MaxBytesReader(w, r.Body, maxBody)
	dec := json.NewDecoder(body)
	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
	err := dec.Decode(dst)
	if err == nil {
		if _, tail := dec.Token(); !errors.Is(tail, io.EOF) {
			err = errors.New("trailing data after JSON value")
		}
	}
	if err != nil {
		var tooLarge *http.MaxBytesError
		if errors.As(err, &tooLarge) {
			s.writeError(w, r, ErrBodyTooLarge)
		} else {
			s.writeError(w, r, ErrBadRequest)
		}
		return false
	}
	return true
}

// sessionTokenFrom returns the raw session cookie value, or "".
func sessionTokenFrom(r *http.Request) string { return cookieValue(r, sessionCookie) }

// setupTokenFrom returns the raw setup (wizard) cookie value, or "".
func setupTokenFrom(r *http.Request) string { return cookieValue(r, setupCookie) }

func cookieValue(r *http.Request, name string) string {
	c, err := r.Cookie(name)
	if err != nil {
		return ""
	}
	return c.Value
}

// meta collects the request facts the services record in sessions and audit events.
func (s *server) meta(r *http.Request) auth.Meta {
	ua := r.Header.Get("User-Agent")
	if len(ua) > maxUserAgent {
		ua = ua[:maxUserAgent]
		for !utf8.ValidString(ua) {
			ua = ua[:len(ua)-1]
		}
	}
	return auth.Meta{IP: clientIP(r, s.deps.TrustedProxies), UserAgent: ua, RequestID: RequestID(r.Context())}
}

func setCookie(w http.ResponseWriter, name, value string, maxAge int) {
	http.SetCookie(w, &http.Cookie{
		Name:     name,
		Value:    value,
		Path:     "/",
		MaxAge:   maxAge,
		Secure:   true,
		HttpOnly: true,
		SameSite: http.SameSiteStrictMode,
	})
}

// setSessionCookie sets the browser-session cookie (SEC-018). It has no
// Max-Age: the server enforces the idle and absolute limits.
func setSessionCookie(w http.ResponseWriter, token string) { setCookie(w, sessionCookie, token, 0) }
func clearSessionCookie(w http.ResponseWriter)             { setCookie(w, sessionCookie, "", -1) }
func setSetupCookie(w http.ResponseWriter, token string)   { setCookie(w, setupCookie, token, 0) }
func clearSetupCookie(w http.ResponseWriter)               { setCookie(w, setupCookie, "", -1) }

// pathUUID parses a UUID path parameter.
func pathUUID(r *http.Request, name string) (uuid.UUID, bool) {
	id, err := uuid.Parse(r.PathValue(name))
	return id, err == nil
}

// clientIP returns the caller's address. X-Forwarded-For is honoured only when
// the direct peer is a configured trusted proxy; then the right-most address
// that is not itself a trusted proxy wins (SEC-021).
func clientIP(r *http.Request, trusted []netip.Prefix) netip.Addr {
	peer := parseHostAddr(r.RemoteAddr)
	if !peer.IsValid() || !isTrusted(peer, trusted) {
		return peer
	}
	hops := strings.Split(r.Header.Get("X-Forwarded-For"), ",")
	for _, hop := range slices.Backward(hops) {
		a, err := netip.ParseAddr(strings.TrimSpace(hop))
		if err != nil {
			return peer
		}
		a = a.Unmap()
		if !isTrusted(a, trusted) {
			return a
		}
	}
	return peer
}

func parseHostAddr(remote string) netip.Addr {
	host, _, err := net.SplitHostPort(remote)
	if err != nil {
		host = remote
	}
	a, err := netip.ParseAddr(host)
	if err != nil {
		return netip.Addr{}
	}
	return a.Unmap()
}

func isTrusted(a netip.Addr, trusted []netip.Prefix) bool {
	for _, p := range trusted {
		if p.Contains(a) {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Implement the error registry and mapping**

Replace `internal/api/errors.go` completely:

```go
package api

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"strconv"
	"time"

	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/platform/validate"
	"alice/internal/setup"
)

// ErrorCode is a stable, documented API error. Codes are never reused.
type ErrorCode struct {
	Code      string
	Reason    string
	Status    int
	Retryable bool
}

var (
	ErrNotFound     = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-SYS-0001", Reason: "system.not_found", Status: http.StatusNotFound}
	ErrUnavailable  = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-SYS-0002", Reason: "system.unavailable", Status: http.StatusServiceUnavailable, Retryable: true}
	ErrUnexpected   = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-SYS-0003", Reason: "internal.unexpected", Status: http.StatusInternalServerError}
	ErrBadRequest   = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-SYS-0004", Reason: "system.bad_request", Status: http.StatusBadRequest}
	ErrRateLimited  = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-SYS-0005", Reason: "rate.limited", Status: http.StatusTooManyRequests, Retryable: true}
	ErrValidation   = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-SYS-0006", Reason: "validation.failed", Status: http.StatusUnprocessableEntity}
	ErrBodyTooLarge = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-SYS-0007", Reason: "system.body_too_large", Status: http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge}

	ErrUnauthenticated        = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-AUTH-0001", Reason: "auth.unauthenticated", Status: http.StatusUnauthorized}
	ErrForbidden              = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-AUTH-0002", Reason: "auth.forbidden", Status: http.StatusForbidden}
	ErrStepUpRequired         = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-AUTH-0003", Reason: "auth.stepup_required", Status: http.StatusForbidden}
	ErrCSRF                   = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-AUTH-0004", Reason: "auth.csrf_failed", Status: http.StatusForbidden}
	ErrInvalidCredentials     = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-AUTH-0005", Reason: "auth.invalid_credentials", Status: http.StatusUnauthorized}
	ErrMFAInvalid             = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-AUTH-0006", Reason: "auth.mfa_invalid", Status: http.StatusUnauthorized}
	ErrMFAAlreadyEnrolled     = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-AUTH-0007", Reason: "auth.mfa_already_enrolled", Status: http.StatusConflict}
	ErrPasswordTooShort       = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-AUTH-0008", Reason: "auth.password_too_short", Status: http.StatusUnprocessableEntity}
	ErrPasswordTooLong        = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-AUTH-0009", Reason: "auth.password_too_long", Status: http.StatusUnprocessableEntity}
	ErrPasswordBreached       = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-AUTH-0010", Reason: "auth.password_breached", Status: http.StatusUnprocessableEntity}
	ErrCurrentPasswordInvalid = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-AUTH-0011", Reason: "auth.current_password_invalid", Status: http.StatusUnprocessableEntity}

	ErrUsernameTaken        = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-USR-0001", Reason: "user.username_taken", Status: http.StatusConflict}
	ErrLastAdmin            = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-USR-0002", Reason: "user.last_admin", Status: http.StatusConflict}
	ErrTokenScopeNotAllowed = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-TOK-0001", Reason: "token.scope_not_allowed", Status: http.StatusUnprocessableEntity}

	ErrSetupCodeInvalid       = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-SET-0001", Reason: "setup.code_invalid", Status: http.StatusUnauthorized}
	ErrSetupLocked            = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-SET-0002", Reason: "setup.locked", Status: http.StatusTooManyRequests, Retryable: true}
	ErrSetupSessionExpired    = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-SET-0003", Reason: "setup.session_expired", Status: http.StatusUnauthorized}
	ErrSetupEnrollmentMissing = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-SET-0004", Reason: "setup.enrollment_missing", Status: http.StatusConflict}
	ErrPassphraseTooShort     = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-SET-0005", Reason: "setup.passphrase_too_short", Status: http.StatusUnprocessableEntity}
	ErrPassphraseMismatch     = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-SET-0006", Reason: "setup.passphrase_mismatch", Status: http.StatusUnprocessableEntity}
	ErrPassphraseAlreadySet   = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-SET-0007", Reason: "setup.passphrase_already_set", Status: http.StatusConflict}
	ErrSetupIncomplete        = ErrorCode{Code: "ALC-SET-0008", Reason: "setup.incomplete", Status: http.StatusConflict}
)

// AllErrorCodes lists every registered code; tests check format, uniqueness, translations and docs.
func AllErrorCodes() []ErrorCode {
	return []ErrorCode{
		ErrNotFound, ErrUnavailable, ErrUnexpected, ErrBadRequest, ErrRateLimited, ErrValidation, ErrBodyTooLarge,
		ErrUnauthenticated, ErrForbidden, ErrStepUpRequired, ErrCSRF, ErrInvalidCredentials, ErrMFAInvalid,
		ErrMFAAlreadyEnrolled, ErrPasswordTooShort, ErrPasswordTooLong, ErrPasswordBreached, ErrCurrentPasswordInvalid,
		ErrUsernameTaken, ErrLastAdmin, ErrTokenScopeNotAllowed,
		ErrSetupCodeInvalid, ErrSetupLocked, ErrSetupSessionExpired, ErrSetupEnrollmentMissing,
		ErrPassphraseTooShort, ErrPassphraseMismatch, ErrPassphraseAlreadySet, ErrSetupIncomplete,
	}
}

// errorMap translates domain errors into API codes. Order matters only for
// errors that wrap each other; none of these do.
var errorMap = []struct {
	err    error
	code   ErrorCode
	params map[string]string
}{
	{auth.ErrNotFound, ErrNotFound, nil},
	{auth.ErrUnauthenticated, ErrUnauthenticated, nil},
	{auth.ErrInvalidCredentials, ErrInvalidCredentials, nil},
	{auth.ErrMFAInvalid, ErrMFAInvalid, nil},
	{auth.ErrMFAAlreadyEnrolled, ErrMFAAlreadyEnrolled, nil},
	{auth.ErrPasswordTooShort, ErrPasswordTooShort, map[string]string{"min": strconv.Itoa(auth.MinPasswordLength)}},
	{auth.ErrPasswordTooLong, ErrPasswordTooLong, map[string]string{"max": strconv.Itoa(auth.MaxPasswordLength)}},
	{auth.ErrPasswordBreached, ErrPasswordBreached, nil},
	{auth.ErrCurrentPasswordInvalid, ErrCurrentPasswordInvalid, nil},
	{auth.ErrUsernameTaken, ErrUsernameTaken, nil},
	{auth.ErrLastAdmin, ErrLastAdmin, nil},
	{auth.ErrTokenScopeNotAllowed, ErrTokenScopeNotAllowed, nil},
	{setup.ErrAlreadySetUp, ErrNotFound, nil},
	{setup.ErrCodeInvalid, ErrSetupCodeInvalid, nil},
	{setup.ErrSessionInvalid, ErrSetupSessionExpired, nil},
	{setup.ErrEnrollmentMissing, ErrSetupEnrollmentMissing, nil},
	{setup.ErrPassphraseTooShort, ErrPassphraseTooShort, map[string]string{"min": strconv.Itoa(setup.MinPassphraseLength)}},
	{setup.ErrPassphraseMismatch, ErrPassphraseMismatch, nil},
	{setup.ErrPassphraseAlreadySet, ErrPassphraseAlreadySet, nil},
	{setup.ErrIncomplete, ErrSetupIncomplete, nil},
}

type errorPayload struct {
	Code       string                `json:"code"`
	Reason     string                `json:"reason"`
	MessageKey string                `json:"message_key"`
	Message    string                `json:"message"`
	RequestID  string                `json:"request_id"`
	Retryable  bool                  `json:"retryable"`
	Params     map[string]string     `json:"params,omitempty"`
	Details    []validate.FieldError `json:"details,omitempty"`
}

func (s *server) writeError(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, ec ErrorCode) {
	s.writeErrorWith(w, r, ec, nil, nil)
}

// writeErrorWith writes the error body of spec §9.3. message is the fallback
// text in the request language; clients render message_key with params.
func (s *server) writeErrorWith(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, ec ErrorCode, params map[string]string, details []validate.FieldError) {
	lang := s.deps.Catalog.Negotiate(r.Header.Get("Accept-Language"))
	key := "errors." + ec.Reason
	msg := s.deps.Catalog.T(lang, key+".title", params) + " " + s.deps.Catalog.T(lang, key+".action", params)
	writeJSON(w, ec.Status, map[string]errorPayload{"error": {
		Code:       ec.Code,
		Reason:     ec.Reason,
		MessageKey: key,
		Message:    msg,
		RequestID:  RequestID(r.Context()),
		Retryable:  ec.Retryable,
		Params:     params,
		Details:    details,
	}})
}

// fail maps a service error to its API error. Unknown errors are logged with
// the request ID and answered with a generic 500 (SEC-052).
func (s *server) fail(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, err error) {
	var verrs validate.Errors
	if errors.As(err, &verrs) {
		s.writeErrorWith(w, r, ErrValidation, nil, verrs)
		return
	}
	var locked *setup.LockedError
	if errors.As(err, &locked) {
		secs := int(locked.RetryAfter.Round(time.Second) / time.Second)
		w.Header().Set("Retry-After", strconv.Itoa(max(1, secs)))
		s.writeError(w, r, ErrSetupLocked)
		return
	}
	for _, m := range errorMap {
		if errors.Is(err, m.err) {
			s.writeErrorWith(w, r, m.code, m.params, nil)
			return
		}
	}
	s.deps.Log.Error("request failed", "request_id", RequestID(r.Context()), "path", r.URL.Path, "error", err.Error())
	s.writeError(w, r, ErrUnexpected)
}

func writeJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, v any) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
	w.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "no-store")
	w.WriteHeader(status)
	_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(v)
}
```

```json
      "action": "Her iki alana da aynı parolayı yazın."
    },
    "passphrase_already_set": {
      "title": "Kurtarma parolası zaten belirlenmiş.",
      "cause": "Kurtarma parolası kurulum sırasında yalnızca bir kez seçilebilir.",
      "action": "Sonraki adımla devam edin."
    },
    "incomplete": {
      "title": "Kurulum henüz tamamlanmadı.",
      "cause": "Kurtarma parolası henüz seçilmedi.",
      "action": "Bir kurtarma parolası seçip kurulumu tamamlayın."
    }
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Document every new code**

Append to `docs/troubleshooting.md` (after the `ALC-SYS-0003` section):

```markdown

## ALC-SYS-0004: Request could not be read

**Meaning.** The request body is not valid JSON, contains fields the endpoint does not accept, or has data after the JSON value (HTTP 400).

**Likely cause.** An outdated browser tab after an upgrade, or an API client sending extra or misspelled fields.

**What to do.** Reload the page. API users: compare the request with the schemas in `api/openapi.yaml`; unknown fields are always rejected.

## ALC-SYS-0005: Too many requests

**Meaning.** A rate limit was reached (HTTP 429, retryable). The `Retry-After` header says how many seconds to wait.

**Likely cause.** More than 300 requests per minute for one session or token, more than 60 per minute from one address without signing in, more than 10 sign-in attempts or 5 verification codes per minute from one address.

**What to do.** Wait for the time in `Retry-After`. If many people share one address through a proxy, ask the administrator to set `ALICE_TRUSTED_PROXIES` so Alice sees real client addresses.

## ALC-SYS-0006: Some fields need attention

**Meaning.** One or more fields failed validation (HTTP 422). `details` lists each field with a `validation.*` code.

**Likely cause.** A required field is empty, a value is too long, or a value does not have the expected format (for example a username with spaces).

**What to do.** Correct the fields named in `details` and send the request again.

## ALC-SYS-0007: Request too large

**Meaning.** The request body is larger than 1 MiB (HTTP 413).

**Likely cause.** A very large paste or file was sent to an endpoint that accepts small JSON documents.

**What to do.** Send less data. Certificate and license uploads (later versions) have their own, larger limit.

## ALC-AUTH-0001: Session ended

**Meaning.** The request has no valid session or API token (HTTP 401).

**Likely cause.** The user signed out, was idle for 30 minutes, reached the 12-hour session limit, was disabled, or an administrator ended their sessions; or an API token expired or was revoked.

**What to do.** Sign in again. API users: create a new token.

## ALC-AUTH-0002: Permission denied

**Meaning.** The signed-in user or token is not allowed to perform this action (HTTP 403). The attempt is recorded in the audit log as `authz.denied`.

**Likely cause.** The user's role (Administrator, Operator, Auditor) does not include the action, or an API token is used for an action that needs a browser session.

**What to do.** Ask an administrator for the right role. See `docs/authz-matrix.md` for who may do what.

## ALC-AUTH-0003: Confirmation required

**Meaning.** A sensitive action needs a fresh verification code (HTTP 403). The confirmation lasts 5 minutes.

**Likely cause.** More than 5 minutes passed since the last sign-in or confirmation, and the action changes users, tokens, recovery codes or backup settings.

**What to do.** Enter the 6-digit code from the authenticator app when Alice asks (`POST /api/v1/auth/stepup`), then repeat the action.

## ALC-AUTH-0004: Request blocked (CSRF)

**Meaning.** A state-changing request did not carry the page's security token, or came from another site (HTTP 403).

**Likely cause.** An old browser tab, a browser extension changing requests, or a malicious page trying to act on the user's behalf.

**What to do.** Reload the page. API clients should use an API token (`Authorization: Bearer alice_...`), which needs no CSRF token.

## ALC-AUTH-0005: Incorrect username or password

**Meaning.** Sign-in failed (HTTP 401). The message is the same whether the user exists, the password is wrong, or the account is temporarily blocked, so attackers learn nothing.

**Likely cause.** A typo, a wrong password, a disabled account, or too many failed attempts: after 5 failures Alice waits 1, 2, 4, ... seconds between attempts, after 10 it blocks the account for 15 minutes.

**What to do.** Wait 15 minutes and try again carefully. An administrator can reset the password in Users. Failed attempts are listed in the audit log (`auth.login`, `auth.lockout`).

## ALC-AUTH-0006: Wrong verification code

**Meaning.** The 6-digit code or the recovery code was not accepted (HTTP 401).

**Likely cause.** A typo, a code that was already used (each code works once), or a phone or server clock that is more than 30 seconds off.

**What to do.** Wait for the next code and try again, or use a recovery code. Check that the server and phone clocks are correct (NTP).

## ALC-AUTH-0007: Authenticator already set up

**Meaning.** The account already has an authenticator app enrolled (HTTP 409).

**Likely cause.** The enrollment page was opened twice.

**What to do.** Enter a code from the app. If the device is lost, an administrator can reset it (Users, "Reset MFA"), or the host administrator can run `alice admin reset-mfa <username>`.

## ALC-AUTH-0008: Password too short

**Meaning.** The new password has fewer than 12 characters (HTTP 422, `params.min`).

**Likely cause.** A short password was chosen.

**What to do.** Use at least 12 characters; a short sentence works well.

## ALC-AUTH-0009: Password too long

**Meaning.** The new password has more than 128 characters (HTTP 422, `params.max`).

**Likely cause.** A very long value was pasted.

**What to do.** Use at most 128 characters.

## ALC-AUTH-0010: Password too common

**Meaning.** The password is on Alice's built-in list of 100,000 commonly used passwords (HTTP 422). The check is offline.

**Likely cause.** A well-known password such as `qwerty123456` was chosen.

**What to do.** Choose a different password that is not used anywhere else.

## ALC-AUTH-0011: Current password incorrect

**Meaning.** When changing a password, the current password did not match (HTTP 422).

**Likely cause.** A typo in the current password field.

**What to do.** Enter the current password again. If it is forgotten, ask an administrator to reset it.

## ALC-USR-0001: Username already in use

**Meaning.** Another user already has this username (HTTP 409). Usernames ignore case, so `Admin` and `admin` are the same.

**Likely cause.** The person already has an account, or the name is taken.

**What to do.** Choose a different username, or edit the existing user.

## ALC-USR-0002: Last administrator

**Meaning.** The change would leave no enabled local administrator (HTTP 409). Alice always keeps one as a break-glass account.

**Likely cause.** Disabling or demoting the only remaining administrator, including yourself.

**What to do.** Give the Administrator role to another person first, then repeat the change.

## ALC-TOK-0001: Token access not allowed

**Meaning.** The requested token access exceeds what the owner's role allows (HTTP 422).

**Likely cause.** An Auditor asked for a token with "operate" access.

**What to do.** Create a read-only token.

## ALC-SET-0001: Wrong setup code

**Meaning.** The one-time setup code entered in the setup wizard is not correct (HTTP 401).

**Likely cause.** A typo, or the code was replaced by running `setup-token --reset` again.

**What to do.** Type the code exactly as printed (dashes and case do not matter). To get a new code, run `alice setup-token --reset` on the server (in development: `make setup-token`).

## ALC-SET-0002: Setup temporarily blocked

**Meaning.** Five wrong setup codes were entered from this address; setup is blocked for 15 minutes from it (HTTP 429, `Retry-After`).

**Likely cause.** Repeated typos, or someone guessing the code.

**What to do.** Wait 15 minutes. If you did not make these attempts, check who can reach the server.

## ALC-SET-0003: Setup session ended

**Meaning.** The wizard session is missing or expired (HTTP 401).

**Likely cause.** The wizard was idle for 60 minutes, cookies were cleared, or setup was completed in another browser.

**What to do.** Enter the setup code again. If an administrator already exists, sign in instead.

## ALC-SET-0004: Administrator details missing

**Meaning.** The verification-code step of the wizard was sent before the administrator form (HTTP 409).

**Likely cause.** The page was reloaded between the two steps after the wizard session was renewed.

**What to do.** Go back one step and submit the administrator form again.

## ALC-SET-0005: Recovery passphrase too short

**Meaning.** The recovery passphrase has fewer than 16 characters (HTTP 422, `params.min`).

**Likely cause.** A short passphrase was chosen.

**What to do.** Choose at least 16 characters, for example several unrelated words.

## ALC-SET-0006: Passphrases do not match

**Meaning.** The passphrase and its repetition differ (HTTP 422).

**Likely cause.** A typo in one of the two fields.

**What to do.** Type the same passphrase in both fields.

## ALC-SET-0007: Recovery passphrase already set

**Meaning.** A recovery passphrase was already chosen for this installation (HTTP 409).

**Likely cause.** The step was submitted twice.

**What to do.** Continue with the next step. The passphrase cannot be changed in this version.

## ALC-SET-0008: Setup not finished

**Meaning.** Setup cannot be completed yet (HTTP 409).

**Likely cause.** The recovery passphrase has not been chosen.

**What to do.** Choose the recovery passphrase in the wizard, then finish setup.
```

```markdown
# Authorization matrix

Generated by `make authz-doc` from the route table in `internal/api` and the policies in
`internal/rbac`. Do not edit by hand; CI fails when this file is out of date.

Anonymous: no sign-in needed. Wizard: only with a setup-wizard session. Pending: only between
password and verification code. Token columns: minimum API token access (the token owner's role
must also allow the route). Step-up: a fresh verification code (5 minutes) is required.

| Method | Path | Permission | Anonymous | Admin | Operator | Auditor | API token | Step-up |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/livez` | `public` | yes | yes | yes | yes | yes | no |
| GET | `/healthz` | `public` | yes | yes | yes | yes | yes | no |
| GET | `/api/v1/version` | `read` | no | yes | yes | yes | read | no |
| GET | `/api/v1/i18n/locales` | `public` | yes | yes | yes | yes | yes | no |
```

### Task 14: API: setup and auth endpoints

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/api/dto.go`, `internal/api/setup_handlers.go`, `internal/api/auth_handlers.go`
- Modify: `internal/api/server.go` (`apiRoutes`), `api/openapi.yaml`, `docs/authz-matrix.md` (regenerated)
- Test: `internal/api/setup_handlers_test.go`, `internal/api/auth_handlers_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 13): `route`, `limitLogin`, `limitMFA`, `(s *server) decode`, `(s *server) fail`, `(s *server) meta`, `principalFrom`, `sessionTokenFrom`, `setupTokenFrom`, `setSessionCookie`, `clearSessionCookie`, `setSetupCookie`, `clearSetupCookie`, `writeJSON`, `Deps.Auth` / `Deps.Setup`, test fixtures `testDeps`, `call`, `as`, `decodeErr`, `fakeAuth`, `fakeSetup`, `adminID`, `operatorID`, `auditorID`, `testNow`. Services: `setup.Service` methods (Task 12) and `auth.Service` methods (Task 10) through the `SetupService` / `AuthService` interfaces; `auth.TOTPEnrollment`, `auth.SessionResult`, `auth.SessionInfo`, `setup.Status`, `setup.AdminInput`, `setup.LockedError`.
- Produces:
  - Routes (method, path, permission, limit): `GET /api/v1/setup/status` public; `POST /api/v1/setup/session` public, login; `PUT /api/v1/setup/language` setup; `POST /api/v1/setup/admin/enrollment` setup; `POST /api/v1/setup/admin` setup, mfa; `POST /api/v1/setup/recovery-passphrase` settings.manage; `POST /api/v1/setup/complete` setup.finish; `POST /api/v1/auth/login` public, login; `POST /api/v1/auth/mfa/enroll` mfa_pending; `POST /api/v1/auth/mfa/verify` mfa_pending, mfa; `GET /api/v1/auth/session` session; `POST /api/v1/auth/logout` session; `POST /api/v1/auth/stepup` session, mfa; `POST /api/v1/auth/password` session, login; `POST /api/v1/auth/recovery-codes` session.stepup; `PUT /api/v1/auth/locale` session.
  - Wire JSON: `userDTO` (`id, username, display_name, email, role, locale, disabled, mfa_enrolled, last_login_at|null, created_at`), `sessionDTO` (`user, csrf_token, expires_at, idle_expires_at, stepup_until|null, recovery_codes?`), `enrollmentDTO` (`secret, otpauth_uri, qr_png` base64), `setupStatusDTO` (`admin_exists, recovery_passphrase_set, completed, default_locale`); `POST /setup/session` → `{"csrf_token": ...}`; login → `{"status": "mfa_required"|"mfa_enrollment_required", "csrf_token": CSRFToken(pending session token)}`; recovery codes → `{"recovery_codes": [...]}`; 204 for language, recovery passphrase, logout, locale.
  - Helpers for Task 15: `userJSON(auth.User) userDTO`, `noContent(w)`.
  - Cookie behaviour: login sets the pending session cookie; MFA verify, step-up and password change set a new session cookie; admin creation sets the session cookie and clears `__Host-alice_setup` (Max-Age -1); logout clears the session cookie.
  - OpenAPI schemas `CSRFToken`, `SetupStatus`, `MFAEnrollment`, `User`, `SessionInfo`, `SessionWithCodes` (the only schema carrying `recovery_codes`, used by `/setup/admin` and `/auth/mfa/verify`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the setup endpoint tests**

`internal/api/setup_handlers_test.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/platform/validate"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
	"alice/internal/setup"
)

// cookieNamed returns the Set-Cookie with the given name, or nil.
func cookieNamed(rec *httptest.ResponseRecorder, name string) *http.Cookie {
	for _, c := range rec.Result().Cookies() {
		if c.Name == name {
			return c
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func assertStrictCookie(t *testing.T, c *http.Cookie) {
	t.Helper()
	require.NotNil(t, c)
	assert.True(t, strings.HasPrefix(c.Name, "__Host-"))
	assert.True(t, c.Secure)
	assert.True(t, c.HttpOnly)
	assert.Equal(t, http.SameSiteStrictMode, c.SameSite)
	assert.Equal(t, "/", c.Path)
	assert.Empty(t, c.Domain)
}

// sampleSession is a SessionResult as the services return it.
func sampleSession(token string, codes []string) auth.SessionResult {
	stepUp := testNow.Add(5 * time.Minute)
	return auth.SessionResult{
		Token: token,
		Info: auth.SessionInfo{
			User: auth.User{ID: adminID, Username: "admin", DisplayName: "Ayşe Yılmaz", Email: "ayse@example.com",
				Role: rbac.Admin, Locale: "tr", MFAEnrolled: true, CreatedAt: testNow},
			CSRFToken:     "csrf-" + token,
			ExpiresAt:     testNow.Add(12 * time.Hour),
			IdleExpiresAt: testNow.Add(30 * time.Minute),
			StepUpUntil:   &stepUp,
		},
		RecoveryCodes: codes,
	}
}

func TestSetupStatus(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Setup.(*fakeSetup).StatusFn = func(context.Context) (setup.Status, error) {
		return setup.Status{AdminExists: true, RecoveryPassphraseSet: false, Completed: false, DefaultLocale: "tr"}, nil
	}
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/setup/status", "", "")
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code)
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"admin_exists":true,"recovery_passphrase_set":false,"completed":false,"default_locale":"tr"}`, rec.Body.String())
}

func TestSetupSessionSetsStrictCookieAndReturnsCSRFToken(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	var gotCode string
	d.Setup.(*fakeSetup).StartSessionFn = func(_ context.Context, code string, m auth.Meta) (string, error) {
		gotCode = code
		assert.Equal(t, "192.0.2.1", m.IP.String())
		return "wizard-token", nil
	}
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/session", "", `{"code":"K7QX2M-9TPD4R-WB5H8N-3VZC6A"}`)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
	assert.Equal(t, "K7QX2M-9TPD4R-WB5H8N-3VZC6A", gotCode)
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"csrf_token":"csrf-wizard-token"}`, rec.Body.String())
	c := cookieNamed(rec, setupCookie)
	assertStrictCookie(t, c)
	assert.Equal(t, "wizard-token", c.Value)
}

func TestSetupSessionErrors(t *testing.T) {
	cases := []struct {
		err    error
		status int
		code   string
	}{
		{setup.ErrCodeInvalid, http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-SET-0001"},
		{setup.ErrAlreadySetUp, http.StatusNotFound, "ALC-SYS-0001"},
		{&setup.LockedError{RetryAfter: 15 * time.Minute}, http.StatusTooManyRequests, "ALC-SET-0002"},
	}
	for _, tc := range cases {
		d := testDeps(t, nil)
		d.Setup.(*fakeSetup).StartSessionFn = func(context.Context, string, auth.Meta) (string, error) { return "", tc.err }
		rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/session", "", `{"code":"WRONG"}`)
		assert.Equal(t, tc.status, rec.Code, tc.code)
		assert.Equal(t, tc.code, decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
		assert.Nil(t, cookieNamed(rec, setupCookie), tc.code)
		if tc.status == http.StatusTooManyRequests {
			assert.Equal(t, "900", rec.Header().Get("Retry-After"))
		}
	}
}

func TestSetupLanguageUsesTheWizardSession(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	var token, lang string
	d.Setup.(*fakeSetup).SetLanguageFn = func(_ context.Context, tok, l string) error {
		token, lang = tok, l
		return nil
	}
	h := NewHandler(d)
	rec := call(h, http.MethodPut, "/api/v1/setup/language", "setup", `{"language":"tr"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNoContent, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "setup-ok", token)
	assert.Equal(t, "tr", lang)

	rec = call(h, http.MethodPut, "/api/v1/setup/language", "", `{"language":"tr"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnauthorized, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-SET-0003", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
}

func TestSetupEnrollmentReturnsSecretAndQR(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	var got setup.AdminInput
	d.Setup.(*fakeSetup).BeginAdminEnrollmentFn = func(_ context.Context, _ string, in setup.AdminInput) (auth.TOTPEnrollment, error) {
		got = in
		return auth.TOTPEnrollment{Secret: "JBSWY3DPEHPK3PXP", URI: "otpauth://totp/Alice:admin?secret=JBSWY3DPEHPK3PXP", QRPNG: []byte{0x89, 'P', 'N', 'G'}}, nil
	}
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/admin/enrollment", "setup",
		`{"username":"admin","display_name":"Ayşe Yılmaz","email":"ayse@example.com","password":"correct horse battery"}`)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
	assert.Equal(t, setup.AdminInput{Username: "admin", DisplayName: "Ayşe Yılmaz", Email: "ayse@example.com", Password: "correct horse battery"}, got)
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"secret":"JBSWY3DPEHPK3PXP","otpauth_uri":"otpauth://totp/Alice:admin?secret=JBSWY3DPEHPK3PXP","qr_png":"iVBORw=="}`, rec.Body.String())
}

func TestSetupEnrollmentValidationErrorsCarryDetails(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Setup.(*fakeSetup).BeginAdminEnrollmentFn = func(context.Context, string, setup.AdminInput) (auth.TOTPEnrollment, error) {
		var v validate.Errors
		v.Add("username", validate.Pattern, nil)
		return auth.TOTPEnrollment{}, v
	}
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/admin/enrollment", "setup",
		`{"username":"İsmail","display_name":"x","email":"","password":"correct horse battery"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, rec.Code)
	assert.Contains(t, rec.Body.String(), `"details":[{"field":"username","code":"validation.pattern"}]`)
}

func TestSetupCreateAdminSignsInAndClearsSetupCookie(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Setup.(*fakeSetup).CreateAdminFn = func(_ context.Context, tok, code string, _ auth.Meta) (auth.SessionResult, error) {
		assert.Equal(t, "setup-ok", tok)
		assert.Equal(t, "123456", code)
		return sampleSession("full-session", []string{"AAAA-BBBB-CCCC-DDDD"}), nil
	}
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/admin", "setup", `{"code":"123456"}`)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code, rec.Body.String())

	session := cookieNamed(rec, sessionCookie)
	assertStrictCookie(t, session)
	assert.Equal(t, "full-session", session.Value)
	cleared := cookieNamed(rec, setupCookie)
	assertStrictCookie(t, cleared)
	assert.Equal(t, -1, cleared.MaxAge)

	var body map[string]any
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &body))
	assert.Equal(t, "csrf-full-session", body["csrf_token"])
	assert.Equal(t, []any{"AAAA-BBBB-CCCC-DDDD"}, body["recovery_codes"])
	user := body["user"].(map[string]any)
	assert.Equal(t, "Ayşe Yılmaz", user["display_name"])
	assert.Equal(t, "admin", user["role"])
	assert.Nil(t, user["last_login_at"])
	assert.NotContains(t, rec.Body.String(), `"full-session"`, "the session token itself is never in the body")
}

func TestSetupCreateAdminWrongCodeKeepsSetupCookie(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Setup.(*fakeSetup).CreateAdminFn = func(context.Context, string, string, auth.Meta) (auth.SessionResult, error) {
		return auth.SessionResult{}, auth.ErrMFAInvalid
	}
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/admin", "setup", `{"code":"000000"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnauthorized, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-AUTH-0006", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
	assert.Nil(t, cookieNamed(rec, setupCookie))
	assert.Nil(t, cookieNamed(rec, sessionCookie))
}

func TestSetupRecoveryPassphrase(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	var pass, confirm string
	var who auth.Principal
	d.Setup.(*fakeSetup).SetRecoveryPassphraseFn = func(_ context.Context, p auth.Principal, _ auth.Meta, a, b string) error {
		who, pass, confirm = p, a, b
		if a != b {
			return setup.ErrPassphraseMismatch
		}
		return nil
	}
	h := NewHandler(d)
	rec := call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/recovery-passphrase", "admin-stepup",
		`{"passphrase":"kırmızı elma yeşil armut","passphrase_confirm":"kırmızı elma yeşil armut"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNoContent, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "kırmızı elma yeşil armut", pass)
	assert.Equal(t, pass, confirm)
	assert.Equal(t, adminID, who.UserID)

	rec = call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/recovery-passphrase", "admin-stepup",
		`{"passphrase":"kırmızı elma yeşil armut","passphrase_confirm":"different words entirely"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-SET-0006", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)

	rec = call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/recovery-passphrase", "admin", `{"passphrase":"a","passphrase_confirm":"a"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-AUTH-0003", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code, "needs a fresh verification code")
}

func TestSetupComplete(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Setup.(*fakeSetup).CompleteFn = func(context.Context, auth.Principal, auth.Meta) (setup.Status, error) {
		return setup.Status{AdminExists: true, RecoveryPassphraseSet: true, Completed: true, DefaultLocale: "en"}, nil
	}
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/complete", "admin", "")
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code)
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"admin_exists":true,"recovery_passphrase_set":true,"completed":true,"default_locale":"en"}`, rec.Body.String())

	d.Setup.(*fakeSetup).CompleteFn = func(context.Context, auth.Principal, auth.Meta) (setup.Status, error) {
		return setup.Status{}, setup.ErrIncomplete
	}
	rec = call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/complete", "admin", "")
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusConflict, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-SET-0008", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
}
```

```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

func TestLoginSetsPendingCookie(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	var user, pass string
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).LoginFn = func(_ context.Context, u, p string, _ auth.Meta) (auth.LoginResult, error) {
		user, pass = u, p
		return auth.LoginResult{Status: "mfa_required", SessionToken: "pending-token"}, nil
	}
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", "", `{"username":"Admin","password":"correct horse battery"}`)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"status":"mfa_required","csrf_token":"csrf-pending-token"}`, rec.Body.String())
	assert.Equal(t, "Admin", user)
	assert.Equal(t, "correct horse battery", pass)
	c := cookieNamed(rec, sessionCookie)
	assertStrictCookie(t, c)
	assert.Equal(t, "pending-token", c.Value)
}

func TestLoginFailureIsGenericAndSetsNoCookie(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).LoginFn = func(context.Context, string, string, auth.Meta) (auth.LoginResult, error) {
		return auth.LoginResult{}, auth.ErrInvalidCredentials
	}
	req := `{"username":"nobody","password":"wrong password here"}`
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", "", req)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnauthorized, rec.Code)
	b := decodeErr(t, rec)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-AUTH-0005", b.Error.Code)
	assert.Contains(t, b.Error.Message, "Incorrect username or password.")
	assert.NotContains(t, rec.Body.String(), "wrong password here")
	assert.Nil(t, cookieNamed(rec, sessionCookie))
}

func TestLoginRejectsUnknownFieldsAndHugeBodies(t *testing.T) {
	h := NewHandler(testDeps(t, nil))
	rec := call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", "", `{"username":"a","password":"b","role":"admin"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusBadRequest, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-SYS-0004", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)

	rec = call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", "", `{"username":"a","password":"`+strings.Repeat("x", maxBody)+`"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-SYS-0007", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
}

func TestEnrollMFAForPendingSession(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).EnrollMFAFn = func(_ context.Context, p auth.Principal, _ auth.Meta) (auth.TOTPEnrollment, error) {
		assert.Equal(t, rbac.PendingSession, p.Kind)
		return auth.TOTPEnrollment{Secret: "S", URI: "otpauth://totp/Alice:operator?secret=S", QRPNG: []byte("png")}, nil
	}
	h := NewHandler(d)
	rec := call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/enroll", "pending", "")
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"secret":"S","otpauth_uri":"otpauth://totp/Alice:operator?secret=S","qr_png":"cG5n"}`, rec.Body.String())

	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).EnrollMFAFn = func(context.Context, auth.Principal, auth.Meta) (auth.TOTPEnrollment, error) {
		return auth.TOTPEnrollment{}, auth.ErrMFAAlreadyEnrolled
	}
	rec = call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/enroll", "pending", "")
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusConflict, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-AUTH-0007", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
}

func TestVerifyMFARotatesCookieAndReturnsRecoveryCodes(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	var in auth.MFAInput
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).VerifyMFAFn = func(_ context.Context, p auth.Principal, got auth.MFAInput, _ auth.Meta) (auth.SessionResult, error) {
		in = got
		assert.Equal(t, rbac.PendingSession, p.Kind)
		return sampleSession("new-full", []string{"AAAA-BBBB-CCCC-DDDD"}), nil
	}
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", "pending", `{"code":"123456"}`)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
	assert.Equal(t, auth.MFAInput{Code: "123456"}, in)
	c := cookieNamed(rec, sessionCookie)
	assertStrictCookie(t, c)
	assert.Equal(t, "new-full", c.Value)
	var body map[string]any
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &body))
	assert.Equal(t, "csrf-new-full", body["csrf_token"])
	assert.Equal(t, []any{"AAAA-BBBB-CCCC-DDDD"}, body["recovery_codes"])
}

func TestVerifyMFAWithRecoveryCodeAndErrors(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	var in auth.MFAInput
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).VerifyMFAFn = func(_ context.Context, _ auth.Principal, got auth.MFAInput, _ auth.Meta) (auth.SessionResult, error) {
		in = got
		return auth.SessionResult{}, auth.ErrMFAInvalid
	}
	h := NewHandler(d)
	rec := call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", "pending", `{"recovery_code":"aaaa-bbbb-cccc-dddd"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnauthorized, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-AUTH-0006", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "aaaa-bbbb-cccc-dddd", in.RecoveryCode)

	rec = call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", "pending", `{}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, rec.Code)
	assert.Contains(t, rec.Body.String(), `"field":"code","code":"validation.required"`)

	rec = call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", "admin", `{"code":"123456"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusForbidden, rec.Code, "a full session cannot use the MFA step")
}

func TestSessionReturnsUserAndCSRFToken(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).SessionInfoFn = func(_ context.Context, p auth.Principal, tok string) (auth.SessionInfo, error) {
		assert.Equal(t, "operator", tok)
		assert.Equal(t, operatorID, p.UserID)
		info := sampleSession(tok, nil).Info
		info.StepUpUntil = nil
		return info, nil
	}
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", "operator", "")
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
	var body map[string]any
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &body))
	assert.Equal(t, "csrf-operator", body["csrf_token"])
	assert.Nil(t, body["stepup_until"])
	assert.NotContains(t, body, "recovery_codes")
	assert.Equal(t, "2026-09-30T12:30:00Z", body["idle_expires_at"])

	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnauthorized, call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", "expired", "").Code)
}

func TestLogoutClearsCookie(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	var loggedOut uuid.UUID
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).LogoutFn = func(_ context.Context, p auth.Principal, _ auth.Meta) error {
		loggedOut = p.UserID
		return nil
	}
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/logout", "auditor", "")
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNoContent, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, auditorID, loggedOut)
	c := cookieNamed(rec, sessionCookie)
	assertStrictCookie(t, c)
	assert.Equal(t, -1, c.MaxAge)
}

func TestStepUpRotatesCookie(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).StepUpFn = func(_ context.Context, _ auth.Principal, code string, _ auth.Meta) (auth.SessionResult, error) {
		if code != "654321" {
			return auth.SessionResult{}, auth.ErrMFAInvalid
		}
		return sampleSession("rotated", nil), nil
	}
	h := NewHandler(d)
	rec := call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/stepup", "admin", `{"code":"654321"}`)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
	assert.Equal(t, "rotated", cookieNamed(rec, sessionCookie).Value)
	assert.Contains(t, rec.Body.String(), `"csrf_token":"csrf-rotated"`)

	rec = call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/stepup", "admin", `{"code":"000000"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnauthorized, rec.Code)
	assert.Nil(t, cookieNamed(rec, sessionCookie))
}

func TestChangePassword(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).ChangePasswordFn = func(_ context.Context, _ auth.Principal, cur, next string, _ auth.Meta) (auth.SessionResult, error) {
		if cur != "old password 123" {
			return auth.SessionResult{}, auth.ErrCurrentPasswordInvalid
		}
		if next == "qwerty123456" {
			return auth.SessionResult{}, auth.ErrPasswordBreached
		}
		return sampleSession("after-change", nil), nil
	}
	h := NewHandler(d)
	rec := call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/password", "operator", `{"current_password":"old password 123","new_password":"a much better passphrase"}`)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "after-change", cookieNamed(rec, sessionCookie).Value)

	rec = call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/password", "operator", `{"current_password":"nope","new_password":"a much better passphrase"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-AUTH-0011", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
	rec = call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/password", "operator", `{"current_password":"old password 123","new_password":"qwerty123456"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-AUTH-0010", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
}

func TestRegenerateRecoveryCodesNeedsStepUp(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).RegenerateRecoveryCodesFn = func(context.Context, auth.Principal, auth.Meta) ([]string, error) {
		return []string{"1111-2222-3333-4444"}, nil
	}
	h := NewHandler(d)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-AUTH-0003", decodeErr(t, call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/recovery-codes", "auditor", "")).Error.Code)
	rec := call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/recovery-codes", "auditor-stepup", "")
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code)
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"recovery_codes":["1111-2222-3333-4444"]}`, rec.Body.String())
}

func TestSetLocale(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	var got string
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).SetLocaleFn = func(_ context.Context, _ auth.Principal, l string) error {
		got = l
		return nil
	}
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodPut, "/api/v1/auth/locale", "operator", `{"locale":"tr"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNoContent, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "tr", got)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the wire DTOs**

`internal/api/dto.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"encoding/base64"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/setup"
)

// userDTO is the wire form of auth.User.
type userDTO struct {
	ID          string     `json:"id"`
	Username    string     `json:"username"`
	DisplayName string     `json:"display_name"`
	Email       string     `json:"email"`
	Role        string     `json:"role"`
	Locale      string     `json:"locale"`
	Disabled    bool       `json:"disabled"`
	MFAEnrolled bool       `json:"mfa_enrolled"`
	LastLoginAt *time.Time `json:"last_login_at"`
	CreatedAt   time.Time  `json:"created_at"`
}

func userJSON(u auth.User) userDTO {
	var last *time.Time
	if u.LastLoginAt != nil {
		t := u.LastLoginAt.UTC()
		last = &t
	}
	return userDTO{
		ID:          u.ID.String(),
		Username:    u.Username,
		DisplayName: u.DisplayName,
		Email:       u.Email,
		Role:        string(u.Role),
		Locale:      u.Locale,
		Disabled:    u.Disabled,
		MFAEnrolled: u.MFAEnrolled,
		LastLoginAt: last,
		CreatedAt:   u.CreatedAt.UTC(),
	}
}

// sessionDTO is the wire form of auth.SessionInfo; RecoveryCodes is present
// only right after MFA enrollment or admin creation.
type sessionDTO struct {
	User          userDTO    `json:"user"`
	CSRFToken     string     `json:"csrf_token"`
	ExpiresAt     time.Time  `json:"expires_at"`
	IdleExpiresAt time.Time  `json:"idle_expires_at"`
	StepUpUntil   *time.Time `json:"stepup_until"`
	RecoveryCodes []string   `json:"recovery_codes,omitempty"`
}

func sessionJSON(si auth.SessionInfo, recoveryCodes []string) sessionDTO {
	var stepUp *time.Time
	if si.StepUpUntil != nil {
		t := si.StepUpUntil.UTC()
		stepUp = &t
	}
	return sessionDTO{
		User:          userJSON(si.User),
		CSRFToken:     si.CSRFToken,
		ExpiresAt:     si.ExpiresAt.UTC(),
		IdleExpiresAt: si.IdleExpiresAt.UTC(),
		StepUpUntil:   stepUp,
		RecoveryCodes: recoveryCodes,
	}
}

// enrollmentDTO carries a new TOTP secret. It is shown once (SEC-007).
type enrollmentDTO struct {
	Secret     string `json:"secret"`
	OTPAuthURI string `json:"otpauth_uri"`
	QRPNG      string `json:"qr_png"`
}

func enrollmentJSON(e auth.TOTPEnrollment) enrollmentDTO {
	return enrollmentDTO{Secret: e.Secret, OTPAuthURI: e.URI, QRPNG: base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(e.QRPNG)}
}

type setupStatusDTO struct {
	AdminExists           bool   `json:"admin_exists"`
	RecoveryPassphraseSet bool   `json:"recovery_passphrase_set"`
	Completed             bool   `json:"completed"`
	DefaultLocale         string `json:"default_locale"`
}

func setupStatusJSON(st setup.Status) setupStatusDTO {
	return setupStatusDTO{
		AdminExists:           st.AdminExists,
		RecoveryPassphraseSet: st.RecoveryPassphraseSet,
		Completed:             st.Completed,
		DefaultLocale:         st.DefaultLocale,
	}
}

// noContent answers 204 without a body.
func noContent(w http.ResponseWriter) {
	w.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "no-store")
	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the setup handlers**

`internal/api/setup_handlers.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"errors"
	"net/http"

	"alice/internal/rbac"
	"alice/internal/setup"
)

// setupRoutes serve the first-run wizard (spec §18.5). Until an administrator
// exists only the setup session can use them; afterwards the session routes
// answer 404 and the remaining steps need the signed-in administrator.
func (s *server) setupRoutes() []route {
	return []route{
		{method: http.MethodGet, pattern: "/api/v1/setup/status", perm: rbac.Public, handler: s.setupStatus},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/setup/session", perm: rbac.Public, limit: limitLogin, handler: s.setupStartSession},
		{method: http.MethodPut, pattern: "/api/v1/setup/language", perm: rbac.Setup, handler: s.setupLanguage},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/setup/admin/enrollment", perm: rbac.Setup, handler: s.setupEnrollment},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/setup/admin", perm: rbac.Setup, limit: limitMFA, handler: s.setupCreateAdmin},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/setup/recovery-passphrase", perm: rbac.SettingsManage, handler: s.setupRecoveryPassphrase},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/setup/complete", perm: rbac.SetupFinish, handler: s.setupComplete},
	}
}

func (s *server) setupStatus(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	st, err := s.deps.Setup.Status(r.Context())
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, setupStatusJSON(st))
}

func (s *server) setupStartSession(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req struct {
		Code string `json:"code"`
	}
	if !s.decode(w, r, &req) {
		return
	}
	m := s.meta(r)
	token, err := s.deps.Setup.StartSession(r.Context(), req.Code, m)
	if err != nil {
		var locked *setup.LockedError
		if errors.Is(err, setup.ErrCodeInvalid) || errors.As(err, &locked) {
			// Spec §18.5: every attempt is logged. The code itself never is.
			s.deps.Log.Warn("setup code rejected", "request_id", m.RequestID, "ip", m.IP.String())
		}
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	s.deps.Log.Info("setup session started", "request_id", m.RequestID, "ip", m.IP.String())
	setSetupCookie(w, token)
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{"csrf_token": s.deps.Auth.CSRFToken(token)})
}

func (s *server) setupLanguage(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req struct {
		Language string `json:"language"`
	}
	if !s.decode(w, r, &req) {
		return
	}
	if err := s.deps.Setup.SetLanguage(r.Context(), setupTokenFrom(r), req.Language); err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	noContent(w)
}

func (s *server) setupEnrollment(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req struct {
		Username    string `json:"username"`
		DisplayName string `json:"display_name"`
		Email       string `json:"email"`
		Password    string `json:"password"`
	}
	if !s.decode(w, r, &req) {
		return
	}
	e, err := s.deps.Setup.BeginAdminEnrollment(r.Context(), setupTokenFrom(r), setup.AdminInput{
		Username: req.Username, DisplayName: req.DisplayName, Email: req.Email, Password: req.Password,
	})
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, enrollmentJSON(e))
}

func (s *server) setupCreateAdmin(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req struct {
		Code string `json:"code"`
	}
	if !s.decode(w, r, &req) {
		return
	}
	res, err := s.deps.Setup.CreateAdmin(r.Context(), setupTokenFrom(r), req.Code, s.meta(r))
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	setSessionCookie(w, res.Token)
	clearSetupCookie(w)
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, sessionJSON(res.Info, res.RecoveryCodes))
}

func (s *server) setupRecoveryPassphrase(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req struct {
		Passphrase        string `json:"passphrase"`
		PassphraseConfirm string `json:"passphrase_confirm"`
	}
	if !s.decode(w, r, &req) {
		return
	}
	if err := s.deps.Setup.SetRecoveryPassphrase(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()), s.meta(r), req.Passphrase, req.PassphraseConfirm); err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	noContent(w)
}

func (s *server) setupComplete(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	st, err := s.deps.Setup.Complete(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()), s.meta(r))
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, setupStatusJSON(st))
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the auth handlers**

`internal/api/auth_handlers.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"net/http"

	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/platform/validate"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

// authRoutes implement sign-in, MFA and the signed-in user's own account
// (spec §9.2, SEC-006..SEC-009, SEC-018, SEC-019).
func (s *server) authRoutes() []route {
	return []route{
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/auth/login", perm: rbac.Public, limit: limitLogin, handler: s.authLogin},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/auth/mfa/enroll", perm: rbac.MFAPending, handler: s.authEnroll},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", perm: rbac.MFAPending, limit: limitMFA, handler: s.authVerify},
		{method: http.MethodGet, pattern: "/api/v1/auth/session", perm: rbac.Session, handler: s.authSession},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/auth/logout", perm: rbac.Session, handler: s.authLogout},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/auth/stepup", perm: rbac.Session, limit: limitMFA, handler: s.authStepUp},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/auth/password", perm: rbac.Session, limit: limitLogin, handler: s.authPassword},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/auth/recovery-codes", perm: rbac.SessionStepUp, handler: s.authRecoveryCodes},
		{method: http.MethodPut, pattern: "/api/v1/auth/locale", perm: rbac.Session, handler: s.authLocale},
	}
}

func (s *server) authLogin(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req struct {
		Username string `json:"username"`
		Password string `json:"password"`
	}
	if !s.decode(w, r, &req) {
		return
	}
	res, err := s.deps.Auth.Login(r.Context(), req.Username, req.Password, s.meta(r))
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	setSessionCookie(w, res.SessionToken) // pending session: only MFA routes accept it
	// The MFA enroll/verify calls that follow are cookie-authenticated POSTs, so
	// the client needs the CSRF token bound to the pending session.
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{"status": res.Status, "csrf_token": s.deps.Auth.CSRFToken(res.SessionToken)})
}

func (s *server) authEnroll(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	e, err := s.deps.Auth.EnrollMFA(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()), s.meta(r))
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, enrollmentJSON(e))
}

func (s *server) authVerify(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req struct {
		Code         string `json:"code"`
		RecoveryCode string `json:"recovery_code"`
	}
	if !s.decode(w, r, &req) {
		return
	}
	if req.Code == "" && req.RecoveryCode == "" {
		var v validate.Errors
		v.Add("code", validate.Required, nil)
		s.fail(w, r, v)
		return
	}
	res, err := s.deps.Auth.VerifyMFA(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()), auth.MFAInput{Code: req.Code, RecoveryCode: req.RecoveryCode}, s.meta(r))
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	setSessionCookie(w, res.Token) // new session ID after MFA (fixation defence)
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, sessionJSON(res.Info, res.RecoveryCodes))
}

func (s *server) authSession(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	si, err := s.deps.Auth.SessionInfo(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()), sessionTokenFrom(r))
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, sessionJSON(si, nil))
}

func (s *server) authLogout(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if err := s.deps.Auth.Logout(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()), s.meta(r)); err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	clearSessionCookie(w)
	noContent(w)
}

func (s *server) authStepUp(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req struct {
		Code string `json:"code"`
	}
	if !s.decode(w, r, &req) {
		return
	}
	res, err := s.deps.Auth.StepUp(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()), req.Code, s.meta(r))
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	setSessionCookie(w, res.Token) // session ID is regenerated on step-up (SEC-019)
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, sessionJSON(res.Info, nil))
}

func (s *server) authPassword(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req struct {
		CurrentPassword string `json:"current_password"`
		NewPassword     string `json:"new_password"`
	}
	if !s.decode(w, r, &req) {
		return
	}
	res, err := s.deps.Auth.ChangePassword(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()), req.CurrentPassword, req.NewPassword, s.meta(r))
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	setSessionCookie(w, res.Token) // every other session was revoked
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, sessionJSON(res.Info, nil))
}

func (s *server) authRecoveryCodes(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	codes, err := s.deps.Auth.RegenerateRecoveryCodes(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()), s.meta(r))
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string][]string{"recovery_codes": codes})
}

func (s *server) authLocale(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req struct {
		Locale string `json:"locale"`
	}
	if !s.decode(w, r, &req) {
		return
	}
	if err := s.deps.Auth.SetLocale(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()), req.Locale); err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	noContent(w)
}
```

```yaml
        "422": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
  /api/v1/auth/session:
    get:
      operationId: getSession

      x-permission: session
      responses:

        "401": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
  /api/v1/auth/logout:
    post:

  /api/v1/auth/stepup:
    post:
      operationId: stepUp
      summary: Confirm a TOTP code to allow sensitive actions for 5 minutes (session ID is regenerated)
      x-permission: session
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:

              required: [code]

      responses:
        "200":
          description: Step-up active
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/SessionInfo" }
        "401": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
  /api/v1/auth/password:
    post:
      operationId: changePassword
      summary: Change the own password; ends every other session
      x-permission: session
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              additionalProperties: false
              required: [current_password, new_password]
              properties:
                current_password: { type: string, maxLength: 1024 }
                new_password: { type: string, maxLength: 1024 }
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Password changed; new session cookie
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/SessionInfo" }
        "422": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
  /api/v1/auth/recovery-codes:
    post:
      operationId: regenerateRecoveryCodes
      summary: Replace the own recovery codes (the old ones stop working)
      x-permission: session.stepup

          description: New recovery codes, shown once
          content:

        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
  /api/v1/auth/locale:
    put:
      operationId: setMyLocale
      summary: Save the own UI language
      x-permission: session
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              additionalProperties: false
              required: [locale]
              properties:
                locale: { type: string, enum: [en, tr] }
      responses:
        "204": { description: Saved }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
```

and append these schemas at the end of `components.schemas` (the end of the file):

```yaml
    CSRFToken:
      type: object
      required: [csrf_token]
      properties:
        csrf_token: { type: string, description: Send as X-CSRF-Token on state-changing requests }
    SetupStatus:
      type: object
      required: [admin_exists, recovery_passphrase_set, completed, default_locale]
      properties:
        admin_exists: { type: boolean }
        recovery_passphrase_set: { type: boolean }
        completed: { type: boolean }
        default_locale: { type: string, examples: [tr] }
    MFAEnrollment:
      type: object
      required: [secret, otpauth_uri, qr_png]
      properties:
        secret: { type: string, description: Base32 TOTP secret }
        otpauth_uri: { type: string }
        qr_png: { type: string, contentEncoding: base64, description: PNG image of otpauth_uri }
    User:
      type: object
      required: [id, username, display_name, email, role, locale, disabled, mfa_enrolled, last_login_at, created_at]
      properties:
        id: { type: string, format: uuid }
        username: { type: string }
        display_name: { type: string }
        email: { type: string }
        role: { type: string, enum: [admin, operator, auditor] }
        locale: { type: string }
        disabled: { type: boolean }
        mfa_enrolled: { type: boolean }
        last_login_at: { type: [string, "null"], format: date-time }
        created_at: { type: string, format: date-time }
    SessionInfo:
      type: object
      required: [user, csrf_token, expires_at, idle_expires_at, stepup_until]
      properties:
        user: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/User" }
        csrf_token: { type: string }
        expires_at: { type: string, format: date-time }
        idle_expires_at: { type: string, format: date-time }
        stepup_until: { type: [string, "null"], format: date-time }
    SessionWithCodes:
      description: SessionInfo plus the one-time recovery codes (only from administrator creation and MFA verification)
      allOf:
        - { $ref: "#/components/schemas/SessionInfo" }
        - type: object
          properties:
            recovery_codes:
              type: array
              items: { type: string }
              description: Present only right after MFA enrollment or administrator creation
```
