### Task 15: API: users, tokens and audit endpoints

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/api/users_handlers.go`, `internal/api/tokens_handlers.go`, `internal/api/audit_handlers.go`
- Modify: `internal/api/server.go` (`apiRoutes`), `api/openapi.yaml`, `docs/authz-matrix.md` (regenerated)
- Test: `internal/api/users_handlers_test.go`, `internal/api/tokens_handlers_test.go`, `internal/api/audit_handlers_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 13/14): `route`, `(s *server) decode|fail|meta|writeError`, `ErrNotFound`, `principalFrom`, `pathUUID`, `writeJSON`, `noContent`, `userJSON`, `userDTO`, fixtures (`testDeps`, `call`, `decodeErr`, `fakeAuth`, `fakeAudit`, `adminID`, `operatorID`, `auditorID`, `testNow`). Services: `auth.Service` user/token methods (Task 11), `audit.Service` `List`, `Iterate`, `Verify`, `Write` (Task 6) through `AuthService` / `AuditService`; `auth.NewUser`, `auth.UserUpdate`, `auth.NewToken`, `auth.Token`, `audit.Filter`, `audit.Entry`, `audit.Report`, `audit.Problem*`, `rbac.ParseRole`, `rbac.ParseScope`, `rbac.Roles`, `validate.*`.
- Produces:
  - Routes: `GET /api/v1/users` users.read; `POST /api/v1/users` users.manage; `GET /api/v1/users/{id}` users.read; `PATCH /api/v1/users/{id}` users.manage; `POST /api/v1/users/{id}/actions/reset-mfa|reset-password|revoke-sessions` users.manage; `GET /api/v1/tokens` session (optional `?user_id=`); `POST /api/v1/tokens` session.stepup; `DELETE /api/v1/tokens/{id}` session; `GET /api/v1/audit` audit.read (`limit` 1..200 default 50, `cursor` = decimal seq, `action` prefix, `actor`, `result`); `GET /api/v1/audit/export?format=csv|json` audit.read; `POST /api/v1/audit/verify` audit.read.
  - Wire JSON: users `{"items":[User]}` (empty list is `[]`), create → 201 User; token `{id, user_id, name, prefix, scope, expires_at, last_used_at|null, created_at}`, list `{"items":[...]}`, create → 201 `{"token": "<secret, once>", "item": Token}`; audit entry `{seq, at, actor_type, actor_id|null, actor_name, ip|null, action, object_type, object_id, result, details, request_id, hash(hex)}`, list `{"items":[...], "next_cursor": "<seq>"|null}`; report `{intact, checked, head_seq, head_hash, first_bad_seq|null, problem|null}`.
  - Export: the chain is verified first (`Audit.Verify`, which records `audit.verified`), then `audit.exported` (details `format`, `intact`) is written; only then are entries streamed. Headers `X-Alice-Audit-Intact: true|false`, `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="alice-audit-<UTC date>.<csv|json>"`, `Cache-Control: no-store`. CSV: UTF-8 BOM, header row `seq,at,actor_type,actor_id,actor_name,ip,action,object_type,object_id,result,details,request_id,hash`, cells beginning with `= + - @ TAB CR` prefixed with `'`. JSON: `{"verification": Report, "entries": [...]}`. A failure after streaming started aborts the connection (`http.ErrAbortHandler`).
  - Invalid role/scope values → 422 `validation.failed` with `validation.one_of` details (`values: "admin, operator, auditor"` / `"read, operate"`); malformed `{id}` → 404.
  - OpenAPI: `components.parameters.ID`, schemas `APIToken`, `AuditEntry`, `AuditReport`.

`internal/api/users_handlers_test.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"net/http"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

func sampleUser(id uuid.UUID, name string, role rbac.Role) auth.User {
	last := testNow.Add(-time.Hour)
	return auth.User{ID: id, Username: name, DisplayName: "Çağla " + name, Email: name + "@example.com",
		Role: role, Locale: "tr", MFAEnrolled: true, LastLoginAt: &last, CreatedAt: testNow.Add(-24 * time.Hour)}
}

func TestListUsers(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).ListUsersFn = func(context.Context) ([]auth.User, error) {
		return []auth.User{sampleUser(adminID, "admin", rbac.Admin), sampleUser(operatorID, "operator", rbac.Operator)}, nil
	}
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/users", "admin", "")
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code)
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"items":[
	  {"id":"0190f5a0-0000-7000-8000-000000000001","username":"admin","display_name":"Çağla admin","email":"admin@example.com","role":"admin","locale":"tr","disabled":false,"mfa_enrolled":true,"last_login_at":"2026-09-30T11:00:00Z","created_at":"2026-09-29T12:00:00Z"},
	  {"id":"0190f5a0-0000-7000-8000-000000000002","username":"operator","display_name":"Çağla operator","email":"operator@example.com","role":"operator","locale":"tr","disabled":false,"mfa_enrolled":true,"last_login_at":"2026-09-30T11:00:00Z","created_at":"2026-09-29T12:00:00Z"}]}`, rec.Body.String())
}

func TestListUsersIsEmptyArrayNotNull(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).ListUsersFn = func(context.Context) ([]auth.User, error) { return nil, nil }
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/users", "admin", "")
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"items":[]}`, rec.Body.String())
}

func TestCreateUser(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	var got auth.NewUser
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).CreateUserFn = func(_ context.Context, p auth.Principal, _ auth.Meta, in auth.NewUser) (auth.User, error) {
		got = in
		assert.Equal(t, adminID, p.UserID)
		return sampleUser(auditorID, in.Username, in.Role), nil
	}
	h := NewHandler(d)
	rec := call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/users", "admin-stepup",
		`{"username":"denetci","display_name":"Ömer Işık","email":"","role":"auditor","password":"uzun ve güvenli parola"}`)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusCreated, rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
	assert.Equal(t, auth.NewUser{Username: "denetci", DisplayName: "Ömer Işık", Role: rbac.Auditor, Password: "uzun ve güvenli parola"}, got)
	assert.Contains(t, rec.Body.String(), `"role":"auditor"`)
	assert.NotContains(t, rec.Body.String(), "uzun ve güvenli parola")

	rec = call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/users", "admin-stepup",
		`{"username":"x1","display_name":"X","email":"","role":"root","password":"uzun ve güvenli parola"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, rec.Code)
	assert.Contains(t, rec.Body.String(), `{"field":"role","code":"validation.one_of","params":{"values":"admin, operator, auditor"}}`)
}

func TestCreateUserMapsServiceErrors(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).CreateUserFn = func(context.Context, auth.Principal, auth.Meta, auth.NewUser) (auth.User, error) {
		return auth.User{}, auth.ErrUsernameTaken
	}
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/users", "admin-stepup",
		`{"username":"ADMIN","display_name":"Dup","email":"","role":"operator","password":"uzun ve güvenli parola"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusConflict, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-USR-0001", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
}

func TestGetUser(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).GetUserFn = func(_ context.Context, id uuid.UUID) (auth.User, error) {
		if id == operatorID {
			return sampleUser(operatorID, "operator", rbac.Operator), nil
		}
		return auth.User{}, auth.ErrNotFound
	}
	h := NewHandler(d)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/users/"+operatorID.String(), "admin", "").Code)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNotFound, call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/users/"+uuid.NewString(), "admin", "").Code)
	rec := call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/users/not-a-uuid", "admin", "")
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNotFound, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-SYS-0001", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
}

func TestUpdateUserPassesOnlyGivenFields(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	var got auth.UserUpdate
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).UpdateUserFn = func(_ context.Context, _ auth.Principal, _ auth.Meta, id uuid.UUID, in auth.UserUpdate) (auth.User, error) {
		got = in
		return sampleUser(id, "operator", rbac.Auditor), nil
	}
	h := NewHandler(d)
	rec := call(h, http.MethodPatch, "/api/v1/users/"+operatorID.String(), "admin-stepup", `{"role":"auditor","disabled":true}`)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
	require.NotNil(t, got.Role)
	assert.Equal(t, rbac.Auditor, *got.Role)
	require.NotNil(t, got.Disabled)
	assert.True(t, *got.Disabled)
	assert.Nil(t, got.DisplayName)
	assert.Nil(t, got.Email)

	rec = call(h, http.MethodPatch, "/api/v1/users/"+operatorID.String(), "admin-stepup", `{"role":"superuser"}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, rec.Code)
}

func TestUpdateUserLastAdmin(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).UpdateUserFn = func(context.Context, auth.Principal, auth.Meta, uuid.UUID, auth.UserUpdate) (auth.User, error) {
		return auth.User{}, auth.ErrLastAdmin
	}
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodPatch, "/api/v1/users/"+adminID.String(), "admin-stepup", `{"disabled":true}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusConflict, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-USR-0002", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)
}

func TestUserActions(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	fa := d.Auth.(*fakeAuth)
	var calls []string
	fa.ResetMFAFn = func(_ context.Context, _ auth.Principal, _ auth.Meta, id uuid.UUID) error {
		calls = append(calls, "mfa:"+id.String())
		return nil
	}
	fa.ResetPasswordFn = func(_ context.Context, _ auth.Principal, _ auth.Meta, id uuid.UUID, pw string) error {
		calls = append(calls, "password:"+pw)
		return nil
	}
	fa.RevokeSessionsFn = func(_ context.Context, _ auth.Principal, _ auth.Meta, id uuid.UUID) error {
		calls = append(calls, "sessions:"+id.String())
		return nil
	}
	h := NewHandler(d)
	base := "/api/v1/users/" + operatorID.String() + "/actions/"
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNoContent, call(h, http.MethodPost, base+"reset-mfa", "admin-stepup", "").Code)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNoContent, call(h, http.MethodPost, base+"reset-password", "admin-stepup", `{"password":"yeni uzun parola 42"}`).Code)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNoContent, call(h, http.MethodPost, base+"revoke-sessions", "admin-stepup", "").Code)
	assert.Equal(t, []string{"mfa:" + operatorID.String(), "password:yeni uzun parola 42", "sessions:" + operatorID.String()}, calls)

	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNotFound, call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/users/nope/actions/reset-mfa", "admin-stepup", "").Code)
}
```

```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"net/http"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

var tokenID = uuid.MustParse("0190f5a0-0000-7000-8000-0000000000aa")

func sampleToken(owner uuid.UUID) auth.Token {
	return auth.Token{ID: tokenID, UserID: owner, Name: "CI pipeline", Prefix: "alice_Xy12Ab", Scope: rbac.ScopeRead,
		ExpiresAt: testNow.Add(90 * 24 * time.Hour), CreatedAt: testNow}
}

func TestListTokensDefaultsToCaller(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	var asked uuid.UUID
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).ListTokensFn = func(_ context.Context, _ auth.Principal, userID uuid.UUID) ([]auth.Token, error) {
		asked = userID
		if userID != operatorID {
			return nil, auth.ErrNotFound
		}
		return []auth.Token{sampleToken(operatorID)}, nil
	}
	h := NewHandler(d)
	rec := call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/tokens", "operator", "")
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, operatorID, asked)
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"items":[{"id":"0190f5a0-0000-7000-8000-0000000000aa","user_id":"0190f5a0-0000-7000-8000-000000000002",
	  "name":"CI pipeline","prefix":"alice_Xy12Ab","scope":"read","expires_at":"2026-12-29T12:00:00Z","last_used_at":null,
	  "created_at":"2026-09-30T12:00:00Z"}]}`, rec.Body.String())

	rec = call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/tokens?user_id="+adminID.String(), "operator", "")
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNotFound, rec.Code, "another user's tokens are invisible to non-admins")
	assert.Equal(t, adminID, asked)

	rec = call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/tokens?user_id=garbage", "operator", "")
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, rec.Code)
}

func TestCreateTokenReturnsSecretOnce(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	var got auth.NewToken
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).CreateTokenFn = func(_ context.Context, _ auth.Principal, _ auth.Meta, in auth.NewToken) (string, auth.Token, error) {
		got = in
		if in.Scope == rbac.ScopeOperate {
			return "", auth.Token{}, auth.ErrTokenScopeNotAllowed
		}
		return "alice_Xy12AbSECRETSECRETSECRETSECRETSECRETSEC", sampleToken(auditorID), nil
	}
	h := NewHandler(d)
	rec := call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/tokens", "auditor-stepup", `{"name":"CI pipeline","scope":"read","expires_in_days":90}`)
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusCreated, rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
	assert.Equal(t, auth.NewToken{Name: "CI pipeline", Scope: rbac.ScopeRead, ExpiresInDays: 90}, got)
	assert.Contains(t, rec.Body.String(), `"token":"alice_Xy12AbSECRETSECRETSECRETSECRETSECRETSEC"`)
	assert.Contains(t, rec.Body.String(), `"item":{"id":"0190f5a0-0000-7000-8000-0000000000aa"`)

	rec = call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/tokens", "auditor-stepup", `{"name":"x","scope":"operate","expires_in_days":90}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, rec.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-TOK-0001", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code)

	rec = call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/tokens", "auditor-stepup", `{"name":"x","scope":"admin","expires_in_days":90}`)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, rec.Code)
	assert.Contains(t, rec.Body.String(), `"field":"scope","code":"validation.one_of"`)

	rec = call(h, http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/tokens", "auditor", `{"name":"x","scope":"read","expires_in_days":90}`)
	assert.Equal(t, "ALC-AUTH-0003", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code, "creating a token needs step-up")
}

func TestRevokeToken(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Auth.(*fakeAuth).RevokeTokenFn = func(_ context.Context, p auth.Principal, _ auth.Meta, id uuid.UUID) error {
		if id != tokenID || p.UserID != operatorID {
			return auth.ErrNotFound
		}
		return nil
	}
	h := NewHandler(d)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNoContent, call(h, http.MethodDelete, "/api/v1/tokens/"+tokenID.String(), "operator", "").Code)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNotFound, call(h, http.MethodDelete, "/api/v1/tokens/"+tokenID.String(), "auditor", "").Code)
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNotFound, call(h, http.MethodDelete, "/api/v1/tokens/xyz", "operator", "").Code)
}
```

`internal/api/audit_handlers_test.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/csv"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/audit"
)

func sampleEntries() []audit.Entry {
	return []audit.Entry{
		{Seq: 1, At: testNow, ActorType: audit.ActorUser, ActorID: adminID, ActorName: "admin", IP: "192.0.2.1",
			Action: "setup.admin_created", ObjectType: "user", ObjectID: adminID.String(), Result: audit.Success,
			Details: map[string]string{"city": "İzmir"}, RequestID: "req-1", Hash: []byte{0xab, 0xcd}},
		{Seq: 2, At: testNow.Add(time.Second), ActorType: audit.ActorUser, ActorName: "=HYPERLINK(\"http://evil\")",
			Action: "auth.login", Result: audit.Failure, Details: map[string]string{"reason": "unknown_user"}, Hash: []byte{0x01}},
	}
}

func intactReport() audit.Report {
	return audit.Report{Intact: true, Checked: 2, HeadSeq: 2, HeadHash: []byte{0x01}}
}

func TestListAuditPassesFiltersAndReturnsCursor(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	var got audit.Filter
	d.Audit.(*fakeAudit).ListFn = func(_ context.Context, f audit.Filter) ([]audit.Entry, int64, error) {
		got = f
		return sampleEntries()[:1], 1, nil
	}
	h := NewHandler(d)
	rec := call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/audit?limit=1&cursor=10&action=auth.&actor=admin&result=failure", "auditor", "")
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
	assert.Equal(t, audit.Filter{Action: "auth.", Actor: "admin", Result: audit.Failure, BeforeSeq: 10, Limit: 1}, got)
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"items":[{"seq":1,"at":"2026-09-30T12:00:00Z","actor_type":"user","actor_id":"0190f5a0-0000-7000-8000-000000000001",
	  "actor_name":"admin","ip":"192.0.2.1","action":"setup.admin_created","object_type":"user","object_id":"0190f5a0-0000-7000-8000-000000000001",
	  "result":"success","details":{"city":"İzmir"},"request_id":"req-1","hash":"abcd"}],"next_cursor":"1"}`, rec.Body.String())

	d.Audit.(*fakeAudit).ListFn = func(_ context.Context, f audit.Filter) ([]audit.Entry, int64, error) {
		got = f
		return nil, 0, nil
	}
	rec = call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/audit", "admin", "")
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"items":[],"next_cursor":null}`, rec.Body.String())
	assert.Equal(t, 50, got.Limit, "default page size")
}

func TestListAuditRejectsBadParameters(t *testing.T) {
	h := NewHandler(testDeps(t, nil))
	for _, q := range []string{"limit=0", "limit=201", "limit=x", "cursor=-1", "cursor=abc", "result=maybe"} {
		rec := call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/audit?"+q, "admin", "")
		assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, rec.Code, q)
		assert.Equal(t, "ALC-SYS-0006", decodeErr(t, rec).Error.Code, q)
	}
}

func TestVerifyAudit(t *testing.T) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	var actor audit.Actor
	d.Audit.(*fakeAudit).VerifyFn = func(_ context.Context, a audit.Actor) (audit.Report, error) {
		actor = a
		return audit.Report{Intact: false, Checked: 7, HeadSeq: 9, HeadHash: []byte{0xff}, FirstBadSeq: 7, Problem: audit.ProblemHashMismatch}, nil
	}
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/audit/verify", "auditor", "")
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"intact":false,"checked":7,"head_seq":9,"head_hash":"ff","first_bad_seq":7,"problem":"hash_mismatch"}`, rec.Body.String())
	assert.Equal(t, auditorID, actor.ID)
	assert.Equal(t, audit.ActorUser, actor.Type)

	d.Audit.(*fakeAudit).VerifyFn = func(context.Context, audit.Actor) (audit.Report, error) { return intactReport(), nil }
	rec = call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/audit/verify", "admin", "")
	assert.JSONEq(t, `{"intact":true,"checked":2,"head_seq":2,"head_hash":"01","first_bad_seq":null,"problem":null}`, rec.Body.String())
}

// exportDeps wires fakes so that Iterate fails the test if the export was not
// audited first.
func exportDeps(t *testing.T) (Deps, *fakeAudit) {
	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	fa := d.Audit.(*fakeAudit)
	fa.VerifyFn = func(context.Context, audit.Actor) (audit.Report, error) { return intactReport(), nil }
	fa.IterateFn = func(_ context.Context, fn func(audit.Entry) error) error {
		assert.Equal(t, 1, fa.Count("audit.exported"), "the export must be audited before entries are streamed")
		for _, e := range sampleEntries() {
			if err := fn(e); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return nil
	}
	return d, fa
}

func TestExportCSV(t *testing.T) {
	d, fa := exportDeps(t)
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/audit/export?format=csv", "auditor", "")
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
	assert.Equal(t, "text/csv; charset=utf-8", rec.Header().Get("Content-Type"))
	assert.Equal(t, "true", rec.Header().Get("X-Alice-Audit-Intact"))
	assert.Equal(t, `attachment; filename="alice-audit-2026-09-30.csv"`, rec.Header().Get("Content-Disposition"))
	assert.Equal(t, "no-store", rec.Header().Get("Cache-Control"))

	body := rec.Body.String()
	require.True(t, strings.HasPrefix(body, "\xEF\xBB\xBF"), "UTF-8 BOM")
	rows, err := csv.NewReader(strings.NewReader(strings.TrimPrefix(body, "\xEF\xBB\xBF"))).ReadAll()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.Len(t, rows, 3)
	assert.Equal(t, "seq", rows[0][0])
	assert.Equal(t, "hash", rows[0][12])
	assert.Equal(t, `{"city":"İzmir"}`, rows[1][10])
	assert.Equal(t, `'=HYPERLINK("http://evil")`, rows[2][4], "formula injection is neutralised")

	events := fa.Events()
	require.Len(t, events, 1)
	assert.Equal(t, "audit.exported", events[0].Action)
	assert.Equal(t, map[string]string{"format": "csv", "intact": "true"}, events[0].Details)
	assert.Equal(t, auditorID, events[0].Actor.ID)
}

func TestExportJSON(t *testing.T) {
	d, _ := exportDeps(t)
	rec := call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/audit/export?format=json", "admin", "")
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code, rec.Body.String())
	assert.Equal(t, "application/json; charset=utf-8", rec.Header().Get("Content-Type"))
	var body struct {
		Verification auditReportDTO  `json:"verification"`
		Entries      []auditEntryDTO `json:"entries"`
	}
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &body))
	assert.True(t, body.Verification.Intact)
	require.Len(t, body.Entries, 2)
	assert.Equal(t, "=HYPERLINK(\"http://evil\")", body.Entries[1].ActorName, "JSON keeps values verbatim")
	assert.Nil(t, body.Entries[1].ActorID)
}

func TestExportRejectsUnknownFormatAndStopsWhenAuditFails(t *testing.T) {
	h := NewHandler(testDeps(t, nil))
	rec := call(h, http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/audit/export?format=xlsx", "admin", "")
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, rec.Code)

	d := testDeps(t, nil)
	d.Audit = failingWriteAudit{fakeAudit: &fakeAudit{VerifyFn: func(context.Context, audit.Actor) (audit.Report, error) { return intactReport(), nil }}}
	rec = call(NewHandler(d), http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/audit/export?format=csv", "admin", "")
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusInternalServerError, rec.Code, "no export without an audit record")
	assert.Equal(t, "application/json; charset=utf-8", rec.Header().Get("Content-Type"))
}

// failingWriteAudit is a fakeAudit whose Write always fails.
type failingWriteAudit struct{ *fakeAudit }

func (failingWriteAudit) Write(context.Context, audit.Event) error {
	return errors.New("database down")
}

func TestCSVSafe(t *testing.T) {
	for in, want := range map[string]string{
		"=1+1": "'=1+1", "+1": "'+1", "-1": "'-1", "@SUM(A1)": "'@SUM(A1)", "\tx": "'\tx", "\rx": "'\rx",
		"admin": "admin", "": "", "İzmir": "İzmir",
	} {
		assert.Equal(t, want, csvSafe(in), in)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the user handlers**

`internal/api/users_handlers.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"net/http"
	"strings"

	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/platform/validate"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

// userRoutes are the administrator's user management (spec §9.6, SEC-009:
// every change needs step-up; R10 break-glass is enforced by auth).
func (s *server) userRoutes() []route {
	return []route{
		{method: http.MethodGet, pattern: "/api/v1/users", perm: rbac.UsersRead, handler: s.listUsers},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/users", perm: rbac.UsersManage, handler: s.createUser},
		{method: http.MethodGet, pattern: "/api/v1/users/{id}", perm: rbac.UsersRead, handler: s.getUser},
		{method: http.MethodPatch, pattern: "/api/v1/users/{id}", perm: rbac.UsersManage, handler: s.updateUser},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/users/{id}/actions/reset-mfa", perm: rbac.UsersManage, handler: s.resetUserMFA},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/users/{id}/actions/reset-password", perm: rbac.UsersManage, handler: s.resetUserPassword},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/users/{id}/actions/revoke-sessions", perm: rbac.UsersManage, handler: s.revokeUserSessions},
	}
}

// roleValues is the params value for validate.OneOf on role fields.
func roleValues() string {
	names := make([]string, 0, 3)
	for _, r := range rbac.Roles() {
		names = append(names, string(r))
	}
	return strings.Join(names, ", ")
}

func (s *server) listUsers(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	users, err := s.deps.Auth.ListUsers(r.Context())
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	items := make([]userDTO, 0, len(users))
	for _, u := range users {
		items = append(items, userJSON(u))
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string][]userDTO{"items": items})
}

func (s *server) createUser(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req struct {
		Username    string `json:"username"`
		DisplayName string `json:"display_name"`
		Email       string `json:"email"`
		Role        string `json:"role"`
		Password    string `json:"password"`
	}
	if !s.decode(w, r, &req) {
		return
	}
	role, ok := rbac.ParseRole(req.Role)
	if !ok {
		var v validate.Errors
		v.Add("role", validate.OneOf, map[string]string{"values": roleValues()})
		s.fail(w, r, v)
		return
	}
	u, err := s.deps.Auth.CreateUser(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()), s.meta(r), auth.NewUser{
		Username: req.Username, DisplayName: req.DisplayName, Email: req.Email, Role: role, Password: req.Password,
	})
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusCreated, userJSON(u))
}

func (s *server) getUser(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id, ok := pathUUID(r, "id")
	if !ok {
		s.writeError(w, r, ErrNotFound)
		return
	}
	u, err := s.deps.Auth.GetUser(r.Context(), id)
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, userJSON(u))
}

func (s *server) updateUser(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id, ok := pathUUID(r, "id")
	if !ok {
		s.writeError(w, r, ErrNotFound)
		return
	}
	var req struct {
		DisplayName *string `json:"display_name"`
		Email       *string `json:"email"`
		Role        *string `json:"role"`
		Disabled    *bool   `json:"disabled"`
	}
	if !s.decode(w, r, &req) {
		return
	}
	in := auth.UserUpdate{DisplayName: req.DisplayName, Email: req.Email, Disabled: req.Disabled}
	if req.Role != nil {
		role, ok := rbac.ParseRole(*req.Role)
		if !ok {
			var v validate.Errors
			v.Add("role", validate.OneOf, map[string]string{"values": roleValues()})
			s.fail(w, r, v)
			return
		}
		in.Role = &role
	}
	u, err := s.deps.Auth.UpdateUser(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()), s.meta(r), id, in)
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, userJSON(u))
}

func (s *server) resetUserMFA(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id, ok := pathUUID(r, "id")
	if !ok {
		s.writeError(w, r, ErrNotFound)
		return
	}
	if err := s.deps.Auth.ResetMFA(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()), s.meta(r), id); err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	noContent(w)
}

func (s *server) resetUserPassword(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id, ok := pathUUID(r, "id")
	if !ok {
		s.writeError(w, r, ErrNotFound)
		return
	}
	var req struct {
		Password string `json:"password"`
	}
	if !s.decode(w, r, &req) {
		return
	}
	if err := s.deps.Auth.ResetPassword(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()), s.meta(r), id, req.Password); err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	noContent(w)
}

func (s *server) revokeUserSessions(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id, ok := pathUUID(r, "id")
	if !ok {
		s.writeError(w, r, ErrNotFound)
		return
	}
	if err := s.deps.Auth.RevokeSessions(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()), s.meta(r), id); err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	noContent(w)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the token handlers**

`internal/api/tokens_handlers.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/platform/validate"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

// tokenRoutes manage personal API tokens (SEC-025, SEC-026). Creating a token
// needs step-up; the secret is returned exactly once.
func (s *server) tokenRoutes() []route {
	return []route{
		{method: http.MethodGet, pattern: "/api/v1/tokens", perm: rbac.Session, handler: s.listTokens},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/tokens", perm: rbac.SessionStepUp, handler: s.createToken},
		{method: http.MethodDelete, pattern: "/api/v1/tokens/{id}", perm: rbac.Session, handler: s.revokeToken},
	}
}

type tokenDTO struct {
	ID         string     `json:"id"`
	UserID     string     `json:"user_id"`
	Name       string     `json:"name"`
	Prefix     string     `json:"prefix"`
	Scope      string     `json:"scope"`
	ExpiresAt  time.Time  `json:"expires_at"`
	LastUsedAt *time.Time `json:"last_used_at"`
	CreatedAt  time.Time  `json:"created_at"`
}

func tokenJSON(t auth.Token) tokenDTO {
	var last *time.Time
	if t.LastUsedAt != nil {
		u := t.LastUsedAt.UTC()
		last = &u
	}
	return tokenDTO{
		ID:         t.ID.String(),
		UserID:     t.UserID.String(),
		Name:       t.Name,
		Prefix:     t.Prefix,
		Scope:      string(t.Scope),
		ExpiresAt:  t.ExpiresAt.UTC(),
		LastUsedAt: last,
		CreatedAt:  t.CreatedAt.UTC(),
	}
}

// listTokens lists the caller's tokens, or another user's when ?user_id= is
// given (auth answers ErrNotFound unless the caller is an administrator).
func (s *server) listTokens(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	p := principalFrom(r.Context())
	userID := p.UserID
	if raw := r.URL.Query().Get("user_id"); raw != "" {
		id, err := uuid.Parse(raw)
		if err != nil {
			var v validate.Errors
			v.Add("user_id", validate.Pattern, nil)
			s.fail(w, r, v)
			return
		}
		userID = id
	}
	tokens, err := s.deps.Auth.ListTokens(r.Context(), p, userID)
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	items := make([]tokenDTO, 0, len(tokens))
	for _, t := range tokens {
		items = append(items, tokenJSON(t))
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string][]tokenDTO{"items": items})
}

func (s *server) createToken(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req struct {
		Name          string `json:"name"`
		Scope         string `json:"scope"`
		ExpiresInDays int    `json:"expires_in_days"`
	}
	if !s.decode(w, r, &req) {
		return
	}
	scope, ok := rbac.ParseScope(req.Scope)
	if !ok {
		var v validate.Errors
		v.Add("scope", validate.OneOf, map[string]string{"values": "read, operate"})
		s.fail(w, r, v)
		return
	}
	secret, t, err := s.deps.Auth.CreateToken(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()), s.meta(r), auth.NewToken{
		Name: req.Name, Scope: scope, ExpiresInDays: req.ExpiresInDays,
	})
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusCreated, map[string]any{"token": secret, "item": tokenJSON(t)})
}

func (s *server) revokeToken(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id, ok := pathUUID(r, "id")
	if !ok {
		s.writeError(w, r, ErrNotFound)
		return
	}
	if err := s.deps.Auth.RevokeToken(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()), s.meta(r), id); err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	noContent(w)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement the audit handlers**

`internal/api/audit_handlers.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"encoding/csv"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"strconv"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/platform/validate"
	"alice/internal/rbac"
)

// auditRoutes expose the audit log to Administrators and Auditors (SEC-070..075).
func (s *server) auditRoutes() []route {
	return []route{
		{method: http.MethodGet, pattern: "/api/v1/audit", perm: rbac.AuditRead, handler: s.listAudit},
		{method: http.MethodGet, pattern: "/api/v1/audit/export", perm: rbac.AuditRead, handler: s.exportAudit},
		{method: http.MethodPost, pattern: "/api/v1/audit/verify", perm: rbac.AuditRead, handler: s.verifyAudit},
	}
}

type auditEntryDTO struct {
	Seq        int64             `json:"seq"`
	At         time.Time         `json:"at"`
	ActorType  string            `json:"actor_type"`
	ActorID    *string           `json:"actor_id"`
	ActorName  string            `json:"actor_name"`
	IP         *string           `json:"ip"`
	Action     string            `json:"action"`
	ObjectType string            `json:"object_type"`
	ObjectID   string            `json:"object_id"`
	Result     string            `json:"result"`
	Details    map[string]string `json:"details"`
	RequestID  string            `json:"request_id"`
	Hash       string            `json:"hash"`
}

func auditEntryJSON(e audit.Entry) auditEntryDTO {
	d := auditEntryDTO{
		Seq: e.Seq, At: e.At.UTC(), ActorType: string(e.ActorType), ActorName: e.ActorName,
		Action: e.Action, ObjectType: e.ObjectType, ObjectID: e.ObjectID, Result: string(e.Result),
		Details: e.Details, RequestID: e.RequestID, Hash: hex.EncodeToString(e.Hash),
	}
	if e.ActorID != uuid.Nil {
		id := e.ActorID.String()
		d.ActorID = &id
	}
	if e.IP != "" {
		ip := e.IP
		d.IP = &ip
	}
	if d.Details == nil {
		d.Details = map[string]string{}
	}
	return d
}

type auditReportDTO struct {
	Intact      bool    `json:"intact"`
	Checked     int64   `json:"checked"`
	HeadSeq     int64   `json:"head_seq"`
	HeadHash    string  `json:"head_hash"`
	FirstBadSeq *int64  `json:"first_bad_seq"`
	Problem     *string `json:"problem"`
}

func auditReportJSON(r audit.Report) auditReportDTO {
	d := auditReportDTO{Intact: r.Intact, Checked: r.Checked, HeadSeq: r.HeadSeq, HeadHash: hex.EncodeToString(r.HeadHash)}
	if !r.Intact {
		seq, problem := r.FirstBadSeq, r.Problem
		d.FirstBadSeq, d.Problem = &seq, &problem
	}
	return d
}

func (s *server) listAudit(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	q := r.URL.Query()
	f := audit.Filter{Action: q.Get("action"), Actor: q.Get("actor"), Limit: 50}
	var v validate.Errors
	if raw := q.Get("limit"); raw != "" {
		n, err := strconv.Atoi(raw)
		if err != nil || n < 1 || n > 200 {
			v.Add("limit", validate.OutOfRange, map[string]string{"min": "1", "max": "200"})
		}
		f.Limit = n
	}
	if raw := q.Get("cursor"); raw != "" {
		n, err := strconv.ParseInt(raw, 10, 64)
		if err != nil || n < 1 {
			v.Add("cursor", validate.Pattern, nil)
		}
		f.BeforeSeq = n
	}
	switch res := audit.Result(q.Get("result")); res {
	case "", audit.Success, audit.Failure, audit.Denied:
		f.Result = res
	default:
		v.Add("result", validate.OneOf, map[string]string{"values": "success, failure, denied"})
	}
	if err := v.Err(); err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	entries, next, err := s.deps.Audit.List(r.Context(), f)
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	items := make([]auditEntryDTO, 0, len(entries))
	for _, e := range entries {
		items = append(items, auditEntryJSON(e))
	}
	var cursor *string
	if next > 0 {
		c := strconv.FormatInt(next, 10)
		cursor = &c
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"items": items, "next_cursor": cursor})
}

func (s *server) verifyAudit(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	report, err := s.deps.Audit.Verify(r.Context(), principalFrom(r.Context()).Actor(s.meta(r)))
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, auditReportJSON(report))
}

// exportAudit streams the whole log as CSV or JSON (SEC-075): the chain is
// verified first, the export itself is audited before any entry leaves, and
// the verification result travels with the file.
func (s *server) exportAudit(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	format := r.URL.Query().Get("format")
	if format != "csv" && format != "json" {
		var v validate.Errors
		v.Add("format", validate.OneOf, map[string]string{"values": "csv, json"})
		s.fail(w, r, v)
		return
	}
	m := s.meta(r)
	actor := principalFrom(r.Context()).Actor(m)
	report, err := s.deps.Audit.Verify(r.Context(), actor)
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	err = s.deps.Audit.Write(r.Context(), audit.Event{
		Actor: actor, Action: "audit.exported", Result: audit.Success,
		Details: map[string]string{"format": format, "intact": strconv.FormatBool(report.Intact)},
	})
	if err != nil {
		s.fail(w, r, err)
		return
	}

	h := w.Header()
	h.Set("Cache-Control", "no-store")
	h.Set("X-Alice-Audit-Intact", strconv.FormatBool(report.Intact))
	h.Set("Content-Disposition", `attachment; filename="alice-audit-`+s.deps.Clock.Now().UTC().Format("2006-01-02")+"."+format+`"`)
	if format == "csv" {
		h.Set("Content-Type", "text/csv; charset=utf-8")
		err = s.writeAuditCSV(w, r)
	} else {
		h.Set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
		err = s.writeAuditJSON(w, r, report)
	}
	if err != nil {
		// Headers are already sent: log and abort so the client sees a broken
		// download instead of a file that looks complete.
		s.deps.Log.Error("audit export failed", "request_id", m.RequestID, "error", err.Error())
		panic(http.ErrAbortHandler)
	}
}

func (s *server) writeAuditCSV(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) error {
	if _, err := w.Write([]byte("\xEF\xBB\xBF")); err != nil { // BOM so spreadsheet tools read UTF-8 (spec §10.5)
		return err
	}
	cw := csv.NewWriter(w)
	header := []string{"seq", "at", "actor_type", "actor_id", "actor_name", "ip", "action", "object_type", "object_id", "result", "details", "request_id", "hash"}
	if err := cw.Write(header); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	err := s.deps.Audit.Iterate(r.Context(), func(e audit.Entry) error {
		d := auditEntryJSON(e)
		details, err := json.Marshal(d.Details)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		actorID, ip := "", ""
		if d.ActorID != nil {
			actorID = *d.ActorID
		}
		if d.IP != nil {
			ip = *d.IP
		}
		rec := []string{
			strconv.FormatInt(d.Seq, 10), d.At.Format(time.RFC3339Nano), d.ActorType, actorID, d.ActorName, ip,
			d.Action, d.ObjectType, d.ObjectID, d.Result, string(details), d.RequestID, d.Hash,
		}
		for i := range rec {
			rec[i] = csvSafe(rec[i])
		}
		return cw.Write(rec)
	})
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	cw.Flush()
	return cw.Error()
}

// csvSafe neutralises spreadsheet formula injection: a cell that starts with
// = + - @ TAB or CR is prefixed with an apostrophe.
func csvSafe(v string) string {
	if v == "" {
		return v
	}
	switch v[0] {
	case '=', '+', '-', '@', '\t', '\r':
		return "'" + v
	}
	return v
}

func (s *server) writeAuditJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, report audit.Report) error {
	head, err := json.Marshal(auditReportJSON(report))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if _, err := w.Write(append(append([]byte(`{"verification":`), head...), []byte(`,"entries":[`)...)); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	first := true
	err = s.deps.Audit.Iterate(r.Context(), func(e audit.Entry) error {
		b, err := json.Marshal(auditEntryJSON(e))
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if !first {
			b = append([]byte{','}, b...)
		}
		first = false
		_, err = w.Write(b)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	_, err = w.Write([]byte("]}\n"))
	return err
}
```

```yaml
  /api/v1/users:
    get:
      operationId: listUsers
      summary: All users (administrators only)
      x-permission: users.read
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Users ordered by username
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                required: [items]
                properties:
                  items: { type: array, items: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/User" } }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
    post:
      operationId: createUser
      summary: Create a local user; they set up their authenticator app at first sign-in
      x-permission: users.manage
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              additionalProperties: false
              required: [username, display_name, email, role, password]
              properties:
                username: { type: string, pattern: "^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,63}$" }
                display_name: { type: string, minLength: 1, maxLength: 100 }
                email: { type: string, maxLength: 254 }
                role: { type: string, enum: [admin, operator, auditor] }
                password: { type: string, minLength: 12, maxLength: 1024 }
      responses:
        "201":
          description: User created
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/User" }
        "409": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        "422": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
  /api/v1/users/{id}:
    parameters:
      - { $ref: "#/components/parameters/ID" }
    get:
      operationId: getUser
      summary: One user
      x-permission: users.read
      responses:
        "200":
          description: The user
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/User" }
        "404": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
    patch:
      operationId: updateUser
      summary: Change name, email, role or disabled state; role changes and disabling end the user's sessions
      x-permission: users.manage
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              additionalProperties: false
              properties:
                display_name: { type: string, minLength: 1, maxLength: 100 }
                email: { type: string, maxLength: 254 }
                role: { type: string, enum: [admin, operator, auditor] }
                disabled: { type: boolean }
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Updated user
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/User" }
        "404": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        "409": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        "422": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
  /api/v1/users/{id}/actions/reset-mfa:
    parameters:
      - { $ref: "#/components/parameters/ID" }
    post:
      operationId: resetUserMFA
      summary: Remove the user's authenticator app and recovery codes; ends their sessions
      x-permission: users.manage
      responses:
        "204": { description: MFA reset }
        "404": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
  /api/v1/users/{id}/actions/reset-password:
    parameters:
      - { $ref: "#/components/parameters/ID" }
    post:
      operationId: resetUserPassword
      summary: Set a new password for the user; ends their sessions and clears a lockout
      x-permission: users.manage
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              additionalProperties: false
              required: [password]
              properties:
                password: { type: string, minLength: 12, maxLength: 1024 }
      responses:
        "204": { description: Password reset }
        "404": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        "422": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
  /api/v1/users/{id}/actions/revoke-sessions:
    parameters:
      - { $ref: "#/components/parameters/ID" }
    post:
      operationId: revokeUserSessions
      summary: Sign the user out everywhere
      x-permission: users.manage
      responses:
        "204": { description: Sessions ended }
        "404": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
  /api/v1/tokens:
    get:
      operationId: listTokens
      summary: Own API tokens, or another user's with user_id (administrators only)
      x-permission: session
      parameters:
        - { name: user_id, in: query, required: false, schema: { type: string, format: uuid } }
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Active (not revoked) tokens
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                required: [items]
                properties:
                  items: { type: array, items: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/APIToken" } }
        "404": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
    post:
      operationId: createToken
      summary: Create an API token; the secret is returned once
      x-permission: session.stepup
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              type: object
              additionalProperties: false
              required: [name, scope, expires_in_days]
              properties:
                name: { type: string, minLength: 1, maxLength: 100 }
                scope: { type: string, enum: [read, operate] }
                expires_in_days: { type: integer, minimum: 1, maximum: 365 }
      responses:
        "201":
          description: Token created
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                required: [token, item]
                properties:
                  token: { type: string, description: "alice_ followed by 43 characters; store it now, it is never shown again" }
                  item: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/APIToken" }
        "422": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
  /api/v1/tokens/{id}:
    parameters:
      - { $ref: "#/components/parameters/ID" }
    delete:
      operationId: revokeToken
      summary: Revoke a token (own, or any token for administrators)
      x-permission: session
      responses:
        "204": { description: Token revoked }
        "404": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
  /api/v1/audit:
    get:
      operationId: listAudit
      summary: Audit log entries, newest first (administrators and auditors)
      x-permission: audit.read
      parameters:
        - { name: limit, in: query, required: false, schema: { type: integer, minimum: 1, maximum: 200, default: 50 } }
        - { name: cursor, in: query, required: false, schema: { type: string, pattern: "^[0-9]+$" }, description: next_cursor of the previous page }
        - { name: action, in: query, required: false, schema: { type: string }, description: Action prefix, e.g. auth. }
        - { name: actor, in: query, required: false, schema: { type: string }, description: Exact actor name }
        - { name: result, in: query, required: false, schema: { type: string, enum: [success, failure, denied] } }
      responses:
        "200":
          description: One page of entries
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                required: [items, next_cursor]
                properties:
                  items: { type: array, items: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/AuditEntry" } }
                  next_cursor: { type: [string, "null"] }
        "422": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
  /api/v1/audit/export:
    get:
      operationId: exportAudit
      summary: Download the whole audit log with its verification result (the export is itself audited)
      x-permission: audit.read
      parameters:
        - { name: format, in: query, required: true, schema: { type: string, enum: [csv, json] } }
      responses:
        "200":
          description: The log. Header X-Alice-Audit-Intact tells whether the chain verified.
          content:
            text/csv:
              schema: { type: string }
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                required: [verification, entries]
                properties:
                  verification: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/AuditReport" }
                  entries: { type: array, items: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/AuditEntry" } }
        "422": { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
  /api/v1/audit/verify:
    post:
      operationId: verifyAudit
      summary: Recompute the hash chain and record the result
      x-permission: audit.read
      responses:
        "200":
          description: Verification result
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: { $ref: "#/components/schemas/AuditReport" }
        default: { $ref: "#/components/responses/Error" }
```

Insert this block directly after the `components:` line (before `  responses:`):

```yaml
  parameters:
    ID:
      name: id
      in: path
      required: true
      schema: { type: string, format: uuid }
```

### Task 16: Wiring, CLI commands, e2e build, compose and CI

**Files:**
- Modify (replace whole file): `cmd/alice/main.go`
- Create: `cmd/alice/services.go`, `cmd/alice/cli.go`, `cmd/alice/testapi.go`, `cmd/alice/testapi_release.go`, `cmd/alice/testapi_e2e.go`
- Test: `cmd/alice/cli_test.go`, `cmd/alice/testapi_release_test.go`, `cmd/alice/testapi_e2e_test.go`, `cmd/alice/cli_integration_test.go`
- Create: `deploy/docker-compose.e2e.yml`
- Modify: `deploy/Dockerfile`, `Makefile`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`
- Modify (replace whole file): `docs/dev/getting-started.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 1: `db.ErrUnreachable`, `db.ErrAuthFailed`, `db.ErrDatabaseMissing`; `db.Open`, `db.Migrate`, `db.NewHealthChecker`.
  - Task 2: `logging.New(w io.Writer, level slog.Leveler) *slog.Logger`; `clock.NewOffset(base Clock) *Offset` with `Advance`, `Reset`; `db.InTx(ctx, pool, fn func(pgx.Tx) error) error`.
  - Task 3: `config.Config.MigrationURL() string`, `SeparateMigrationRole() bool`, `TrustedProxies []netip.Prefix`; `pgtest.Main`, `pgtest.New(t) *pgtest.DB` (`Owner`, `App`, `AppURL`); Makefile target `reset`.
  - Task 5: `secrets.LoadOrCreateMasterKey(ctx, q db.Querier, dir string, now time.Time) (*secrets.Keyring, error)`, `secrets.BackupKDF`.
  - Task 6: `audit.New(clock.Clock) *audit.Log`, `audit.NewService(*audit.Log, *pgxpool.Pool) *audit.Service` (`Write`, `Verify(ctx, audit.Actor) (audit.Report, error)`), `audit.SystemActor`, `audit.Event`, `audit.Success`.
  - Tasks 8, 10, 11: `auth.NewHasher(auth.Argon2Params, int) *auth.Hasher`, `auth.PasswordParams`, `auth.RecoveryCodeParams`, `auth.NewService(auth.Config) *auth.Service`, `(*auth.Service).ResetMFAByUsername(ctx, username string) error`, `auth.ErrNotFound`.
  - Task 12: `setup.New(setup.Config) *setup.Service`, `(*setup.Service).ResetToken(ctx) (string, error)`, `(*setup.Service).ResetLockouts()`, `setup.ErrAlreadySetUp`.
  - Task 13: `api.New(api.Deps) *api.Server`, `(*api.Server).Handler() http.Handler`, `(*api.Server).ResetLimits()`, `api.NewHealthHandler(api.Deps) http.Handler`, `api.Deps` fields `Clock`, `Auth`, `Setup`, `Audit`, `TrustedProxies`, `TestHandler`.
- Produces:
  - CLI: `alice setup-token --reset`, `alice audit verify` (exit 0 intact, 1 broken), `alice admin reset-mfa <username>`; wrong arguments exit 2 with a `usage: alice ...` line.
  - `func newClock() clock.Clock` (release: `clock.Real{}`; e2e: a shared `*clock.Offset`), `func testHandler(d testDeps) http.Handler` (release: `nil`).
  - e2e build endpoints (tag `e2e` only): `GET /test/clock` → `{"now": RFC3339Nano}`; `POST /test/clock {"advance_seconds": n}` → `{"now"}` (400 for negative/malformed); `POST /test/reset` → 204; `POST /test/setup-token` → `{"code"}`; any other `/test/...` → 404. Consumed by Task 24.
  - Make targets `e2e-up`, `setup-token`; `make e2e` and `make ci` run against the e2e build; CI `stack` job uses `make e2e-up`.

Design notes: the owner (migration) pool lives only for the migration; the application pool uses `alice_app`. `buildServices` is shared by `serve` and the CLI so both load the master key the same way. The test handler needs `(*api.Server).ResetLimits`, but the server is built from `Deps` that already contain the handler, so `testDeps.ResetLimits` is a closure over the variable assigned right after `api.New`. `audit verify` does not load the master key (the chain does not need it), so it still works when the key file is broken.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the shared services, the CLI and the release test hooks**

`cmd/alice/services.go`:
```go
package main

import (
	"context"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/platform/clock"
	"alice/internal/secrets"
	"alice/internal/setup"
)

// Argon2id memory is 64 MiB per password hash, so at most two run at once
// (the container memory limit is 512 MiB). Recovery-code hashes use 8 MiB.
const (
	passwordHashConcurrency = 2
	recoveryHashConcurrency = 4
)

// services are the module services shared by serve and the CLI commands.
type services struct {
	keyring *secrets.Keyring
	audit   *audit.Service
	auth    *auth.Service
	setup   *setup.Service
}

// buildServices loads (or, on a brand-new database, creates) the master key
// and wires the auth, setup and audit services on the application pool.
func buildServices(ctx context.Context, pool *pgxpool.Pool, keysDir string, clk clock.Clock) (*services, error) {
	keyring, err := secrets.LoadOrCreateMasterKey(ctx, pool, keysDir, clk.Now())
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	auditLog := audit.New(clk)
	passwords := auth.NewHasher(auth.PasswordParams, passwordHashConcurrency)
	authSvc := auth.NewService(auth.Config{
		Pool:           pool,
		Keyring:        keyring,
		Audit:          auditLog,
		Clock:          clk,
		Hasher:         passwords,
		RecoveryHasher: auth.NewHasher(auth.RecoveryCodeParams, recoveryHashConcurrency),
	})
	setupSvc := setup.New(setup.Config{
		Pool:      pool,
		Auth:      authSvc,
		Keyring:   keyring,
		Audit:     auditLog,
		Clock:     clk,
		Hasher:    passwords,
		BackupKDF: secrets.BackupKDF,
	})
	return &services{
		keyring: keyring,
		audit:   audit.NewService(auditLog, pool),
		auth:    authSvc,
		setup:   setupSvc,
	}, nil
}
```

`cmd/alice/cli.go`:
```go
package main

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/hex"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/platform/config"
	"alice/internal/setup"
)

// cliBudget bounds how long an operator command waits for the database.
const cliBudget = 15 * time.Second

// cliActor names the host-root command line in the audit log.
const cliActor = "cli"

func runSetupToken(args []string, cfg config.Config, stdout, stderr io.Writer) int {
	if len(args) != 1 || args[0] != "--reset" {
		fmt.Fprintln(stderr, "usage: alice setup-token --reset")
		return 2
	}
	return withServices(cfg, stderr, func(ctx context.Context, svc *services) int {
		code, err := svc.setup.ResetToken(ctx)
		if errors.Is(err, setup.ErrAlreadySetUp) {
			fmt.Fprintln(stderr, "An administrator already exists, so a setup code is no longer needed. Sign in instead.")
			return 1
		}
		if err != nil {
			fmt.Fprintln(stderr, "could not create a setup code:", err)
			return 1
		}
		fmt.Fprintf(stdout, "One-time setup code: %s\n", code)
		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, "Enter it on the /setup page. Any earlier code no longer works; this one stops working when the first administrator is created.")
		return 0
	})
}

func runAudit(args []string, cfg config.Config, stdout, stderr io.Writer) int {
	if len(args) != 1 || args[0] != "verify" {
		fmt.Fprintln(stderr, "usage: alice audit verify")
		return 2
	}
	return withPool(cfg, stderr, func(ctx context.Context, pool *pgxpool.Pool) int {
		svc := audit.NewService(audit.New(newClock()), pool)
		rep, err := svc.Verify(ctx, audit.SystemActor(cliActor))
		if err != nil {
			fmt.Fprintln(stderr, "could not verify the audit log:", err)
			return 1
		}
		if !rep.Intact {
			fmt.Fprintf(stdout, "Audit log integrity problem at entry %d (%s). %d entries checked.\n", rep.FirstBadSeq, rep.Problem, rep.Checked)
			return 1
		}
		fmt.Fprintf(stdout, "The audit log is intact: %d entries checked, head entry %d, head hash %s.\n", rep.Checked, rep.HeadSeq, hex.EncodeToString(rep.HeadHash))
		return 0
	})
}

func runAdmin(args []string, cfg config.Config, stdout, stderr io.Writer) int {
	if len(args) != 2 || args[0] != "reset-mfa" || args[1] == "" {
		fmt.Fprintln(stderr, "usage: alice admin reset-mfa <username>")
		return 2
	}
	username := args[1]
	return withServices(cfg, stderr, func(ctx context.Context, svc *services) int {
		err := svc.auth.ResetMFAByUsername(ctx, username)
		if errors.Is(err, auth.ErrNotFound) {
			fmt.Fprintf(stderr, "There is no user named %q.\n", username)
			return 1
		}
		if err != nil {
			fmt.Fprintln(stderr, "could not reset the authenticator app:", err)
			return 1
		}
		fmt.Fprintf(stdout, "The authenticator app of %q was removed. They set up a new one at their next sign-in.\n", username)
		return 0
	})
}

// withPool opens the application pool for one operator command.
func withPool(cfg config.Config, stderr io.Writer, fn func(ctx context.Context, pool *pgxpool.Pool) int) int {
	if err := cfg.RequireDatabase(); err != nil {
		fmt.Fprintln(stderr, err)
		return 1
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2*time.Minute)
	defer cancel()
	pool, err := openDB(ctx, cfg.DatabaseURL, cliBudget)
	if err != nil {
		fmt.Fprintln(stderr, err)
		return 1
	}
	defer pool.Close()
	return fn(ctx, pool)
}

// withServices additionally loads the master key and wires the services.
func withServices(cfg config.Config, stderr io.Writer, fn func(ctx context.Context, svc *services) int) int {
	return withPool(cfg, stderr, func(ctx context.Context, pool *pgxpool.Pool) int {
		svc, err := buildServices(ctx, pool, cfg.KeysDir, newClock())
		if err != nil {
			fmt.Fprintln(stderr, err)
			return 1
		}
		return fn(ctx, svc)
	})
}
```

`cmd/alice/main.go`:
```go
// Command alice is the Alice server and operator CLI.
package main

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"os/signal"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"

	"alice/internal/api"
	"alice/internal/i18n"
	"alice/internal/platform/config"
	"alice/internal/platform/db"
	"alice/internal/platform/logging"
	"alice/internal/platform/tlscert"
	"alice/internal/version"
	"alice/internal/webui"
	"alice/locales"
	"alice/migrations"
)

// dbStartupBudget is how long serve waits for PostgreSQL before giving up.
const dbStartupBudget = 60 * time.Second

const usage = `Usage: alice <command>

Commands:
  serve                       Start the Alice server (default)
  migrate                     Apply database migrations and exit
  healthcheck                 Exit 0 if the local server reports healthy (used by Docker)
  setup-token --reset         Print a new one-time setup code (only before the first administrator exists)
  audit verify                Check the audit log hash chain (exit code 1 if it is broken)
  admin reset-mfa <username>  Remove a user's authenticator app; they set up a new one at next sign-in
  version                     Print version information
  help                        Show this help
`

func main() {
	os.Exit(run(os.Args[1:], os.Getenv, os.Stdout, os.Stderr))
}

func run(args []string, getenv func(string) string, stdout, stderr io.Writer) int {
	cmd := "serve"
	if len(args) > 0 {
		cmd = args[0]
	}
	cfg, err := config.Load(getenv)
	if err != nil {
		fmt.Fprintln(stderr, "configuration error:", err)
		return 1
	}
	log := logging.New(stderr, cfg.LogLevel)

	switch cmd {
	case "serve":
		ctx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), os.Interrupt, syscall.SIGTERM)
		defer stop()
		if err := serve(ctx, cfg, log); err != nil {
			log.Error("alice stopped with an error", "error", err.Error())
			return 1
		}
		return 0
	case "migrate":
		if err := migrate(cfg); err != nil {
			fmt.Fprintln(stderr, "migration failed:", err)
			return 1
		}
		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, "database is up to date")
		return 0
	case "healthcheck":
		return healthcheck(cfg)
	case "setup-token":
		return runSetupToken(args[1:], cfg, stdout, stderr)
	case "audit":
		return runAudit(args[1:], cfg, stdout, stderr)
	case "admin":
		return runAdmin(args[1:], cfg, stdout, stderr)
	case "version":
		v := version.Get()
		fmt.Fprintf(stdout, "alice %s (commit %s, built %s)\n", v.Version, v.Commit, v.BuildTime)
		return 0
	case "help", "-h", "--help":
		fmt.Fprint(stdout, usage)
		return 0
	default:
		fmt.Fprintf(stderr, "unknown command %q\n\n%s", cmd, usage)
		return 2
	}
}

// openDB opens a pool and waits up to budget for the database. Only an
// unreachable database is worth the "not ready" wording; a wrong password or
// a missing database is reported as it is (db.ErrAuthFailed, db.ErrDatabaseMissing).
func openDB(ctx context.Context, url string, budget time.Duration) (*pgxpool.Pool, error) {
	openCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, budget)
	defer cancel()
	pool, err := db.Open(openCtx, url)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, dbStartupError(err, budget)
	}
	return pool, nil
}

// dbStartupError adds the waiting time to "not reachable" errors only; a
// rejected password or a missing database is reported as is (it is final).
// (Kept from Task 3; TestDBStartupErrorMentionsWaitOnlyForUnreachable covers it.)
func dbStartupError(err error, budget time.Duration) error {
	if errors.Is(err, db.ErrUnreachable) {
		return fmt.Errorf("database not ready after %s: %w", budget, err)
	}
	return err
}

// migrateWith applies migrations through a short-lived pool on the owner role.
func migrateWith(ctx context.Context, url string, budget time.Duration) error {
	pool, err := openDB(ctx, url, budget)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer pool.Close()
	return db.Migrate(ctx, pool, migrations.FS)
}

func migrate(cfg config.Config) error {
	if err := cfg.RequireDatabase(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2*time.Minute)
	defer cancel()
	return migrateWith(ctx, cfg.MigrationURL(), time.Minute)
}

func serve(ctx context.Context, cfg config.Config, log *slog.Logger) error {
	if err := cfg.RequireDatabase(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	log.Info("starting alice", "version", version.Get().Version, "config", cfg)
	if !cfg.SeparateMigrationRole() {
		log.Warn("ALICE_MIGRATE_DATABASE_URL is not set, so migrations and the application share one database role; " +
			"the audit log is then protected by database triggers only. Set ALICE_MIGRATE_DATABASE_URL to the owner role.")
	}
	if err := migrateWith(ctx, cfg.MigrationURL(), dbStartupBudget); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	pool, err := openDB(ctx, cfg.DatabaseURL, dbStartupBudget)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer pool.Close()

	clk := newClock()
	svc, err := buildServices(ctx, pool, cfg.KeysDir, clk)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	health, err := db.NewHealthChecker(pool, migrations.FS)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	catalog, err := i18n.Load(locales.FS)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	hosts := append([]string{"localhost", "127.0.0.1"}, cfg.TLSHosts...)
	if h, err := os.Hostname(); err == nil {
		hosts = append(hosts, h)
	}
	cert, err := tlscert.EnsureSelfSigned(cfg.KeysDir, hosts, clk.Now())
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	var apiServer *api.Server
	td := testDeps{
		OwnerURL: cfg.MigrationURL(),
		Setup:    svc.setup,
		Log:      log,
		ResetLimits: func() {
			if apiServer != nil {
				apiServer.ResetLimits()
			}
		},
	}
	deps := api.Deps{
		Health:         health,
		Catalog:        catalog,
		Version:        version.Get(),
		UI:             webui.UI(),
		Log:            log,
		Clock:          clk,
		Auth:           svc.auth,
		Setup:          svc.setup,
		Audit:          svc.audit,
		TrustedProxies: cfg.TrustedProxies,
		TestHandler:    testHandler(td),
	}
	apiServer = api.New(deps)

	errLog := slog.NewLogLogger(log.Handler(), slog.LevelWarn)
	public := &http.Server{
		Addr:              cfg.ListenAddr,
		Handler:           apiServer.Handler(),
		TLSConfig:         tlscert.ServerConfig(cert),
		ReadHeaderTimeout: 10 * time.Second,
		ReadTimeout:       30 * time.Second,
		WriteTimeout:      60 * time.Second,
		IdleTimeout:       120 * time.Second,
		ErrorLog:          errLog,
	}
	internal := &http.Server{
		Addr:              cfg.HealthAddr,
		Handler:           api.NewHealthHandler(deps),
		ReadHeaderTimeout: 10 * time.Second,
		ReadTimeout:       30 * time.Second,
		WriteTimeout:      60 * time.Second,
		IdleTimeout:       120 * time.Second,
		ErrorLog:          errLog,
	}

	errCh := make(chan error, 2)
	go func() { errCh <- ignoreClosed(public.ListenAndServeTLS("", "")) }()
	go func() { errCh <- ignoreClosed(internal.ListenAndServe()) }()
	log.Info("alice is ready", "https", cfg.ListenAddr, "health", cfg.HealthAddr)

	var runErr error
	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
		log.Info("shutting down")
	case runErr = <-errCh:
	}
	shutdownCtx, cancelShutdown := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
	defer cancelShutdown()
	_ = public.Shutdown(shutdownCtx)
	_ = internal.Shutdown(shutdownCtx)
	return runErr
}

func healthcheck(cfg config.Config) int {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 3*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodGet, "http://"+cfg.HealthAddr+"/healthz", nil)
	if err != nil {
		return 1
	}
	resp, err := http.DefaultClient.Do(req) //nolint:gosec // G107: address comes from local configuration, not user input
	if err != nil {
		return 1
	}
	defer func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() }()
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		return 1
	}
	return 0
}

func ignoreClosed(err error) error {
	if errors.Is(err, http.ErrServerClosed) {
		return nil
	}
	return err
}
```

```go
//go:build e2e

package main

func clockNow(t *testing.T, h http.Handler) time.Time {
	t.Helper()
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	h.ServeHTTP(rec, httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/test/clock", nil))
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code)
	var body struct{ Now string }
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &body))
	now, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, body.Now)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return now
}

func TestE2EClockCanBeAdvanced(t *testing.T) {
	t.Cleanup(e2eClock.Reset)
	h := testHandler(testDeps{})
	before := clockNow(t, h)

	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	h.ServeHTTP(rec, httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, "/test/clock", strings.NewReader(`{"advance_seconds":1860}`)))
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rec.Code)

	after := clockNow(t, h)
	assert.GreaterOrEqual(t, after.Sub(before), 31*time.Minute)
	assert.Less(t, after.Sub(before), 32*time.Minute)
	assert.GreaterOrEqual(t, newClock().Now().Sub(before), 31*time.Minute, "the server clock is the advanced clock")
}

func TestE2EClockRejectsNegativeOrMalformedInput(t *testing.T) {
	h := testHandler(testDeps{})
	for _, body := range []string{`{"advance_seconds":-5}`, `not json`} {
		rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
		h.ServeHTTP(rec, httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, "/test/clock", strings.NewReader(body)))
		assert.Equal(t, http.StatusBadRequest, rec.Code, body)
	}
}

func TestE2EUnknownTestEndpointIs404(t *testing.T) {
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	testHandler(testDeps{}).ServeHTTP(rec, httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/test/nope", nil))
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusNotFound, rec.Code)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement the e2e-only endpoints**

`cmd/alice/testapi_e2e.go`:
```go
//go:build e2e

package main

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"

	"alice/internal/platform/clock"
	"alice/internal/platform/db"
)

// e2eClock is the server clock in the e2e build; POST /test/clock moves it.
var e2eClock = clock.NewOffset(clock.Real{})

func newClock() clock.Clock { return e2eClock }

// resetTables are emptied by POST /test/reset. master_key_versions is kept
// because the master key file on the volume stays the same.
const resetSQL = `TRUNCATE users, user_mfa, recovery_codes, sessions, api_tokens, setup_sessions, settings, audit_log`

// testHandler serves the test-only endpoints of the e2e build (never shipped).
func testHandler(d testDeps) http.Handler {
	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /test/clock", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
		writeTestJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{"now": e2eClock.Now().Format(time.RFC3339Nano)})
	})
	mux.HandleFunc("POST /test/clock", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		var body struct {
			AdvanceSeconds float64 `json:"advance_seconds"`
		}
		if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&body); err != nil || body.AdvanceSeconds < 0 {
			writeTestJSON(w, http.StatusBadRequest, map[string]string{"error": "advance_seconds must be a number >= 0"})
			return
		}
		e2eClock.Advance(time.Duration(body.AdvanceSeconds * float64(time.Second)))
		writeTestJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{"now": e2eClock.Now().Format(time.RFC3339Nano)})
	})
	mux.HandleFunc("POST /test/reset", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		if err := resetInstall(r.Context(), d.OwnerURL); err != nil {
			d.Log.Error("test reset failed", "error", err.Error())
			writeTestJSON(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, map[string]string{"error": "reset failed"})
			return
		}
		e2eClock.Reset()
		d.Setup.ResetLockouts()
		d.ResetLimits()
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
	})
	mux.HandleFunc("POST /test/setup-token", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		code, err := d.Setup.ResetToken(r.Context())
		if err != nil {
			writeTestJSON(w, http.StatusConflict, map[string]string{"error": err.Error()})
			return
		}
		writeTestJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{"code": code})
	})
	mux.HandleFunc("/test/", func(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
		writeTestJSON(w, http.StatusNotFound, map[string]string{"error": "unknown test endpoint"})
	})
	return mux
}

// resetInstall empties every application table as the owner role. The audit
// triggers forbid TRUNCATE, so they are disabled inside the transaction only.
func resetInstall(ctx context.Context, ownerURL string) error {
	pool, err := pgxpool.New(ctx, ownerURL)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer pool.Close()
	return db.InTx(ctx, pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		for _, stmt := range []string{
			`ALTER TABLE audit_log DISABLE TRIGGER USER`,
			resetSQL,
			`ALTER TABLE audit_log ENABLE TRIGGER USER`,
		} {
			if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, stmt); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return nil
	})
}

func writeTestJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, v any) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
	w.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "no-store")
	w.WriteHeader(status)
	_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(v)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write the CLI integration test**

`cmd/alice/cli_integration_test.go`:
```go
//go:build integration

package main

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"os"
	"regexp"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/platform/clock"
	"alice/internal/testutil/pgtest"
)

func TestMain(m *testing.M) {
	os.Exit(pgtest.Main(m))
}

var setupCodeRE = regexp.MustCompile(`One-time setup code: [0-9A-Z]{6}-[0-9A-Z]{6}-[0-9A-Z]{6}-[0-9A-Z]{6}\n`)

// cliEnv points the CLI at the test database as the application role.
func cliEnv(t *testing.T, d *pgtest.DB) func(string) string {
	t.Helper()
	return env(map[string]string{"ALICE_DATABASE_URL": d.AppURL, "ALICE_KEYS_DIR": t.TempDir()})
}

func runCLI(t *testing.T, getenv func(string) string, args ...string) (int, string, string) {
	t.Helper()
	var out, errOut bytes.Buffer
	code := run(args, getenv, &out, &errOut)
	return code, out.String(), errOut.String()
}

func insertUser(t *testing.T, d *pgtest.DB, username, role string) uuid.UUID {
	t.Helper()
	id := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	_, err := d.Owner.Exec(context.Background(),
		`INSERT INTO users (id, username, display_name, password_hash, role, created_at, updated_at)
		 VALUES ($1, $2, $3, '$argon2id$v=19$m=65536,t=3,p=2$c2FsdA$aGFzaA', $4, now(), now())`, id, username, username, role)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return id
}

func TestSetupTokenResetCLI(t *testing.T) {
	d := pgtest.New(t)
	getenv := cliEnv(t, d)

	code, out, errOut := runCLI(t, getenv, "setup-token", "--reset")
	require.Equal(t, 0, code, errOut)
	assert.Regexp(t, setupCodeRE, out)

	code, out2, _ := runCLI(t, getenv, "setup-token", "--reset")
	require.Equal(t, 0, code)
	assert.NotEqual(t, out, out2, "every reset issues a new code")

	insertUser(t, d, "admin", "admin")
	code, _, errOut = runCLI(t, getenv, "setup-token", "--reset")
	assert.Equal(t, 1, code)
	assert.Contains(t, errOut, "administrator already exists")
}

func TestAuditVerifyCLI(t *testing.T) {
	d := pgtest.New(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	getenv := cliEnv(t, d)

	svc := audit.NewService(audit.New(clock.Real{}), d.App)
	for _, action := range []string{"test.first", "test.second"} {
		require.NoError(t, svc.Write(ctx, audit.Event{Actor: audit.SystemActor("test"), Action: action, Result: audit.Success}))
	}

	code, out, errOut := runCLI(t, getenv, "audit", "verify")
	require.Equal(t, 0, code, errOut)
	assert.Contains(t, out, "The audit log is intact")

	// Tamper as the owner role: the append-only trigger has to be switched off first.
	for _, stmt := range []string{
		`ALTER TABLE audit_log DISABLE TRIGGER audit_log_no_update_delete`,
		`UPDATE audit_log SET action = 'test.forged' WHERE seq = 1`,
		`ALTER TABLE audit_log ENABLE TRIGGER audit_log_no_update_delete`,
	} {
		_, err := d.Owner.Exec(ctx, stmt)
		require.NoError(t, err)
	}
	code, out, _ = runCLI(t, getenv, "audit", "verify")
	assert.Equal(t, 1, code)
	assert.Contains(t, out, "integrity problem at entry 1")
}

func TestAdminResetMFACLI(t *testing.T) {
	d := pgtest.New(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	getenv := cliEnv(t, d)

	id := insertUser(t, d, "bob", "operator")
	_, err := d.Owner.Exec(ctx,
		`INSERT INTO user_mfa (user_id, totp_secret_ct, totp_nonce, totp_wrapped_dek, totp_key_version, enrolled_at, created_at)
		 VALUES ($1, '\x01', '\x02', '\x03', 1, now(), now())`, id)
	require.NoError(t, err)

	code, out, errOut := runCLI(t, getenv, "admin", "reset-mfa", "BOB")
	require.Equal(t, 0, code, errOut)
	assert.Contains(t, out, "was removed")

	var left int
	require.NoError(t, d.Owner.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM user_mfa WHERE user_id = $1`, id).Scan(&left))
	assert.Equal(t, 0, left)
	var audited int
	require.NoError(t, d.Owner.QueryRow(ctx,
		`SELECT count(*) FROM audit_log WHERE action = 'user.mfa_reset' AND actor_type = 'system' AND actor_name = 'cli'`).Scan(&audited))
	assert.Equal(t, 1, audited)

	code, _, errOut = runCLI(t, getenv, "admin", "reset-mfa", "nobody")
	assert.Equal(t, 1, code)
	assert.Contains(t, errOut, `no user named "nobody"`)
}
```
