- [ ] **Step 12: Developer guide**

Replace `docs/dev/getting-started.md` with:

````markdown
# Getting Started (Developers)

## Prerequisites

- Linux x86_64 with Docker Engine 24+ and the Compose plugin
- Go 1.27.1 (see `go.mod`); install into `~/.local/go` and add `~/.local/go/bin:~/go/bin` to `PATH`
- Node.js 24 and npm
- `make`, `openssl`, `jq`

## First run

```bash
make tools        # golangci-lint and govulncheck at pinned versions
make web-install  # web dependencies from the lockfile
make up           # creates deploy/.env, builds the image, starts Alice and PostgreSQL
make setup-token  # prints the one-time setup code for the wizard
```

Open https://localhost (accept the temporary certificate warning). Alice opens the setup wizard at
`/setup`; enter the code printed by `make setup-token`. If port 443 is in use, set `ALICE_PORT=8443` in
`deploy/.env` and run `make up` again, then open https://localhost:8443.

`make up` is the development equivalent of `docker compose up`. It generates `deploy/.env` with random
database passwords on first use and keeps an existing file (adding any password that is missing).

The setup code works only while no administrator exists. `make setup-token` always issues a new code and
invalidates the previous one. After the first administrator is created, sign in with the username,
password and the 6-digit code from your authenticator app.

Upgrading a Phase 0 development stack: the database now needs the `alice_app` role, which PostgreSQL only
creates when its data volume is new. Run `make reset` once (this deletes the development database).

## Everyday commands

| Command | What it does |
|---|---|
| `make test` | Go unit tests with the race detector |
| `make test-integration` | Go integration tests (starts PostgreSQL in Docker) |
| `make web-test` | Web unit tests (Vitest) |
| `make up` / `make down` | Start / stop the release build of the stack (data is kept) |
| `make reset` | Delete the development database and keys and start again (asks nothing; development only) |
| `make setup-token` | Print a new one-time setup code for the wizard |
| `make e2e-up` | Start the **e2e build** of the stack (adds test-only `/test/*` endpoints) |
| `make e2e` | Browser tests against the e2e build (run `make e2e-up` first) |
| `make lint` / `make web-lint` | Linters |
| `make i18n-check` | English and Turkish catalogs have the same keys and placeholders |
| `make authz-doc` | Regenerate `docs/authz-matrix.md` from the route table |
| `make hardening-check` | Verify container hardening of the running stack (`docker inspect`) |
| `make vuln` / `make secrets-scan` / `make fs-scan` / `make image-scan` | Vulnerability and secret scanning |
| `make ci` | The full pipeline, as run in GitHub Actions |
| `make logs` | Follow the Alice logs |

### Operator commands inside the container

```bash
docker compose -f deploy/docker-compose.yml --env-file deploy/.env exec alice /usr/local/bin/alice setup-token --reset
docker compose -f deploy/docker-compose.yml --env-file deploy/.env exec alice /usr/local/bin/alice audit verify
docker compose -f deploy/docker-compose.yml --env-file deploy/.env exec alice /usr/local/bin/alice admin reset-mfa <username>
```

- `setup-token --reset` prints a new setup code; it refuses once an administrator exists.
- `audit verify` recomputes the audit log hash chain; exit code 0 means intact, 1 means the output names
  the first broken entry.
- `admin reset-mfa <username>` removes a user's authenticator app (for example the only administrator lost
  their phone and their recovery codes). The user sets up a new app at their next sign-in. The action is
  recorded in the audit log with the actor `cli`.

### The e2e build

`make e2e-up` builds the image with the Go build tag `e2e` (`deploy/docker-compose.e2e.yml`). Only that
build serves `GET/POST /test/clock` (read or move the server clock forward), `POST /test/reset` (empty
all tables) and `POST /test/setup-token` (issue a setup code). Release images are built without the tag;
a unit test in `cmd/alice` fails if the release build ever contains a test endpoint or the demo clock.
Never run the e2e build for real data.

## Configuration

Alice reads these environment variables (`internal/platform/config`):

| Variable | Default | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `ALICE_DATABASE_URL` | none (required) | PostgreSQL URL for the application role `alice_app`. Contains the password; never logged. |
| `ALICE_MIGRATE_DATABASE_URL` | empty | PostgreSQL URL for the owner role that runs migrations. If empty, `ALICE_DATABASE_URL` is used for both and Alice logs a warning, because the audit log is then protected by triggers only. |
| `ALICE_LISTEN_ADDR` | `:8443` | Public HTTPS listener |
| `ALICE_HEALTH_ADDR` | `127.0.0.1:8081` | Container-local plain HTTP listener for `/livez` and `/healthz` |
| `ALICE_KEYS_DIR` | `/opt/alice/keys` | Directory for the HTTPS certificate and the master key (`master.key`, mode 0400) |
| `ALICE_LOG_LEVEL` | `info` | `debug`, `info`, `warn` or `error` |
| `ALICE_TLS_HOSTS` | empty | Extra host names or IPs for the temporary certificate, comma-separated |
| `ALICE_TRUSTED_PROXIES` | empty | Comma-separated IPs or CIDRs of reverse proxies whose `X-Forwarded-For` is believed. Empty means the header is ignored. |

The compose stack reads `deploy/.env`:

| Variable | Default | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `ALICE_DB_PASSWORD` | generated | Password of the owner role `alice` (migrations) |
| `ALICE_APP_DB_PASSWORD` | generated | Password of the application role `alice_app`, created by `deploy/postgres/initdb` on a new volume |
| `ALICE_PORT` | `443` | Host port published for the HTTPS listener (container port 8443) |
| `ALICE_TLS_HOSTS` | empty | Passed through to Alice |

## Notes

- The database connection uses `sslmode=disable` because PostgreSQL is only reachable on the
  internal Docker network `backend`, which has no route outside the host.
- Two database roles: migrations run as the owner `alice`; the running application uses `alice_app`,
  which may only read and append to `audit_log` (no update, delete or truncate).
- **The master key.** On the very first start Alice creates `master.key` in the keys directory. It
  encrypts TOTP secrets and, later, all stored credentials. If the database already holds encrypted data
  and the file is missing, has other contents, or is readable by other users, Alice refuses to start
  and says why; it never creates a new key over existing data. Deleting the `alice-keys` volume without
  also deleting the database therefore stops Alice until you restore the file (or `make reset`).
- Passwords, database passwords and secrets never appear in logs: the logger redacts fields such as
  `password`, `token` and `secret`.
- Alice creates a temporary self-signed certificate (valid 90 days, checked on every start and renewed
  when it is expired or within 7 days of expiry; restart the container to renew it). A certificate you
  place in the keys directory yourself is never modified.
- **Deleting `deploy/.env` requires `make reset`.** PostgreSQL keeps the passwords it was first
  initialised with in the `pgdata` volume, so new random passwords in a regenerated `.env` will not match.
- Translations: shared strings live in `locales/common/{en,tr}.json`, UI-only strings in
  `web/src/locales/{en,tr}.json`. Add every key to both languages; `make i18n-check` enforces it.
- The web build is written to `internal/webui/dist/app` (git-ignored) and embedded into the binary.

## Troubleshooting

Every API error carries a stable `ALC-<AREA>-<NNNN>` code. Meaning, likely cause and remedy for each
code are in [docs/troubleshooting.md](../troubleshooting.md). Add a section there whenever you add an
error code; a unit test fails if one is missing.
````

  - Task 5: `secrets.LoadOrCreateMasterKey`, `secrets.KDFParams`; Task 4: `(*secrets.Keyring).Seal(secrets.Location, []byte) (secrets.Envelope, error)`.
  - Task 6: `audit.New`, `audit.NewService`; Task 8: `auth.Argon2Params`, `auth.NewHasher`; Task 10: `auth.NewService`, `auth.Config`; Task 12: `setup.New`, `setup.Config`, `(*setup.Service).ResetToken`.
  - Task 13: `api.New(Deps).Handler()`, constants `sessionCookie`, `setupCookie`; the Phase 0 helpers `httpMethod`, `specOps()` and `operation.key()` in `internal/api/openapi_test.go`; every route and error code of Tasks 13-15 (the suite drives them over HTTP).

- Produces (package `api`, build tag `integration`, used by Task 18):
  - `TestMain` for all `internal/api` integration tests (no other integration test file in this package may declare one).
  - `newStack(t, trusted ...netip.Prefix) *stack`; `(*stack).start()` (rebuild services = restart); `client(ip) *client`, `freshClient() *client`; `(*client).do(method, path string, body any, opts ...reqOpt) *httptest.ResponseRecorder`; type `reqOpt func(*http.Request)`.
  - `setupAdmin(a *account, passphrase string, afterEnroll func()) *client`, `signIn(c, a)`, `firstSignIn(c, a)`, `stepUp(c, a)`, `createUser(admin *client, adminAcct *account, username, role, password string) *account`, `login(username, password) *httptest.ResponseRecorder`, `count(table) int`, `totpNow(secret) string`, `recorded() []exchange`.
  - Helpers `requireStatus`, `requireError(t, rec, status, code)`, `decodeBody[T]`, types `account`, `exchange`, `sessionBody`, `enrollmentBody`.

What the suite proves (spec §25.2): S-01 no planted secret in logs at DEBUG level; S-02/S-03 responses (success and error) carry a secret only in its documented one-time response — enrollment secret in the two enrollment responses, recovery codes in the three responses that issue them, the API token in `POST /api/v1/tokens` — and session IDs never appear in a body; S-04 the raw database (every table, bytea hex-encoded) holds no plaintext secret, passwords and recovery codes are Argon2id hashes, each TOTP secret has its own DEK, identical plaintexts encrypt differently; S-05 audit rows (part of the dump) hold no secret and failed sign-ins are audited; S-08 no response schema in `api/openapi.yaml` exposes password/passphrase/hash/private-key fields, and the one-time fields appear only where documented; S-10 `/test/*` is absent in this build. S-06, S-07, S-09 and S-11 concern backups, panics with key material and private-key download, which arrive in later phases.

`internal/api/stack_integration_test.go`:
```go
//go:build integration

package api

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"net/netip"
	"os"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"sync/atomic"
	"testing"
	"testing/fstest"
	"time"

	"github.com/pquerna/otp"
	"github.com/pquerna/otp/totp"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/audit"
	"alice/internal/auth"
	"alice/internal/i18n"
	"alice/internal/platform/clock"
	"alice/internal/platform/db"
	"alice/internal/platform/logging"
	"alice/internal/secrets"
	"alice/internal/setup"
	"alice/internal/testutil/pgtest"
	"alice/internal/version"
	"alice/locales"
	"alice/migrations"
)

// Integration suites for the whole HTTP stack: real services, real PostgreSQL
// (one fresh database per test), a fake clock and cheap Argon2id parameters.
// Production Argon2id parameters are asserted in internal/auth and internal/secrets.

func TestMain(m *testing.M) {
	os.Exit(pgtest.Main(m))
}

var stackStart = time.Date(2026, 10, 1, 9, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

var fastArgon = auth.Argon2Params{MemoryKiB: 64, Time: 1, Threads: 1, SaltLen: 16, KeyLen: 32}

const stackIndexHTML = `<!doctype html><html><head><meta name="csp-nonce" content="__CSP_NONCE__"></head><body><div id="root"></div></body></html>`

// syncBuffer collects log output from concurrent handlers.
type syncBuffer struct {
	mu sync.Mutex
	b  bytes.Buffer
}

func (s *syncBuffer) Write(p []byte) (int, error) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	return s.b.Write(p)
}

func (s *syncBuffer) String() string {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	return s.b.String()
}

// exchange is one recorded request/response pair, scanned by the leak tests.
type exchange struct {
	method, path string
	status       int
	header       http.Header
	body         []byte
}

func (e exchange) key() string { return e.method + " " + e.path }

type stack struct {
	t         *testing.T
	db        *pgtest.DB
	clock     *clock.Fake
	keysDir   string
	logs      *syncBuffer
	trusted   []netip.Prefix
	keyring   *secrets.Keyring
	auth      *auth.Service
	setup     *setup.Service
	handler   http.Handler
	setupCode string

	mu        sync.Mutex
	exchanges []exchange
	ips       atomic.Int64
}

func newStack(t *testing.T, trusted ...netip.Prefix) *stack {
	t.Helper()
	s := &stack{
		t:       t,
		db:      pgtest.New(t),
		clock:   clock.NewFake(stackStart),
		keysDir: t.TempDir(),
		logs:    &syncBuffer{},
		trusted: trusted,
	}
	s.start()
	return s
}

// start builds every service and the HTTP handler on the stack's database,
// key directory and clock. Calling it again simulates a process restart:
// in-memory state (rate limiters, lockout counters of the setup code) is lost.
func (s *stack) start() {
	t := s.t
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	keyring, err := secrets.LoadOrCreateMasterKey(ctx, s.db.App, s.keysDir, s.clock.Now())
	require.NoError(t, err)
	auditLog := audit.New(s.clock)
	hasher := auth.NewHasher(fastArgon, 4)
	s.keyring = keyring
	s.auth = auth.NewService(auth.Config{
		Pool: s.db.App, Keyring: keyring, Audit: auditLog, Clock: s.clock, Hasher: hasher, RecoveryHasher: hasher,
	})
	s.setup = setup.New(setup.Config{
		Pool: s.db.App, Auth: s.auth, Keyring: keyring, Audit: auditLog, Clock: s.clock, Hasher: hasher,
		BackupKDF: secrets.KDFParams{MemoryKiB: 64, Time: 1, Threads: 1},
	})
	catalog, err := i18n.Load(locales.FS)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	health, err := db.NewHealthChecker(s.db.App, migrations.FS)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	s.handler = New(Deps{
		Health:         health,
		Catalog:        catalog,
		Version:        version.Info{Version: "test", Commit: "test", BuildTime: "test"},
		UI:             fstest.MapFS{"index.html": {Data: []byte(stackIndexHTML)}},
		Log:            logging.New(s.logs, slog.LevelDebug),
		Clock:          s.clock,
		Auth:           s.auth,
		Setup:          s.setup,
		Audit:          audit.NewService(auditLog, s.db.App),
		TrustedProxies: s.trusted,
	}).Handler()
}

func (s *stack) record(method, path string, rec *httptest.ResponseRecorder) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.exchanges = append(s.exchanges, exchange{
		method: method, path: strings.SplitN(path, "?", 2)[0], status: rec.Code,
		header: rec.Header().Clone(), body: append([]byte(nil), rec.Body.Bytes()...),
	})
}

func (s *stack) recorded() []exchange {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]exchange(nil), s.exchanges...)
}

// client is one browser (or API client) with its own cookies, CSRF token and IP.
type client struct {
	s       *stack
	ip      string
	cookies map[string]string
	csrf    string
	bearer  string
}

func (s *stack) client(ip string) *client {
	return &client{s: s, ip: ip, cookies: map[string]string{}}
}

// freshClient returns a client with a new, unique source IP (so per-IP login limits never interfere).
func (s *stack) freshClient() *client {
	n := s.ips.Add(1)
	return s.client(fmt.Sprintf("10.9.%d.%d", n/250, n%250+1))
}

type reqOpt func(*http.Request)

// do sends one request the way the SPA does: same-origin headers, the CSRF
// token on state-changing requests, and the cookies the server set earlier.
func (c *client) do(method, path string, body any, opts ...reqOpt) *httptest.ResponseRecorder {
	c.s.t.Helper()
	var rdr io.Reader
	switch b := body.(type) {
	case nil:
	case string:
		rdr = strings.NewReader(b)
	case []byte:
		rdr = bytes.NewReader(b)
	default:
		j, err := json.Marshal(b)
		require.NoError(c.s.t, err)
		rdr = bytes.NewReader(j)
	}
	req := httptest.NewRequest(method, "https://alice.test"+path, rdr)
	req.RemoteAddr = c.ip + ":40000"
	req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/json")
	if rdr != nil {
		req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	}
	if method != http.MethodGet && method != http.MethodHead {
		req.Header.Set("Origin", "https://alice.test")
		req.Header.Set("Sec-Fetch-Site", "same-origin")
		if c.csrf != "" {
			req.Header.Set("X-CSRF-Token", c.csrf)
		}
	}
	for name, value := range c.cookies {
		req.AddCookie(&http.Cookie{Name: name, Value: value})
	}
	if c.bearer != "" {
		req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+c.bearer)
	}
	for _, o := range opts {
		o(req)
	}
	rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
	c.s.handler.ServeHTTP(rec, req)
	c.s.record(method, path, rec)

	for _, ck := range responseCookies(rec) {
		if ck.MaxAge < 0 || ck.Value == "" {
			delete(c.cookies, ck.Name)
		} else {
			c.cookies[ck.Name] = ck.Value
		}
	}
	var probe struct {
		CSRFToken string `json:"csrf_token"`
	}
	if json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &probe) == nil && probe.CSRFToken != "" {
		c.csrf = probe.CSRFToken
	}
	return rec
}

func responseCookies(rec *httptest.ResponseRecorder) []*http.Cookie {
	res := rec.Result()
	defer func() { _ = res.Body.Close() }()
	return res.Cookies()
}

func requireStatus(t *testing.T, rec *httptest.ResponseRecorder, want int) {
	t.Helper()
	require.Equal(t, want, rec.Code, "body: %s", rec.Body.String())
}

type apiError struct {
	Error struct {
		Code       string `json:"code"`
		Reason     string `json:"reason"`
		MessageKey string `json:"message_key"`
		Message    string `json:"message"`
		RequestID  string `json:"request_id"`
	} `json:"error"`
}

// requireError asserts the status and the stable ALC code of an error response.
func requireError(t *testing.T, rec *httptest.ResponseRecorder, status int, code string) {
	t.Helper()
	requireStatus(t, rec, status)
	var e apiError
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &e), rec.Body.String())
	require.Equal(t, code, e.Error.Code, rec.Body.String())
}

func decodeBody[T any](t *testing.T, rec *httptest.ResponseRecorder) T {
	t.Helper()
	var v T
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &v), rec.Body.String())
	return v
}

// totpNow is the code an authenticator app shows at the stack's current time.
func (s *stack) totpNow(secret string) string {
	s.t.Helper()
	code, err := totp.GenerateCodeCustom(secret, s.clock.Now(), totp.ValidateOpts{
		Period: 30, Digits: otp.DigitsSix, Algorithm: otp.AlgorithmSHA1,
	})
	require.NoError(s.t, err)
	return code
}

// account is a user as the tests know it.
type account struct {
	id       string
	username string
	password string
	secret   string   // TOTP secret base32
	recovery []string // current recovery codes
}

type enrollmentBody struct {
	Secret     string `json:"secret"`
	OtpauthURI string `json:"otpauth_uri"`
	QRPNG      string `json:"qr_png"`
}

type sessionBody struct {
	User struct {
		ID       string `json:"id"`
		Username string `json:"username"`
		Role     string `json:"role"`
	} `json:"user"`
	CSRFToken     string   `json:"csrf_token"`
	RecoveryCodes []string `json:"recovery_codes"`
}

// setupAdmin runs the Phase 1 wizard on a fresh install and returns the
// signed-in administrator's browser. afterEnroll (optional) runs while the
// pending administrator is stored in setup_sessions.
func (s *stack) setupAdmin(a *account, passphrase string, afterEnroll func()) *client {
	t := s.t
	t.Helper()
	code, err := s.setup.ResetToken(context.Background())
	require.NoError(t, err)
	s.setupCode = code

	c := s.client("198.51.100.10")
	requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/session", map[string]string{"code": code}), http.StatusOK)
	requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPut, "/api/v1/setup/language", map[string]string{"language": "en"}), http.StatusNoContent)
	rec := c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/admin/enrollment", map[string]string{
		"username": a.username, "display_name": "Ada Admin", "email": "", "password": a.password,
	})
	requireStatus(t, rec, http.StatusOK)
	a.secret = decodeBody[enrollmentBody](t, rec).Secret
	require.NotEmpty(t, a.secret)
	if afterEnroll != nil {
		afterEnroll()
	}
	rec = c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/admin", map[string]string{"code": s.totpNow(a.secret)})
	requireStatus(t, rec, http.StatusOK)
	sess := decodeBody[sessionBody](t, rec)
	a.id, a.recovery = sess.User.ID, sess.RecoveryCodes
	require.Len(t, a.recovery, 10)
	require.NotEmpty(t, c.cookies[sessionCookie])
	require.Empty(t, c.cookies[setupCookie], "the wizard cookie is cleared once the admin exists")

	requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/recovery-passphrase", map[string]string{
		"passphrase": passphrase, "passphrase_confirm": passphrase,
	}), http.StatusNoContent)
	requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/complete", nil), http.StatusOK)
	return c
}

// signIn logs an enrolled account in on c. The clock moves 31 s first so the
// TOTP code is never one that was already used.
func (s *stack) signIn(c *client, a *account) {
	t := s.t
	t.Helper()
	s.clock.Advance(31 * time.Second)
	rec := c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", map[string]string{"username": a.username, "password": a.password})
	requireStatus(t, rec, http.StatusOK)
	require.Equal(t, "mfa_required", decodeBody[map[string]string](t, rec)["status"])
	requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", map[string]string{"code": s.totpNow(a.secret)}), http.StatusOK)
}

// firstSignIn logs a new user in for the first time, enrolling TOTP.
func (s *stack) firstSignIn(c *client, a *account) {
	t := s.t
	t.Helper()
	s.clock.Advance(31 * time.Second)
	rec := c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", map[string]string{"username": a.username, "password": a.password})
	requireStatus(t, rec, http.StatusOK)
	require.Equal(t, "mfa_enrollment_required", decodeBody[map[string]string](t, rec)["status"])
	rec = c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/enroll", nil)
	requireStatus(t, rec, http.StatusOK)
	a.secret = decodeBody[enrollmentBody](t, rec).Secret
	rec = c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", map[string]string{"code": s.totpNow(a.secret)})
	requireStatus(t, rec, http.StatusOK)
	a.recovery = decodeBody[sessionBody](t, rec).RecoveryCodes
	require.Len(t, a.recovery, 10)
}

// stepUp re-confirms the TOTP code so admin-only changes are allowed for 5 minutes.
func (s *stack) stepUp(c *client, a *account) {
	s.t.Helper()
	s.clock.Advance(31 * time.Second)
	requireStatus(s.t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/stepup", map[string]string{"code": s.totpNow(a.secret)}), http.StatusOK)
}

// createUser creates a user as the (stepped-up) admin and returns the account.
func (s *stack) createUser(admin *client, adminAcct *account, username, role, password string) *account {
	t := s.t
	t.Helper()
	s.stepUp(admin, adminAcct)
	rec := admin.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/users", map[string]string{
		"username": username, "display_name": "User " + username, "email": "", "role": role, "password": password,
	})
	requireStatus(t, rec, http.StatusCreated)
	return &account{id: decodeBody[map[string]any](t, rec)["id"].(string), username: username, password: password}
}

// login attempts the password step from a fresh IP address.
func (s *stack) login(username, password string) *httptest.ResponseRecorder {
	return s.freshClient().do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", map[string]string{"username": username, "password": password})
}

// count returns the number of rows in table (read as the owner role).
func (s *stack) count(table string) int {
	s.t.Helper()
	var n int
	require.NoError(s.t, s.db.Owner.QueryRow(context.Background(), "SELECT count(*) FROM "+table).Scan(&n))
	return n
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the leak suite**

`internal/api/leak_integration_test.go`:
```go
//go:build integration

package api

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"encoding/hex"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"path"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
	"go.yaml.in/yaml/v3"

	apispec "alice/api"
	"alice/internal/secrets"
)

// Secret-leak suite (spec §25.2, S-01..S-05, S-08, S-10).

// canary returns a recognizable secret value that is unique per run.
func canary(label string) string {
	return "CANARY-" + rand.Text() + "-" + label
}

// secretValue is one secret the tests planted or received, and the responses
// (method + path) in which it may legitimately appear.
type secretValue struct {
	name    string
	value   string
	allowed map[string]bool
}

func only(keys ...string) map[string]bool {
	m := map[string]bool{}
	for _, k := range keys {
		m[k] = true
	}
	return m
}

// variants are the spellings of a secret that must not appear: as typed,
// without dashes (recovery codes), and hex-encoded (bytea columns in to_jsonb).
func variants(v string) []string {
	out := []string{v, hex.EncodeToString([]byte(v))}
	if stripped := strings.ReplaceAll(v, "-", ""); stripped != v {
		out = append(out, stripped)
	}
	return out
}

var privateKeyMarkers = []string{"PRIVATE KEY-----", "OPENSSH PRIVATE KEY", "PuTTY-User-Key-File"}

var dumpTables = []string{
	"settings", "users", "user_mfa", "recovery_codes", "sessions", "api_tokens",
	"setup_sessions", "master_key_versions", "audit_log",
}

// dump renders every row of every Alice table as JSON text (bytea as hex).
func (s *stack) dump() string {
	s.t.Helper()
	var b strings.Builder
	for _, table := range dumpTables {
		var rows string
		require.NoError(s.t, s.db.Owner.QueryRow(context.Background(),
			"SELECT coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(t)::text, E'\\n'), '') FROM "+table+" t").Scan(&rows))
		b.WriteString(table + ":\n" + rows + "\n")
	}
	return b.String()
}

func TestSecretsNeverLeak(t *testing.T) {
	s := newStack(t)
	ctx := context.Background()

	adminPW := canary("admin-password")
	passphrase := canary("recovery-passphrase")
	operatorPW := canary("operator-password")
	wrongPW := canary("wrong-password")
	junk := canary("junk")

	// A wrong setup code before the wizard starts (S-03).
	anon := s.client("198.51.100.99")
	rec := anon.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/session", map[string]string{"code": junk})
	assert.GreaterOrEqual(t, rec.Code, 400)

	admin := &account{username: "ada", password: adminPW}
	var duringWizard string
	adminBrowser := s.setupAdmin(admin, passphrase, func() { duringWizard = s.dump() })
	bootstrapCodes := admin.recovery

	// Sign in again, step up, and regenerate recovery codes.
	browser := s.client("198.51.100.11")
	s.signIn(browser, admin)
	s.stepUp(browser, admin)
	rec = browser.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/recovery-codes", nil)
	requireStatus(t, rec, http.StatusOK)
	regenerated := decodeBody[map[string][]string](t, rec)["recovery_codes"]
	require.Len(t, regenerated, 10)

	// Create and use an API token.
	rec = browser.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/tokens", map[string]any{"name": "ci", "scope": "read", "expires_in_days": 30})
	requireStatus(t, rec, http.StatusCreated)
	token := decodeBody[map[string]any](t, rec)["token"].(string)
	require.True(t, strings.HasPrefix(token, "alice_"))
	api := s.client("198.51.100.12")
	api.bearer = token
	requireStatus(t, api.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/version", nil), http.StatusOK)

	// Create an operator who enrolls TOTP at first sign-in.
	operator := s.createUser(adminBrowser, admin, "otto", "operator", operatorPW)
	s.firstSignIn(s.client("198.51.100.13"), operator)

	// Sign in with a recovery code.
	rc := s.client("198.51.100.14")
	s.clock.Advance(31 * time.Second)
	requireStatus(t, rc.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", map[string]string{"username": "ada", "password": adminPW}), http.StatusOK)
	requireStatus(t, rc.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", map[string]string{"recovery_code": regenerated[0]}), http.StatusOK)

	// Failure paths (S-03): wrong password, unknown user, wrong code, bad JSON, oversized body.
	requireError(t, s.login("ada", wrongPW), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0005")
	requireError(t, s.login("nobody-"+junk, wrongPW), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0005")
	bad := s.freshClient()
	requireStatus(t, bad.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", map[string]string{"username": "otto", "password": operatorPW}), http.StatusOK)
	requireError(t, bad.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", map[string]string{"code": "000000"}), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0006")
	requireError(t, s.freshClient().do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login",
		`{"username":"ada","password":"`+junk+`","extra":"`+junk+`"}`), http.StatusBadRequest, "ALC-SYS-0004")
	requireError(t, s.freshClient().do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login",
		`{"username":"ada","password":"`+junk), http.StatusBadRequest, "ALC-SYS-0004")
	big := `{"username":"ada","password":"` + strings.Repeat(junk, (2<<20)/len(junk)) + `"}`
	requireError(t, s.freshClient().do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", big), http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge, "ALC-SYS-0007")
	rec = browser.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/setup/recovery-passphrase", map[string]string{"passphrase": junk, "passphrase_confirm": junk + "x"})
	assert.GreaterOrEqual(t, rec.Code, 400)

	secretsUnderTest := []secretValue{
		{"admin password", adminPW, nil},
		{"operator password", operatorPW, nil},
		{"wrong password", wrongPW, nil},
		{"junk request data", junk, nil},
		{"recovery passphrase", passphrase, nil},
		{"setup code", s.setupCode, nil},
		{"admin TOTP secret", admin.secret, only("POST /api/v1/setup/admin/enrollment")},
		{"operator TOTP secret", operator.secret, only("POST /api/v1/auth/mfa/enroll")},
		{"API token", token, only("POST /api/v1/tokens")},
	}
	for _, c := range bootstrapCodes {
		secretsUnderTest = append(secretsUnderTest, secretValue{"bootstrap recovery code", c, only("POST /api/v1/setup/admin")})
	}
	for _, c := range regenerated {
		secretsUnderTest = append(secretsUnderTest, secretValue{"regenerated recovery code", c, only("POST /api/v1/auth/recovery-codes")})
	}
	for _, c := range operator.recovery {
		secretsUnderTest = append(secretsUnderTest, secretValue{"operator recovery code", c, only("POST /api/v1/auth/mfa/verify")})
	}
	// Session cookie values may only travel in Set-Cookie headers, never in a body, log or row.
	for _, e := range s.recorded() {
		for _, line := range e.header.Values("Set-Cookie") {
			if ck, err := http.ParseSetCookie(line); err == nil && ck.Value != "" {
				secretsUnderTest = append(secretsUnderTest, secretValue{"cookie " + ck.Name, ck.Value, nil})
			}
		}
	}

	t.Run("S-01 logs contain no secret", func(t *testing.T) {
		logs := s.logs.String()
		for _, sec := range secretsUnderTest {
			for _, v := range variants(sec.value) {
				assert.NotContains(t, logs, v, sec.name)
			}
		}
	})

	t.Run("S-02 S-03 responses contain secrets only where documented", func(t *testing.T) {
		exchanges := s.recorded()
		require.NotEmpty(t, exchanges)
		for _, e := range exchanges {
			headers := e.header.Clone()
			headers.Del("Set-Cookie")
			var hdr strings.Builder
			_ = headers.Write(&hdr)
			text := string(e.body) + "\n" + hdr.String()
			for _, sec := range secretsUnderTest {
				if sec.allowed[e.key()] {
					continue
				}
				for _, v := range variants(sec.value) {
					assert.NotContains(t, text, v, "%s leaked in %s (status %d)", sec.name, e.key(), e.status)
				}
			}
			for _, m := range privateKeyMarkers {
				assert.NotContains(t, text, m, e.key())
			}
		}
	})

	t.Run("S-04 S-05 database rows hold no plaintext secret", func(t *testing.T) {
		assert.Contains(t, duringWizard, "$argon2id$", "the snapshot must include the pending administrator")
		all := duringWizard + "\n" + s.dump()
		for _, sec := range secretsUnderTest {
			for _, v := range variants(sec.value) {
				assert.NotContains(t, all, v, sec.name)
			}
		}
		for _, m := range privateKeyMarkers {
			assert.NotContains(t, all, m)
		}

		rows, err := s.db.Owner.Query(ctx, `SELECT password_hash FROM users`)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		for rows.Next() {
			var h string
			require.NoError(t, rows.Scan(&h))
			assert.True(t, strings.HasPrefix(h, "$argon2id$v=19$"), h)
		}
		require.NoError(t, rows.Err())
		rows.Close()

		var hashes, distinct int
		require.NoError(t, s.db.Owner.QueryRow(ctx,
			`SELECT count(*), count(DISTINCT code_hash) FROM recovery_codes WHERE code_hash LIKE '$argon2id$%'`).Scan(&hashes, &distinct))
		assert.Equal(t, hashes, distinct)
		assert.Equal(t, s.count("recovery_codes"), hashes, "every recovery code is stored as an Argon2id hash")

		var mfaRows, distinctDEKs, distinctCT int
		require.NoError(t, s.db.Owner.QueryRow(ctx,
			`SELECT count(*), count(DISTINCT totp_wrapped_dek), count(DISTINCT totp_secret_ct) FROM user_mfa`).Scan(&mfaRows, &distinctDEKs, &distinctCT))
		assert.Equal(t, 2, mfaRows)
		assert.Equal(t, mfaRows, distinctDEKs, "every secret has its own DEK")
		assert.Equal(t, mfaRows, distinctCT)

		loc := secrets.Location{Table: "user_mfa", RowID: admin.id, Column: "totp_secret"}
		one, err := s.keyring.Seal(loc, []byte(adminPW))
		require.NoError(t, err)
		two, err := s.keyring.Seal(loc, []byte(adminPW))
		require.NoError(t, err)
		assert.NotEqual(t, one.Ciphertext, two.Ciphertext, "identical secrets encrypt differently")
		assert.NotEqual(t, one.WrappedDEK, two.WrappedDEK)
		assert.NotEqual(t, one.Nonce, two.Nonce)

		var failures int
		require.NoError(t, s.db.Owner.QueryRow(ctx,
			`SELECT count(*) FROM audit_log WHERE action = 'auth.login' AND result = 'failure'`).Scan(&failures))
		assert.GreaterOrEqual(t, failures, 2, "failed sign-ins are audited (without the typed password)")
	})

	t.Run("S-10 test endpoints are absent", func(t *testing.T) {
		for _, m := range []string{http.MethodGet, http.MethodPost} {
			requireError(t, s.freshClient().do(m, "/test/clock", nil), http.StatusNotFound, "ALC-SYS-0001")
		}
	})
}

// --- S-08: response schemas in the OpenAPI document ---

// forbiddenResponseFields may never appear in any response schema.
var forbiddenResponseFields = []string{
	"password", "current_password", "new_password", "password_hash", "passphrase", "passphrase_confirm",
	"private_key", "token_hash", "totp_secret", "code_hash", "master_key",
}

// oneTimeFields may appear only in the responses of the listed operations.
var oneTimeFields = map[string]map[string]bool{
	"secret":         only("POST /api/v1/setup/admin/enrollment", "POST /api/v1/auth/mfa/enroll"),
	"otpauth_uri":    only("POST /api/v1/setup/admin/enrollment", "POST /api/v1/auth/mfa/enroll"),
	"qr_png":         only("POST /api/v1/setup/admin/enrollment", "POST /api/v1/auth/mfa/enroll"),
	"token":          only("POST /api/v1/tokens"),
	"recovery_codes": only("POST /api/v1/setup/admin", "POST /api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", "POST /api/v1/auth/recovery-codes"),
}

// asMap accepts both map shapes yaml.v3 produces (unquoted numeric keys such
// as 200: decode as ints, so their keys are rendered with fmt.Sprint).
func asMap(v any) map[string]any {
	switch m := v.(type) {
	case map[string]any:
		return m
	case map[any]any:
		out := make(map[string]any, len(m))
		for k, val := range m {
			out[fmt.Sprint(k)] = val
		}
		return out
	}
	return nil
}

// schemaProperties collects every property name reachable from schema.
func schemaProperties(schema any, schemas map[string]any, seen map[string]bool, out map[string]bool) {
	m := asMap(schema)
	if m == nil {
		return
	}
	if ref, ok := m["$ref"].(string); ok {
		name := path.Base(ref)
		if strings.Contains(ref, "/schemas/") && !seen[name] {
			seen[name] = true
			schemaProperties(schemas[name], schemas, seen, out)
		}
		return
	}
	for name, prop := range asMap(m["properties"]) {
		out[name] = true
		schemaProperties(prop, schemas, seen, out)
	}
	for _, key := range []string{"items", "additionalProperties"} {
		schemaProperties(m[key], schemas, seen, out)
	}
	for _, key := range []string{"allOf", "oneOf", "anyOf"} {
		if list, ok := m[key].([]any); ok {
			for _, sub := range list {
				schemaProperties(sub, schemas, seen, out)
			}
		}
	}
}

// responseFields maps "METHOD path" to the property names of its 2xx responses.
func responseFields(t *testing.T) map[string]map[string]bool {
	t.Helper()
	var doc map[string]any
	require.NoError(t, yaml.Unmarshal(apispec.YAML, &doc))
	components := asMap(doc["components"])
	schemas := asMap(components["schemas"])
	sharedResponses := asMap(components["responses"])
	out := map[string]map[string]bool{}
	for p, item := range asMap(doc["paths"]) {
		for method, op := range asMap(item) {
			hm := httpMethod(method)
			if hm == "" {
				continue
			}
			fields := map[string]bool{}
			for status, resp := range asMap(asMap(op)["responses"]) {
				if !strings.HasPrefix(status, "2") {
					continue
				}
				r := asMap(resp)
				if ref, ok := r["$ref"].(string); ok {
					r = asMap(sharedResponses[path.Base(ref)])
				}
				for _, media := range asMap(r["content"]) {
					schemaProperties(asMap(media)["schema"], schemas, map[string]bool{}, fields)
				}
			}
			out[hm+" "+p] = fields
		}
	}
	return out
}

func TestOpenAPIResponsesExposeNoSecretFields(t *testing.T) {
	fields := responseFields(t)
	require.True(t, fields["GET /api/v1/auth/session"]["csrf_token"], "the walker must see real response schemas")

	var ops []string
	for op := range fields {
		ops = append(ops, op)
	}
	sort.Strings(ops)
	for _, op := range ops {
		for _, f := range forbiddenResponseFields {
			assert.False(t, fields[op][f], "%s exposes %q in a response", op, f)
		}
		for f, allowed := range oneTimeFields {
			if fields[op][f] {
				assert.True(t, allowed[op], "%s exposes the one-time field %q", op, f)
			}
		}
	}
	for f, allowed := range oneTimeFields {
		for op := range allowed {
			assert.True(t, fields[op][f], "%s should document %q in its response", op, f)
		}
	}
}
```

### Task 18: Session, CSRF and rate-limit integration suite

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/api/session_integration_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the Task 17 harness (`newStack`, `start`, `client`, `freshClient`, `do`, `reqOpt`, `setupAdmin`, `signIn`, `firstSignIn`, `stepUp`, `createUser`, `login`, `count`, `totpNow`, `recorded`, `requireStatus`, `requireError`, `account`); Task 10 constants `auth.DelayThreshold`, `auth.LockThreshold`, `auth.LockDuration`, `auth.MaxDelay`; Task 13 constants `sessionCookie`, `setupCookie`, the Phase 0 `specOps()`; the routes and error codes of Tasks 13-15.
- Produces: `withHeader(name, value string) reqOpt`, `withoutHeader(name string) reqOpt`, `(*stack).countWhere(table, cond string) int` (test helpers only).

Coverage (spec §25.3, §25.4): A-01 cookie attributes and no session ID in bodies; A-02 idle timeout (29 min valid, activity resets, 31 min → 401); A-03 absolute 12 h even when active; A-04 new session ID after login, after MFA and after step-up; A-05 logout, "sign out everywhere", role change, disable and password change end sessions; A-06 missing/wrong CSRF token, foreign `Origin`, `Sec-Fetch-Site: cross-site` (also for login) and every documented GET leaves stored state unchanged; A-07 no CORS; A-08 headers on API responses (success, 401, 400); A-09 bearer-only tokens, no cookie use, session-only routes refused, scope capped by role, object-level revoke, immediate revocation, expiry; R-01/R-02 progressive delay 1-2-4-8-16 s then 15 min lockout with responses identical to normal failures; R-04 unknown and existing users fail alike (shape; timing is Task 8/10 unit-tested with the dummy hash); R-05 TOTP replay (including the enrollment code), recovery codes single use, 6th guess per minute per IP → 429; R-06 300 requests/min per session with `Retry-After`, other sessions unaffected, `/livez` exempt; R-08 `X-Forwarded-For` ignored unless the peer is a trusted proxy; R-09 the account lockout survives a restart (new services on the same database). R-03 (no lockout for unknown accounts) is implied by R-08, R-07 and R-10 are covered by the MFA/login limits exercised here.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the suite**

`internal/api/session_integration_test.go`:
```go
//go:build integration

package api

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"net/netip"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"alice/internal/auth"
)

// countWhere counts rows of table matching a constant SQL condition.
func (s *stack) countWhere(table, cond string) int {
	s.t.Helper()
	var n int
	require.NoError(s.t, s.db.Owner.QueryRow(context.Background(), "SELECT count(*) FROM "+table+" WHERE "+cond).Scan(&n))
	return n
}

// Session, CSRF and rate-limit suite (spec §25.3 R-01..R-09, §25.4 A-01..A-09).

func withHeader(name, value string) reqOpt {
	return func(r *http.Request) { r.Header.Set(name, value) }
}

func withoutHeader(name string) reqOpt {
	return func(r *http.Request) { r.Header.Del(name) }
}

const (
	adminPassword    = "correct horse battery staple"
	operatorPassword = "operator password 2026"
	backupPassphrase = "a long recovery passphrase for backups"
)

// installed returns a stack with a finished wizard and the admin's browser.
func installed(t *testing.T, trusted ...netip.Prefix) (*stack, *client, *account) {
	t.Helper()
	s := newStack(t, trusted...)
	admin := &account{username: "ada", password: adminPassword}
	c := s.setupAdmin(admin, backupPassphrase, nil)
	return s, c, admin
}

// errorWithoutRequestID decodes an error body and drops the per-request ID.
func errorWithoutRequestID(t *testing.T, rec *httptest.ResponseRecorder) map[string]any {
	t.Helper()
	var body map[string]map[string]any
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &body))
	delete(body["error"], "request_id")
	return body["error"]
}

func TestA01CookieAttributes(t *testing.T) {
	s, _, admin := installed(t)
	s.signIn(s.client("198.51.100.20"), admin)

	seen := map[string]bool{}
	var values []string
	for _, e := range s.recorded() {
		for _, line := range e.header.Values("Set-Cookie") {
			ck, err := http.ParseSetCookie(line)
			require.NoError(t, err)
			if ck.Value == "" || ck.MaxAge < 0 {
				continue // clearing cookies
			}
			seen[ck.Name] = true
			values = append(values, ck.Value)
			assert.True(t, strings.HasPrefix(ck.Name, "__Host-"), line)
			assert.True(t, ck.Secure, line)
			assert.True(t, ck.HttpOnly, line)
			assert.Equal(t, http.SameSiteStrictMode, ck.SameSite, line)
			assert.Equal(t, "/", ck.Path, line)
			assert.Empty(t, ck.Domain, line)
		}
	}
	assert.True(t, seen[sessionCookie], "session cookie observed")
	assert.True(t, seen[setupCookie], "setup cookie observed")
	for _, e := range s.recorded() {
		for _, v := range values {
			assert.NotContains(t, string(e.body), v, "a session ID appeared in the body of %s", e.key())
		}
	}
}

func TestA02IdleTimeout(t *testing.T) {
	s, c, _ := installed(t)
	s.clock.Advance(29 * time.Minute)
	requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusOK)
	s.clock.Advance(29 * time.Minute) // activity above reset the idle timer
	requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusOK)
	s.clock.Advance(31 * time.Minute)
	requireError(t, c.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0001")
}

func TestA03AbsoluteTimeout(t *testing.T) {
	s, c, _ := installed(t)
	for i := 0; i < 28; i++ { // 28 x 25 min = 11 h 40 min, always active
		s.clock.Advance(25 * time.Minute)
		requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusOK)
	}
	s.clock.Advance(25 * time.Minute) // 12 h 05 min after sign-in
	requireError(t, c.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0001")
}

func TestA04SessionIDChangesAtLoginMFAAndStepUp(t *testing.T) {
	s, _, admin := installed(t)
	c := s.client("198.51.100.21")
	s.clock.Advance(31 * time.Second)
	requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", map[string]string{"username": "ada", "password": adminPassword}), http.StatusOK)
	pending := c.cookies[sessionCookie]
	require.NotEmpty(t, pending)
	requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", map[string]string{"code": s.totpNow(admin.secret)}), http.StatusOK)
	full := c.cookies[sessionCookie]
	assert.NotEqual(t, pending, full, "MFA completion issues a new session ID")

	old := s.client("198.51.100.22")
	old.cookies[sessionCookie] = pending
	requireError(t, old.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0001")

	s.stepUp(c, admin)
	stepped := c.cookies[sessionCookie]
	assert.NotEqual(t, full, stepped, "step-up issues a new session ID")
	old.cookies[sessionCookie] = full
	requireError(t, old.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0001")
	requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusOK)
}

func TestA05LogoutRevocationRoleChangeAndPasswordChange(t *testing.T) {
	s, adminBrowser, admin := installed(t)

	t.Run("logout invalidates the cookie server-side", func(t *testing.T) {
		c := s.client("198.51.100.30")
		s.signIn(c, admin)
		cookie := c.cookies[sessionCookie]
		requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/logout", nil), http.StatusNoContent)
		replay := s.client("198.51.100.31")
		replay.cookies[sessionCookie] = cookie
		requireError(t, replay.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0001")
	})

	operator := s.createUser(adminBrowser, admin, "otto", "operator", operatorPassword)
	op := s.client("198.51.100.32")
	s.firstSignIn(op, operator)

	t.Run("admin signs the user out everywhere", func(t *testing.T) {
		s.stepUp(adminBrowser, admin)
		requireStatus(t, adminBrowser.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/users/"+operator.id+"/actions/revoke-sessions", nil), http.StatusNoContent)
		requireError(t, op.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0001")
	})

	t.Run("a role change ends the user's sessions", func(t *testing.T) {
		s.signIn(op, operator)
		s.stepUp(adminBrowser, admin)
		requireStatus(t, adminBrowser.do(http.MethodPatch, "/api/v1/users/"+operator.id, map[string]string{"role": "auditor"}), http.StatusOK)
		requireError(t, op.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0001")
	})

	t.Run("disabling ends the sessions and blocks sign-in", func(t *testing.T) {
		s.signIn(op, operator)
		s.stepUp(adminBrowser, admin)
		requireStatus(t, adminBrowser.do(http.MethodPatch, "/api/v1/users/"+operator.id, map[string]bool{"disabled": true}), http.StatusOK)
		requireError(t, op.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0001")
		requireError(t, s.login("otto", operatorPassword), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0005")
	})

	t.Run("a password change ends the other sessions", func(t *testing.T) {
		first, second := s.client("198.51.100.33"), s.client("198.51.100.34")
		s.signIn(first, admin)
		s.signIn(second, admin)
		before := first.cookies[sessionCookie]
		requireStatus(t, first.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/password", map[string]string{
			"current_password": adminPassword, "new_password": "a brand new admin password",
		}), http.StatusOK)
		assert.NotEqual(t, before, first.cookies[sessionCookie], "the current session gets a new ID")
		requireStatus(t, first.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusOK)
		requireError(t, second.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0001")
	})
}

func TestA06CSRFAndOriginChecks(t *testing.T) {
	s, c, _ := installed(t)

	requireError(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/logout", nil, withoutHeader("X-CSRF-Token")), http.StatusForbidden, "ALC-AUTH-0004")
	requireError(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/logout", nil, withHeader("X-CSRF-Token", "forged")), http.StatusForbidden, "ALC-AUTH-0004")
	requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/logout", nil, withHeader("Origin", "https://evil.example")), http.StatusForbidden)
	requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/logout", nil, withHeader("Sec-Fetch-Site", "cross-site")), http.StatusForbidden)
	requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusOK)

	// Login CSRF: a cross-site form cannot sign the victim in either.
	requireStatus(t, s.freshClient().do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login",
		map[string]string{"username": "ada", "password": adminPassword}, withHeader("Origin", "https://evil.example")), http.StatusForbidden)
}

func TestA06GETRoutesNeverChangeState(t *testing.T) {
	s, c, admin := installed(t)
	tables := []string{"users", "user_mfa", "recovery_codes", "sessions", "api_tokens", "setup_sessions", "settings", "master_key_versions"}
	counts := func() map[string]int {
		m := map[string]int{}
		for _, table := range tables {
			m[table] = s.count(table)
		}
		return m
	}
	before := counts()
	var gets int
	for _, op := range specOps() {
		if op.method != "get" {
			continue
		}
		gets++
		rec := c.do(http.MethodGet, strings.ReplaceAll(op.path, "{id}", admin.id), nil)
		assert.Less(t, rec.Code, 500, op.key())
		assert.Equal(t, before, counts(), "GET %s changed stored state", op.path)
	}
	assert.Greater(t, gets, 5)
}

func TestA07NoCrossOriginResourceSharing(t *testing.T) {
	s, c, _ := installed(t)
	pre := s.freshClient().do(http.MethodOptions, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil,
		withHeader("Origin", "https://evil.example"), withHeader("Access-Control-Request-Method", "POST"))
	assert.Empty(t, pre.Header().Get("Access-Control-Allow-Origin"))
	assert.Empty(t, pre.Header().Get("Access-Control-Allow-Credentials"))
	assert.NotEqual(t, http.StatusNoContent, pre.Code, "preflight is not answered")

	rec := c.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil, withHeader("Origin", "https://evil.example"))
	assert.Empty(t, rec.Header().Get("Access-Control-Allow-Origin"))
}

func TestA08SecurityHeadersOnAPIResponses(t *testing.T) {
	s, c, _ := installed(t)
	for name, rec := range map[string]*httptest.ResponseRecorder{
		"authenticated": c.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil),
		"unauthorized":  s.freshClient().do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil),
		"bad request":   s.freshClient().do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", `{"username":`),
	} {
		h := rec.Header()
		assert.Equal(t, "max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains", h.Get("Strict-Transport-Security"), name)
		assert.Equal(t, "default-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'", h.Get("Content-Security-Policy"), name)
		assert.Equal(t, "nosniff", h.Get("X-Content-Type-Options"), name)
		assert.Equal(t, "DENY", h.Get("X-Frame-Options"), name)
		assert.Equal(t, "no-referrer", h.Get("Referrer-Policy"), name)
		assert.Equal(t, "no-store", h.Get("Cache-Control"), name)
		assert.Equal(t, "application/json; charset=utf-8", h.Get("Content-Type"), name)
	}
}

func createToken(t *testing.T, s *stack, c *client, a *account, scope string, days int) (string, string) {
	t.Helper()
	s.stepUp(c, a)
	rec := c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/tokens", map[string]any{"name": "ci-" + scope, "scope": scope, "expires_in_days": days})
	requireStatus(t, rec, http.StatusCreated)
	var body struct {
		Token string `json:"token"`
		Item  struct {
			ID string `json:"id"`
		} `json:"item"`
	}
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(rec.Body.Bytes(), &body))
	return body.Token, body.Item.ID
}

func TestA09APITokenRules(t *testing.T) {
	s, adminBrowser, admin := installed(t)
	token, tokenID := createToken(t, s, adminBrowser, admin, "read", 1)

	api := s.client("198.51.100.40")
	api.bearer = token
	requireStatus(t, api.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/version", nil), http.StatusOK)
	// Bearer requests carry no ambient credentials, so no CSRF token is needed.
	requireStatus(t, api.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/audit/verify", nil, withoutHeader("Origin"), withoutHeader("Sec-Fetch-Site")), http.StatusOK)
	// Session-only and step-up operations are never available to tokens.
	requireError(t, api.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusForbidden, "ALC-AUTH-0002")
	requireError(t, api.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/users", map[string]string{"username": "x"}), http.StatusForbidden, "ALC-AUTH-0002")
	requireError(t, api.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/tokens", map[string]any{"name": "x", "scope": "read", "expires_in_days": 1}), http.StatusForbidden, "ALC-AUTH-0002")

	// A token is never accepted as a cookie.
	viaCookie := s.client("198.51.100.41")
	viaCookie.cookies[sessionCookie] = token
	requireError(t, viaCookie.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/version", nil), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0001")

	// An auditor cannot mint an operate token, nor revoke someone else's token.
	auditor := s.createUser(adminBrowser, admin, "audrey", "auditor", operatorPassword)
	aud := s.client("198.51.100.42")
	s.firstSignIn(aud, auditor)
	s.stepUp(aud, auditor)
	requireError(t, aud.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/tokens", map[string]any{"name": "x", "scope": "operate", "expires_in_days": 1}),
		http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "ALC-TOK-0001")
	requireError(t, aud.do(http.MethodDelete, "/api/v1/tokens/"+tokenID, nil), http.StatusNotFound, "ALC-SYS-0001")
	requireStatus(t, api.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/version", nil), http.StatusOK)

	// Revocation takes effect immediately.
	requireStatus(t, adminBrowser.do(http.MethodDelete, "/api/v1/tokens/"+tokenID, nil), http.StatusNoContent)
	requireError(t, api.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/version", nil), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0001")

	// Expired tokens are rejected.
	second, _ := createToken(t, s, adminBrowser, admin, "read", 1)
	api.bearer = second
	requireStatus(t, api.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/version", nil), http.StatusOK)
	s.clock.Advance(24*time.Hour + time.Minute)
	requireError(t, api.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/version", nil), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0001")
}

// installedWithOperator adds an enrolled operator "olga".
func installedWithOperator(t *testing.T) (*stack, *account) {
	t.Helper()
	s, adminBrowser, admin := installed(t)
	olga := s.createUser(adminBrowser, admin, "olga", "operator", operatorPassword)
	s.firstSignIn(s.client("198.51.100.50"), olga)
	return s, olga
}

func TestR01R02ProgressiveDelayAndLockout(t *testing.T) {
	s, olga := installedWithOperator(t)
	wrong := func() *httptest.ResponseRecorder {
		rec := s.login("olga", "not the password at all")
		requireError(t, rec, http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0005")
		return rec
	}
	right := func() *httptest.ResponseRecorder { return s.login("olga", olga.password) }

	normal := errorWithoutRequestID(t, wrong())
	for i := 2; i <= auth.DelayThreshold; i++ {
		wrong()
	}
	// Five consecutive failures: the next attempt within 1 s fails even with
	// the right password, and looks exactly like a normal failure.
	delayed := right()
	requireError(t, delayed, http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0005")
	assert.Equal(t, normal, errorWithoutRequestID(t, delayed))
	s.clock.Advance(time.Second)
	requireStatus(t, right(), http.StatusOK)

	// Failures 6..9 double the delay (2, 4, 8, 16 s); failure 10 locks for 15 minutes.
	for n, wait := 6, time.Duration(0); n <= auth.LockThreshold; n++ {
		s.clock.Advance(wait)
		wrong()
		wait = time.Duration(1<<(n-auth.DelayThreshold)) * time.Second
	}
	locked := right()
	requireError(t, locked, http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0005")
	assert.Equal(t, normal, errorWithoutRequestID(t, locked))
	s.clock.Advance(14 * time.Minute)
	requireError(t, right(), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0005")
	s.clock.Advance(61 * time.Second)
	requireStatus(t, right(), http.StatusOK)

	assert.Equal(t, 1, s.countWhere("audit_log", "action = 'auth.lockout'"))
}

func TestR04UnknownAndExistingUsersFailAlike(t *testing.T) {
	// Timing equality is covered by the Argon2id dummy verification unit test
	// in internal/auth; with cheap test parameters only the shape is compared.
	s, _ := installedWithOperator(t)
	existing := s.login("olga", "not the password at all")
	unknown := s.login("nobody", "not the password at all")
	assert.Equal(t, existing.Code, unknown.Code)
	assert.Equal(t, errorWithoutRequestID(t, existing), errorWithoutRequestID(t, unknown))
	assert.Equal(t, existing.Header().Get("Content-Type"), unknown.Header().Get("Content-Type"))
}

func TestR05TOTPReplayBruteForceAndRecoveryCodes(t *testing.T) {
	t.Run("codes are single use, also right after enrollment", func(t *testing.T) {
		s, _, admin := installed(t)
		c := s.client("198.51.100.60")
		requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", map[string]string{"username": "ada", "password": adminPassword}), http.StatusOK)
		// Same 30 s step as the enrollment code used by the wizard.
		requireError(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", map[string]string{"code": s.totpNow(admin.secret)}), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0006")
		s.clock.Advance(30 * time.Second)
		code := s.totpNow(admin.secret)
		requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", map[string]string{"code": code}), http.StatusOK)
		requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/logout", nil), http.StatusNoContent)

		requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", map[string]string{"username": "ada", "password": adminPassword}), http.StatusOK)
		requireError(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", map[string]string{"code": code}), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0006")
	})

	t.Run("recovery codes are single use", func(t *testing.T) {
		s, _, admin := installed(t)
		c := s.client("198.51.100.61")
		requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", map[string]string{"username": "ada", "password": adminPassword}), http.StatusOK)
		requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", map[string]string{"recovery_code": admin.recovery[0]}), http.StatusOK)
		requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/logout", nil), http.StatusNoContent)
		requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", map[string]string{"username": "ada", "password": adminPassword}), http.StatusOK)
		requireError(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", map[string]string{"recovery_code": admin.recovery[0]}), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0006")
		requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", map[string]string{"recovery_code": admin.recovery[1]}), http.StatusOK)
	})

	t.Run("guessing codes is rate limited per IP", func(t *testing.T) {
		s, _, _ := installed(t)
		c := s.client("198.51.100.62")
		s.clock.Advance(31 * time.Second)
		requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", map[string]string{"username": "ada", "password": adminPassword}), http.StatusOK)
		for i := 0; i < 5; i++ {
			requireError(t, c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", map[string]string{"code": fmt.Sprintf("%06d", i)}), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0006")
		}
		rec := c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify", map[string]string{"code": "123456"})
		requireError(t, rec, http.StatusTooManyRequests, "ALC-SYS-0005")
		assert.NotEmpty(t, rec.Header().Get("Retry-After"))
	})
}

func TestR06GeneralRateLimitPerIdentity(t *testing.T) {
	s, other, admin := installed(t)
	c := s.client("198.51.100.70")
	s.signIn(c, admin)

	for i := 0; i < 300; i++ {
		requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusOK)
	}
	rec := c.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil)
	requireError(t, rec, http.StatusTooManyRequests, "ALC-SYS-0005")
	retry, err := strconv.Atoi(rec.Header().Get("Retry-After"))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.GreaterOrEqual(t, retry, 1)

	requireStatus(t, other.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusOK) // a different session is unaffected
	probe := s.client("198.51.100.70")                                                     // same IP as the limited session
	for i := 0; i < 400; i++ {
		requireStatus(t, probe.do(http.MethodGet, "/livez", nil), http.StatusOK)
	}
	s.clock.Advance(time.Minute)
	requireStatus(t, c.do(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/auth/session", nil), http.StatusOK)
}

func TestR08ForwardedForIsIgnoredUnlessProxyIsTrusted(t *testing.T) {
	attempts := func(s *stack) []int {
		var codes []int
		c := s.client("203.0.113.9")
		for i := 0; i < 15; i++ {
			rec := c.do(http.MethodPost, "/api/v1/auth/login", map[string]string{"username": "nobody", "password": "not the password at all"},
				withHeader("X-Forwarded-For", fmt.Sprintf("10.0.0.%d", i+1)))
			codes = append(codes, rec.Code)
		}
		return codes
	}

	untrusted := attempts(newStack(t))
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnauthorized, untrusted[9], "the 10th attempt is still evaluated")
	assert.Equal(t, http.StatusTooManyRequests, untrusted[10], "rotating X-Forwarded-For does not evade the per-IP limit")

	trusted := attempts(newStack(t, netip.MustParsePrefix("203.0.113.9/32")))
	for i, code := range trusted {
		assert.Equal(t, http.StatusUnauthorized, code, "attempt %d from a distinct client behind the trusted proxy", i+1)
	}
}

func TestR09LockoutSurvivesRestart(t *testing.T) {
	s, olga := installedWithOperator(t)
	for n := 1; n <= auth.LockThreshold; n++ {
		if n > auth.DelayThreshold {
			s.clock.Advance(auth.MaxDelay)
		}
		requireError(t, s.login("olga", "not the password at all"), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0005")
	}
	s.start() // new services and handler: in-memory limiters are gone, the lockout is in the database
	requireError(t, s.login("olga", olga.password), http.StatusUnauthorized, "ALC-AUTH-0005")
	s.clock.Advance(auth.LockDuration + time.Second)
	requireStatus(t, s.login("olga", olga.password), http.StatusOK)
}
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the wire JSON and routes of Tasks 13–15 (DTOs of Tasks 14 and 15); the shared error keys `errors.<reason>.title|action` from Task 13 (this task's tests read `errors.auth.invalid_credentials.title` = "Incorrect username or password." / "Kullanıcı adı veya parola hatalı."); `validation.*` keys from Task 2; `make i18n-check` from Task 1 (identical EN/TR values are errors; this task adds none). **Requires from Task 14:** `POST /api/v1/auth/login` answers `{status, csrf_token}` where `csrf_token` = `Auth.CSRFToken(pendingSessionToken)`, because the following `POST /auth/mfa/enroll|verify` are cookie-authenticated and CSRF-checked.
- Produces (used by Tasks 20–24):
  - `web/src/api/client.ts`: `class ApiError extends Error { status; code; reason; messageKey; params: Record<string,string>; details: FieldError[]; requestId; retryable }`, `isApiError(e: unknown, reason?: string): e is ApiError`, `apiFetch<T>(path: string, opts?: { method?: 'GET'|'POST'|'PUT'|'PATCH'|'DELETE'; body?: unknown; signal?: AbortSignal; quietUnauthorized?: boolean }): Promise<T>` (204 → `undefined`), `setCsrfToken(t: string | null)`, `getCsrfToken()`, `onUnauthorized(cb: () => void): () => void` (fires only for 401 with reason `auth.unauthenticated`).
  - `web/src/api/types.ts`: `Role`, `TokenScope`, `VersionInfo`, `User`, `SessionInfo`, `SessionWithCodes`, `LoginResponse`, `MfaEnrollment`, `MfaInput`, `SetupStatus`, `SetupSessionResponse`, `AdminEnrollmentRequest`, `UserCreateRequest`, `UserUpdateRequest`, `ApiToken`, `TokenCreateRequest`, `CreatedToken`, `AuditResult`, `AuditEntry`, `AuditQuery`, `AuditPage`, `AuditReport`, `FieldError`, `ApiErrorBody`.
  - `web/src/api/endpoints.ts`: `getSetupStatus()`, `startSetupSession(code)` (stores the setup CSRF token), `setSetupLanguage(language)`, `startAdminEnrollment(req)`, `createAdmin(code)`, `setRecoveryPassphrase(passphrase, confirm)`, `completeSetup()`, `login(username, password)` (stores the pending CSRF token), `enrollMfa()`, `verifyMfa(input)`, `getSession()` (quiet on 401), `logout()`, `stepUp(code)`, `changePassword(current, next)`, `regenerateRecoveryCodes()`, `setMyLocale(locale)`, `getVersion()`, `listUsers()`, `createUser(req)`, `updateUser(id, req)`, `resetUserMfa(id)`, `resetUserPassword(id, password)`, `revokeUserSessions(id)`, `listTokens(userId?)`, `createToken(req)`, `revokeToken(id)`, `listAudit(query)`, `verifyAudit()`, `auditExportUrl(format)`.
  - `web/src/auth/AuthProvider.tsx`: `AuthProvider`, `useAuth(): { session: SessionInfo | null; setupStatus: SetupStatus | null; loading: boolean; sessionEnded: boolean; refresh(); refreshSetupStatus(): Promise<SetupStatus | null>; setSession(s: SessionInfo | null) (also sets the CSRF token); signOut() }`.
  - `web/src/auth/guards.tsx`: `PageLoader`, `RequireAuth`, `RequireRole({ roles: Role[]; children })` (renders `ui.noAccess` "You do not have access to this page." with `role="alert"`).
  - `web/src/components/ErrorAlert.tsx`: `ErrorAlert({ error: unknown })` (red `role="alert"`, localized `title` + `action`, field details, "Request ID: …").
  - `web/src/components/StepUpProvider.tsx`: `StepUpProvider`, `useStepUp(): { run<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> }` (modal "Confirm it's you", field "6-digit code", buttons "Confirm"/"Cancel").
  - `web/src/layout/AppLayout.tsx`: `AppLayout`, `NAV_ITEMS`; `web/src/LanguageSwitcher.tsx`: `LanguageSwitcher({ signedIn?: boolean })`.
  - `web/src/router.tsx`: `routes(): RouteObject[]`. Signed-in pages are children of the `path: '/'` route: `children: [{ index: true, element: <HomePage /> }, ...]`; Tasks 21–23 add `{ path: 'account' | 'users' | 'tokens' | 'audit', element: ... }` entries to that array.
  - Test helpers: `web/src/test/server.ts` (`server`, `handlers`); `web/src/test/fixtures.ts` (`userFor(role, locale?)`, `sessionFor(role, locale?)` with csrf `csrf-<role>` and display names "Ayşe Yılmaz"/"Olcay Öztürk"/"Ilgın Işık", `adminSession`, `operatorSession`, `auditorSession`, `installedStatus`, `freshStatus`, `errorResponse(status, code, reason, { params?, details? })` (request id `req-1`), `sessionHandlers(session, status?)`); `web/src/test/render.tsx` (`renderApp(ui, languages?)`, `renderRoute(path, { languages? })` → `{ ...render, i18n, router }`); `web/src/test/i18nAssert.ts` (`expectNoRawKeys(root?)`); `SCREENS` array in `web/src/screens.i18n.test.tsx` (Tasks 21–23 append their pages).
  - `data-testid`s: `server-status` (home page), `current-user` (header), `setup-banner` (admin setup reminder).
  - Shared catalog keys `enum.role.admin|operator|auditor`.

React Router 7 is used because version 8 requires React 19 (this app is on React 18); msw replaces `vi.stubGlobal('fetch', ...)` in component tests (R34 carry). `msw` is a dev dependency only and never reaches the bundle.

```ts
import '@testing-library/jest-dom/vitest';
import { cleanup } from '@testing-library/react';
import { setCsrfToken } from '../api/client';

// jsdom lacks these browser APIs that Mantine uses.
Object.defineProperty(window, 'matchMedia', {
  writable: true,
  value: (query: string) => ({
    matches: false,
    media: query,
    onchange: null,
    addListener: () => {},
    removeListener: () => {},
    addEventListener: () => {},
    removeEventListener: () => {},
    dispatchEvent: () => false,
  }),
});
class ResizeObserverStub {
  observe() {}
  unobserve() {}
  disconnect() {}
}
window.ResizeObserver = ResizeObserverStub as unknown as typeof ResizeObserver;
window.HTMLElement.prototype.scrollIntoView = () => {};

beforeAll(() => server.listen({ onUnhandledRequest: 'error' }));
beforeEach(() => {
  localStorage.clear();
  document.documentElement.lang = 'en';
  setCsrfToken(null);
});
afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  server.resetHandlers();
});
afterAll(() => server.close());
```

```ts
import { setupServer } from 'msw/node';
```

```ts
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import type { Role, SessionInfo, SetupStatus, User } from '../api/types';

export function userFor(role: Role, locale = 'en'): User {
  return {
    id: `00000000-0000-7000-8000-00000000000${role === 'admin' ? 1 : role === 'operator' ? 2 : 3}`,
    username: role,
    display_name: { admin: 'Ayşe Yılmaz', operator: 'Olcay Öztürk', auditor: 'Ilgın Işık' }[role],
    email: `${role}@example.com`,
    role,
    locale,
    disabled: false,
    mfa_enrolled: true,
    last_login_at: null,
    created_at: '2026-09-30T08:00:00Z',
  };
}

export function sessionFor(role: Role, locale = 'en'): SessionInfo {
  return {
    user: userFor(role, locale),
    csrf_token: `csrf-${role}`,
    expires_at: '2026-09-30T20:00:00Z',
    idle_expires_at: '2026-09-30T08:30:00Z',
    stepup_until: null,
  };
}

export const adminSession = sessionFor('admin');
export const operatorSession = sessionFor('operator');
export const auditorSession = sessionFor('auditor');

export const installedStatus: SetupStatus = {
  admin_exists: true,
  recovery_passphrase_set: true,
  completed: true,
  default_locale: 'en',
};

export const freshStatus: SetupStatus = {
  admin_exists: false,
  recovery_passphrase_set: false,
  completed: false,
  default_locale: 'en',
};

// errorResponse builds an error body shaped like the server's (spec §9.3).
export function errorResponse(
  status: number,
  code: string,
  reason: string,
  extra: { params?: Record<string, string>; details?: { field: string; code: string; params?: Record<string, string> }[] } = {},
) {
  return HttpResponse.json(
    {
      error: {
        code,
        reason,
        message_key: `errors.${reason}`,
        message: `server text for ${reason}`,
        request_id: 'req-1',
        retryable: status === 429,
        ...extra,
      },
    },
    { status },
  );
}

// sessionHandlers installs handlers for a signed-in user (or none) and the setup status.
export function sessionHandlers(session: SessionInfo | null, status: SetupStatus = installedStatus) {
  return [
    http.get('/api/v1/setup/status', () => HttpResponse.json(status)),
    http.get('/api/v1/auth/session', () =>
      session ? HttpResponse.json(session) : errorResponse(401, 'ALC-AUTH-0001', 'auth.unauthenticated'),
    ),
  ];
}
```

`web/src/test/i18nAssert.ts`:
```ts
// A translation key rendered as text means a missing translation (spec §22.3).
const RAW_KEY = /(?:^|\s)(?:home|language|nav|ui|login|setup|account|users|tokens|audit|errors|enum|validation)\.[a-z][A-Za-z_.]*/;

// visibleText joins the text nodes outside <style> and <script>, separated by spaces.
function visibleText(root: HTMLElement): string {
  const walker = document.createTreeWalker(root, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
  const parts: string[] = [];
  for (let n = walker.nextNode(); n; n = walker.nextNode()) {
    const tag = n.parentElement?.tagName;
    if (tag !== 'STYLE' && tag !== 'SCRIPT') parts.push(n.textContent ?? '');
  }
  return parts.join(' ');
}

export function expectNoRawKeys(root: HTMLElement = document.body): void {
  expect(visibleText(root)).not.toMatch(RAW_KEY);
}
```

```ts
import { expectNoRawKeys } from './i18nAssert';

describe('expectNoRawKeys', () => {
  afterEach(() => document.body.replaceChildren());

  it('fails on a rendered translation key', () => {
    document.body.innerHTML = '<main><h1>nav.home</h1></main>';
    expect(() => expectNoRawKeys()).toThrow();
  });

  it('accepts translated text, e-mail addresses and styles', () => {
    document.body.innerHTML =
      '<style>.ui.x{}</style><main><h1>Ana sayfa</h1><p>admin@example.com</p><p>Hesabım</p></main>';
    expect(() => expectNoRawKeys()).not.toThrow();
  });
});
```

Replace `web/src/test/render.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render } from '@testing-library/react';
import { MantineProvider } from '@mantine/core';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { I18nextProvider } from 'react-i18next';
import { createMemoryRouter, RouterProvider } from 'react-router';
import type { ReactElement } from 'react';
import { createI18n } from '../i18n';
import { routes } from '../router';

function providers(ui: ReactElement, i18n: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof createI18n>>) {
  const client = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  return (
    <I18nextProvider i18n={i18n}>
      <QueryClientProvider client={client}>
        <MantineProvider>{ui}</MantineProvider>
      </QueryClientProvider>
    </I18nextProvider>
  );
}

// renderApp renders a single component with the app providers (no router).
export async function renderApp(ui: ReactElement, languages: readonly string[] = ['en-US']) {
  const i18n = await createI18n(languages);
  return { ...render(providers(ui, i18n)), i18n };
}

// renderRoute renders the whole application at path, using the real route table.
export async function renderRoute(path: string, opts: { languages?: readonly string[] } = {}) {
  const i18n = await createI18n(opts.languages ?? ['en-US']);
  const router = createMemoryRouter(routes(), { initialEntries: [path] });
  return { ...render(providers(<RouterProvider router={router} />, i18n)), i18n, router };
}
```

Create `web/src/api/client.test.ts`:
```ts
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import { server } from '../test/server';
import { errorResponse } from '../test/fixtures';
import { apiFetch, ApiError, onUnauthorized, setCsrfToken } from './client';

describe('apiFetch', () => {
  it('sends JSON with the CSRF token on state-changing requests only', async () => {
    const seen: { method: string; csrf: string | null; type: string | null }[] = [];
    server.use(
      http.all('/api/v1/echo', async ({ request }) => {
        seen.push({
          method: request.method,
          csrf: request.headers.get('X-CSRF-Token'),
          type: request.headers.get('Content-Type'),
        });
        return HttpResponse.json({ ok: true });
      }),
    );
    setCsrfToken('tok-1');
    await apiFetch('/api/v1/echo');
    await apiFetch('/api/v1/echo', { method: 'POST', body: { a: 1 } });
    expect(seen).toEqual([
      { method: 'GET', csrf: null, type: null },
      { method: 'POST', csrf: 'tok-1', type: 'application/json' },
    ]);
  });

  it('returns undefined for 204', async () => {
    server.use(http.delete('/api/v1/echo', () => new HttpResponse(null, { status: 204 })));
    await expect(apiFetch('/api/v1/echo', { method: 'DELETE' })).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('turns an error body into ApiError', async () => {
    server.use(
      http.post('/api/v1/echo', () =>
        errorResponse(422, 'ALC-SYS-0006', 'validation.failed', {
          details: [{ field: 'username', code: 'validation.required' }],
        }),
      ),
    );
    const err = await apiFetch('/api/v1/echo', { method: 'POST', body: {} }).catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(ApiError);
    const e = err as ApiError;
    expect([e.status, e.code, e.reason, e.messageKey, e.requestId]).toEqual([
      422,
      'ALC-SYS-0006',
      'validation.failed',
      'errors.validation.failed',
      'req-1',
    ]);
    expect(e.details).toEqual([{ field: 'username', code: 'validation.required' }]);
  });

  it('keeps a non-JSON error body usable', async () => {
    server.use(http.get('/api/v1/echo', () => new HttpResponse('<html>bad gateway</html>', { status: 502 })));
    const e = (await apiFetch('/api/v1/echo').catch((x: unknown) => x)) as ApiError;
    expect(e.status).toBe(502);
    expect(e.reason).toBe('');
  });

  it('announces an ended session, but not wrong codes or quiet probes', async () => {
    const calls: string[] = [];
    const off = onUnauthorized(() => calls.push('ended'));
    server.use(
      http.get('/api/v1/ended', () => errorResponse(401, 'ALC-AUTH-0001', 'auth.unauthenticated')),
      http.post('/api/v1/wrong', () => errorResponse(401, 'ALC-AUTH-0006', 'auth.mfa_invalid')),
    );
    await apiFetch('/api/v1/ended').catch(() => undefined);
    await apiFetch('/api/v1/wrong', { method: 'POST' }).catch(() => undefined);
    await apiFetch('/api/v1/ended', { quietUnauthorized: true }).catch(() => undefined);
    off();
    await apiFetch('/api/v1/ended').catch(() => undefined);
    expect(calls).toEqual(['ended']);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { createI18n, pickLanguage, setLanguage } from './i18n';

describe('pickLanguage', () => {
  beforeEach(() => localStorage.clear());

  it('uses a stored supported language first', () => {
    expect(pickLanguage('tr', ['en-US'])).toBe('tr');
  });
  it('ignores an unsupported stored value', () => {
    expect(pickLanguage('xx', ['tr-TR'])).toBe('tr');
  });
  it('matches the browser language by its base code', () => {
    expect(pickLanguage(null, ['de-DE', 'tr-TR'])).toBe('tr');
    expect(pickLanguage(null, ['TR'])).toBe('tr');
  });
  it('falls back to English', () => {
    expect(pickLanguage(null, ['de-DE'])).toBe('en');
    expect(pickLanguage(null, [])).toBe('en');
  });
});

describe('lazy locale loading', () => {
  beforeEach(() => localStorage.clear());

  it('loads Turkish only when it is selected', async () => {
    const i18n = await createI18n(['en-US']);
    expect(i18n.hasResourceBundle('en', 'web')).toBe(true);
    expect(i18n.hasResourceBundle('tr', 'web')).toBe(false);

    await setLanguage(i18n, 'tr');
    expect(i18n.hasResourceBundle('tr', 'web')).toBe(true);
    expect(i18n.hasResourceBundle('tr', 'common')).toBe(true);
    expect(i18n.t('nav.home')).toBe('Ana sayfa');
    expect(document.documentElement.lang).toBe('tr');
  });

  it('loads the browser language at start and keeps English as fallback', async () => {
    const i18n = await createI18n(['tr-TR']);
    expect(i18n.language).toBe('tr');
    expect(i18n.hasResourceBundle('tr', 'web')).toBe(true);
    expect(i18n.hasResourceBundle('en', 'web')).toBe(true);
  });
});
```
