Create `web/src/components/ErrorAlert.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { ApiError } from '../api/client';
import { renderApp } from '../test/render';
import { ErrorAlert } from './ErrorAlert';

function apiError(reason: string, extra: Partial<ConstructorParameters<typeof ApiError>[1]> = {}) {
  return new ApiError(422, {
    code: 'ALC-X-0001',
    reason,
    message_key: `errors.${reason}`,
    message: 'server fallback text',
    request_id: 'req-42',
    retryable: false,
    ...extra,
  });
}

describe('ErrorAlert', () => {
  it('renders nothing without an error', async () => {
    await renderApp(<ErrorAlert error={null} />);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('alert')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('translates a known error and shows the request ID', async () => {
    await renderApp(<ErrorAlert error={apiError('auth.invalid_credentials')} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Incorrect username or password.');
    expect(screen.getByText('Request ID: req-42')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('translates in Turkish', async () => {
    await renderApp(<ErrorAlert error={apiError('auth.invalid_credentials')} />, ['tr-TR']);
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Kullanıcı adı veya parola hatalı.');
    expect(screen.getByText('İstek kimliği: req-42')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('falls back to the server message for an unknown key', async () => {
    await renderApp(<ErrorAlert error={apiError('brand.new_reason')} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('server fallback text');
  });

  it('lists field problems with translated field names', async () => {
    const err = apiError('validation.failed', {
      details: [{ field: 'username', code: 'validation.required' }],
    });
    const { i18n } = await renderApp(<ErrorAlert error={err} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent(
      i18n.t('ui.fieldProblem', { field: 'Username', problem: i18n.t('validation.required') }),
    );
  });

  it('explains network failures', async () => {
    await renderApp(<ErrorAlert error={new TypeError('Failed to fetch')} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Alice cannot be reached.');
  });
});
```

Replace `web/src/App.test.tsx` (the old `vi.stubGlobal` tests go away; the app is now the router):

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import { errorResponse, freshStatus, sessionFor, sessionHandlers } from './test/fixtures';
import { renderRoute } from './test/render';
import { server } from './test/server';

function navLabels(): string[] {
  const nav = screen.getByRole('navigation');
  return within(nav)
    .getAllByRole('link')
    .map((a) => a.textContent ?? '');
}

describe('application shell', () => {
  it('sends a fresh install to the setup wizard', async () => {
    server.use(...sessionHandlers(null, freshStatus));
    const { router } = await renderRoute('/');
    await waitFor(() => expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/setup'));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Set up Alice' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('sends a signed-out visitor to the sign-in page', async () => {
    const { router } = await renderRoute('/');
    await waitFor(() => expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/login'));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Sign in to Alice' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows the admin home page with the server version and the full navigation', async () => {
    server.use(...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('admin')));
    await renderRoute('/');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Welcome, Ayşe Yılmaz' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByTestId('server-status')).toHaveTextContent('Server version: 1.2.3'));
    expect(navLabels()).toEqual(['Home', 'Users', 'Audit log', 'API tokens', 'My account']);
  });

  it('hides Users and Audit log from operators', async () => {
    server.use(...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('operator')));
    await renderRoute('/');
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Welcome, Olcay Öztürk' });
    expect(navLabels()).toEqual(['Home', 'API tokens', 'My account']);
  });

  it('shows the audit log but not Users to auditors, in Turkish', async () => {
    server.use(...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('auditor', 'tr')));
    await renderRoute('/', { languages: ['tr-TR'] });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Hoş geldiniz, Ilgın Işık' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(navLabels()).toEqual(['Ana sayfa', 'Denetim kaydı', 'API anahtarları', 'Hesabım']);
    expect(document.documentElement.lang).toBe('tr');
  });

  it('switches to the language saved on the account after sign-in', async () => {
    server.use(...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('admin', 'tr')));
    await renderRoute('/');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Hoş geldiniz, Ayşe Yılmaz' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('saves a language change on the account when signed in', async () => {
    let saved = '';
    server.use(
      ...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('admin')),
      http.put('/api/v1/auth/locale', async ({ request }) => {
        saved = ((await request.json()) as { locale: string }).locale;
        return new HttpResponse(null, { status: 204 });
      }),
    );
    await renderRoute('/');
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Welcome, Ayşe Yılmaz' });
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByText('Türkçe'));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Hoş geldiniz, Ayşe Yılmaz' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await waitFor(() => expect(saved).toBe('tr'));
    expect(localStorage.getItem('alice.lang')).toBe('tr');
  });

  it('shows the setup banner to an admin until the recovery passphrase is set', async () => {
    server.use(
      ...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('admin'), {
        admin_exists: true,
        recovery_passphrase_set: false,
        completed: false,
        default_locale: 'en',
      }),
    );
    await renderRoute('/');
    expect(await screen.findByTestId('setup-banner')).toHaveTextContent(
      'Finish setting up: choose a recovery passphrase.',
    );
  });

  it('does not show the setup banner to operators', async () => {
    server.use(
      ...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('operator'), {
        admin_exists: true,
        recovery_passphrase_set: false,
        completed: false,
        default_locale: 'en',
      }),
    );
    await renderRoute('/');
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Welcome, Olcay Öztürk' });
    expect(screen.queryByTestId('setup-banner')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('returns to the sign-in page with a notice when the session ends', async () => {
    server.use(
      ...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('admin')),
      http.get('/api/v1/version', () => errorResponse(401, 'ALC-AUTH-0001', 'auth.unauthenticated')),
    );
    const { router } = await renderRoute('/');
    await waitFor(() => expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/login'));
    expect(router.state.location.search).toBe('?ended=1');
    expect(await screen.findByText('Your session ended. Please sign in again.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('signs out', async () => {
    let loggedOut = false;
    server.use(
      ...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('admin')),
      http.post('/api/v1/auth/logout', () => {
        loggedOut = true;
        return new HttpResponse(null, { status: 204 });
      }),
    );
    const { router } = await renderRoute('/');
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Sign out' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/login'));
    expect(loggedOut).toBe(true);
  });

  it('shows a not-found page for unknown addresses, including ones with a dot', async () => {
    await renderRoute('/no/such.page');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Page not found' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import type { SessionInfo, SetupStatus } from './api/types';
import { freshStatus, installedStatus, sessionFor, sessionHandlers } from './test/fixtures';
import { expectNoRawKeys } from './test/i18nAssert';
import { renderRoute } from './test/render';
import { server } from './test/server';

interface Screen {
  path: string;
  session: SessionInfo | null;
  status: SetupStatus;
  // text proves the screen finished loading; a function of the language
  ready: { en: string; tr: string };
}

// SCREENS lists every routed screen. Later tasks append their pages here.
export const SCREENS: Screen[] = [
  { path: '/', session: sessionFor('admin'), status: installedStatus, ready: { en: 'Home', tr: 'Ana sayfa' } },
  { path: '/login', session: null, status: installedStatus, ready: { en: 'Sign in to Alice', tr: "Alice'e giriş yapın" } },
  { path: '/setup', session: null, status: freshStatus, ready: { en: 'Set up Alice', tr: 'Alice kurulumu' } },
  { path: '/missing', session: null, status: installedStatus, ready: { en: 'Page not found', tr: 'Sayfa bulunamadı' } },
];

describe.each(['en', 'tr'] as const)('every screen in %s', (lang) => {
  it.each(SCREENS.map((s) => [s.path, s] as const))('%s has no untranslated keys', async (_path, s) => {
    const session = s.session ? { ...s.session, user: { ...s.session.user, locale: lang } } : null;
    server.use(...sessionHandlers(session, s.status));
    await renderRoute(s.path, { languages: [lang] });
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getAllByText(s.ready[lang]).length).toBeGreaterThan(0));
    expectNoRawKeys();
  });
});
```

```ts
// Wire types for the Alice REST API (snake_case, see api/openapi.yaml).

  code: string;
  params?: Record<string, string>;
}

export interface ApiErrorBody {
  error: {
    code: string;
    reason: string;
    message_key: string;
    message: string;
    request_id: string;
    retryable: boolean;
    params?: Record<string, string>;
    details?: FieldError[];
  };
}
```

`web/src/api/client.ts`:
```ts
import type { ApiErrorBody, FieldError } from './types';

// ApiError carries the stable error fields from the server (spec §9.3).
export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;
  readonly code: string;
  readonly reason: string;
  readonly messageKey: string;
  readonly params: Record<string, string>;
  readonly details: FieldError[];
  readonly requestId: string;
  readonly retryable: boolean;

  constructor(status: number, body: ApiErrorBody['error'] | null) {
    super(body?.message ?? `request failed with status ${status}`);
    this.name = 'ApiError';
    this.status = status;
    this.code = body?.code ?? '';
    this.reason = body?.reason ?? '';
    this.messageKey = body?.message_key ?? '';
    this.params = body?.params ?? {};
    this.details = body?.details ?? [];
    this.requestId = body?.request_id ?? '';
    this.retryable = body?.retryable ?? false;
  }
}

export function isApiError(e: unknown, reason?: string): e is ApiError {
  return e instanceof ApiError && (reason === undefined || e.reason === reason);
}

let csrfToken: string | null = null;

// setCsrfToken stores the token sent as X-CSRF-Token on state-changing requests.
// It lives in memory only; a page reload fetches it again from /auth/session.
export function setCsrfToken(token: string | null): void {
  csrfToken = token;
}

export function getCsrfToken(): string | null {
  return csrfToken;
}

type Listener = () => void;
const unauthorizedListeners = new Set<Listener>();

// onUnauthorized registers a callback for "your session ended" (reason auth.unauthenticated).
export function onUnauthorized(cb: Listener): () => void {
  unauthorizedListeners.add(cb);
  return () => {
    unauthorizedListeners.delete(cb);
  };
}

export interface FetchOptions {
  method?: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'PATCH' | 'DELETE';
  body?: unknown;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
  // quietUnauthorized suppresses the session-ended event (used by the initial session probe).
  quietUnauthorized?: boolean;
}

function parseErrorBody(text: string): ApiErrorBody['error'] | null {
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(text) as Partial<ApiErrorBody>;
    return parsed.error ?? null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export async function apiFetch<T>(path: string, opts: FetchOptions = {}): Promise<T> {
  const method = opts.method ?? 'GET';
  const headers: Record<string, string> = { Accept: 'application/json' };
  if (opts.body !== undefined) headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
  if (method !== 'GET' && csrfToken) headers['X-CSRF-Token'] = csrfToken;

  const res = await fetch(new URL(path, window.location.origin), {
    method,
    headers,
    body: opts.body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(opts.body),
    credentials: 'same-origin',
    signal: opts.signal,
  });
  if (res.status === 204) return undefined as T;
  const text = await res.text();
  if (!res.ok) {
    const err = new ApiError(res.status, parseErrorBody(text));
    if (err.status === 401 && err.reason === 'auth.unauthenticated' && !opts.quietUnauthorized) {
      unauthorizedListeners.forEach((l) => l());
    }
    throw err;
  }
  return (text === '' ? undefined : JSON.parse(text)) as T;
}
```

`web/src/api/endpoints.ts`:
```ts
import { apiFetch, setCsrfToken } from './client';
import type {
  AdminEnrollmentRequest,
  ApiToken,
  AuditPage,
  AuditQuery,
  AuditReport,
  CreatedToken,
  LoginResponse,
  MfaEnrollment,
  MfaInput,
  SessionInfo,
  SessionWithCodes,
  SetupSessionResponse,
  SetupStatus,
  TokenCreateRequest,
  User,
  UserCreateRequest,
  UserUpdateRequest,
  VersionInfo,
} from './types';

const v1 = '/api/v1';

// Setup wizard
export const getSetupStatus = () => apiFetch<SetupStatus>(`${v1}/setup/status`);

export async function startSetupSession(code: string): Promise<SetupSessionResponse> {
  const res = await apiFetch<SetupSessionResponse>(`${v1}/setup/session`, { method: 'POST', body: { code } });
  setCsrfToken(res.csrf_token);
  return res;
}

export const setSetupLanguage = (language: string) =>
  apiFetch<void>(`${v1}/setup/language`, { method: 'PUT', body: { language } });

export const startAdminEnrollment = (req: AdminEnrollmentRequest) =>
  apiFetch<MfaEnrollment>(`${v1}/setup/admin/enrollment`, { method: 'POST', body: req });

export const createAdmin = (code: string) =>
  apiFetch<SessionWithCodes>(`${v1}/setup/admin`, { method: 'POST', body: { code } });

export const setRecoveryPassphrase = (passphrase: string, passphraseConfirm: string) =>
  apiFetch<void>(`${v1}/setup/recovery-passphrase`, {
    method: 'POST',
    body: { passphrase, passphrase_confirm: passphraseConfirm },
  });

export const completeSetup = () => apiFetch<SetupStatus>(`${v1}/setup/complete`, { method: 'POST' });

// Authentication
export async function login(username: string, password: string): Promise<LoginResponse> {
  const res = await apiFetch<LoginResponse>(`${v1}/auth/login`, { method: 'POST', body: { username, password } });
  setCsrfToken(res.csrf_token);
  return res;
}

export const enrollMfa = () => apiFetch<MfaEnrollment>(`${v1}/auth/mfa/enroll`, { method: 'POST' });

export const verifyMfa = (input: MfaInput) =>
  apiFetch<SessionWithCodes>(`${v1}/auth/mfa/verify`, { method: 'POST', body: input });

export const getSession = () => apiFetch<SessionInfo>(`${v1}/auth/session`, { quietUnauthorized: true });

export const logout = () => apiFetch<void>(`${v1}/auth/logout`, { method: 'POST' });

export const stepUp = (code: string) => apiFetch<SessionInfo>(`${v1}/auth/stepup`, { method: 'POST', body: { code } });

export const changePassword = (currentPassword: string, newPassword: string) =>
  apiFetch<SessionInfo>(`${v1}/auth/password`, {
    method: 'POST',
    body: { current_password: currentPassword, new_password: newPassword },
  });

export const regenerateRecoveryCodes = () =>
  apiFetch<{ recovery_codes: string[] }>(`${v1}/auth/recovery-codes`, { method: 'POST' });

export const setMyLocale = (locale: string) => apiFetch<void>(`${v1}/auth/locale`, { method: 'PUT', body: { locale } });

export const getVersion = () => apiFetch<VersionInfo>(`${v1}/version`);

// Users (Admin)
export const listUsers = () => apiFetch<{ items: User[] }>(`${v1}/users`);

export const createUser = (req: UserCreateRequest) => apiFetch<User>(`${v1}/users`, { method: 'POST', body: req });

export const updateUser = (id: string, req: UserUpdateRequest) =>
  apiFetch<User>(`${v1}/users/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, { method: 'PATCH', body: req });

export const resetUserMfa = (id: string) =>
  apiFetch<void>(`${v1}/users/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/actions/reset-mfa`, { method: 'POST' });

export const resetUserPassword = (id: string, password: string) =>
  apiFetch<void>(`${v1}/users/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/actions/reset-password`, {
    method: 'POST',
    body: { password },
  });

export const revokeUserSessions = (id: string) =>
  apiFetch<void>(`${v1}/users/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/actions/revoke-sessions`, { method: 'POST' });

// API tokens
export function listTokens(userId?: string): Promise<{ items: ApiToken[] }> {
  const q = userId ? `?user_id=${encodeURIComponent(userId)}` : '';
  return apiFetch<{ items: ApiToken[] }>(`${v1}/tokens${q}`);
}

export const createToken = (req: TokenCreateRequest) =>
  apiFetch<CreatedToken>(`${v1}/tokens`, { method: 'POST', body: req });

export const revokeToken = (id: string) =>
  apiFetch<void>(`${v1}/tokens/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, { method: 'DELETE' });

// Audit log
export function listAudit(q: AuditQuery = {}): Promise<AuditPage> {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  if (q.limit) params.set('limit', String(q.limit));
  if (q.cursor) params.set('cursor', q.cursor);
  if (q.action) params.set('action', q.action);
  if (q.actor) params.set('actor', q.actor);
  if (q.result) params.set('result', q.result);
  const qs = params.toString();
  return apiFetch<AuditPage>(`${v1}/audit${qs ? `?${qs}` : ''}`);
}

export const verifyAudit = () => apiFetch<AuditReport>(`${v1}/audit/verify`, { method: 'POST' });

export const auditExportUrl = (format: 'csv' | 'json') => `${v1}/audit/export?format=${format}`;
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Lazy locale loading and the new strings**

English (source and fallback) stays in the main bundle; every other language becomes its own chunk loaded by `setLanguage` (spec §10.2). Replace `web/src/i18n.ts`:

```ts
import i18next, { type i18n as I18n } from 'i18next';
import { initReactI18next } from 'react-i18next';
import commonEn from '@locales/common/en.json';
import manifest from '@locales/manifest.json';
import webEn from './locales/en.json';

export type Lang = 'en' | 'tr';
export const LANGUAGES: { code: Lang; name: string }[] = manifest.locales.map((l) => ({
  code: l.code as Lang,
  name: l.name,
}));

const STORAGE_KEY = 'alice.lang';

interface Bundle {
  web: Record<string, unknown>;
  common: Record<string, unknown>;
}

// English is the source language and the fallback, so it is always bundled.
// Every other language is a separate chunk loaded on demand (spec §10.2).
const lazyBundles: Record<Exclude<Lang, 'en'>, () => Promise<Bundle>> = {
  tr: async () => ({
    web: (await import('./locales/tr.json')).default,
    common: (await import('@locales/common/tr.json')).default,
  }),
};

export function isSupported(code: string): code is Lang {
  return LANGUAGES.some((l) => l.code === code);
}

// Language tags are ASCII protocol data, so invariant lowercasing is correct here.
function baseCode(tag: string): string {
  return tag.split('-')[0].toLocaleLowerCase('en-US');
}

export function pickLanguage(stored: string | null, browser: readonly string[]): Lang {
  if (stored && isSupported(stored)) return stored;
  for (const tag of browser) {
    const base = baseCode(tag);
    if (isSupported(base)) return base;
  }
  return 'en';
}

function readStored(): string | null {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

async function loadBundle(lang: Lang): Promise<Bundle> {
  if (lang === 'en') return { web: webEn, common: commonEn };
  return lazyBundles[lang]();
}

async function ensureLoaded(i18n: I18n, lang: Lang): Promise<void> {
  if (i18n.hasResourceBundle(lang, 'web')) return;
  const bundle = await loadBundle(lang);
  i18n.addResourceBundle(lang, 'web', bundle.web, true, true);
  i18n.addResourceBundle(lang, 'common', bundle.common, true, true);
}

export async function createI18n(browserLanguages: readonly string[]): Promise<I18n> {
  const lng = pickLanguage(readStored(), browserLanguages);
  const instance = i18next.createInstance();
  await instance.use(initReactI18next).init({
    resources: { en: { web: webEn, common: commonEn } },
    partialBundledLanguages: true,
    lng: 'en',
    fallbackLng: 'en',
    ns: ['web', 'common'],
    defaultNS: 'web',
    fallbackNS: 'common',
    interpolation: { escapeValue: false }, // React escapes output
  });
  if (lng !== 'en') {
    await ensureLoaded(instance, lng);
    await instance.changeLanguage(lng);
  }
  document.documentElement.lang = lng;
  return instance;
}

export async function setLanguage(i18n: I18n, lang: Lang): Promise<void> {
  await ensureLoaded(i18n, lang);
  await i18n.changeLanguage(lang);
  document.documentElement.lang = lang;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, lang);
  } catch {
    // Storage can be blocked (private mode); the choice then lasts for this page only.
  }
}
```

`web/src/auth/AuthProvider.tsx`:
```tsx
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router';
import { onUnauthorized, setCsrfToken } from '../api/client';
import { getSession, getSetupStatus, logout } from '../api/endpoints';
import type { SessionInfo, SetupStatus } from '../api/types';
import { isSupported, setLanguage } from '../i18n';

export interface AuthState {
  session: SessionInfo | null;
  setupStatus: SetupStatus | null;
  loading: boolean;
  // sessionEnded is true after the server said the session expired or was revoked.
  sessionEnded: boolean;
  refresh: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshSetupStatus: () => Promise<SetupStatus | null>;
  setSession: (s: SessionInfo | null) => void;
  signOut: () => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthState | null>(null);

export function useAuth(): AuthState {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAuth must be used inside <AuthProvider>');
  return ctx;
}

async function loadSession(): Promise<SessionInfo | null> {
  try {
    return await getSession();
  } catch {
    return null; // 401 = signed out; server trouble also shows the sign-in page, which reports errors
  }
}

async function loadSetupStatus(): Promise<SetupStatus | null> {
  try {
    return await getSetupStatus();
  } catch {
    return null; // treat as "installed"; the sign-in page then shows any server problem
  }
}

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { i18n } = useTranslation();
  const [session, setSessionState] = useState<SessionInfo | null>(null);
  const [setupStatus, setSetupStatus] = useState<SetupStatus | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [sessionEnded, setSessionEnded] = useState(false);

  const setSession = useCallback((s: SessionInfo | null) => {
    setCsrfToken(s?.csrf_token ?? null);
    setSessionState(s);
    if (s) setSessionEnded(false);
  }, []);

  // The signed-in user's saved language wins over the browser language (spec §10.2).
  // It is applied once per sign-in so a later switch in the header is not undone.
  const userLocale = session?.user.locale ?? null;
  const appliedLocale = useRef<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!userLocale || appliedLocale.current === userLocale) return;
    appliedLocale.current = userLocale;
    if (userLocale !== i18n.language && isSupported(userLocale)) void setLanguage(i18n, userLocale);
  }, [userLocale, i18n]);

  const refreshSetupStatus = useCallback(async () => {
    const st = await loadSetupStatus();
    setSetupStatus(st);
    return st;
  }, []);

  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    const [st, s] = await Promise.all([loadSetupStatus(), loadSession()]);
    setSetupStatus(st);
    setSession(s);
  }, [setSession]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    void Promise.all([loadSetupStatus(), loadSession()]).then(([st, s]) => {
      if (!active) return;
      setSetupStatus(st);
      setSession(s);
      setLoading(false);
    });
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [setSession]);

  useEffect(
    () =>
      onUnauthorized(() => {
        // RequireAuth sees the missing session and sends the user to /login?ended=1.
        setCsrfToken(null);
        setSessionState(null);
        setSessionEnded(true);
      }),
    [],
  );

  const signOut = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      await logout();
    } catch {
      // The session may already be gone; signing out locally is still correct.
    }
    appliedLocale.current = null;
    setSession(null);
    navigate('/login', { replace: true });
  }, [navigate, setSession]);

  const value = useMemo<AuthState>(
    () => ({ session, setupStatus, loading, sessionEnded, refresh, refreshSetupStatus, setSession, signOut }),
    [session, setupStatus, loading, sessionEnded, refresh, refreshSetupStatus, setSession, signOut],
  );
  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}
```

`web/src/auth/guards.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Center, Loader, Text } from '@mantine/core';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { Navigate } from 'react-router';
import type { Role } from '../api/types';
import { useAuth } from './AuthProvider';

export function PageLoader() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  return (
    <Center mih={200}>
      <Loader aria-label={t('ui.loading')} />
    </Center>
  );
}

// RequireAuth sends visitors to the setup wizard (fresh install) or the sign-in page.
export function RequireAuth({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { session, setupStatus, loading, sessionEnded } = useAuth();
  if (loading) return <PageLoader />;
  if (setupStatus && !setupStatus.admin_exists) return <Navigate to="/setup" replace />;
  if (!session) return <Navigate to={sessionEnded ? '/login?ended=1' : '/login'} replace />;
  return <>{children}</>;
}

// RequireRole hides a page from roles that may not use it. The server enforces the same rule.
export function RequireRole({ roles, children }: { roles: Role[]; children: ReactNode }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { session } = useAuth();
  if (!session || !roles.includes(session.user.role)) {
    return (
      <Text role="alert" mt="xl">
        {t('ui.noAccess')}
      </Text>
    );
  }
  return <>{children}</>;
}
```

`web/src/components/ErrorAlert.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Alert, List, Text } from '@mantine/core';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { ApiError } from '../api/client';

// ErrorAlert shows an error in the user's language: what happened, what to do, and the request ID.
export function ErrorAlert({ error }: { error: unknown }) {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  if (!error) return null;

  if (!(error instanceof ApiError)) {
    return (
      <Alert color="red" role="alert" title={t('ui.networkErrorTitle')}>
        {t('ui.networkErrorAction')}
      </Alert>
    );
  }

  const base = error.messageKey;
  const known = base !== '' && i18n.exists(`${base}.title`);
  const title = known ? t(`${base}.title`, error.params) : error.message || t('ui.unexpectedError');
  const action = known && i18n.exists(`${base}.action`) ? t(`${base}.action`, error.params) : '';

  return (
    <Alert color="red" role="alert" title={title}>
      {action && <Text size="sm">{action}</Text>}
      {error.details.length > 0 && (
        <List size="sm" mt="xs">
          {error.details.map((d) => (
            <List.Item key={`${d.field}-${d.code}`}>
              {t('ui.fieldProblem', {
                field: i18n.exists(`ui.fields.${d.field}`) ? t(`ui.fields.${d.field}`) : d.field,
                problem: t(d.code, d.params),
              })}
            </List.Item>
          ))}
        </List>
      )}
      {error.requestId && (
        <Text size="xs" c="gray.7" mt="xs">
          {t('ui.requestId', { id: error.requestId })}
        </Text>
      )}
    </Alert>
  );
}
```

`web/src/components/StepUpProvider.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Button, Group, Modal, Stack, Text, TextInput } from '@mantine/core';
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useMemo, useRef, useState, type FormEvent, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { isApiError } from '../api/client';
import { stepUp } from '../api/endpoints';
import { useAuth } from '../auth/AuthProvider';
import { ErrorAlert } from './ErrorAlert';

interface StepUpApi {
  // run calls fn; when the server answers auth.stepup_required it asks for a fresh
  // authenticator code, re-confirms the session and calls fn once more.
  run: <T>(fn: () => Promise<T>) => Promise<T>;
}

const StepUpContext = createContext<StepUpApi | null>(null);

export function useStepUp(): StepUpApi {
  const ctx = useContext(StepUpContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useStepUp must be used inside <StepUpProvider>');
  return ctx;
}

interface Pending {
  resolve: () => void;
  reject: (e: unknown) => void;
  original: unknown;
}

export function StepUpProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { setSession } = useAuth();
  const [opened, setOpened] = useState(false);
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<unknown>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const pending = useRef<Pending | null>(null);

  const askForCode = useCallback(
    (original: unknown) =>
      new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
        pending.current = { resolve, reject, original };
        setCode('');
        setError(null);
        setOpened(true);
      }),
    [],
  );

  const run = useCallback(
    async <T,>(fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> => {
      try {
        return await fn();
      } catch (e) {
        if (!isApiError(e, 'auth.stepup_required')) throw e;
        await askForCode(e);
        return fn();
      }
    },
    [askForCode],
  );

  const cancel = () => {
    setOpened(false);
    pending.current?.reject(pending.current.original);
    pending.current = null;
  };

  const submit = async (ev: FormEvent) => {
    ev.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      setSession(await stepUp(code.trim()));
      setOpened(false);
      pending.current?.resolve();
      pending.current = null;
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const api = useMemo(() => ({ run }), [run]);
  return (
    <StepUpContext.Provider value={api}>
      {children}
      {/* zIndex above page modals (default 200): step-up often opens on top of an open form dialog. */}
      <Modal opened={opened} onClose={cancel} title={t('ui.stepUp.title')} centered zIndex={1000} transitionProps={{ duration: 0 }}>
        <form onSubmit={submit}>
          <Stack>
            <Text size="sm">{t('ui.stepUp.help')}</Text>
            <ErrorAlert error={error} />
            <TextInput
              label={t('ui.codeLabel')}
              value={code}
              onChange={(e) => setCode(e.currentTarget.value)}
              inputMode="numeric"
              autoComplete="one-time-code"
              maxLength={6}
              data-autofocus
            />
            <Group justify="flex-end">
              <Button variant="default" onClick={cancel}>
                {t('ui.cancel')}
              </Button>
              <Button type="submit" loading={busy}>
                {t('ui.stepUp.confirm')}
              </Button>
            </Group>
          </Stack>
        </form>
      </Modal>
    </StepUpContext.Provider>
  );
}
```

Notes: the CSRF token lives in memory only (never `localStorage`); a reload gets it again from `GET /auth/session`. `onUnauthorized` fires only for reason `auth.unauthenticated`, so a wrong step-up or sign-in code (`auth.mfa_invalid`, `auth.invalid_credentials`) never signs the user out. The user's saved `locale` is applied once per sign-in, so switching language in the header afterwards is not undone.

Replace `web/src/theme.ts` (the Task 24 axe checks fail otherwise: indigo shade 6 with white text is only 4.3:1 and the light/subtle variants draw shade-6 text on a pale tint, both below the WCAG AA 4.5:1):

```ts
import { InputWrapper, createTheme, defaultVariantColorsResolver, type VariantColorsResolver } from '@mantine/core';

// Mantine's "light" and "subtle" variants draw shade-6 text on a pale tint of the
// same color (for example green 2.2:1, indigo 3.8:1), below WCAG AA (4.5:1).
// Keep the tint as the color cue and use black text instead.
const variantColorResolver: VariantColorsResolver = (input) => {
  const colors = defaultVariantColorsResolver(input);
  if (input.variant === 'light' || input.variant === 'subtle') {
    return { ...colors, color: 'var(--mantine-color-black)', hoverColor: 'var(--mantine-color-black)' };
  }
  return colors;
};

export const theme = createTheme({
  primaryColor: 'indigo',
  // Shade 7 keeps white text on filled buttons and the active nav item above 4.5:1 (5.0:1).
  primaryShade: 7,
  defaultRadius: 'md',
  variantColorResolver,
  components: {
    // Field help texts default to the "dimmed" gray (3.3:1); gray 7 is 8.2:1.
    InputWrapper: InputWrapper.extend({ styles: { description: { color: 'var(--mantine-color-gray-7)' } } }),
  },
});
```

Replace `web/src/LanguageSwitcher.tsx`:

```tsx
import { SegmentedControl } from '@mantine/core';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { setMyLocale } from './api/endpoints';
import { LANGUAGES, setLanguage, type Lang } from './i18n';

// LanguageSwitcher changes the UI language. When signedIn is true the choice is also
// saved on the user's account so it follows them to other browsers.
export function LanguageSwitcher({ signedIn = false }: { signedIn?: boolean }) {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  const change = async (value: string) => {
    await setLanguage(i18n, value as Lang);
    if (signedIn) {
      try {
        await setMyLocale(value);
      } catch {
        // Saving the preference is best effort; the page is already translated.
      }
    }
  };
  return (
    <SegmentedControl
      aria-label={t('language.label')}
      value={i18n.language}
      onChange={(value) => void change(value)}
      data={LANGUAGES.map((l) => ({ value: l.code, label: l.name }))}
    />
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Alert, Anchor, AppShell, Button, Group, NavLink, Text } from '@mantine/core';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { Link, Outlet, useLocation } from 'react-router';
import type { Role } from '../api/types';
import { useAuth } from '../auth/AuthProvider';
import { LanguageSwitcher } from '../LanguageSwitcher';

interface NavItem {
  to: string;
  labelKey: string;
  roles: Role[];
}

const ALL: Role[] = ['admin', 'operator', 'auditor'];

// NAV_ITEMS lists the side navigation. Roles mirror the server permissions (docs/authz-matrix.md).
export const NAV_ITEMS: NavItem[] = [
  { to: '/', labelKey: 'nav.home', roles: ALL },
  { to: '/users', labelKey: 'nav.users', roles: ['admin'] },
  { to: '/audit', labelKey: 'nav.audit', roles: ['admin', 'auditor'] },
  { to: '/tokens', labelKey: 'nav.tokens', roles: ALL },
  { to: '/account', labelKey: 'nav.account', roles: ALL },
];

function SetupBanner() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { session, setupStatus } = useAuth();
  if (session?.user.role !== 'admin' || !setupStatus || setupStatus.completed) return null;
  const text = setupStatus.recovery_passphrase_set ? t('nav.setupBannerFinish') : t('nav.setupBanner');
  return (
    <Alert color="red" mb="md" data-testid="setup-banner">
      <Anchor component={Link} to="/setup">
        {text}
      </Anchor>
    </Alert>
  );
}

export function AppLayout() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { session, signOut } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();
  if (!session) return null;
  const items = NAV_ITEMS.filter((i) => i.roles.includes(session.user.role));

  return (
    <AppShell header={{ height: 60 }} navbar={{ width: 220, breakpoint: 'sm' }} padding="md">
      <AppShell.Header>
        <Group h="100%" px="md" justify="space-between">
          <Text fw={700} size="xl">
            {t('app.name', { ns: 'common' })}
          </Text>
          <Group>
            <LanguageSwitcher signedIn />
            <Text size="sm" data-testid="current-user">
              {session.user.display_name}
            </Text>
            <Button variant="default" onClick={() => void signOut()}>
              {t('nav.signOut')}
            </Button>
          </Group>
        </Group>
      </AppShell.Header>
      <AppShell.Navbar p="xs" aria-label={t('nav.label')}>
        {items.map((i) => (
          <NavLink
            key={i.to}
            component={Link}
            to={i.to}
            label={t(i.labelKey)}
            active={i.to === '/' ? location.pathname === '/' : location.pathname.startsWith(i.to)}
            // "filled": white on indigo 7 (5.0:1); the default "light" active style fails WCAG AA contrast.
            variant="filled"
          />
        ))}
      </AppShell.Navbar>
      <AppShell.Main>
        <SetupBanner />
        <Outlet />
      </AppShell.Main>
    </AppShell>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Stack, Text, Title } from '@mantine/core';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { getVersion } from '../api/endpoints';
import { useAuth } from '../auth/AuthProvider';

export function HomePage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { session } = useAuth();
  const version = useQuery({ queryKey: ['version'], queryFn: getVersion });

  let status = t('home.serverChecking');
  if (version.isSuccess) status = t('home.serverVersion', { version: version.data.version });
  if (version.isError) status = t('home.serverUnreachable');

  return (
    <Stack gap="sm">
      <Title order={1}>{t('home.title', { name: session?.user.display_name ?? '' })}</Title>
      <Text>{t('home.subtitle')}</Text>
      <Text size="sm" c="gray.7" data-testid="server-status" aria-live="polite">
        {status}
      </Text>
    </Stack>
  );
}
```

`web/src/router.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Outlet, type RouteObject } from 'react-router';
import { AuthProvider } from './auth/AuthProvider';
import { RequireAuth } from './auth/guards';
import { StepUpProvider } from './components/StepUpProvider';
import { AppLayout } from './layout/AppLayout';
import { HomePage } from './pages/HomePage';
import { LoginPage } from './pages/LoginPage';
import { NotFoundPage } from './pages/NotFoundPage';
import { SetupPage } from './pages/SetupPage';

function Root() {
  return (
    <AuthProvider>
      <StepUpProvider>
        <Outlet />
      </StepUpProvider>
    </AuthProvider>
  );
}

// routes is shared by the browser router (main.tsx) and the memory router in tests.
export function routes(): RouteObject[] {
  return [
    {
      element: <Root />,
      children: [
        { path: '/setup', element: <SetupPage /> },
        { path: '/login', element: <LoginPage /> },
        {
          path: '/',
          element: (
            <RequireAuth>
              <AppLayout />
            </RequireAuth>
          ),
          children: [{ index: true, element: <HomePage /> }],
        },
        { path: '*', element: <NotFoundPage /> },
      ],
    },
  ];
}
```

### Task 20: Web: setup wizard

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/components/TotpEnrollment.tsx`, `web/src/components/RecoveryCodes.tsx`
- Create: `web/src/pages/setup/CodeStep.tsx`, `LanguageStep.tsx`, `AdminStep.tsx`, `PassphraseStep.tsx`, `DoneStep.tsx`, `strength.ts` (all under `web/src/pages/setup/`)
- Modify (replace): `web/src/pages/SetupPage.tsx`
- Modify: `web/src/locales/en.json`, `web/src/locales/tr.json`
- Test: `web/src/pages/SetupPage.test.tsx`, `web/src/pages/setup/strength.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 19): `startSetupSession`, `setSetupLanguage`, `startAdminEnrollment`, `createAdmin`, `setRecoveryPassphrase`, `completeSetup` from `web/src/api/endpoints.ts`; `useAuth()` (`loading`, `setupStatus`, `session`, `setSession`, `refreshSetupStatus`), `PageLoader`, `ErrorAlert`, `useStepUp().run`, `LANGUAGES`/`setLanguage`; test helpers `renderRoute`, `server`, `sessionHandlers`, `sessionFor`, `freshStatus`, `installedStatus`, `errorResponse`, `expectNoRawKeys`. API behaviour from Tasks 12/14: `POST /setup/session` → `{csrf_token}` + setup cookie; `POST /setup/admin` → SessionWithCodes; `POST /setup/recovery-passphrase` needs step-up (`settings.manage`); error reasons `setup.code_invalid`, `setup.locked`, `auth.password_too_short` (params `min`).
- Produces (used by Tasks 21 and 24):
  - `TotpEnrollment({ enrollment: MfaEnrollment; onVerify: (code: string) => Promise<void> })` — heading "Set up your authenticator app", QR `<img alt="QR code for your authenticator app">`, key in `data-testid="totp-secret"`, field "6-digit code", button "Verify"; a rejected `onVerify` is shown with `ErrorAlert`.
  - `RecoveryCodes({ codes: string[]; onContinue: () => void })` — heading "Save your recovery codes", list `data-testid="recovery-codes"` (one `<code>` per code), checkbox "I saved these codes in a safe place", button "Continue" (disabled until ticked).
  - `startOf(status, session): WizardStep | 'home' | 'login'` and `type WizardStep = 'code' | 'language' | 'admin' | 'totp' | 'codes' | 'passphrase' | 'done'` in `SetupPage.tsx`; `passphraseStrength(p): 'tooShort' | 'fair' | 'good' | 'strong'` in `pages/setup/strength.ts`.
  - Wizard copy (EN / TR, exact): page heading "Set up Alice" / "Alice kurulumu"; code step heading "Enter the setup code" / "Kurulum kodunu girin", field "Setup code" / "Kurulum kodu"; buttons "Continue" / "Devam"; "Choose your language" / "Dilinizi seçin" with radio buttons "English" / "Türkçe"; "Create the administrator account" / "Yönetici hesabını oluşturun" with fields "Username", "Full name", "Email (optional)", "Password", "Repeat password" (TR "Kullanıcı adı", "Ad soyad", "E-posta (isteğe bağlı)", "Parola", "Parolayı tekrar girin"); "Choose a recovery passphrase" / "Kurtarma parolası belirleyin" with "Recovery passphrase", "Repeat the passphrase", checkbox "I stored the passphrase safely", button "Save passphrase" (disabled until ticked), strength text in `data-testid="passphrase-strength"`; "Alice is ready" / "Alice hazır" with button "Go to the dashboard" / "Ana sayfaya git".
  - Flow: code → language (the UI switches language immediately; Continue saves it with `PUT /setup/language`) → admin form → TOTP → recovery codes → passphrase (step-up aware) → done → `POST /setup/complete` → `/`. A reload during the wizard starts again at the setup code, because the setup CSRF token is kept in memory only; the server-side setup session and pending enrollment are unaffected. When an admin exists, only that signed-in admin reaches `/setup`, starting at the passphrase (or "done") step; completed installs redirect to `/`, signed-out visitors to `/login`.

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import type { SetupStatus } from '../api/types';
import { errorResponse, freshStatus, installedStatus, sessionFor, sessionHandlers } from '../test/fixtures';
import { expectNoRawKeys } from '../test/i18nAssert';
import { renderRoute } from '../test/render';
import { server } from '../test/server';
import { startOf } from './SetupPage';

const enrollment = { secret: 'JBSWY3DPEHPK3PXPJBSWY3DPEHPK3PXP', otpauth_uri: 'otpauth://totp/Alice:admin', qr_png: 'iVBORw0KGgo=' };
const codes = ['AAAA-BBBB-CCCC-DDDD', 'EEEE-FFFF-GGGG-HHHH'];

async function enterCode(code = 'K7QX2M-9TPD4R-WB5H8N-3VZC6A') {
  await userEvent.type(await screen.findByLabelText('Setup code'), code);
  await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Continue' }));
}

describe('setup wizard', () => {
  it('walks a fresh install from the setup code to the dashboard', async () => {
    let status: SetupStatus = { ...freshStatus };
    const csrfSeen: (string | null)[] = [];
    const bodies: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    server.use(
      http.get('/api/v1/setup/status', () => HttpResponse.json(status)),
      http.post('/api/v1/setup/session', async ({ request }) => {
        bodies.session = await request.json();
        return HttpResponse.json({ csrf_token: 'setup-csrf' });
      }),
      http.put('/api/v1/setup/language', async ({ request }) => {
        csrfSeen.push(request.headers.get('X-CSRF-Token'));
        bodies.language = await request.json();
        return new HttpResponse(null, { status: 204 });
      }),
      http.post('/api/v1/setup/admin/enrollment', async ({ request }) => {
        bodies.enrollment = await request.json();
        return HttpResponse.json(enrollment);
      }),
      http.post('/api/v1/setup/admin', async ({ request }) => {
        bodies.admin = await request.json();
        status = { ...status, admin_exists: true };
        return HttpResponse.json({ ...sessionFor('admin'), recovery_codes: codes });
      }),
      http.post('/api/v1/setup/recovery-passphrase', async ({ request }) => {
        csrfSeen.push(request.headers.get('X-CSRF-Token'));
        bodies.passphrase = await request.json();
        status = { ...status, recovery_passphrase_set: true };
        return new HttpResponse(null, { status: 204 });
      }),
      http.post('/api/v1/setup/complete', () => {
        status = { ...installedStatus };
        return HttpResponse.json(status);
      }),
    );
    const { router } = await renderRoute('/setup');

    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Set up Alice' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await enterCode();

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('English'));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Continue' }));
    expect(bodies.language).toEqual({ language: 'en' });

    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Create the administrator account' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.clear(screen.getByLabelText('Username'));
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Username'), 'ayse');
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Full name'), 'Ayşe Yılmaz');
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Password'), 'correct horse battery');
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Repeat password'), 'correct horse battery');

      username: 'ayse',

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Password'), 'correct horse battery');

  it('sends visitors of a finished installation to the right page', async () => {

});

describe('startOf', () => {
  const admin = sessionFor('admin');
  it('chooses the first step from the setup status', () => {
    expect(startOf(null, null)).toBe('code');
    expect(startOf(freshStatus, null)).toBe('code');
    expect(startOf(installedStatus, null)).toBe('login');
    expect(startOf(installedStatus, admin)).toBe('home');
    expect(startOf({ ...installedStatus, completed: false, recovery_passphrase_set: false }, admin)).toBe('passphrase');
    expect(startOf({ ...installedStatus, completed: false }, admin)).toBe('done');
    expect(startOf({ ...installedStatus, completed: false }, sessionFor('operator'))).toBe('home');
  });
});
```

`web/src/components/TotpEnrollment.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Button, Code, Image, Stack, Text, TextInput, Title } from '@mantine/core';
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import type { MfaEnrollment } from '../api/types';
import { ErrorAlert } from './ErrorAlert';

interface Props {
  enrollment: MfaEnrollment;
  // onVerify sends the code to the server; a rejected promise is shown as an error.
  onVerify: (code: string) => Promise<void>;
}

// TotpEnrollment shows the QR code and key for an authenticator app and asks for the first code.
export function TotpEnrollment({ enrollment, onVerify }: Props) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<unknown>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const submit = async (ev: FormEvent) => {
    ev.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await onVerify(code.trim());
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e);
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit}>
      <Stack>
        <Title order={2}>{t('ui.totp.title')}</Title>
        <Text>{t('ui.totp.help')}</Text>
        <Image
          src={`data:image/png;base64,${enrollment.qr_png}`}
          alt={t('ui.totp.qrAlt')}
          w={200}
          h={200}
          fit="contain"
        />
        <Text size="sm">{t('ui.totp.manual')}</Text>
        <Code block data-testid="totp-secret">
          {enrollment.secret}
        </Code>
        <ErrorAlert error={error} />
        <TextInput
          label={t('ui.codeLabel')}
          value={code}
          onChange={(e) => setCode(e.currentTarget.value)}
          inputMode="numeric"
          autoComplete="one-time-code"
          maxLength={6}
        />
        <Button type="submit" loading={busy}>
          {t('ui.verify')}
        </Button>
      </Stack>
    </form>
  );
}
```

`web/src/components/RecoveryCodes.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Button, Checkbox, Code, List, Stack, Text, Title } from '@mantine/core';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';

interface Props {
  codes: string[];
  onContinue: () => void;
}

// RecoveryCodes shows one-time recovery codes once and makes the user confirm they saved them.
export function RecoveryCodes({ codes, onContinue }: Props) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  return (
    <Stack>
      <Title order={2}>{t('ui.recoveryCodes.title')}</Title>
      <Text>{t('ui.recoveryCodes.help')}</Text>
      <List data-testid="recovery-codes" listStyleType="none" spacing={4}>
        {codes.map((c) => (
          <List.Item key={c}>
            <Code>{c}</Code>
          </List.Item>
        ))}
      </List>
      <Checkbox
        label={t('ui.recoveryCodes.saved')}
        checked={saved}
        onChange={(e) => setSaved(e.currentTarget.checked)}
      />
      <Button onClick={onContinue} disabled={!saved}>
        {t('ui.continue')}
      </Button>
    </Stack>
  );
}
```

The QR image is a `data:` URL, which the CSP allows (`img-src 'self' data:`, SEC-033). Inputs deliberately do not use Mantine's `required` prop: its asterisk becomes part of the label text, and the server validates every field anyway.

- [ ] **Step 4: Wizard steps and page**

Create:

`web/src/pages/setup/strength.ts`:
```ts
export type Strength = 'tooShort' | 'fair' | 'good' | 'strong';

export const MIN_PASSPHRASE_LENGTH = 16;

// passphraseStrength is a simple, local estimate for the strength meter. The server
// enforces the real minimum (16 characters); this only guides the user.
export function passphraseStrength(p: string): Strength {
  const length = [...p].length;
  if (length < MIN_PASSPHRASE_LENGTH) return 'tooShort';
  const classes = [/[a-z]/, /[A-Z]/, /[0-9]/, /[^A-Za-z0-9\s]/].filter((re) => re.test(p)).length;
  const words = p.trim().split(/\s+/).length;
  const score = Number(length >= 20) + Number(length >= 28) + Number(classes >= 3 || words >= 4);
  if (score >= 2) return 'strong';
  return score === 1 ? 'good' : 'fair';
}
```

`web/src/pages/setup/CodeStep.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Button, Stack, Text, TextInput, Title } from '@mantine/core';
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { startSetupSession } from '../../api/endpoints';
import { ErrorAlert } from '../../components/ErrorAlert';

export function CodeStep({ onDone }: { onDone: () => void }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<unknown>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const submit = async (ev: FormEvent) => {
    ev.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await startSetupSession(code.trim());
      onDone();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e);
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit}>
      <Stack>
        <Title order={2}>{t('setup.code.title')}</Title>
        <Text>{t('setup.code.help')}</Text>
        <ErrorAlert error={error} />
        <TextInput
          label={t('setup.code.label')}
          value={code}
          onChange={(e) => setCode(e.currentTarget.value)}
          autoComplete="off"
          spellCheck={false}
        />
        <Text size="sm" c="gray.7">
          {t('setup.code.lost')}
        </Text>
        <Button type="submit" loading={busy}>
          {t('ui.continue')}
        </Button>
      </Stack>
    </form>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Button, Group, Radio, Stack, Title } from '@mantine/core';
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { setSetupLanguage } from '../../api/endpoints';
import { ErrorAlert } from '../../components/ErrorAlert';
import { LANGUAGES, setLanguage, type Lang } from '../../i18n';

export function LanguageStep({ onDone }: { onDone: () => void }) {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  const [error, setError] = useState<unknown>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const submit = async (ev: FormEvent) => {
    ev.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await setSetupLanguage(i18n.language);
      onDone();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e);
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit}>
      <Stack>
        <Title order={2}>{t('setup.language.title')}</Title>
        <ErrorAlert error={error} />
        <Radio.Group
          label={t('setup.language.label')}
          value={i18n.language}
          onChange={(v) => void setLanguage(i18n, v as Lang)}
        >
          <Group mt="xs">
            {LANGUAGES.map((l) => (
              <Radio key={l.code} value={l.code} label={l.name} />
            ))}
          </Group>
        </Radio.Group>
        <Button type="submit" loading={busy}>
          {t('ui.continue')}
        </Button>
      </Stack>
    </form>
  );
}
```

`web/src/pages/setup/AdminStep.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Alert, Button, PasswordInput, Stack, Text, TextInput, Title } from '@mantine/core';
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { startAdminEnrollment } from '../../api/endpoints';
import type { MfaEnrollment } from '../../api/types';
import { ErrorAlert } from '../../components/ErrorAlert';

export function AdminStep({ onEnrolled }: { onEnrolled: (e: MfaEnrollment) => void }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('admin');
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [repeat, setRepeat] = useState('');
  const [mismatch, setMismatch] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<unknown>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const submit = async (ev: FormEvent) => {
    ev.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setMismatch(password !== repeat);
    if (password !== repeat) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      onEnrolled(
        await startAdminEnrollment({ username: username.trim(), display_name: displayName.trim(), email: email.trim(), password }),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e);
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit}>
      <Stack>
        <Title order={2}>{t('setup.admin.title')}</Title>
        <Text>{t('setup.admin.help')}</Text>
        <ErrorAlert error={error} />
        {mismatch && (
          <Alert color="red" role="alert">
            {t('setup.admin.passwordMismatch')}
          </Alert>
        )}
        <TextInput
          label={t('setup.admin.username')}
          value={username}
          onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.currentTarget.value)}
          autoComplete="username"
        />
        <TextInput
          label={t('setup.admin.fullName')}
          value={displayName}
          onChange={(e) => setDisplayName(e.currentTarget.value)}
          autoComplete="name"
        />
        <TextInput
          label={t('setup.admin.email')}
          type="email"
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.currentTarget.value)}
          autoComplete="email"
        />
        <PasswordInput
          label={t('setup.admin.password')}
          description={t('setup.admin.passwordHelp')}
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.currentTarget.value)}
          autoComplete="new-password"
        />
        <PasswordInput
          label={t('setup.admin.repeatPassword')}
          value={repeat}
          onChange={(e) => setRepeat(e.currentTarget.value)}
          autoComplete="new-password"
        />
        <Button type="submit" loading={busy}>
          {t('ui.continue')}
        </Button>
      </Stack>
    </form>
  );
}
```

`web/src/pages/setup/PassphraseStep.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Alert, Button, Checkbox, PasswordInput, Progress, Stack, Text, Title } from '@mantine/core';
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { setRecoveryPassphrase } from '../../api/endpoints';
import { ErrorAlert } from '../../components/ErrorAlert';
import { useStepUp } from '../../components/StepUpProvider';
import { passphraseStrength, type Strength } from './strength';

const METER: Record<Strength, { value: number; color: string }> = {
  tooShort: { value: 10, color: 'red' },
  fair: { value: 40, color: 'orange' },
  good: { value: 70, color: 'yellow' },
  strong: { value: 100, color: 'green' },
};

export function PassphraseStep({ onDone }: { onDone: () => void }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { run } = useStepUp();
  const [passphrase, setPassphrase] = useState('');
  const [repeat, setRepeat] = useState('');
  const [stored, setStored] = useState(false);
  const [mismatch, setMismatch] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<unknown>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const strength = passphraseStrength(passphrase);

  const submit = async (ev: FormEvent) => {
    ev.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setMismatch(passphrase !== repeat);
    if (passphrase !== repeat) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await run(() => setRecoveryPassphrase(passphrase, repeat));
      onDone();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e);
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit}>
      <Stack>
        <Title order={2}>{t('setup.passphrase.title')}</Title>
        <Alert color="yellow">{t('setup.passphrase.warning')}</Alert>
        <ErrorAlert error={error} />
        {mismatch && (
          <Alert color="red" role="alert">
            {t('setup.passphrase.mismatch')}
          </Alert>
        )}
        <PasswordInput
          label={t('setup.passphrase.label')}
          description={t('setup.passphrase.help')}
          value={passphrase}
          onChange={(e) => setPassphrase(e.currentTarget.value)}
          autoComplete="new-password"
        />
        <Progress
          value={METER[strength].value}
          color={METER[strength].color}
          aria-label={t('setup.passphrase.strengthLabel')}
        />
        <Text size="sm" data-testid="passphrase-strength" aria-live="polite">
          {t(`setup.passphrase.strength.${strength}`)}
        </Text>
        <PasswordInput
          label={t('setup.passphrase.repeat')}
          value={repeat}
          onChange={(e) => setRepeat(e.currentTarget.value)}
          autoComplete="new-password"
        />
        <Checkbox
          label={t('setup.passphrase.stored')}
          checked={stored}
          onChange={(e) => setStored(e.currentTarget.checked)}
        />
        <Button type="submit" loading={busy} disabled={!stored}>
          {t('setup.passphrase.save')}
        </Button>
      </Stack>
    </form>
  );
}
```

`web/src/pages/setup/DoneStep.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Button, Stack, Text, Title } from '@mantine/core';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router';
import { completeSetup } from '../../api/endpoints';
import { useAuth } from '../../auth/AuthProvider';
import { ErrorAlert } from '../../components/ErrorAlert';

export function DoneStep() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { refreshSetupStatus } = useAuth();
  const [error, setError] = useState<unknown>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const finish = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await completeSetup();
      await refreshSetupStatus();
      navigate('/', { replace: true });
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e);
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Stack>
      <Title order={2}>{t('setup.done.title')}</Title>
      <Text>{t('setup.done.help')}</Text>
      <ErrorAlert error={error} />
      <Button onClick={() => void finish()} loading={busy}>
        {t('setup.done.button')}
      </Button>
    </Stack>
  );
}
```

Replace `web/src/pages/SetupPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Container, Stack, Title } from '@mantine/core';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { Navigate } from 'react-router';
import { createAdmin } from '../api/endpoints';
import type { MfaEnrollment, SessionInfo, SetupStatus } from '../api/types';
import { useAuth } from '../auth/AuthProvider';
import { PageLoader } from '../auth/guards';
import { RecoveryCodes } from '../components/RecoveryCodes';
import { TotpEnrollment } from '../components/TotpEnrollment';
import { AdminStep } from './setup/AdminStep';
import { CodeStep } from './setup/CodeStep';
import { DoneStep } from './setup/DoneStep';
import { LanguageStep } from './setup/LanguageStep';
import { PassphraseStep } from './setup/PassphraseStep';

export type WizardStep = 'code' | 'language' | 'admin' | 'totp' | 'codes' | 'passphrase' | 'done';

// startOf decides where the wizard begins. Before an administrator exists everybody
// starts with the setup code; afterwards only the signed-in administrator may finish
// the remaining steps (spec §18.5).
export function startOf(status: SetupStatus | null, session: SessionInfo | null): WizardStep | 'home' | 'login' {
  if (!status || !status.admin_exists) return 'code';
  if (!session) return 'login';
  if (session.user.role !== 'admin' || status.completed) return 'home';
  return status.recovery_passphrase_set ? 'done' : 'passphrase';
}

export function SetupPage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { loading, setupStatus, session, setSession } = useAuth();
  const [step, setStep] = useState<WizardStep | null>(null);
  const [enrollment, setEnrollment] = useState<MfaEnrollment | null>(null);
  const [codes, setCodes] = useState<string[]>([]);

  if (loading) return <PageLoader />;
  const start = startOf(setupStatus, session);
  if (step === null && start === 'home') return <Navigate to="/" replace />;
  if (step === null && start === 'login') return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  const current: WizardStep = step ?? (start as WizardStep);

  const verifyAdmin = async (code: string) => {
    const res = await createAdmin(code);
    setSession(res);
    setCodes(res.recovery_codes ?? []);
    setStep('codes');
  };

  return (
    <Container size="sm" my="xl">
      <Stack>
        <Title order={1}>{t('setup.title')}</Title>
        {current === 'code' && <CodeStep onDone={() => setStep('language')} />}
        {current === 'language' && <LanguageStep onDone={() => setStep('admin')} />}
        {current === 'admin' && (
          <AdminStep
            onEnrolled={(e) => {
              setEnrollment(e);
              setStep('totp');
            }}
          />
        )}
        {current === 'totp' && enrollment && <TotpEnrollment enrollment={enrollment} onVerify={verifyAdmin} />}
        {current === 'codes' && <RecoveryCodes codes={codes} onContinue={() => setStep('passphrase')} />}
        {current === 'passphrase' && <PassphraseStep onDone={() => setStep('done')} />}
        {current === 'done' && <DoneStep />}
      </Stack>
    </Container>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `web/src/components/StepUpProvider.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router';
import { apiFetch, getCsrfToken, isApiError } from '../api/client';
import { AuthProvider } from '../auth/AuthProvider';
import { errorResponse, sessionFor, sessionHandlers } from '../test/fixtures';
import { renderApp } from '../test/render';
import { server } from '../test/server';
import { StepUpProvider, useStepUp } from './StepUpProvider';

function Harness() {
  const { run } = useStepUp();
  const [result, setResult] = useState('');
  const go = async () => {
    try {
      await run(() => apiFetch('/api/v1/sensitive', { method: 'POST' }));
      setResult('done');
    } catch (e) {
      setResult(isApiError(e) ? `failed: ${e.reason}` : 'failed');
    }
  };
  return (
    <>
      <button onClick={() => void go()}>Do it</button>
      <output data-testid="result">{result}</output>
    </>
  );
}

async function renderHarness() {
  return renderApp(
    <MemoryRouter>
      <AuthProvider>
        <StepUpProvider>
          <Harness />
        </StepUpProvider>
      </AuthProvider>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

describe('StepUpProvider', () => {
  it('runs directly when no step-up is needed', async () => {
    server.use(...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('admin')), http.post('/api/v1/sensitive', () => HttpResponse.json({})));
    await renderHarness();
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Do it' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByTestId('result')).toHaveTextContent('done'));
    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('asks for a code, survives a wrong one and retries with the renewed session', async () => {
    let attempts = 0;
    const csrfSeen: (string | null)[] = [];
    server.use(
      ...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('admin')),
      http.post('/api/v1/sensitive', ({ request }) => {
        attempts += 1;
        csrfSeen.push(request.headers.get('X-CSRF-Token'));
        return attempts === 1 ? errorResponse(403, 'ALC-AUTH-0003', 'auth.stepup_required') : HttpResponse.json({});
      }),
      http.post('/api/v1/auth/stepup', async ({ request }) => {
        const { code } = (await request.json()) as { code: string };
        return code === '111111'
          ? HttpResponse.json({ ...sessionFor('admin'), csrf_token: 'csrf-stepped-up' })
          : errorResponse(401, 'ALC-AUTH-0006', 'auth.mfa_invalid');
      }),
    );
    await renderHarness();
    await waitFor(() => expect(getCsrfToken()).toBe('csrf-admin'));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Do it' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: "Confirm it's you" })).toBeInTheDocument();

    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('6-digit code'), '000000');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('The verification code is not correct.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog')).toBeInTheDocument();

    await userEvent.clear(screen.getByLabelText('6-digit code'));
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('6-digit code'), '111111');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByTestId('result')).toHaveTextContent('done'));
    expect(csrfSeen).toEqual(['csrf-admin', 'csrf-stepped-up']);
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument());
  });

  it('gives up with the original error when the user cancels', async () => {
    server.use(
      ...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('admin')),
      http.post('/api/v1/sensitive', () => errorResponse(403, 'ALC-AUTH-0003', 'auth.stepup_required')),
    );
    await renderHarness();
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Do it' }));
    await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: "Confirm it's you" });
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByTestId('result')).toHaveTextContent('failed: auth.stepup_required'));
  });
});
```
