Create `web/src/pages/LoginPage.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import type { SessionInfo } from '../api/types';
import { errorResponse, freshStatus, installedStatus, sessionFor, sessionHandlers } from '../test/fixtures';
import { expectNoRawKeys } from '../test/i18nAssert';
import { renderRoute } from '../test/render';
import { server } from '../test/server';

const enrollment = { secret: 'JBSWY3DPEHPK3PXP', otpauth_uri: 'otpauth://totp/Alice:olcay', qr_png: 'iVBORw0KGgo=' };

// signedInAfter makes GET /auth/session answer 401 until verify succeeded.
function loginHandlers(status: 'mfa_required' | 'mfa_enrollment_required', verify: Parameters<typeof http.post>[1]) {
  let session: SessionInfo | null = null;
  return [
    http.get('/api/v1/setup/status', () => HttpResponse.json(installedStatus)),
    http.get('/api/v1/auth/session', () =>
      session ? HttpResponse.json(session) : errorResponse(401, 'ALC-AUTH-0001', 'auth.unauthenticated'),
    ),
    http.post('/api/v1/auth/login', () => HttpResponse.json({ status, csrf_token: 'pending-csrf' })),
    http.post('/api/v1/auth/mfa/enroll', () => HttpResponse.json(enrollment)),
    http.post('/api/v1/auth/mfa/verify', async (info) => {
      const res = await verify(info);
      if (res instanceof Response && res.ok) session = (await res.clone().json()) as SessionInfo;
      return res;
    }),
  ];
}

async function signIn(username = 'olcay', password = 'correct horse battery') {
  await userEvent.type(await screen.findByLabelText('Username'), username);
  await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Password'), password);
  await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Sign in' }));
}

describe('sign-in page', () => {
  it('signs in with password and authenticator code', async () => {
    let csrf: string | null = null;
    let body: unknown = null;
    server.use(
      ...loginHandlers('mfa_required', async ({ request }) => {
        csrf = request.headers.get('X-CSRF-Token');
        body = await request.json();
        return HttpResponse.json(sessionFor('operator'));
      }),
    );
    const { router } = await renderRoute('/login');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Sign in to Alice' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await signIn();
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Enter your verification code' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('6-digit code'), '123456');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Verify' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/'));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Welcome, Olcay Öztürk' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(body).toEqual({ code: '123456' });
    expect(csrf).toBe('pending-csrf');
  });

  it('shows the generic message for a wrong username or password', async () => {
    server.use(http.post('/api/v1/auth/login', () => errorResponse(401, 'ALC-AUTH-0005', 'auth.invalid_credentials')));
    const { router } = await renderRoute('/login');
    await signIn('nobody', 'wrong password!');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Incorrect username or password.');
    expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/login');
  });

  it('keeps the code step after a wrong code and accepts the next one', async () => {
    let tries = 0;
    server.use(
      ...loginHandlers('mfa_required', () => {
        tries += 1;
        return tries === 1
          ? errorResponse(401, 'ALC-AUTH-0006', 'auth.mfa_invalid')
          : HttpResponse.json(sessionFor('operator'));
      }),
    );
    const { router } = await renderRoute('/login');
    await signIn();
    await userEvent.type(await screen.findByLabelText('6-digit code'), '000000');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Verify' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('The verification code is not correct.');
    await userEvent.clear(screen.getByLabelText('6-digit code'));
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('6-digit code'), '123456');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Verify' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/'));
  });

  it('accepts a recovery code instead of an authenticator code', async () => {
    let body: unknown = null;
    server.use(
      ...loginHandlers('mfa_required', async ({ request }) => {
        body = await request.json();
        return HttpResponse.json(sessionFor('operator'));
      }),
    );
    const { router } = await renderRoute('/login');
    await signIn();
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Use a recovery code' }));
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Recovery code'), 'AAAA-BBBB-CCCC-DDDD');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Verify' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/'));
    expect(body).toEqual({ recovery_code: 'AAAA-BBBB-CCCC-DDDD' });
  });

  it('enrolls the authenticator app on the first sign-in and shows recovery codes', async () => {
    server.use(
      ...loginHandlers('mfa_enrollment_required', () =>
        HttpResponse.json({ ...sessionFor('operator'), recovery_codes: ['AAAA-BBBB-CCCC-DDDD'] }),
      ),
    );
    const { router } = await renderRoute('/login');
    await signIn();
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Set up your authenticator app' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('totp-secret')).toHaveTextContent('JBSWY3DPEHPK3PXP');
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('6-digit code'), '123456');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Verify' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Save your recovery codes' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/login');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('I saved these codes in a safe place'));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Continue' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/'));
  });

  it('starts again when the pending sign-in expired', async () => {
    server.use(...loginHandlers('mfa_required', () => errorResponse(401, 'ALC-AUTH-0001', 'auth.unauthenticated')));
    await renderRoute('/login');
    await signIn();
    await userEvent.type(await screen.findByLabelText('6-digit code'), '123456');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Verify' }));
    expect(await screen.findByLabelText('Username')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Your session ended. Please sign in again.');
  });

  it('tells the user when the session ended', async () => {
    await renderRoute('/login?ended=1');
    expect(await screen.findByText('Your session ended. Please sign in again.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('is shown in Turkish', async () => {
    await renderRoute('/login', { languages: ['tr-TR'] });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: "Alice'e giriş yapın" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Kullanıcı adı')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Giriş yap' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expectNoRawKeys();
  });

  it('sends signed-in users home and a fresh install to setup', async () => {
    server.use(...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('admin')));
    const first = await renderRoute('/login');
    await waitFor(() => expect(first.router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/'));
    first.unmount();

    server.use(...sessionHandlers(null, freshStatus));
    const second = await renderRoute('/login');
    await waitFor(() => expect(second.router.state.location.pathname).toBe('/setup'));
  });
});
```

Create `web/src/pages/AccountPage.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import { errorResponse, sessionFor, sessionHandlers } from '../test/fixtures';
import { renderRoute } from '../test/render';
import { server } from '../test/server';

async function fillPasswords(current: string, next: string, repeat: string) {
  await userEvent.type(await screen.findByLabelText('Current password'), current);
  await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('New password'), next);
  await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Repeat new password'), repeat);
  await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Change password' }));
}

describe('account page', () => {
  it('shows who is signed in', async () => {
    server.use(...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('auditor')));
    await renderRoute('/account');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'My account' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByTestId('account-summary')).toHaveTextContent('Signed in as auditor (Auditor)');
  });

  it('changes the password and keeps the user signed in with the new session', async () => {
    let body: unknown = null;
    let csrfAfter: string | null = null;
    const renewed = { ...sessionFor('operator'), csrf_token: 'csrf-renewed' };
    server.use(
      ...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('operator')),
      http.post('/api/v1/auth/password', async ({ request }) => {
        body = await request.json();
        return HttpResponse.json(renewed);
      }),
      http.post('/api/v1/auth/recovery-codes', ({ request }) => {
        csrfAfter = request.headers.get('X-CSRF-Token');
        return HttpResponse.json({ recovery_codes: ['AAAA-BBBB-CCCC-DDDD'] });
      }),
    );
    await renderRoute('/account');
    await fillPasswords('old password here', 'a much longer new one', 'a much longer new one');
    expect(await screen.findByText('Your password was changed. Your other sessions were signed out.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(body).toEqual({ current_password: 'old password here', new_password: 'a much longer new one' });
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create new recovery codes' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(csrfAfter).toBe('csrf-renewed'));
  });

  it('catches different new passwords locally', async () => {
    let called = false;
    server.use(
      ...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('operator')),
      http.post('/api/v1/auth/password', () => {
        called = true;
        return HttpResponse.json(sessionFor('operator'));
      }),
    );
    await renderRoute('/account');
    await fillPasswords('old password here', 'a much longer new one', 'a much longer new two');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('The two new passwords are different.');
    expect(called).toBe(false);
  });

  it('explains a wrong current password', async () => {
    server.use(
      ...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('operator')),
      http.post('/api/v1/auth/password', () => errorResponse(422, 'ALC-AUTH-0011', 'auth.current_password_invalid')),
    );
    const { i18n } = await renderRoute('/account');
    await fillPasswords('wrong', 'a much longer new one', 'a much longer new one');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent(i18n.t('errors.auth.current_password_invalid.title'));
  });

  it('creates new recovery codes after confirming with a code', async () => {
    let calls = 0;
    server.use(
      ...sessionHandlers(sessionFor('admin')),
      http.post('/api/v1/auth/recovery-codes', () => {
        calls += 1;
        return calls === 1
          ? errorResponse(403, 'ALC-AUTH-0003', 'auth.stepup_required')
          : HttpResponse.json({ recovery_codes: ['AAAA-BBBB-CCCC-DDDD', 'EEEE-FFFF-GGGG-HHHH'] });
      }),
      http.post('/api/v1/auth/stepup', () => HttpResponse.json(sessionFor('admin'))),
    );
    await renderRoute('/account');
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Create new recovery codes' }));
    await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: "Confirm it's you" });
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('6-digit code'), '123456');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm' }));
    expect(await screen.findByTestId('recovery-codes')).toHaveTextContent('EEEE-FFFF-GGGG-HHHH');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('I saved these codes in a safe place'));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Continue' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Your new recovery codes are active.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Sign-in page**

Replace `web/src/pages/LoginPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Alert, Button, Container, Group, PasswordInput, Stack, TextInput, Title } from '@mantine/core';
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { Navigate, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router';
import { isApiError } from '../api/client';
import { enrollMfa, login, verifyMfa } from '../api/endpoints';
import type { MfaEnrollment, SessionInfo, SessionWithCodes } from '../api/types';
import { useAuth } from '../auth/AuthProvider';
import { PageLoader } from '../auth/guards';
import { ErrorAlert } from '../components/ErrorAlert';
import { RecoveryCodes } from '../components/RecoveryCodes';
import { TotpEnrollment } from '../components/TotpEnrollment';
import { LanguageSwitcher } from '../LanguageSwitcher';

type Phase = 'password' | 'mfa' | 'enroll' | 'codes';

function PasswordForm({ onDone, onError }: { onDone: (p: Phase, e?: MfaEnrollment) => void; onError: (e: unknown) => void }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const submit = async (ev: FormEvent) => {
    ev.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    onError(null);
    try {
      const res = await login(username.trim(), password);
      if (res.status === 'mfa_required') onDone('mfa');
      else onDone('enroll', await enrollMfa());
    } catch (e) {
      onError(e);
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit}>
      <Stack>
        <TextInput
          label={t('login.username')}
          value={username}
          onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.currentTarget.value)}
          autoComplete="username"
        />
        <PasswordInput
          label={t('login.password')}
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.currentTarget.value)}
          autoComplete="current-password"
        />
        <Button type="submit" loading={busy}>
          {t('login.signIn')}
        </Button>
      </Stack>
    </form>
  );
}

function MfaForm({ onSession, onError }: { onSession: (s: SessionInfo) => void; onError: (e: unknown) => void }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [recovery, setRecovery] = useState(false);
  const [value, setValue] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const submit = async (ev: FormEvent) => {
    ev.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    onError(null);
    try {
      onSession(await verifyMfa(recovery ? { recovery_code: value.trim() } : { code: value.trim() }));
    } catch (e) {
      onError(e);
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit}>
      <Stack>
        <Title order={2}>{t('login.mfaTitle')}</Title>
        {recovery ? (
          <TextInput
            key="recovery"
            label={t('login.recoveryCode')}
            value={value}
            onChange={(e) => setValue(e.currentTarget.value)}
            autoComplete="off"
            spellCheck={false}
          />
        ) : (
          <TextInput
            key="code"
            label={t('ui.codeLabel')}
            value={value}
            onChange={(e) => setValue(e.currentTarget.value)}
            inputMode="numeric"
            autoComplete="one-time-code"
            maxLength={6}
          />
        )}
        <Group justify="space-between">
          <Button
            variant="subtle"
            onClick={() => {
              setRecovery(!recovery);
              setValue('');
            }}
          >
            {recovery ? t('login.useApp') : t('login.useRecovery')}
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" loading={busy}>
            {t('ui.verify')}
          </Button>
        </Group>
      </Stack>
    </form>
  );
}

export function LoginPage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const { loading, session, setupStatus, setSession } = useAuth();
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<Phase>('password');
  const [enrollment, setEnrollment] = useState<MfaEnrollment | null>(null);
  const [withCodes, setWithCodes] = useState<SessionWithCodes | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<unknown>(null);

  if (loading) return <PageLoader />;
  if (phase === 'password' && setupStatus && !setupStatus.admin_exists) return <Navigate to="/setup" replace />;
  if (phase === 'password' && session) return <Navigate to="/" replace />;

  const finish = (s: SessionInfo) => {
    setSession(s);
    navigate('/', { replace: true });
  };

  // A pending sign-in lasts a few minutes; when it expires the user starts again.
  const onMfaError = (e: unknown) => {
    setError(e);
    if (isApiError(e, 'auth.unauthenticated')) setPhase('password');
  };

  const verifyEnrollment = async (code: string) => {
    const res = await verifyMfa({ code });
    if (res.recovery_codes && res.recovery_codes.length > 0) {
      setWithCodes(res);
      setPhase('codes');
    } else {
      finish(res);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Container size="xs" my="xl">
      <Stack>
        <Group justify="flex-end">
          <LanguageSwitcher />
        </Group>
        <Title order={1}>{t('login.title')}</Title>
        {phase === 'password' && params.get('ended') === '1' && !error && (
          <Alert color="yellow">{t('login.sessionEnded')}</Alert>
        )}
        {phase !== 'enroll' && <ErrorAlert error={error} />}
        {phase === 'password' && (
          <PasswordForm
            onError={setError}
            onDone={(next, e) => {
              setEnrollment(e ?? null);
              setPhase(next);
            }}
          />
        )}
        {phase === 'mfa' && <MfaForm onSession={finish} onError={onMfaError} />}
        {phase === 'enroll' && enrollment && <TotpEnrollment enrollment={enrollment} onVerify={verifyEnrollment} />}
        {phase === 'codes' && withCodes && <RecoveryCodes codes={withCodes.recovery_codes ?? []} onContinue={() => finish(withCodes)} />}
      </Stack>
    </Container>
  );
}
```

Recovery codes after a first enrollment are shown before the new session is stored, so the redirect to `/` cannot skip them. When the pending sign-in expires (5 minutes, `auth.unauthenticated` from `/auth/mfa/verify`), the page returns to the password form and shows why.

- [ ] **Step 4: Account page and route**

Create `web/src/pages/AccountPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Alert, Button, Paper, PasswordInput, Stack, Text, Title } from '@mantine/core';
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { changePassword, regenerateRecoveryCodes } from '../api/endpoints';
import { useAuth } from '../auth/AuthProvider';
import { ErrorAlert } from '../components/ErrorAlert';
import { RecoveryCodes } from '../components/RecoveryCodes';
import { useStepUp } from '../components/StepUpProvider';

function ChangePassword() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { setSession } = useAuth();
  const [current, setCurrent] = useState('');
  const [next, setNext] = useState('');
  const [repeat, setRepeat] = useState('');
  const [mismatch, setMismatch] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<unknown>(null);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const submit = async (ev: FormEvent) => {
    ev.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setDone(false);
    setMismatch(next !== repeat);
    if (next !== repeat) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      setSession(await changePassword(current, next));
      setCurrent('');
      setNext('');
      setRepeat('');
      setDone(true);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Paper withBorder p="md">
      <form onSubmit={submit}>
        <Stack>
          <Title order={2}>{t('account.password.title')}</Title>
          {done && <Alert color="green">{t('account.password.changed')}</Alert>}
          {mismatch && (
            <Alert color="red" role="alert">
              {t('account.password.mismatch')}
            </Alert>
          )}
          <ErrorAlert error={error} />
          <PasswordInput
            label={t('account.password.current')}
            value={current}
            onChange={(e) => setCurrent(e.currentTarget.value)}
            autoComplete="current-password"
          />
          <PasswordInput
            label={t('account.password.new')}
            description={t('account.password.newHelp')}
            value={next}
            onChange={(e) => setNext(e.currentTarget.value)}
            autoComplete="new-password"
          />
          <PasswordInput
            label={t('account.password.repeat')}
            value={repeat}
            onChange={(e) => setRepeat(e.currentTarget.value)}
            autoComplete="new-password"
          />
          <Button type="submit" loading={busy}>
            {t('account.password.submit')}
          </Button>
        </Stack>
      </form>
    </Paper>
  );
}

function NewRecoveryCodes() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { run } = useStepUp();
  const [codes, setCodes] = useState<string[] | null>(null);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<unknown>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const create = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    setSaved(false);
    try {
      setCodes((await run(() => regenerateRecoveryCodes())).recovery_codes);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Paper withBorder p="md">
      <Stack>
        <Title order={2}>{t('account.recovery.title')}</Title>
        <Text>{t('account.recovery.help')}</Text>
        <ErrorAlert error={error} />
        {saved && <Alert color="green">{t('account.recovery.active')}</Alert>}
        {codes ? (
          <RecoveryCodes
            codes={codes}
            onContinue={() => {
              setCodes(null);
              setSaved(true);
            }}
          />
        ) : (
          <Button variant="default" onClick={() => void create()} loading={busy}>
            {t('account.recovery.create')}
          </Button>
        )}
      </Stack>
    </Paper>
  );
}

export function AccountPage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { session } = useAuth();
  if (!session) return null;
  return (
    <Stack maw={640}>
      <Title order={1}>{t('account.title')}</Title>
      <Text data-testid="account-summary">
        {t('account.summary', {
          username: session.user.username,
          role: t(`enum.role.${session.user.role}`, { ns: 'common' }),
        })}
      </Text>
      <Text size="sm" c="gray.7">
        {t('account.languageHelp')}
      </Text>
      <ChangePassword />
      <NewRecoveryCodes />
    </Stack>
  );
}
```

In `web/src/router.tsx` import the page and add the route to the signed-in children:

```tsx
import { AccountPage } from './pages/AccountPage';
```

```tsx
          children: [
            { index: true, element: <HomePage /> },
            { path: 'account', element: <AccountPage /> },
          ],
```

The resulting file:

```tsx
import { Outlet, type RouteObject } from 'react-router';
import { AuthProvider } from './auth/AuthProvider';
import { RequireAuth } from './auth/guards';
import { StepUpProvider } from './components/StepUpProvider';
import { AppLayout } from './layout/AppLayout';
import { AccountPage } from './pages/AccountPage';
import { HomePage } from './pages/HomePage';
import { LoginPage } from './pages/LoginPage';
import { NotFoundPage } from './pages/NotFoundPage';
import { SetupPage } from './pages/SetupPage';

function Root() {
  return (
    <AuthProvider>
      <StepUpProvider>
        <Outlet />
      </StepUpProvider>
    </AuthProvider>
  );
}

// routes is shared by the browser router (main.tsx) and the memory router in tests.
export function routes(): RouteObject[] {
  return [
    {
      element: <Root />,
      children: [
        { path: '/setup', element: <SetupPage /> },
        { path: '/login', element: <LoginPage /> },
        {
          path: '/',
          element: (
            <RequireAuth>
              <AppLayout />
            </RequireAuth>
          ),
          children: [
            { index: true, element: <HomePage /> },
            { path: 'account', element: <AccountPage /> },
          ],
        },
        { path: '*', element: <NotFoundPage /> },
      ],
    },
  ];
}
```

### Task 22: Web: users and API tokens pages

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/datetime.ts`
- Create or replace (Task 19 may have left a placeholder): `web/src/pages/UsersPage.tsx`, `web/src/pages/TokensPage.tsx`
- Modify: `web/src/router.tsx` (add the `users` and `tokens` child routes)
- Modify: `web/src/locales/en.json`, `web/src/locales/tr.json` (top-level `users` and `tokens` objects)
- Test: `web/src/pages/UsersPage.test.tsx`, `web/src/pages/TokensPage.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Tasks 15, 19, 21): the endpoint functions and components listed below; wire JSON of Task 15 (`User`, `Token`); `enum.role.admin|operator|auditor` in the shared catalog (Task 19).
- Produces: `export function UsersPage(): JSX.Element`, `export function TokensPage(): JSX.Element`, `export function formatDateTime(iso: string | null, locale: string): string` (`web/src/datetime.ts`, reused by Task 23). UI strings used by Task 24: heading "Users" / "Kullanıcılar", button "Add user" / "Kullanıcı ekle" (also the create-dialog title), labels "Username", "Full name", "Email (optional)", "Role", "Initial password", submit "Create user" / "Kullanıcıyı oluştur"; heading "API tokens" / "API anahtarları", "Create token" / "Anahtar oluştur", `data-testid="new-token"`.

**Names used from Tasks 19–21 (do not rename):** `apiFetch`/`ApiError` (`web/src/api/client.ts`); types `User`, `Role`, `TokenScope`, `ApiToken`, `CreatedToken`, `AuditEntry`, `AuditResult`, `AuditPage`, `AuditReport`, `SessionInfo` (`web/src/api/types.ts`); endpoint functions returning the wire JSON unchanged (`listUsers(): Promise<{ items: User[] }>`, `createUser(req)`, `updateUser(id, req)`, `resetUserMfa(id)`, `resetUserPassword(id, password)`, `revokeUserSessions(id)`, `listTokens(userId?): Promise<{ items: ApiToken[] }>`, `createToken(req): Promise<CreatedToken>`, `revokeToken(id)`, `listAudit(q: { limit?; cursor?; action?; actor?; result? }): Promise<AuditPage>`, `verifyAudit(): Promise<AuditReport>`, `auditExportUrl(format: 'csv' | 'json'): string`); `useAuth()` (`web/src/auth/AuthProvider.tsx`); `RequireRole` (`web/src/auth/guards.tsx`); `ErrorAlert` (renders `role="alert"` with the request id); `useStepUp().run` (`web/src/components/StepUpProvider.tsx`); `renderRoute(path, { languages })` (`web/src/test/render.tsx`); msw `server` (`web/src/test/server.ts`, started with `onUnhandledRequest: 'error'`); fixtures `adminSession`, `operatorSession`, `auditorSession`, `sessionFor(role, locale)` (`web/src/test/fixtures.ts`).

Design notes: every mutating user call goes through `useStepUp().run` (users.manage and token creation require step-up; the provider asks for a TOTP code and retries). Per-row actions open one "Manage <name>" dialog with plain buttons (Mantine `Menu` dropdowns do not reopen reliably in jsdom, and a dialog is keyboard friendly). Choices use `NativeSelect` (a real `<select>`: accessible, easy to drive from Playwright). Usernames and token prefixes are technical identifiers and are shown in monospace, never case-transformed. The last-admin rule is enforced by the server; the page shows its error (`user.last_admin`) inside the dialog, which stays open.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests**

`web/src/pages/UsersPage.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import type { SessionInfo, User } from '../api/types';
import { adminSession, sessionFor } from '../test/fixtures';
import { renderRoute } from '../test/render';
import { server } from '../test/server';

const operator: User = {
  id: '0192a000-0000-7000-8000-00000000000a',
  username: 'operator1',
  display_name: 'Oğuz Işık',
  email: '',
  role: 'operator',
  locale: 'tr',
  disabled: false,
  mfa_enrolled: false,
  last_login_at: null,
  created_at: '2026-09-30T08:00:00Z',
};

function signedInAs(session: SessionInfo) {
  server.use(
    http.get('/api/v1/auth/session', () => HttpResponse.json(session)),
    http.get('/api/v1/setup/status', () =>
      HttpResponse.json({ admin_exists: true, recovery_passphrase_set: true, completed: true, default_locale: 'en' }),
    ),
    http.get('/api/v1/version', () => HttpResponse.json({ version: '1.2.3', commit: 'abc', build_time: 't' })),
  );
}

function withUsers(items: User[]) {
  server.use(http.get('/api/v1/users', () => HttpResponse.json({ items })));
}

describe('UsersPage', () => {
  beforeEach(() => localStorage.clear());

  it('lists users with localized roles in English', async () => {
    signedInAs(adminSession);
    withUsers([adminSession.user, operator]);
    await renderRoute('/users');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Users' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    const row = (await screen.findByText('operator1')).closest('tr')!;
    expect(within(row).getByText('Operator')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(row).getByText('Not set up yet')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(row).getByText('Oğuz Işık')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('is fully translated in Turkish', async () => {
    signedInAs(sessionFor('admin', 'tr')); // the signed-in user's locale wins over the browser language
    withUsers([operator]);
    await renderRoute('/users', { languages: ['tr-TR'] });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Kullanıcılar' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await screen.findByText('Operatör')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Kullanıcı ekle' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('creates a user with the chosen role and initial password', async () => {
    signedInAs(adminSession);
    withUsers([adminSession.user]);
    let body: unknown = null;
    server.use(
      http.post('/api/v1/users', async ({ request }) => {
        body = await request.json();
        return HttpResponse.json({ ...operator, username: 'auditor1', role: 'auditor' }, { status: 201 });
      }),
    );
    await renderRoute('/users');
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Add user' }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog');
    await userEvent.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText(/^Username/), 'auditor1');
    await userEvent.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText(/^Full name/), 'Ayşe Denetçi');
    await userEvent.selectOptions(within(dialog).getByLabelText(/^Role/), 'auditor');
    await userEvent.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText(/^Initial password/), 'auditor-password-2026');
    await userEvent.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Create user' }));
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(body).toEqual({
        username: 'auditor1',
        display_name: 'Ayşe Denetçi',
        email: '',
        role: 'auditor',
        password: 'auditor-password-2026',
      }),
    );
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument());
  });

  it('shows the server error when the last administrator would be demoted', async () => {
    signedInAs(adminSession);
    withUsers([adminSession.user]);
    server.use(
      http.patch('/api/v1/users/:id', () =>
        HttpResponse.json(
          {
            error: {
              code: 'ALC-USR-0002',
              reason: 'user.last_admin',
              message_key: 'errors.user.last_admin',
              message: 'At least one administrator must remain.',
              request_id: 'req-last-admin',
              retryable: false,
            },
          },
          { status: 409 },
        ),
      ),
    );
    await renderRoute('/users');
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Manage admin' }));
    await userEvent.click(within(await screen.findByRole('dialog')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Edit' }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog');
    await userEvent.selectOptions(within(dialog).getByLabelText(/^Role/), 'operator');
    await userEvent.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Save changes' }));
    const alert = await within(dialog).findByRole('alert');
    expect(alert).toHaveTextContent('req-last-admin');
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('resets two-factor sign-in and signs a user out everywhere', async () => {
    signedInAs(adminSession);
    withUsers([adminSession.user, operator]);
    const calls: string[] = [];
    server.use(
      http.post('/api/v1/users/:id/actions/:action', ({ params }) => {
        calls.push(`${String(params.id)} ${String(params.action)}`);
        return new HttpResponse(null, { status: 204 });
      }),
    );
    await renderRoute('/users');
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Manage operator1' }));
    await userEvent.click(within(await screen.findByRole('dialog')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Reset two-factor sign-in' }));
    let dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog');
    await userEvent.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Reset two-factor sign-in' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument());

    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Manage operator1' }));
    await userEvent.click(within(await screen.findByRole('dialog')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Sign out everywhere' }));
    dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog');
    await userEvent.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Sign out everywhere' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(calls).toEqual([`${operator.id} reset-mfa`, `${operator.id} revoke-sessions`]));
  });

  it('disables a user', async () => {
    signedInAs(adminSession);
    withUsers([operator]);
    let body: unknown = null;
    server.use(
      http.patch('/api/v1/users/:id', async ({ request }) => {
        body = await request.json();
        return HttpResponse.json({ ...operator, disabled: true });
      }),
    );
    await renderRoute('/users');
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Manage operator1' }));
    await userEvent.click(within(await screen.findByRole('dialog')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Disable' }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog');
    await userEvent.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Disable user' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(body).toEqual({ disabled: true }));
  });
});
```

`web/src/pages/TokensPage.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import type { ApiToken, SessionInfo } from '../api/types';
import { operatorSession, sessionFor } from '../test/fixtures';
import { renderRoute } from '../test/render';
import { server } from '../test/server';

const token: ApiToken = {
  id: '0192a000-0000-7000-8000-0000000000t1',
  user_id: operatorSession.user.id,
  name: 'backup script',
  prefix: 'alice_Ab12Cd',
  scope: 'read',
  expires_at: '2026-12-29T08:00:00Z',
  last_used_at: null,
  created_at: '2026-09-30T08:00:00Z',
};

function signedInAs(session: SessionInfo, items: ApiToken[]) {
  server.use(
    http.get('/api/v1/auth/session', () => HttpResponse.json(session)),
    http.get('/api/v1/setup/status', () =>
      HttpResponse.json({ admin_exists: true, recovery_passphrase_set: true, completed: true, default_locale: 'en' }),
    ),
    http.get('/api/v1/version', () => HttpResponse.json({ version: '1.2.3', commit: 'abc', build_time: 't' })),
    http.get('/api/v1/tokens', () => HttpResponse.json({ items })),
  );
}

describe('TokensPage', () => {
  beforeEach(() => localStorage.clear());

  it('lists own tokens in English', async () => {
    signedInAs(operatorSession, [token]);
    await renderRoute('/tokens');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'API tokens' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    const row = (await screen.findByText('backup script')).closest('tr')!;
    expect(within(row).getByText('alice_Ab12Cd')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(row).getByText('Read only')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(row).getByText('Never')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('creates a token and shows the secret once', async () => {
    signedInAs(operatorSession, []);
    let body: unknown = null;
    server.use(
      http.post('/api/v1/tokens', async ({ request }) => {
        body = await request.json();
        return HttpResponse.json({ token: 'alice_SECRET-VALUE', item: { ...token, scope: 'operate' } }, { status: 201 });
      }),
    );
    await renderRoute('/tokens');
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Create token' }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog');
    await userEvent.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText(/^Name/), 'deploy bot');
    await userEvent.selectOptions(within(dialog).getByLabelText(/^Access/), 'operate');
    const days = within(dialog).getByLabelText(/^Valid for \(days\)/);
    await userEvent.clear(days);
    await userEvent.type(days, '30');
    await userEvent.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Create' }));
    expect(await screen.findByTestId('new-token')).toHaveTextContent('alice_SECRET-VALUE');
    expect(screen.getByText('Copy this token now. It will not be shown again.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(body).toEqual({ name: 'deploy bot', scope: 'operate', expires_in_days: 30 });
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Done' }));
    expect(screen.queryByTestId('new-token')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('offers only read access to auditors, in Turkish', async () => {
    signedInAs(sessionFor('auditor', 'tr'), []); // the signed-in user's locale wins over the browser language
    await renderRoute('/tokens', { languages: ['tr-TR'] });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'API anahtarları' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Anahtar oluştur' }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog');
    const access = within(dialog).getByLabelText(/^Erişim/);
    expect(within(access).getAllByRole('option').map((o) => o.textContent)).toEqual(['Salt okunur']);
  });

  it('revokes a token after confirmation', async () => {
    signedInAs(operatorSession, [token]);
    let deleted = '';
    server.use(
      http.delete('/api/v1/tokens/:id', ({ params }) => {
        deleted = String(params.id);
        return new HttpResponse(null, { status: 204 });
      }),
    );
    await renderRoute('/tokens');
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Revoke token backup script' }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog');
    await userEvent.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Revoke token' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(deleted).toBe(token.id));
  });
});
```

`web/src/datetime.ts`:
```ts
// Formats an RFC 3339 UTC timestamp for display in the active UI language.
// Returns an empty string for null so tables can show "never" separately.
export function formatDateTime(iso: string | null, locale: string): string {
  if (!iso) return '';
  const d = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return iso;
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, { dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short' }).format(d);
}
```

`web/src/pages/UsersPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import {
  Badge,
  Button,
  Group,
  Modal,
  NativeSelect,
  PasswordInput,
  Stack,
  Table,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  Title,
  VisuallyHidden,
} from '@mantine/core';
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import {
  createUser,
  listUsers,
  resetUserMfa,
  resetUserPassword,
  revokeUserSessions,
  updateUser,
} from '../api/endpoints';
import type { Role, User } from '../api/types';
import { ErrorAlert } from '../components/ErrorAlert';
import { useStepUp } from '../components/StepUpProvider';
import { formatDateTime } from '../datetime';

const ROLES: Role[] = ['admin', 'operator', 'auditor'];

type Dialog =
  | { kind: 'create' }
  | { kind: 'manage'; user: User }
  | { kind: 'edit'; user: User }
  | { kind: 'resetPassword'; user: User }
  | { kind: 'resetMfa'; user: User }
  | { kind: 'revoke'; user: User }
  | { kind: 'toggle'; user: User };

export function UsersPage() {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  const users = useQuery({ queryKey: ['users'], queryFn: listUsers });
  const [dialog, setDialog] = useState<Dialog | null>(null);
  const close = () => setDialog(null);

  return (
    <Stack gap="md">
      <Group justify="space-between">
        <Title order={1}>{t('users.title')}</Title>
        <Button onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: 'create' })}>{t('users.add')}</Button>
      </Group>
      {users.isError && <ErrorAlert error={users.error} />}
      {users.isSuccess && (
        <Table striped highlightOnHover>
          <Table.Thead>
            <Table.Tr>
              <Table.Th>{t('users.columns.username')}</Table.Th>
              <Table.Th>{t('users.columns.name')}</Table.Th>
              <Table.Th>{t('users.columns.role')}</Table.Th>
              <Table.Th>{t('users.columns.status')}</Table.Th>
              <Table.Th>{t('users.columns.mfa')}</Table.Th>
              <Table.Th>{t('users.columns.lastLogin')}</Table.Th>
              <Table.Th>
                <VisuallyHidden>{t('users.columns.actions')}</VisuallyHidden>
              </Table.Th>
            </Table.Tr>
          </Table.Thead>
          <Table.Tbody>
            {users.data.items.map((u) => (
              <Table.Tr key={u.id}>
                <Table.Td>
                  <Text ff="monospace" size="sm">
                    {u.username}
                  </Text>
                </Table.Td>
                <Table.Td>{u.display_name}</Table.Td>
                <Table.Td>{t(`enum.role.${u.role}`)}</Table.Td>
                <Table.Td>
                  <Badge color={u.disabled ? 'gray' : 'green'} variant="light">
                    {u.disabled ? t('users.status.disabled') : t('users.status.active')}
                  </Badge>
                </Table.Td>
                <Table.Td>{u.mfa_enrolled ? t('users.mfa.enrolled') : t('users.mfa.notEnrolled')}</Table.Td>
                <Table.Td>{u.last_login_at ? formatDateTime(u.last_login_at, i18n.language) : t('users.never')}</Table.Td>
                <Table.Td>
                  <Button size="xs" variant="light" onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: 'manage', user: u })} aria-label={t('users.manageNamed', { name: u.username })}>
                    {t('users.manage')}
                  </Button>
                </Table.Td>
              </Table.Tr>
            ))}
          </Table.Tbody>
        </Table>
      )}
      {dialog?.kind === 'create' && <CreateUserModal onClose={close} />}
      {dialog?.kind === 'manage' && <ManageModal user={dialog.user} onChoose={setDialog} onClose={close} />}
      {dialog?.kind === 'edit' && <EditUserModal user={dialog.user} onClose={close} />}
      {dialog?.kind === 'resetPassword' && <ResetPasswordModal user={dialog.user} onClose={close} />}
      {(dialog?.kind === 'resetMfa' || dialog?.kind === 'revoke' || dialog?.kind === 'toggle') && (
        <ConfirmModal kind={dialog.kind} user={dialog.user} onClose={close} />
      )}
    </Stack>
  );
}

function ManageModal({ user, onChoose, onClose }: { user: User; onChoose: (d: Dialog) => void; onClose: () => void }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  return (
    <Modal opened onClose={onClose} title={t('users.manageTitle', { name: user.username })}>
      <Stack gap="xs">
        <Button variant="default" onClick={() => onChoose({ kind: 'edit', user })}>
          {t('users.actions.edit')}
        </Button>
        <Button variant="default" onClick={() => onChoose({ kind: 'resetMfa', user })}>
          {t('users.actions.resetMfa')}
        </Button>
        <Button variant="default" onClick={() => onChoose({ kind: 'resetPassword', user })}>
          {t('users.actions.resetPassword')}
        </Button>
        <Button variant="default" onClick={() => onChoose({ kind: 'revoke', user })}>
          {t('users.actions.revokeSessions')}
        </Button>
        <Button variant="default" color={user.disabled ? undefined : 'red'} onClick={() => onChoose({ kind: 'toggle', user })}>
          {user.disabled ? t('users.actions.enable') : t('users.actions.disable')}
        </Button>
      </Stack>
    </Modal>
  );
}

// useUserAction runs a mutating call through step-up, then refreshes the list.
function useUserAction(onDone: () => void) {
  const { run } = useStepUp();
  const client = useQueryClient();
  const [error, setError] = useState<unknown>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  async function perform(fn: () => Promise<unknown>) {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await run(fn);
      await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['users'] });
      onDone();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }
  return { perform, error, busy };
}

function RoleSelect({ value, onChange }: { value: Role; onChange: (r: Role) => void }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  return (
    <NativeSelect
      label={t('users.fields.role')}
      value={value}
      onChange={(e) => onChange(e.currentTarget.value as Role)}
      data={ROLES.map((r) => ({ value: r, label: t(`enum.role.${r}`) }))}
    />
  );
}

function CreateUserModal({ onClose }: { onClose: () => void }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [role, setRole] = useState<Role>('operator');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const { perform, error, busy } = useUserAction(onClose);
  const submit = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    void perform(() => createUser({ username, display_name: displayName, email, role, password }));
  };
  return (
    <Modal opened onClose={onClose} title={t('users.add')}>
      <form onSubmit={submit}>
        <Stack gap="sm">
          <TextInput label={t('users.fields.username')} value={username} onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.currentTarget.value)} required autoComplete="off" />
          <TextInput label={t('users.fields.name')} value={displayName} onChange={(e) => setDisplayName(e.currentTarget.value)} required />
          <TextInput label={t('users.fields.email')} type="email" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.currentTarget.value)} />
          <RoleSelect value={role} onChange={setRole} />
          <PasswordInput
            label={t('users.fields.initialPassword')}
            description={t('users.fields.initialPasswordHelp')}
            value={password}
            onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.currentTarget.value)}
            required
            autoComplete="new-password"
          />
          <ErrorAlert error={error} />
          <Button type="submit" loading={busy}>
            {t('users.create')}
          </Button>
        </Stack>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function EditUserModal({ user, onClose }: { user: User; onClose: () => void }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState(user.display_name);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(user.email);
  const [role, setRole] = useState<Role>(user.role);
  const { perform, error, busy } = useUserAction(onClose);
  const submit = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    void perform(() => updateUser(user.id, { display_name: displayName, email, role }));
  };
  return (
    <Modal opened onClose={onClose} title={t('users.editTitle', { name: user.username })}>
      <form onSubmit={submit}>
        <Stack gap="sm">
          <TextInput label={t('users.fields.name')} value={displayName} onChange={(e) => setDisplayName(e.currentTarget.value)} required />
          <TextInput label={t('users.fields.email')} type="email" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.currentTarget.value)} />
          <RoleSelect value={role} onChange={setRole} />
          <ErrorAlert error={error} />
          <Button type="submit" loading={busy}>
            {t('users.save')}
          </Button>
        </Stack>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function ResetPasswordModal({ user, onClose }: { user: User; onClose: () => void }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const { perform, error, busy } = useUserAction(onClose);
  const submit = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    void perform(() => resetUserPassword(user.id, password));
  };
  return (
    <Modal opened onClose={onClose} title={t('users.resetPasswordTitle', { name: user.username })}>
      <form onSubmit={submit}>
        <Stack gap="sm">
          <Text size="sm">{t('users.resetPasswordText')}</Text>
          <PasswordInput label={t('users.fields.newPassword')} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.currentTarget.value)} required autoComplete="new-password" />
          <ErrorAlert error={error} />
          <Button type="submit" loading={busy}>
            {t('users.setPassword')}
          </Button>
        </Stack>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function ConfirmModal({ kind, user, onClose }: { kind: 'resetMfa' | 'revoke' | 'toggle'; user: User; onClose: () => void }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { perform, error, busy } = useUserAction(onClose);
  const name = user.username;
  let title: string;
  let text: string;
  let button: string;
  let action: () => Promise<unknown>;
  if (kind === 'resetMfa') {
    title = t('users.confirm.resetMfaTitle', { name });
    text = t('users.confirm.resetMfaText');
    button = t('users.actions.resetMfa');
    action = () => resetUserMfa(user.id);
  } else if (kind === 'revoke') {
    title = t('users.confirm.revokeTitle', { name });
    text = t('users.confirm.revokeText');
    button = t('users.actions.revokeSessions');
    action = () => revokeUserSessions(user.id);
  } else if (user.disabled) {
    title = t('users.confirm.enableTitle', { name });
    text = t('users.confirm.enableText');
    button = t('users.confirm.enableButton');
    action = () => updateUser(user.id, { disabled: false });
  } else {
    title = t('users.confirm.disableTitle', { name });
    text = t('users.confirm.disableText');
    button = t('users.confirm.disableButton');
    action = () => updateUser(user.id, { disabled: true });
  }
  return (
    <Modal opened onClose={onClose} title={title}>
      <Stack gap="sm">
        <Text size="sm">{text}</Text>
        <ErrorAlert error={error} />
        <Group justify="flex-end">
          <Button variant="default" onClick={onClose}>
            {t('users.cancel')}
          </Button>
          <Button color={kind === 'toggle' && !user.disabled ? 'red' : undefined} loading={busy} onClick={() => void perform(action)}>
            {button}
          </Button>
        </Group>
      </Stack>
    </Modal>
  );
}
```

`web/src/pages/TokensPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import {
  Alert,
  Button,
  Code,
  CopyButton,
  Group,
  Modal,
  NativeSelect,
  NumberInput,
  Stack,
  Table,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  Title,
} from '@mantine/core';
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { createToken, listTokens, revokeToken } from '../api/endpoints';
import type { ApiToken, TokenScope } from '../api/types';
import { useAuth } from '../auth/AuthProvider';
import { ErrorAlert } from '../components/ErrorAlert';
import { useStepUp } from '../components/StepUpProvider';
import { formatDateTime } from '../datetime';

export function TokensPage() {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  const client = useQueryClient();
  const tokens = useQuery({ queryKey: ['tokens'], queryFn: () => listTokens() });
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);
  const [revoking, setRevoking] = useState<ApiToken | null>(null);
  const [created, setCreated] = useState<string | null>(null);

  return (
    <Stack gap="md">
      <Group justify="space-between">
        <Title order={1}>{t('tokens.title')}</Title>
        <Button onClick={() => setCreating(true)}>{t('tokens.create')}</Button>
      </Group>
      <Text size="sm">{t('tokens.intro')}</Text>
      {created && (
        <Alert color="yellow" title={t('tokens.createdTitle')}>
          <Stack gap="xs">
            <Text size="sm">{t('tokens.createdText')}</Text>
            <Code block data-testid="new-token">
              {created}
            </Code>
            <Group>
              <CopyButton value={created}>
                {({ copied, copy }) => (
                  <Button size="xs" variant="light" onClick={copy}>
                    {copied ? t('tokens.copied') : t('tokens.copy')}
                  </Button>
                )}
              </CopyButton>
              <Button size="xs" variant="default" onClick={() => setCreated(null)}>
                {t('tokens.done')}
              </Button>
            </Group>
          </Stack>
        </Alert>
      )}
      {tokens.isError && <ErrorAlert error={tokens.error} />}
      {tokens.isSuccess && tokens.data.items.length === 0 && <Text c="gray.7">{t('tokens.empty')}</Text>}
      {tokens.isSuccess && tokens.data.items.length > 0 && (
        <Table striped>
          <Table.Thead>
            <Table.Tr>
              <Table.Th>{t('tokens.columns.name')}</Table.Th>
              <Table.Th>{t('tokens.columns.prefix')}</Table.Th>
              <Table.Th>{t('tokens.columns.access')}</Table.Th>
              <Table.Th>{t('tokens.columns.expires')}</Table.Th>
              <Table.Th>{t('tokens.columns.lastUsed')}</Table.Th>
              <Table.Th>{t('tokens.columns.actions')}</Table.Th>
            </Table.Tr>
          </Table.Thead>
          <Table.Tbody>
            {tokens.data.items.map((tok) => (
              <Table.Tr key={tok.id}>
                <Table.Td>{tok.name}</Table.Td>
                <Table.Td>
                  <Text ff="monospace" size="sm">
                    {tok.prefix}
                  </Text>
                </Table.Td>
                <Table.Td>{t(`tokens.scope.${tok.scope}`)}</Table.Td>
                <Table.Td>{formatDateTime(tok.expires_at, i18n.language)}</Table.Td>
                <Table.Td>{tok.last_used_at ? formatDateTime(tok.last_used_at, i18n.language) : t('tokens.never')}</Table.Td>
                <Table.Td>
                  <Button size="xs" variant="light" color="red" onClick={() => setRevoking(tok)} aria-label={t('tokens.revokeNamed', { name: tok.name })}>
                    {t('tokens.revoke')}
                  </Button>
                </Table.Td>
              </Table.Tr>
            ))}
          </Table.Tbody>
        </Table>
      )}
      {creating && (
        <CreateTokenModal
          onClose={() => setCreating(false)}
          onCreated={(secret) => {
            setCreating(false);
            setCreated(secret);
            void client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['tokens'] });
          }}
        />
      )}
      {revoking && (
        <RevokeTokenModal
          token={revoking}
          onClose={() => setRevoking(null)}
          onDone={() => {
            setRevoking(null);
            void client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['tokens'] });
          }}
        />
      )}
    </Stack>
  );
}

function CreateTokenModal({ onClose, onCreated }: { onClose: () => void; onCreated: (secret: string) => void }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { session } = useAuth();
  const { run } = useStepUp();
  const readOnly = session?.user.role === 'auditor';
  const scopes: TokenScope[] = readOnly ? ['read'] : ['read', 'operate'];
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [scope, setScope] = useState<TokenScope>('read');
  const [days, setDays] = useState<number | string>(90);
  const [error, setError] = useState<unknown>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const submit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await run(() => createToken({ name, scope, expires_in_days: Number(days) }));
      onCreated(res.token);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal opened onClose={onClose} title={t('tokens.create')}>
      <form onSubmit={(e) => void submit(e)}>
        <Stack gap="sm">
          <TextInput label={t('tokens.fields.name')} value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.currentTarget.value)} required maxLength={100} />
          <NativeSelect
            label={t('tokens.fields.access')}
            value={scope}
            onChange={(e) => setScope(e.currentTarget.value as TokenScope)}
            data={scopes.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: t(`tokens.scope.${s}`) }))}
          />
          <NumberInput
            label={t('tokens.fields.days')}
            value={days}
            onChange={setDays}
            min={1}
            max={365}
            allowDecimal={false}
            required
          />
          <ErrorAlert error={error} />
          <Button type="submit" loading={busy}>
            {t('tokens.submit')}
          </Button>
        </Stack>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function RevokeTokenModal({ token, onClose, onDone }: { token: ApiToken; onClose: () => void; onDone: () => void }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [error, setError] = useState<unknown>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const confirm = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await revokeToken(token.id);
      onDone();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };
  return (
    <Modal opened onClose={onClose} title={t('tokens.revokeTitle', { name: token.name })}>
      <Stack gap="sm">
        <Text size="sm">{t('tokens.revokeText')}</Text>
        <ErrorAlert error={error} />
        <Group justify="flex-end">
          <Button variant="default" onClick={onClose}>
            {t('tokens.cancel')}
          </Button>
          <Button color="red" loading={busy} onClick={() => void confirm()}>
            {t('tokens.revokeConfirm')}
          </Button>
        </Group>
      </Stack>
    </Modal>
  );
}
```

### Task 23: Web: audit log page

**Files:**
- Create or replace (Task 19 may have left a placeholder): `web/src/pages/AuditPage.tsx`
- Modify: `web/src/router.tsx` (add the `audit` child route)
- Modify: `web/src/locales/en.json`, `web/src/locales/tr.json` (top-level `audit` object)
- Test: `web/src/pages/AuditPage.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `listAudit`, `verifyAudit`, `auditExportUrl` (Task 19), `formatDateTime` (Task 22), `ErrorAlert` (Task 19), wire JSON `AuditEntry` / `AuditPage` / `AuditReport` (Task 15), audit action names from Tasks 10–12 and 15.
- Produces: `export function AuditPage(): JSX.Element`; `export function actionKey(action: string): string` (`"auth.login"` → `"audit.actions.auth_login"`). UI strings used by Task 24: heading "Audit log" / "Denetim kaydı", "Verify integrity" / "Bütünlüğü doğrula", "The audit log is intact." / "Denetim kaydı sağlam.", action label "User created" / "Kullanıcı oluşturuldu". The verification result is rendered in an element with `role="status"`.

**Names used from Tasks 19–21 (do not rename):** `apiFetch`/`ApiError` (`web/src/api/client.ts`); types `User`, `Role`, `TokenScope`, `ApiToken`, `CreatedToken`, `AuditEntry`, `AuditResult`, `AuditPage`, `AuditReport`, `SessionInfo` (`web/src/api/types.ts`); endpoint functions returning the wire JSON unchanged (`listUsers(): Promise<{ items: User[] }>`, `createUser(req)`, `updateUser(id, req)`, `resetUserMfa(id)`, `resetUserPassword(id, password)`, `revokeUserSessions(id)`, `listTokens(userId?): Promise<{ items: ApiToken[] }>`, `createToken(req): Promise<CreatedToken>`, `revokeToken(id)`, `listAudit(q: { limit?; cursor?; action?; actor?; result? }): Promise<AuditPage>`, `verifyAudit(): Promise<AuditReport>`, `auditExportUrl(format: 'csv' | 'json'): string`); `useAuth()` (`web/src/auth/AuthProvider.tsx`); `RequireRole` (`web/src/auth/guards.tsx`); `ErrorAlert` (renders `role="alert"` with the request id); `useStepUp().run` (`web/src/components/StepUpProvider.tsx`); `renderRoute(path, { languages })` (`web/src/test/render.tsx`); msw `server` (`web/src/test/server.ts`, started with `onUnhandledRequest: 'error'`); fixtures `adminSession`, `operatorSession`, `auditorSession` (`web/src/test/fixtures.ts`).

Design notes: the table is newest first (server order) and pages with `next_cursor` through TanStack Query's `useInfiniteQuery`. Action names are stored as codes; the page translates known codes (`audit.actions.<code with dots replaced by _>`) and shows unknown codes (from newer versions) as they are, so nothing is ever hidden. "Verify integrity" is a read-type action available to Admin and Auditor (permission `audit.read`); exports are plain links (`GET`, the session cookie authenticates, the server audits every export). Numbers go through `Intl.NumberFormat` (1,234 vs 1.234), hashes and IPs are shown in monospace and never translated.

`web/src/pages/AuditPage.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import { auditExportUrl } from '../api/endpoints';
import type { AuditEntry, SessionInfo } from '../api/types';
import { adminSession, sessionFor } from '../test/fixtures';
import { renderRoute } from '../test/render';
import { server } from '../test/server';

function entry(seq: number, action: string, result: AuditEntry['result'] = 'success'): AuditEntry {
  return {
    seq,
    at: '2026-09-30T08:00:00Z',
    actor_type: 'user',
    actor_id: adminSession.user.id,
    actor_name: 'admin',
    ip: '10.0.0.7',
    action,
    object_type: 'user',
    object_id: 'x',
    result,
    details: {},
    request_id: `req-${seq}`,
    hash: 'ab'.repeat(32),
  };
}

function signedInAs(session: SessionInfo) {
  server.use(
    http.get('/api/v1/auth/session', () => HttpResponse.json(session)),
    http.get('/api/v1/setup/status', () =>
      HttpResponse.json({ admin_exists: true, recovery_passphrase_set: true, completed: true, default_locale: 'en' }),
    ),
    http.get('/api/v1/version', () => HttpResponse.json({ version: '1.2.3', commit: 'abc', build_time: 't' })),
  );
}

describe('AuditPage', () => {
  beforeEach(() => localStorage.clear());

  it('shows localized actions, falls back to the raw action and pages with the cursor', async () => {
    signedInAs(adminSession);
    const cursors: (string | null)[] = [];
    server.use(
      http.get('/api/v1/audit', ({ request }) => {
        const cursor = new URL(request.url).searchParams.get('cursor');
        cursors.push(cursor);
        if (!cursor) {
          return HttpResponse.json({ items: [entry(3, 'user.created'), entry(2, 'custom.future_action', 'denied')], next_cursor: '2' });
        }
        return HttpResponse.json({ items: [entry(1, 'setup.admin_created')], next_cursor: null });
      }),
    );
    await renderRoute('/audit');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Audit log' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await screen.findByText('User created')).toBeInTheDocument();
    const raw = screen.getByText('custom.future_action').closest('tr')!;
    expect(within(raw).getByText('Denied')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Load more' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Administrator created during setup')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(cursors).toEqual([null, '2']);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Load more' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('sends the filters to the server', async () => {
    signedInAs(adminSession);
    const queries: string[] = [];
    server.use(
      http.get('/api/v1/audit', ({ request }) => {
        queries.push(new URL(request.url).search);
        return HttpResponse.json({ items: [], next_cursor: null });
      }),
    );
    await renderRoute('/audit');
    expect(await screen.findByText('No entries match these filters.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText(/^Action starts with/), 'auth.');
    await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText(/^Result/), 'denied');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Apply filters' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(queries).toContain('?limit=50&action=auth.&result=denied'));
  });

  it('reports an intact chain and offers exports', async () => {
    signedInAs(adminSession);
    server.use(
      http.get('/api/v1/audit', () => HttpResponse.json({ items: [entry(1, 'auth.login')], next_cursor: null })),
      http.post('/api/v1/audit/verify', () =>
        HttpResponse.json({ intact: true, checked: 1234, head_seq: 1234, head_hash: 'cd'.repeat(32), first_bad_seq: null, problem: null }),
      ),
    );
    await renderRoute('/audit');
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Verify integrity' }));
    const status = await screen.findByRole('status');
    expect(status).toHaveTextContent('The audit log is intact.');
    expect(status).toHaveTextContent('1,234 entries checked.');
    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Export CSV' })).toHaveAttribute('href', auditExportUrl('csv'));
    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Export JSON' })).toHaveAttribute('href', auditExportUrl('json'));
  });

  it('reports a broken chain in Turkish for an auditor', async () => {
    signedInAs(sessionFor('auditor', 'tr')); // the signed-in user's locale wins over the browser language
    server.use(
      http.get('/api/v1/audit', () => HttpResponse.json({ items: [entry(1, 'auth.logout', 'failure')], next_cursor: null })),
      http.post('/api/v1/audit/verify', () =>
        HttpResponse.json({ intact: false, checked: 40, head_seq: 41, head_hash: 'ef'.repeat(32), first_bad_seq: 17, problem: 'hash_mismatch' }),
      ),
    );
    await renderRoute('/audit', { languages: ['tr-TR'] });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Denetim kaydı' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    const row = (await screen.findByText('Çıkış')).closest('tr')!;
    expect(within(row).getByText('Başarısız')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Bütünlüğü doğrula' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('status')).toHaveTextContent('Denetim kaydında 17 numaralı kayıtta bütünlük sorunu var.');
    // Only the page body counts: the shell header always has language and sign-out controls.
    const buttons = within(screen.getByRole('main')).getAllByRole('button').map((b) => b.textContent);
    expect(buttons).toEqual(['Bütünlüğü doğrula', 'Filtreleri uygula']);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the page**

`web/src/pages/AuditPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Alert, Badge, Button, Code, Group, NativeSelect, Stack, Table, Text, TextInput, Title } from '@mantine/core';
import { useInfiniteQuery, useMutation } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { auditExportUrl, listAudit, verifyAudit } from '../api/endpoints';
import type { AuditEntry, AuditResult } from '../api/types';
import { ErrorAlert } from '../components/ErrorAlert';
import { formatDateTime } from '../datetime';

const PAGE_SIZE = 50;
const RESULT_COLORS: Record<AuditResult, string> = { success: 'green', failure: 'orange', denied: 'red' };

interface Filters {
  action: string;
  result: '' | AuditResult;
}

// actionKey maps a stored action ("auth.login") to its label key ("audit.actions.auth_login").
export function actionKey(action: string): string {
  return `audit.actions.${action.replace(/\./g, '_')}`;
}

export function AuditPage() {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<Filters>({ action: '', result: '' });
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<Filters>({ action: '', result: '' });

  const entries = useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: ['audit', filters],
    queryFn: ({ pageParam }) =>
      listAudit({ limit: PAGE_SIZE, cursor: pageParam, action: filters.action || undefined, result: filters.result || undefined }),
    initialPageParam: null as string | null,
    getNextPageParam: (last) => last.next_cursor,
  });
  const verify = useMutation({ mutationFn: verifyAudit });

  const label = (e: AuditEntry) => (i18n.exists(actionKey(e.action)) ? t(actionKey(e.action)) : e.action);
  const actor = (e: AuditEntry) => (e.actor_type === 'system' ? t('audit.systemActor', { name: e.actor_name }) : e.actor_name);
  const apply = (ev: FormEvent) => {
    ev.preventDefault();
    setFilters({ action: draft.action.trim(), result: draft.result });
  };
  const items = entries.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items) ?? [];

  return (
    <Stack gap="md">
      <Group justify="space-between">
        <Title order={1}>{t('audit.title')}</Title>
        <Group gap="xs">
          <Button onClick={() => verify.mutate()} loading={verify.isPending}>
            {t('audit.verify')}
          </Button>
          <Button component="a" href={auditExportUrl('csv')} download variant="default">
            {t('audit.exportCsv')}
          </Button>
          <Button component="a" href={auditExportUrl('json')} download variant="default">
            {t('audit.exportJson')}
          </Button>
        </Group>
      </Group>

      {verify.isError && <ErrorAlert error={verify.error} />}
      {verify.isSuccess && (
        <Alert
          color={verify.data.intact ? 'green' : 'red'}
          role="status"
          title={verify.data.intact ? t('audit.intact') : t('audit.broken', { seq: verify.data.first_bad_seq ?? 0 })}
        >
          <Stack gap={4}>
            <Text size="sm">
              {t('audit.checked', { count: verify.data.checked, formatted: new Intl.NumberFormat(i18n.language).format(verify.data.checked) })}
            </Text>
            {verify.data.intact && (
              <Text size="sm">
                {t('audit.headHash')} <Code>{verify.data.head_hash}</Code>
              </Text>
            )}
            {!verify.data.intact && <Text size="sm">{t('audit.brokenHelp')}</Text>}
          </Stack>
        </Alert>
      )}

      <form onSubmit={apply}>
        <Group align="flex-end" gap="sm">
          <TextInput
            label={t('audit.filters.action')}
            value={draft.action}
            onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, action: e.currentTarget.value })}
          />
          <NativeSelect
            label={t('audit.filters.result')}
            value={draft.result}
            onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, result: e.currentTarget.value as Filters['result'] })}
            data={[
              { value: '', label: t('audit.filters.anyResult') },
              { value: 'success', label: t('audit.result.success') },
              { value: 'failure', label: t('audit.result.failure') },
              { value: 'denied', label: t('audit.result.denied') },
            ]}
          />
          <Button type="submit" variant="light">
            {t('audit.filters.apply')}
          </Button>
        </Group>
      </form>

      {entries.isError && <ErrorAlert error={entries.error} />}
      {entries.isSuccess && items.length === 0 && <Text c="gray.7">{t('audit.empty')}</Text>}
      {items.length > 0 && (
        <Table striped>
          <Table.Thead>
            <Table.Tr>
              <Table.Th>{t('audit.columns.time')}</Table.Th>
              <Table.Th>{t('audit.columns.user')}</Table.Th>
              <Table.Th>{t('audit.columns.action')}</Table.Th>
              <Table.Th>{t('audit.columns.result')}</Table.Th>
              <Table.Th>{t('audit.columns.ip')}</Table.Th>
            </Table.Tr>
          </Table.Thead>
          <Table.Tbody>
            {items.map((e) => (
              <Table.Tr key={e.seq}>
                <Table.Td>{formatDateTime(e.at, i18n.language)}</Table.Td>
                <Table.Td>{actor(e)}</Table.Td>
                <Table.Td>{label(e)}</Table.Td>
                <Table.Td>
                  <Badge color={RESULT_COLORS[e.result]} variant="light">
                    {t(`audit.result.${e.result}`)}
                  </Badge>
                </Table.Td>
                <Table.Td>
                  <Text ff="monospace" size="sm">
                    {e.ip ?? ''}
                  </Text>
                </Table.Td>
              </Table.Tr>
            ))}
          </Table.Tbody>
        </Table>
      )}
      {entries.hasNextPage && (
        <Button variant="default" onClick={() => void entries.fetchNextPage()} loading={entries.isFetchingNextPage}>
          {t('audit.loadMore')}
        </Button>
      )}
    </Stack>
  );
}
```

### Task 24: End-to-end tests (EN/TR, axe) for the Phase 1 owner journey

**Files:**
- Modify: `web/playwright.config.ts`
- Create: `web/e2e/helpers.ts`, `web/e2e/phase1.spec.ts`
- Replace: `web/e2e/smoke.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the e2e build from Task 16 (`make e2e-up`; endpoints `GET/POST /test/clock`, `POST /test/reset` → 204, `POST /test/setup-token` → `{"code"}`); UI copy of Tasks 19–23; `data-testid` values `totp-secret`, `recovery-codes`, `new-token`; the server's TOTP rules (SHA-1, 6 digits, 30 s, a step is accepted once).
- Produces: `web/e2e/helpers.ts` exports `COPY`, `Lang`, `Copy`, `Account`, `ADMIN`, `PASSPHRASE`, `browserLocale`, `field`, `either`, `uiLang`, `resetInstall(request)`, `serverNow(request): Promise<number>`, `advanceClock(request, seconds)`, `setupCode(request)`, `totp(secretBase32, atMs)`, `freshCode(request, secret)`, `enrollTotp(page, request, c)`, `runWizard(page, request, lang, admin?)` → `{ totpSecret, recoveryCodes }`, `submitLogin(page, c, username, password): Promise<number>`, `signIn(page, request, account, totpSecret)`, `firstSignIn(page, request, account): Promise<string>`, `signOut(page)`, `seriousViolations(page)`, `expectAccessible(page, screen)`.

What the suite proves (spec §26.3 owner steps, in English and Turkish): the wizard refuses a short password and creates the admin with TOTP and recovery codes; sign-in needs the password and a code; a wrong code is refused with a clear message; five wrong passwords slow the account down and ten lock it for 15 minutes (even the right password is refused), after which it works again; sign-out works; 31 idle minutes end the session; Operator and Auditor (enrolling TOTP at their first sign-in) see different things; the audit log verifies as intact; setup, sign-in, home, users, tokens, account and audit screens have no serious or critical axe violations.

Rules baked into the helpers:
- The server clock is shared test state: every test starts with `POST /test/reset` and Playwright runs with one worker.
- TOTP codes are computed from the **server** time (`GET /test/clock`), and `freshCode` first advances the server clock by 31 s so a code for an already used step is never sent (replay protection would reject it).
- Labels are matched exactly with an optional trailing " *" (Mantine marks required fields), so "Password" never matches "Repeat password" and "Parola" never matches "Parolayı tekrar girin" (`\b` does not work after Turkish letters).
- A user created by an admin gets the server default locale; if Task 19 applies the user's own locale after sign-in, the UI may switch language. Screens after such a sign-in pick their strings from `<html lang>` (`uiLang`) or match either language (`either`).
- Chromium logs the expected 401 from `/api/v1/auth/session` of a signed-out app as "Failed to load resource"; the smoke test ignores exactly that message and fails on every other console error (CSP violations included).

- [ ] **Step 1: Configure Playwright for a shared, resettable server**

`web/playwright.config.ts`:
```ts
import { defineConfig, devices } from '@playwright/test';

// The host port is ALICE_PORT (deploy/.env); ALICE_BASE_URL overrides everything.
const port = process.env.ALICE_PORT ?? '443';
const baseURL = process.env.ALICE_BASE_URL ?? (port === '443' ? 'https://localhost' : `https://localhost:${port}`);

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: './e2e',
  // Every test resets the one shared server (POST /test/reset), so tests must never overlap.
  workers: 1,
  fullyParallel: false,
  timeout: 180_000,
  expect: { timeout: 15_000 },
  retries: process.env.CI ? 1 : 0,
  reporter: [['list'], ['html', { open: 'never' }]],
  use: {
    baseURL,
    ignoreHTTPSErrors: true, // the stack uses a temporary self-signed certificate
    trace: 'retain-on-failure',
  },
  projects: [{ name: 'chromium', use: { ...devices['Desktop Chrome'] } }],
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the helpers**

`web/e2e/helpers.ts`:
```ts
import { createHmac } from 'node:crypto';
import AxeBuilder from '@axe-core/playwright';
import { expect, type APIRequestContext, type Locator, type Page } from '@playwright/test';

export type Lang = 'en' | 'tr';

// UI copy from the Phase 1 plan (exact strings shown by the web app).
```
